### [109] [AttentionCap: Transformer Based Capacitance Matrix Learning Toward Full-Chip Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08161)
*Jiechen Huang,Hector R. Rodriguez,Dingcheng Yang,Zuochang Ye,Yibo Lin,Wenjian Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AttentionCap，一种基于Transformer的电容矩阵学习模型，通过Gram表示框架、对称注意力输出层和归一化拉普拉斯损失，实现了多工艺节点下的高精度电容提取，在未见真实设计上自电容/耦合电容误差仅0.67%/3.99%，推理速度比CNN快192倍，且可迁移至新节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着工艺节点进步，基于规则的模式匹配电容提取精度难以维持，而现有MLP和CNN方法只能处理固定金属层组合和特定工艺节点，限制了实际可用性。

Method: 提出AttentionCap，包含：(1) Gram表示框架将电容矩阵转化为适合Transformer处理的形式；(2) 物理对齐的对称注意力输出层保证预测结果满足互易对称性；(3) 归一化拉普拉斯损失函数；(4) 工艺节点嵌入实现多节点联合学习。

Result: 在多层多节点未见真实设计上，自电容误差0.67%，耦合电容误差3.99%；相比CNN-Cap基线，自/耦合误差分别降低4.6倍/5.7倍，推理速度提升192倍；预训练模型仅需5K样本和4K微调步数即可迁移至新节点。

Conclusion: AttentionCap在未见真实设计上具有足够精度，且对新建工艺节点具有强迁移能力，为现代EDA工作流提供了高实用价值的解决方案。

Abstract: As capacitance extraction accuracy of rule-based pattern matching becomes difficult to sustain at advanced nodes, a growing trend emerges to develop deep-learning-based 2D capacitance models. However, existing MLP- and CNN-based methods constrain their input to fixed metal-layer combinations in a specific process node, limiting their usability in practice. Recognizing the inherent similarity between capacitance matrix and the prevailing attention mechanism, we propose AttentionCap, a customized Transformer for capacitance matrix learning, with a Gram representation framework, a physics-aligned symmetric-attention output layer, and a novel normalized Laplacian loss. We also introduce a process-node embedding to enable multi-node learning. Trained on synthetic data, AttentionCap attains 0.67\%/3.99\% self/coupling-capacitance error on unseen real designs under a multi-layer and multi-node setting, surpassing the CNN-Cap baseline with 4.6$\times$/5.7$\times$ lower self/coupling error and 192$\times$ faster inference speed. A pretrained AttentionCap accurately transfers to an unseen node with only 5K samples and 4K finetuning steps. With sufficient accuracy on unseen real designs and strong transferability to new process nodes, AttentionCap offers highly practical value for modern EDA workflows. Code and data are available at https://github.com/THU-numbda/AttentionCap.

</details>


### [110] [Explaining Data Mixing Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08167)
*Rui Dai,Shuran Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的理论框架来解释多领域数据混合中的缩放定律，基于领域间共享基础技能、差异专业技能的分布假设，识别出容量竞争和噪声减少两个关键因素，在拟合损失曲面和跨规模预测高效数据混合方面优于现有经验方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已建立多领域数据混合的经验缩放定律来预测模型性能，但缺乏对这些损失行为的理论理解。

Method: 将标准神经缩放定律（Kaplan和Chinchilla）的理论视角扩展到多领域场景。假设各领域在基础技能上重叠、在专业技能上 diverging，识别出两个关键因素：容量竞争（有限模型容量的分配导致领域损失全局耦合）和噪声减少（最优权重向更难学习的领域偏移以最小化整体噪声）。

Result: 该框架在拟合损失曲面时平均相对误差更低，能识别出更高效的训练混合方案，且成功跨规模外推（用小尺度数据拟合的参数预测大尺度下的高效混合），同时使用的参数量显著少于以往经验定律。

Conclusion: 提出的理论统一框架能够解释多领域数据混合的底层机制，在拟合精度和跨规模预测能力上优于现有基线方法，且更加参数高效。

Abstract: Recent research has established empirical scaling laws to predict model performance on multi-domain data mixtures. However, a theoretical understanding of these model loss behaviors remains absent. In this work, we propose a unified framework to explain the underlying mechanics of data mixing. Our approach extends theoretical perspectives originally developed for standard neural scaling laws (e.g., Kaplan and Chinchilla) to the multi-domain setting. Based on the distributional assumption that domains overlap on fundamental skills while diverging on specialized skills, we identify two key factors that govern the domain losses of models trained on different data mixtures: \textit{Capacity Competition}, where the allocation of finite model capacity couples domain losses globally, and \textit{Noise Reduction}, where optimal weights shift toward harder-to-learn domains to minimize overall noise. Empirical evaluations show that our framework outperforms existing baselines by fitting the loss landscape with a lower Mean Relative Error and identifying higher-performing training mixtures. Most importantly, our model successfully extrapolates across scales, predicting highly effective mixtures for large, unseen scales using parameters fitted on smaller ones. In addition, our model achieves these results using significantly fewer parameters compared to previous empirical laws. Our code is available at https://github.com/meiqwq/Explaining-Data-Mixing-Scaling-Laws.

</details>


### [111] [Frequency-Domain Latent Attention Gating for Cross-Domain Token Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08191)
*Kewei Li,Rongying Zhang,Xueli Wang,Xiwen Gong,Zhongjian Wang,Lan Huang,Ruochi Zhang,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FLaG是一个基于FFT的即插即用聚合模块，通过频域变换、可学习潜在查询和通道门控机制增强token表示，在AMP活性预测、图像分类和文本分类任务上表现优异，并深入分析了频带贡献与模型行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有池化方法仅在原始token域操作，缺乏对频域信息的利用，token聚合成为模型瓶颈。

Method: 利用实值FFT将token表示变换到频域，用可学习潜在查询汇总频谱分量，应用通道级门控，再重建增强后的时域token进行最终池化。

Result: 在ESM2-8M抗菌肽任务和CIFAR-100上取得最明显提升，在IMDB和GLUE上保持竞争力。低频带贡献最大，高频带模式更具样本特异性，门控是广泛共享的频谱重加权阶段，高螺旋肽段表现出更强的平均频谱敏感性。

Conclusion: FLaG作为一种轻量即插即用的频域聚合模块，有效提升了多种任务的性能，且其内部机制具有良好的可解释性。

Abstract: Token aggregation is a common bottleneck in models that map token representations to sample-level predictions, yet most pooling methods operate only in the original token domain. We propose FLaG, a plug-in aggregation module that transforms token representations with the real FFT, summarizes spectral components with learnable latent queries, applies a channel-wise gate, and reconstructs enhanced time-domain tokens for final pooling. We evaluate FLaG on antimicrobial peptide (AMP) activity prediction with ESM2, image classification with ResNet18 on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, and text classification with RoBERTa on IMDB and GLUE. FLaG achieves its clearest gains on the ESM2-8M antimicrobial peptide tasks and on CIFAR-100, while remaining competitive with strong text baselines on IMDB and GLUE. Then we probe its behavior on the AMP setting with band knockouts, gate summaries, residue perturbations, latent-query readouts, and structure-proxy stratification. We find that low-frequency bands contribute the most overall, and the remaining higher-band pattern is more sample-specific. The gate acts as a broadly shared spectral reweighting stage and the cross-attention patterns are sample-specific with mild query-wise differentiation, and higher-helix peptides exhibit stronger average spectral sensitivity in both bacteria. The supplementary materials, source code and data are released at https://www.healthinformaticslab.org/supp/ and https://github.com/Kewei2023/AMPCliff/tree/FLaG.

</details>


### [112] [Neural Field Tokenizations with Hierarchy and Spatial Locality Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08204)
*Alonso Urbano,David W. Romero,Max Zimmer,Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LH-NeF框架，利用局部性和层次性先验，通过前馈编码替代元学习内循环，实现模态无关的神经场表征学习，在图像、3D形状和气候场中达到或超越基线性能，同时内存减少42倍、批次大小提升133倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经场表征学习方法主要依赖逐样本元学习，其内循环优化内存开销大、可扩展性差；而前馈编码方法通常引入模态特异性假设，牺牲了神经场学习的通用性。需要一种既保持模态无关性又高效可扩展的表征学习框架。

Method: 提出LH-NeF框架：设计一个保持局部性的层次化编码器，将原始坐标-值场观测映射为结构化token，在训练过程中从这些token重建场。核心思想是将局部性和层次性作为通用先验注入模型，无需牺牲模态无关性。

Result: 相比最强的模态无关基线，LH-NeF内存使用减少42倍，支持增大133倍的批次大小。在图像、3D形状和气候场等模态上，在重建和下游任务中匹配或超越模态无关、模态特定和专用生成式神经场基线。

Conclusion: 局部性和层次性是有效的通用先验，可在不牺牲模态无关性的前提下提升神经场表征学习效率；前馈编码可替代元学习内循环，显著提升可扩展性。

Abstract: Neural fields parameterize data as functions from coordinates to values, providing a unified framework for representation learning across modalities. Existing approaches are dominated by per-sample meta-learning, which scales poorly due to memory-intensive inner-loop optimization. The natural alternative -- feed-forward encoding -- typically introduces modality-specific assumptions, sacrificing the generality that makes learning with neural fields attractive. We argue that locality and hierarchy are useful priors for learning field representations that can be injected without compromising modality-agnosticism. We propose LH-NeF, a framework to learn general-purpose tokenized representations of continuous signals. A locality-preserving hierarchical encoder maps raw coordinate-value field observations to structured tokens, from which the field is reconstructed during training. By replacing meta-learning's inner loop with a single forward pass, LH-NeF uses 42$\times$ less memory and supports 133$\times$ larger batches than the strongest modality-agnostic baseline. Across images, 3D shapes, and climate fields, our learned representations match or exceed performance of modality-agnostic, modality-specific, and specialized generative neural field baselines on both reconstruction and downstream tasks.

</details>


### [113] [Public Machine Learning Solver Framework for Novices in the Machine Learning Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08212)
*Lokman Saleh,Hafedh Mili,Mounir Boukadoum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向非专家的半自动化机器学习平台，该平台结合专家定义的算法选择准则与迁移学习，自动提取数据特征，通过一阶逻辑推理推荐完整的ML流水线，并支持持续更新和众包专家参与。这是首个免费公开的、系统化捕获专家知识以指导非专家解决ML问题的在线框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习问题的解决通常需要专家知识，而非专家缺乏相关能力。现有系统分为全自动AutoML、专家速查表和基于选择准则的决策支持系统三类，但各自存在不足：全自动系统缺乏透明度，速查表过于简化，决策支持系统推荐单一算法而非完整流水线。

Method: 平台结合类别2（专家速查表）和类别3（基于选择准则的决策支持系统），采用一阶逻辑对知识库进行推理，集成迁移学习，自动从用户数据集中提取数据特征（如类别不平衡、缺失值），推荐按相关性排序的完整流水线，并通过众包平台实现专家知识的持续更新。

Result: 该平台是首个免费公开的在线框架，能够系统化捕获和操作化专家知识，为非专家提供结构化、透明的ML问题求解指导。平台采用增量构建方式，支持新算法、新准则和新领域知识的无缝集成。

Conclusion: 该平台填补了现有AutoML系统和简单速查表之间的空白，通过半自动化、智能化的方式为非专家提供完整的ML流水线推荐，具有透明性、可扩展性和用户友好性。

Abstract: Solving machine learning problems is complex and typically reserved for experts. Over the past two decades, systems have emerged to support non-experts. Based on our review, we identify three categories: (1) fully automated AutoML systems, (2) expert cheat sheets for algorithm selection, and (3) decision-support systems using selection criteria (accuracy, transparency, data requirements). We propose a new platform combining categories 2 and 3 to deliver semi-automated, intelligent solution recommendations for non-experts. Unlike existing approaches that recommend a single algorithm, our platform suggests a complete pipeline tailored to the user's problem. It integrates expert-defined selection criteria with transfer learning and automatically extracts data characteristics (e.g., class imbalance, missing values) from user-provided datasets. The platform uses first-order logic to reason over its knowledge base and recommends suitable algorithms ranked by relevance. It features a user-friendly interface and connects to a crowdsourcing platform for ML experts, ensuring continuous updates. The platform is built incrementally, allowing seamless integration of new algorithms, criteria, and domain knowledge. To our knowledge, this is the first free, publicly accessible online framework that systematically captures and operationalizes expert knowledge to guide non-experts in solving ML problems in a structured, transparent manner.

</details>


### [114] [How Deep Are Deep GPs, Really? A Sharp Threshold and a Non-Gaussian Limit for Compositional GPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08218)
*Mark Kozdoba,Shie Mannor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度高斯过程在层数趋于无穷时的先验行为，发现存在一个尖锐的带宽阈值 r_c(d) = Θ(√d)：高于该阈值时先验退化为常数函数（无用），低于该阈值时先验收敛到非退化、非高斯的极限分布 π_Ẑ，且坐标间存在非零依赖性。实验验证了该阈值并揭示了极限分布的多模态特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解深度贝叶斯模型中复合函数的组合先验性质，特别是深度高斯过程先验在深度增加时的极限行为，以确定是否存在有意义的非平凡极限分布。

Method: 理论分析：识别尖锐带宽阈值 r_c(d) = Θ(√d)；证明阈值上下两个截然不同的极限行为（退化 vs 非退化非高斯极限分布）；实验验证：在不同维度 d 下验证阈值并探索极限分布的多模态特性。

Result: (1) 确定了尖锐阈值 r_c(d) = Θ(√d)，改进了之前较宽松的界；(2) 证明当 r < r_c(d) 时先验收敛到非退化、非高斯的极限分布 π_Ẑ，且坐标间存在非零依赖性；(3) 实验验证了阈值并发现极限分布在阈值附近呈现复杂的多模态行为，且该区域随 d 增大而变窄。

Conclusion: 与已知的退化情况相反，深度高斯过程先验在低于阈值时可以存在有意义的非平凡极限分布，这对深度贝叶斯模型的先验设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Compositional priors describe the generic properties of layered functions in deep Bayesian models, where deep neural networks with random weights are a canonical example.In the wide-network limit, the prior is a Gaussian process with a depth-dependent kernel, and its behaviour as depth grows has been extensively studied through this kernel. Here, we study another case, where each layer itself is a vector valued Gaussian process, and our aim is similarly to understand the limiting behaviour of the prior as depth grows.
  Previous GP work has established that for the RBF kernel and a certain range of bandwidths $r$, the prior degenerates in the limit, converging to the set of constant functions -- which is not useful as a probabilistic model. In this paper we establish several new results. First, we identify a sharp bandwidth threshold $r_c(d) = Θ(\sqrt{d})$ above which the limit is degenerate, strengthening the earlier bounds. Second, and more importantly, we show that for $r$ below the threshold $r_c(d)$ the prior converges to a limit distribution $π_{\bar{Z}}$. We also prove that these distributions are non-degenerate and non-Gaussian, with non-vanishing dependence between coordinates. In contrast to the previously known degenerate regime, deep Gaussian process priors can therefore admit non-trivial limits.
  Empirically, we verify the threshold across a range of dimensions $d$, and demonstrate a complex multimodal behaviour of the limit distributions $π_{\bar{Z}}$ -- a regime that becomes increasingly narrow with $d$ and would be hard to identify without knowing the threshold.

</details>


### [115] [De novo molecular generation with optical property preconditioning at the token level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08221)
*Haozhe Huang,Manuel Gonzalez Lastre,Hyun Suk Park,Jorge A. Campos-Gonzalez-Angulo,Xinjian Liu,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于标记条件自回归语言模型（GPT2）的OLED分子生成方法，在低数据 regime 下进行基准测试。模型通过离散属性标记进行条件控制，生成分子以垂直吸收能、振子强度和HOMO-LUMO gap为目标。TDDFT评估显示生成库能复现训练分布的光学性质，但向低分子量偏移；条件控制具有方向性但不完全正交。化学型分析表明，中等共轭芳香碳骨架能提升目标满足度，而吸电子基团（如芳基腈）则系统性红移并降低可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计具有目标光学性质的OLED分子具有挑战性，原因在于高质量数据的稀缺以及生成模型在化学基序间条件控制的可靠性有限。

Method: 使用GPT2模型在大规模化学语料上预训练，增强离散属性标记，并通过多任务优化进行微调。条件控制目标包括垂直吸收能、振子强度，辅助使用HOMO-LUMO gap作为电子描述符。生成分子在TDDFT水平进行评估。

Result: 生成库能复现训练分布的主要光学性质支持域，但分子量更轻、重原子更少。标记级条件控制在各条件区间内方向一致，但未完全正交且存在局部校准异常。化学型分析发现，中等共轭芳香碳骨架提升目标满足度，而吸电子基团（尤其是芳基腈）导致系统性红移和可控性下降。

Conclusion: 研究建立了条件OLED分子生成的定量基准，并指出模型可靠性必须在化学上有意义的子空间（而非仅聚合性质分布）中评估。

Abstract: Designing OLED molecules with targeted optical properties remains challenging due to the scarcity of high-quality data and the limited reliability of conditional control in generative models across chemical motifs. Here, we benchmark a token-conditioned autoregressive language model for OLED molecular generation in a realistic low-data regime. A GPT2 model is pretrained on large chemical corpora, augmented with discrete property tokens, and fine-tuned using multi-task optimisation. Conditioning targets vertical absorption energy and oscillator strength, with the HOMO-LUMO gap included as an auxiliary electronic descriptor.
  Generated molecules are evaluated at the TDDFT level to assess distributional fidelity and controllability. The generated library reproduces the dominant optical-property support of the training distribution while shifting towards lower molecular weight and fewer heavy atoms. Token-level control is consistently directional across conditioning bins, but is not fully orthogonal and exhibits local calibration irregularities. A chemotype-resolved analysis further shows that controllability depends strongly on local electronic environments: moderately conjugated aromatic-carbon motifs are associated with improved joint target satisfaction, whereas electron-withdrawing motifs, particularly aryl nitriles, show systematic red-shifting and reduced controllability.
  These results establish a quantitative benchmark for conditional OLED molecular generation and show that model reliability must be assessed in chemically meaningful subspaces rather than from aggregate property distributions alone.

</details>


### [116] [GPT-Micro: A large language paradigm for accelerated, inexpensive, and thermodynamics-consistent discovery of constitutive models in manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08238)
*Soumik Dutta,Kiarash Naghavi Khanghah,Sania Shree,Logan McNeil,Thomas Feldhausen,Hongyi Xu,Rajiv Malhotra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GPT-Micro范式，一种结合大语言模型、热力学守恒定律和稀疏数据集的自主本构模型发现框架，在印刷电子工艺测试中实现超70%数据缩减、400倍模型发现加速，并生成符合物理规律、可解释的分析模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统本构模型发现依赖人工经验导致效率低下且增量式改进；传统机器学习需要大量数据生成成本高；现有LLM方法忽略热力学基本定律的不可违背性。需要一种自主、数据稀疏且热力学兼容的模型发现方法。

Method: GPT-Micro框架：从文献中提取语义知识、强制执行基于热力学的守恒定律、利用稀疏数据集，结合LLM驱动模型假设的生成与迭代修正。

Result: 在印刷电子工艺本构建模问题上：相比ML方法减少超70%数据需求且精度不损失；相比人工驱动建模，发现时间从数月缩短至数小时（400倍加速）；发现具有新颖函数形式的模型；生成紧凑、守恒合规、物理完整的分析模型，增强物理可信度和可解释性。

Conclusion: GPT-Micro范式有望实现制造过程中快速、低成本、物理可信且可解释的微观结构建模。

Abstract: Constitutive modeling of the relationship between process-imposed material states and fundamental material properties is critical to control of material microstructure in manufacturing processes. The limited accuracy resulting from the typical reliance on fallible human expertise and intuition for postulation and revision of the models functional form results in incremental and time consuming model discovery. Conventional Machine Learning (ML) incurs significant cost and time of data generation. Model discovery using Large Language Models (LLMs) suffers from the above issues and/or ignores the inviolability of fundamental thermodynamics laws. This work creates a novel GPT-Micro paradigm for autonomous, data sparse, and thermodynamics-compliant discovery of de-novo constitutive models. This framework seamlessly integrates semantic knowledge extraction from literature, enforcement of thermodynamics-based conservation laws, and sparse datasets, with LLM-driven generation and refinement of model hypotheses. Validation is performed for a long-intractable constitutive modeling problem in a printed electronics process testbed. This reveals significant and simultaneous advantages over the state-of-the-art including: (a) More than 70 percent reduction in data burden relative to ML-based modeling without loss in accuracy; (b) 400X reduction in discovery time after data generation, from months to hours, relative to human-driven modeling; (c) Discovery of models with novel functional forms without subjective human choice of a starting hypothesis; (d) Enhanced physics-rooted trustworthiness, human interpretability, and mechanistic insight via synthesis of compact, conservation-compliant, and physically complete analytical models. The potential of GPT-Micro to realize rapid, low-cost, physically trustworthy, and interpretable microstructure modeling across the manufacturing landscape is discussed.

</details>


### [117] [Differentially Private Synthetic Data via APIs 4: Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08259)
*Toan Tran,Arturs Backurs,Zinan Lin,Victor Reis,Li Xiong,Sergey Yekhanin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tab-PE算法，将私有进化（PE）框架扩展至表格数据生成，通过专门设计的启发式算子实现差分隐私保护下的高质量合成数据生成，在高阶相关数据集上性能显著优于现有方法，分类准确率提升高达10%，运行速度快28倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私表格数据生成方法主要关注低阶边缘查询误差最小化，忽略了高阶相关性带来的挑战，导致合成数据质量受限。

Method: 将Private Evolution（PE）框架从图像和文本扩展至表格数据，提出Tab-PE算法。该算法通过迭代进化过程，使用专为表格数据设计的启发式算子生成变体，对候选数据进行私有评分，并选择高质量样本保留和传播，替代了原始PE中依赖大型基础模型的高成本方式。

Result: 在真实和模拟数据集上的实验表明，Tab-PE在高阶相关数据集上显著优于先前基线方法；与最优基线AIM相比，分类准确率提升高达10%，运行速度快28倍。

Conclusion: Tab-PE通过低计算成本的启发式算子使PE框架在表格数据上更具实用性和可扩展性，有效解决了高阶相关性问题。

Abstract: This paper investigates the problem of generating synthetic tabular data with differential privacy (DP) guarantees, enabling data sharing in sensitive domains. Despite extensive study, state-of-the-art methods often focus on minimizing low-order marginal query errors and overlook the challenges posed by high-order correlations. To address this gap, we extend the Private Evolution (PE) framework, originally developed for DP-compliant image and text synthesis, to tabular data. We introduce Tab-PE -- an algorithm for synthetic tabular data generation under DP constraints. Tab-PE iteratively improves a candidate dataset via an evolutionary process that leverages tabular-specialized operators to produce variations, privately scores them, and selects the highest-quality samples to retain and propagate. In contrast to the original PE, which relies on large foundation models, Tab-PE employs heuristic operators with significantly lower computational costs, making PE more practical and scalable for tabular data. Through extensive experiments on real-world and simulation datasets, we demonstrate that Tab-PE substantially outperforms prior baselines on datasets exhibiting high-order correlations. Compared to the best baseline -- AIM, Tab-PE improves classification accuracy by up to 10% while running 28 times faster.

</details>


### [118] [Causal Semantic Alignment for LLM-based Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08262)
*Kexuan Zhang,Xiaobei Zou,Cesare Alippi,Gary G. Yen,Yang Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CVAformer框架，通过将时间序列变量解耦为不变成分和动态成分，并应用因果干预消除动态成分的混杂效应，在变量层面实现LLM与时间序列的对齐。该框架用非因果注意力机制替代标准因果注意力以支持变量级对齐，在长短期、少样本和零样本预测任务上达到或超越SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的时间序列预测方法忽略了时间序列的异质性——动态波动和不变语义相互纠缠，导致在对齐过程中动态成分作为混杂因子引入虚假关联，影响对齐效果。

Method: 提出CVAformer框架：(1) 在对齐前将每个变量显式解耦为不变成分和动态成分；(2) 应用因果干预消除动态成分的混杂效应；(3) 用非因果注意力机制替代标准因果注意力，捕获各时间步上变量间的交互关系，以支持变量级对齐。

Result: 在长期、短期、少样本和零样本预测场景的大量实验中，CVAformer在多数数据集上达到或超越SOTA性能，部分数据集上取得显著更优的精度。

Conclusion: 实验验证了变量级对齐和动态解耦在CVAformer中的有效性，为基于LLM的时间序列任务提供了新视角。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have opened new possibilities for time series forecasting by enabling alignment between temporal patterns and pretrained word embeddings. However, most LLM-based methods overlook the heterogeneous nature of time series, where dynamic fluctuations and invariant semantics are entangled. This entanglement introduces spurious correlations during the alignment, as dynamic components act as confounders by simultaneously influencing invariant components and the resulting aligned embeddings. To address this issue, a variable-level alignment framework CVAformer is proposed. CVAformer explicitly disentangles each variable into invariant and dynamic components just before alignment, and applies causal intervention to mitigate the confounding effect of the dynamics. To better support variable-level alignment, CVAformer replaces the standard causal attention in LLMs with a non-causal attention mechanism that captures interactions among variables at each time step. Extensive experiments across long-term, short-term, few-shot, and zero-shot forecasting settings indicate that CVAformer matches or exceeds state-of-the-art performance on most datasets, and in some cases achieves notably better accuracy. Experimental results validate the effectiveness of variable-level alignment and dynamic disentanglement in CVAformer, offering a new perspective for LLM-based time series tasks.

</details>


### [119] [Causal Agent Replay: Counterfactual Attribution for LLM-Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08275)
*Jaineet Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出因果智能体重演（CAR），通过对智能体运行步骤进行干预式因果分析，精确定位导致失败的关键步骤，而非仅事后观测或整体评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具只能回答"发生了什么"（可观测性）或"是否失败"（评估），但无法定位是哪个步骤导致了失败。简单的启发式方法（如定位执行有害动作的步骤）是错误的，因为执行步骤通常不是决策步骤；LLM裁判归因也不可靠（Who&When基准上步级准确率仅约14%）。

Method: 将智能体运行建模为结构因果模型（SCM），对步骤施加do-操作干预，然后在相同随机策略下前向重放轨迹，测量结果分布的变化。定义了步骤上的干预代数、一个单步对比估计器（含解决随机前向混杂的承诺点规则），以及一个预算有界的蒙特卡洛Shapley估计器（用于在交互步骤间分配信用）。所有效应均报告置信区间。

Result: 在具有植入真实因果关系的合成结构因果模型上验证：对比估计器能恢复关键步骤；Shapley估计器能恢复两步交互（权重0.44, 0.45, ~0；效率总和0.909 vs 解析值0.91）。

Conclusion: CAR是开源的，可在托管或免费本地模型上运行，为智能体失败诊断提供了一种可靠的因果归因方法。

Abstract: When an LLM agent fails -- issues a refund it should not have, calls the wrong tool, leaks data -- existing tooling answers what happened (observability) or whether it passed (evaluation), but not which step caused the failure. The obvious heuristics are wrong: the step that executes the harmful action is usually not the step that decided on it, and LLM-judge attribution is correlational and unreliable (state-of-the-art step-level accuracy on the Who&When benchmark is about 14%). We present Causal Agent Replay (CAR), which answers the question by intervention: it models an agent run as a structural causal model, applies a do-operation to a step, and re-executes the trajectory forward under the same stochastic policy, measuring the shift in the outcome distribution. We define an intervention algebra over agent steps, a single-step contrastive estimator whose point-of-commitment rule resolves a confound specific to stochastic run-forward, and a budget-bounded Monte-Carlo Shapley estimator that splits credit across interacting steps. Every effect is reported with confidence intervals. We validate against synthetic structural causal models with planted ground truth: the contrastive estimator recovers the pivotal step, and Shapley recovers a two-step interaction (0.44, 0.45, ~0; efficiency sum 0.909 versus the analytic 0.91). CAR is open source and runs on hosted or free local models.

</details>


### [120] [Mesh Graph Neural Network Framework for Accelerating Finite Element Simulation for Arbitrary Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08287)
*Josiah D. Kunz,Kamal Choudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于网格图网络（MGN）的代理模型，用于预测具有任意孔洞几何的二维结构部件的冯·米塞斯应力场。该模型利用节点类型、相对边特征和全局特征，而非绝对坐标，实现平移和旋转不变性，从而能够泛化到未见几何形状。在11种板几何形状上训练，在7种未见几何和3种未见载荷条件下测试，最优情况下R²≥0.97，显著优于传统机器学习模型（R²≈0.01-0.86）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限元分析（FEA）在结构设计中至关重要，但计算成本高昂，尤其是在评估多个设计迭代或载荷场景时。机器学习代理模型虽有潜力，但大多数方法难以泛化到不同几何形状，这是关键局限性。

Method: 提出网格图网络（MGN），编码节点类型（如固定边界、自由表面、孔边）、相对边特征（邻居间距离）和全局特征（施加载荷），而非使用绝对节点坐标。该架构具有平移和旋转不变性，无需重新训练即可泛化到未见几何。

Result: 在11种板几何形状上训练，在7种未见几何和3种未见载荷条件下评估。最优情况R²≥0.97，而传统模型（随机森林、梯度提升、K近邻）R²≈0.01-0.86。即使在较差情况下，MGN仍优于传统模型。

Conclusion: 该工作将Pfaff等人的网格基仿真框架扩展到结构力学领域，证明了图神经网络可作为有限元分析的高效代理模型，能够跨不同几何形状进行泛化。

Abstract: Finite element analysis (FEA) is essential for structural design but remains computationally expensive, particularly when evaluating multiple design iterations or load scenarios. Machine learning surrogate models offer a promising alternative, yet most approaches struggle with a critical limitation: generalizing across varying geometries. This work presents a mesh graph network (MGN) for predicting von Mises stress fields in 2D structural components with arbitrary hole geometries. Unlike traditional machine learning approaches that use absolute node coordinates as features, the proposed model builds on existing MGN frameworks that encode node types (e.g., fixed boundary, free surface, hole edge), relative edge features (distance between neighbors), and global features (applied load). This architecture is inherently translation- and rotation-invariant, enabling generalization to unseen geometries without retraining. The MGN was trained on 11 plate geometries under 20 load conditions and evaluated on 7 unseen geometries and 3 unseen loads. In the most favorable case, the model achieves $R^2 \geq 0.97$ on an unseen geometry and unseen load, compared to $R^2 \approx 0.01$--$0.86$ for conventional models (Random Forest, Gradient Boosting , K-Nearest Neighbors) trained on identical data. However, even in less favorable cases, the MGN model still outperforms conventional models. This work extends the mesh-based simulation framework of Pfaff et al. (arXiv:2010.03409) to structural mechanics, demonstrating that graph neural networks can serve as efficient surrogates for finite element analysis across varying geometries.

</details>


### [121] [On solving symmetric multi-type orthogonal non-negative matrix tri-factorization problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08291)
*Rok Hribar,Gregor Papa,Janez Povh,Andrej Kastrin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了对称多类型正交非负矩阵三分解问题，提出了两种启发式算法（基于KKT条件的定点法和基于ADAM的三阶段方法），在合成数据和真实引文网络上的实验表明，两种方法均能恢复接近最优的分解结果，在链接预测、节点聚类和分类任务上优于或媲美SVD、node2vec等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类和网络分析中，非负性提升可解释性，正交性提供自然的分配型结构，但现有方法难以同时保证非负性、正交性和共享因子结构，且优化问题高度非凸。

Method: 提出两种启发式算法：1) 基于KKT条件添加正交惩罚项的定点迭代法；2) 三阶段ADAM方法，结合非负保留优化、正交化及受限ADAM精调。

Result: 合成数据上两种方法均能恢复接近最优的分解且对噪声鲁棒；真实网络上学到的嵌入在链接预测、节点聚类和分类任务中优于或媲美SVD、node2vec等基线。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效求解对称多类型正交非负矩阵三分解问题，在多个网络分析任务中具有竞争力。

Abstract: We study the symmetric multi-type orthogonal non-negative matrix tri-factorization problem, where several symmetric non-negative matrices are simultaneously approximated by factors of the form $GS_{i}G^{\top}$, with a shared non-negative and orthogonal factor $G$. This model is motivated by clustering and network analysis, where non-negativity improves interpretability and orthogonality gives a natural assignment-type structure to the latent factor. Since the resulting optimization problem is highly non-convex, we develop two heuristic algorithms for computing high-quality local solutions. The first one is a fixed point method derived from the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker conditions after adding a penalty term for the orthogonality constraint. The second one is a three-stage ADAM-based method that combines non-negativity-preserving optimization, orthogonalization, and restricted ADAM refinement on the feasible set. We evaluate both methods on synthetic data, including noisy instances, and on citation network benchmarks. The synthetic experiments show that both algorithms recover factorizations close to the optimum and remain stable under noise. On real networks, the learned embeddings are competitive with or better than standard baselines such as SVD, node2vec, and classical link prediction heuristics in link prediction, node clustering, and node classification tasks.

</details>


### [122] [QueryWeaver: Reliable Multi-Tool Query Execution Planning via LLM-Based Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08300)
*Aishwarya Chakravarthy,Vidhi Kulkarni,Duen Horng Chau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个将自然语言查询转换为结构化图并通过确定性规划器执行的系统，使用深度优先搜索解决跨工具依赖问题，实现多步跨工具查询的可靠执行，即使使用小型或本地LLM也能保持高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中许多个人数据查询需要跨多个应用程序并涉及结构化规划，而单个工具仅暴露部分信息；尽管LLM展现出强大的推理和工具使用能力，但可靠地执行多步跨工具查询仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 将自然语言查询转换为结构化图，通过确定性规划器执行；采用深度优先搜索来解析依赖关系并跨工具组合结果。

Result: 提高了查询执行的可靠性，能够实现超越传统基于关键词搜索的查询；即使使用较小或本地托管的LLM也能展现出高准确率。

Conclusion: 该方法有效提升了多步跨工具查询的可靠性，且不依赖大型LLM，适合本地部署场景。

Abstract: Many real-world queries over personal data span multiple applications and require structured planning, as individual tools expose only partial information. While LLMs show strong reasoning and tool use, reliably executing multi-step, cross-tool queries remains challenging. We introduce a system that converts natural language queries into structured graphs and executes them via a deterministic planner. Our approach uses depth-first search to resolve dependencies and combine results across tools, improving reliability and enabling queries beyond traditional keyword-based search. We demonstrate high accuracy even with smaller or locally hosted LLMs.

</details>


### [123] [GeoGNN: Time Series Geo-Localization using Two-Tower Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08303)
*Toan Tran,Waqwoya Abebe,Abhishek Potnis,Supriya Chinthavali,Cyrus Shahabi,Li Xiong,Dalton Lunga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出时间序列地理定位的新概念，旨在推断每个原始时间序列的地理来源。作者形式化了该问题，借鉴图像地理定位的核心思想建立强基线，并提出GeoGNN双塔架构：空间塔利用地理邻接图学习地理单元嵌入，时间塔提取时间序列表征。推理时通过点积相似度匹配和辅助分类头预测地理来源。在美国全国电力消费数据集上，GeoGNN在粗细粒度地理定位准确率上平均提升约27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为时间序列提供空间上下文信息，使下游位置感知应用成为可能。当前缺乏针对原始时间序列的地理定位形式化定义和专用方法。

Method: 提出GeoGNN双塔架构：1) 空间塔利用地理邻接图(GNN)学习候选地理单元的嵌入表示；2) 时间塔提取时间序列的信息表征；3) 推理时通过点积相似度匹配和辅助分类头联合预测地理来源。

Result: 在大规模全国性电力消费数据集上，GeoGNN在所有数据集上取得最佳性能，细粒度和粗粒度地理定位准确率平均提升约27%。

Conclusion: 时间序列地理定位是一个可行且有价值的研究方向，GeoGNN通过结合图结构的地理空间信息与时间序列表征学习，显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: This paper investigates a novel concept of time series geolocalization, where the goal is to infer the geographic origin of each raw time series. Successful geolocalization can provide spatial context to time series, enabling downstream location-aware applications. We formalize the problem, adapt core ideas from image geolocalization to establish strong baselines, and propose GeoGNN, a two-tower architecture. During training, GeoGNN's spatial tower learns embeddings of geographic cell candidates by leveraging the geographic adjacency graph, while the temporal tower extracts informative representations from time series. During inference, each temporal representation is matched against candidate geographic embeddings using dot-product similarity, combined with an auxiliary classification head, to predict the time series' associated geographic origin. Experiments on large-scale, countrywide electricity-consumption datasets demonstrate that GeoGNN achieves the best performance across datasets and enhances both fine- and coarse-grained geolocalization accuracy by ~27% on average.

</details>


### [124] [Towards Graph Foundation Models for Dynamics in Complex Networked Systems: Lessons from Super-Spreader Identification in Multilayer Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08306)
*Michał Czuba,Mateusz Stolarski,Adam Piróg,Piotr Bielak,Piotr Bródka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了跨网络归纳泛化是图基础模型在网络动力学领域的必要前提，提出了四个设计属性，并通过在合成多层网络上训练的ts-net模型在真实世界多层网络上实现了零样本泛化，在四个指标中的三个上优于传统基线和直推式方法，最后指出了构建网络动力学图基础模型的五个开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前网络动力学（传播、影响力最大化、流行病建模等）研究局限于直推式范式，即模型在单个网络上训练后无法直接用于未见过的图，这阻碍了图基础模型在该领域的发展。作者认为跨网络归纳泛化是实现图基础模型的必要前提。

Method: 提出了面向跨网络归纳泛化的四个设计属性，并基于此构建了ts-net（TopSpreadersNetwork）作为概念验证。ts-net仅在合成多层网络上训练，无需在目标真实网络上进行微调或重新训练，即可实现零样本泛化。

Result: ts-net在真实世界多层网络上展现出零样本泛化能力，在四个评估指标中的三个上优于经典启发式方法和直推式基线模型，且能够处理不同规模和层数的网络。

Conclusion: ts-net的成功验证了跨网络归纳泛化的可行性，但距离真正的图基础模型仍有差距。作者进一步提出了五个开放挑战：规模扩展、多层泛化、自监督预训练、跨任务迁移和节点属性融合。

Abstract: Network dynamics - including spreading, influence maximisation, and epidemic modelling - remain largely confined to the transductive paradigm, where models are trained on a single network and cannot be reused on unseen graphs without retraining. We argue that inductive cross-network generalisation is a necessary prerequisite for Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) in this domain and propose four design properties towards this goal. As a proof of concept, ts-net (TopSpreadersNetwork), trained solely on synthetic multilayer networks (MLNs), demonstrates zero-shot generalisation to real-world MLNs of varying size and layer count, outperforming classical heuristics and transductive baselines on three of four metrics. Based on ts-net's performance, we further outline five open challenges towards building GFMs for network dynamics: scale, many-layer generalisation, self-supervised pretraining, cross-task transfer, and node-attribute integration.

</details>


### [125] [Fourier fractal dimension to predict the generalization of deep neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08308)
*Joao B. Florindo,Davi Wanderley Misturini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于傅里叶分形维数的泛化度量，通过分析Lévy驱动随机微分方程的特征函数提取频率域的几何复杂度指标，并设计傅里叶优化器主动正则化该分形维数，在CIFAR-10、SVHN、MNIST上实现了与真实泛化差距的强相关性，Kendall秩相关系数达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在不依赖留出验证数据的情况下预测泛化性能是机器学习中的基本挑战，现有基于范数、边际和PAC-Bayes的度量存在局限。

Method: (1) 基于网络权重变化的傅里叶分形维数构建泛化度量；(2) 在频域分析Lévy驱动随机微分方程的特征函数；(3) 设计定制化傅里叶优化器主动正则化该分形维数。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、SVHN和MNIST数据集上，所提度量与真实泛化差距强相关，Kendall秩相关系数超过现有多种基于范数、边际和PAC-Bayes的度量，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 频率域分形分析有潜力成为模型泛化性的强预测器，并为开发更稳定的优化算法提供原理性基础。

Abstract: Predicting the generalization performance of deep neural networks without relying on hold-out validation data is a fundamental challenge in machine learning. While Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) drives the optimization of these highly parameterized models, its heavy-tailed, non-Gaussian dynamics induce complex, scale-invariant trajectories in the parameter space. In this paper, we propose a novel generalization measure based on the Fourier fractal dimension of the network's weight variations. By analyzing the characteristic function of the Lévy-driven stochastic differential equations in the frequency domain, we extract a metric that robustly captures the geometric complexity of the learning process. Furthermore, we introduce a customized Fourier-based optimizer designed to actively regularize this fractal dimension during training. Extensive empirical evaluations on the CIFAR-10, SVHN, and MNIST datasets demonstrate that our proposed Fourier generalization measure exhibits a strong correlation with the actual generalization gap. Our method achieves state-of-the-art Kendall rank correlation coefficients, outperforming a wide array of existing norm-based, margin-based, and PAC-Bayesian measures. Ultimately, this work highlights the potential of frequency-domain fractal analysis as both a powerful predictor for model generalizability and a principled foundation for developing more stable optimization algorithms.

</details>


### [126] [Where the Score Lives: A Wavelet View of Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08309)
*Emma Finn,Binxu Wang,T. Anderson Keller,Demba E. Ba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于2D正交小波基展开的解析可解得分函数参数化方法，通过数据分布的矩来解释得分网络架构对扩散模型生成行为的影响，并部分模仿U-Net和CNN等架构的归纳偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管CNN、U-Net、Transformer等多种架构已被用作扩散模型中的得分近似网络，但目前对这些架构选择如何影响生成行为的理解仍然有限。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供架构无关的、基于矩的分析来揭示数据分布的哪些属性对去噪最为关键。

Method: 提出了一种在2D正交小波基上展开的解析可解得分函数参数化方法，推导出以数据分布矩表示的最优得分函数，该参数化方法足够灵活以部分模仿包括U-Net和CNN在内的多种架构的相关归纳偏置。

Result: 得到了一个可解析表达的得分机器，能够通过数据分布的矩来理解得分网络与数据分布之间的相互作用，揭示了不同得分架构为何会表现出不同的生成行为。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解扩散模型中架构选择对生成行为的影响提供了理论分析工具，通过基于小波的得分参数化方法，架起了数据分布特性与神经网络架构归纳偏置之间的桥梁。

Abstract: Score-based generative models have had remarkable success over the last decade in generating a diverse set of visually plausible images. A variety of architectures including CNNs, U-Nets, and Transformers have been used as the score-approximation network in such diffusion modeling; however, to date, relatively little is known about how these architectural choices impact generative behavior. In this work, to provide insight into this area, we propose an analytically solvable parameterization of the score function using an expansion in a 2D orthogonal wavelet basis. In particular, we derive interpretable optimal score functions in terms of the moments of the data distribution. We use this parametrization to provide an architecture-agnostic, moment-based analysis that reveals which attributes of the data distribution tend to matter most for denoising. Our score machine is flexible enough to partially mimic the relevant inductive biases of multiple architectures, including U-Nets, and CNNs, taking a step towards understanding why different score architectures can exhibit distinct generative behavior. Since our score is solvable in terms of the moments of the data, we can begin to understand how the data distribution interacts with the score network to produce the behavior we observe in diffusion models.

</details>


### [127] [Orthogonality and Dimensionality in Airline Cluster Analysis using PCA and Kernel PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08322)
*Andreas Schlapbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文复现了Renold et al. (2023)对美国航空业利润周期（1995-2020）的k-means聚类实验，验证了六聚类分类在几何上的鲁棒性（3维PC空间与7维原始空间产生逐位一致的聚类结果），并通过核PCA发现数据流形本质上是线性的（无隐藏曲率）。但轮廓系数表明数据集在结构上仅支持3个聚类，原始7维空间的共线性抑制了这一信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Renold et al. (2023)提出的六聚类分类在几何上的鲁棒性，并检查数据是否存在非线性结构（隐藏曲率）。

Method: 在三个空间（原始7维、3维PC、4维PC）中复现k-means聚类；应用六种核函数（涵盖三个系列加线性基线）的核PCA进行非线性检测；使用轮廓系数评估聚类结构支持度。

Result: 3维PC空间与7维原始空间的k-means产生逐位一致的聚类分配；所有六种核函数在2D中均保持六聚类分配；1D诊断显示线性核将COVID年份C_3与峰值利润簇C_0混淆，而非线性核将C_3移至仅与金融危机后簇C_5重叠；轮廓系数显示数据集仅支持3个聚类，而非6个。

Conclusion: 美国航空利润数据流形本质上是线性的（无隐藏曲率）；Renold et al.的六聚类分类在几何上鲁棒但结构上过度细分，数据集真正支持的是3个聚类；原始7维空间的共线性掩盖了正确的聚类数信号。

Abstract: To characterize the US airline profit cycles from 1995 to 2020, the authors of Renold et al. (2023) combine k-means clustering, principal component analysis, and system dynamic modelling. We replicate their clustering experiment in three spaces -- the original 7-dimensional raw-variable space, a 3-dimensional PC score space, and a 4-dimensional PC score space using their dataset gratefully included in the paper. We show that the six-cluster taxonomy is geometrically robust: k-means in 3-PC space produces bit-for-bit identical cluster assignments relative to 7D raw space. As a nonlinearity check we apply kernel PCA under six kernels spanning three families plus a linear baseline. All six kernels preserve the six-cluster assignment in 2D. A 1D diagnostic tightens this: the linear kernel conflates the COVID year C_3 with the peak-profit cluster C_0, whereas all five non-baseline kernels shift C_3 to overlap only the post-financial-crisis cluster C_5. Agreement across the kernel families confirms an intrinsically linear manifold with no hidden curvature. The silhouette criterion reveals that the dataset structurally supports only three clusters, not six. Collinearity in the raw 7D space suppresses the silhouette signal that would otherwise identify k=3 as the structurally motivated choice.

</details>


### [128] [GENERIC-FNO: Embedding Energy Conservation and Entropy Production into Fourier Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08343)
*Jason Sulskis,Sathya Ravi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GENERIC-FNO是首个将完整GENERIC（度量-辛）非平衡热力学结构嵌入函数空间的神经算子，通过构造精确满足退化条件（残差~10^-13），实现了可逆能量守恒与不可逆熵增动力学的耦合学习，在多种PDE和算子骨干上保持结构保证，性能优于或持平现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构保持神经算子最多只能执行单一守恒律或可逆（哈密顿）结构，而热力学一致的学习仅限于有限维、图或粒子系统，缺乏在函数空间中嵌入完整GENERIC结构的方法。

Method: 将能量和熵泛函学习为神经算子，将泊松算子与摩擦算子参数化为对角傅里叶乘子夹在秩一投影之间，通过构造精确强制退化条件，无需惩罚项、更新投影或残差项。

Result: 退化恒等式在机器精度（残差~10^-13）下成立；显式时间步进仅引入微小O(dt^2)漂移（每步残差~10^-6）；在4倍超分辨率范围（64到256）零样本保持结构保证；恢复物理耗散顺序；在耗散和混合问题上优于强基线。

Conclusion: GENERIC-FNO成功将完整热力学结构嵌入神经算子，实现了精确可逆-不可逆动力学分解，并引入了规范不变耗散诊断，为热力学一致的学习物理系统提供了函数空间框架。

Abstract: We introduce GENERIC-FNO, the first neural operator to embed the full GENERIC (metriplectic) structure of nonequilibrium thermodynamics -- reversible, energy-conserving dynamics and irreversible, entropy-producing dynamics coupled through the degeneracy conditions -- directly in function space. Existing structure-preserving neural operators enforce at most a single conservation law or reversible (Hamiltonian) structure, while thermodynamically consistent learning has been confined to finite-dimensional, graph, or particle systems. GENERIC-FNO closes this gap: it learns the energy and entropy functionals as neural operators and parameterizes the Poisson and friction operators as diagonal Fourier multipliers sandwiched between rank-one projections that enforce the degeneracy conditions exactly, by construction, with no penalty term, update projection, or residual. The degeneracy identities hold to machine precision (residuals ~10^-13) for any initialization, dimension, or resolution, so the continuous-time dynamics conserve the learned energy and produce entropy exactly; the explicit time stepping adds only a small O(dt^2) drift (per-step residual ~10^-6). We further note that the (E,S,L,M) decomposition of a given flow is not unique, and introduce a gauge-invariant dissipation diagnostic separating reversible from dissipative dynamics independently of the learned functionals. Across three operator backbones (1D/2D FNOs and DeepONet) and four PDEs spanning reversible, dissipative, and mixed regimes, GENERIC-FNO preserves its exact structural guarantees zero-shot across a 4x super-resolution range (64 to 256), recovers the ground-truth ordering of physical dissipation, and is competitive with strong unconstrained and energy-penalized baselines, outperforming them on several dissipative and mixed problems at comparable or fewer parameters.

</details>


### [129] [Generative Frontier Planning for Adaptive Peer-Referral Recruitment under Covariate-Dependent Arrivals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08360)
*Lingkai Kong,Hezi Jiang,Andrew Ma,Keyu Wang,Akseli Kangaslahti,Milind Tambe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为生成式前沿规划（GFP）的方法，用于在同行推荐招募系统（如受访者驱动抽样）中自适应分配有限的推荐资源。GFP利用条件生成模型和截断计数模型学习推荐者依赖的招募行为，通过确定性备份替代蒙特卡洛采样，并利用边际收益递减特性实现(1-1/e)近似的贪婪分配。在基于真实数据校准的仿真环境中，GFP在四个折扣因子下均优于随机、强化学习和i.i.d.动态规划基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共卫生机构需要自适应地分配有限的推荐资源以加速对受传染病影响的隐藏人群的招募。现有工作假设推荐来自同质总体的独立同分布抽样，忽略了真实推荐中存在的同质性（homophily）和共享背景，导致规划问题不切实际。

Method: 采用更现实的模型，其中推荐能力和新个体的协变量均依赖于推荐者，通过截断计数模型和条件生成模型从数据中学习。提出生成式前沿规划（GFP），用确定性备份替代每步蒙特卡洛采样，在潜在协变量-覆盖率值代理上进行规划。该代理使得下一前沿的期望值仅依赖于通过离线摊销获得的有限维摘要，且每轮目标函数具有单调性和边际收益递减性，从而使得贪婪分配达到(1-1/e)近似。

Result: 在基于真实受访者驱动抽样数据集校准的仿真环境中，GFP在四个折扣因子下均优于随机、强化学习以及i.i.d.动态规划基线方法。

Conclusion: GFP通过在规划中考虑推荐者依赖的招募异质性，有效提升了隐藏人群招募的效率，为公共卫生资源分配提供了可行的决策框架。

Abstract: Peer-referral recruitment systems such as respondent-driven sampling are critical for studying and intervening on hidden populations affected by infectious diseases. To accelerate recruitment, public health agencies must adaptively allocate limited referral resources across multiple rounds, where current decisions shape both the number and the covariates of future recruits. Prior work makes this problem tractable by assuming that referrals are drawn i.i.d.\ from a homogeneous population, an assumption that ignores the homophily and shared context that drive real peer recruitment. We instead consider a more realistic model in which both referral capacity and the covariates of newly referred individuals are conditioned on the referrer, learned from data with a censored count model and a conditional generative model. The resulting planning problem is challenging because each candidate allocation induces a different distribution over future recruits. We propose \emph{Generative Frontier Planning} (GFP), a model-based planner that replaces per-step Monte-Carlo sampling with a deterministic backup over a latent covariate-coverage value surrogate. The surrogate is designed so that the expected value of the next frontier depends on the offspring generative model only through finite-dimensional summaries that are amortized offline, and so that the resulting per-round objective is monotone with diminishing returns. Together, these two properties make planning tractable: the deterministic backup eliminates Monte-Carlo sampling, and the diminishing-returns structure lets a marginal greedy allocation achieve a \((1-1/e)\)-approximation for the per-round problem. On a simulation environment calibrated to a real respondent-driven sampling dataset, GFP outperforms random, reinforcement-learning, and i.i.d.\ dynamic-programming baselines across four discount factors.

</details>


### [130] [Pre-Intervention Prediction of Sparse Autoencoder Steering Side Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08365)
*Evan Duan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预干预筛选框架，利用SAE特征统计量（解码器几何、激活统计、共激活结构、直接logit足迹）预测语言模型特征干预的副效应（效果稳定性与附带扩散），在多种模型和SAE字典上验证了预测有效性，但最佳预测指标和改善维度因模型/字典设置而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE特征越来越多用于语言模型操控，但特征干预常不干净：同一干预在不同上下文中行为不一致，且会扰动无关特征。现有方法缺乏对干预副效应的预测量化手段。

Method: 提出预干预筛选框架，从干预前计算的特征统计量预测SAE操控的模块性副效应（效果稳定性与附带扩散）。在GPT-2-small、Pythia-70M-deduped、Gemma-2-2B、Llama-3.1-8B上评估ReLU、JumpReLU、TopK SAE字典，使用解码器几何、激活统计、共激活结构、直接logit足迹作为预测因子。

Result: 解码器几何等特征统计量比频率和激活幅度基线更好地预测干预模块性；信号在GPT-2-small、Pythia-70M、Llama-3.1-8B中强且在残余化后仍存，在Gemma-2-2B中较弱；留出筛选可选出更干净的特征，但改善维度因模型而异（GPT-2整体改善，Pythia改善稳定性，Llama改善附带扩散，Gemma仅部分改善）；信号在32K至128K字典宽度变化下持续存在，但筛选收益变不稳定。

Conclusion: SAE干预副效应可以提前预测，但有用的预测因子和可迁移的模块性维度依赖于模型和字典设置，不同模型的最优预测策略不同。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoder (SAE) features are increasingly used to steer language models, but feature steering is rarely clean: the same intervention can behave inconsistently across contexts and perturb unrelated features. We introduce a pre-intervention screening framework for forecasting SAE steering side effects from feature statistics computed before steering. We operationalize side effects along two axes of steering modularity, effect stability and collateral spread, and evaluate GPT-2-small, Pythia-70M-deduped, Gemma-2-2B, and Llama-3.1-8B across ReLU, JumpReLU, and TopK SAE dictionaries. Across these settings, decoder geometry, activation statistics, co-activation structure, and direct-logit footprint predict steering modularity better than frequency-only and activation-magnitude baselines. The signal is strongest in GPT-2-small, Pythia-70M, and Llama-3.1-8B, where it survives residualization against magnitude-related confounds, and weaker in Gemma-2-2B. Held-out screening shows that ranking unseen features by predicted cleanliness can select features that steer more cleanly on fresh contexts, but the successful axis varies by setting: GPT-2 improves most cleanly, Pythia improves mainly on stability, Llama mainly on collateral, and Gemma only partially. A controlled Llama Scope width comparison shows that the predictive signal persists under a 32K-to-128K dictionary-width change, although the screening payoff becomes less stable. Overall, SAE steering side effects are predictable in advance, but the useful predictor signature and transferred modularity axis are model- and dictionary-setting dependent.

</details>


### [131] [An Information-Theoretic Definition for Open-Ended Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08369)
*Wanqiao Xu,Yifan Zhu,Benjamin Van Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于信息论的开端式学习定义，引入"比特等价"概念量化达到各期望奖励所需信息，将环境是否开端式定义为代理能否实现比特等价的线性增长，并证明经典赌博机环境非开端式，同时设计了一种在该环境中实现开端式学习的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于开端式AI系统的研究缺乏统一定义和理论基础，亟需一个形式化的框架来界定什么是开端式环境以及智能体应如何探索此类环境。

Method: 引入"比特等价"（bit-equivalent）概念，量化达到每一期望奖励水平所需的信息量，并以此信息论标准定义开端式环境：当智能体在比特等价上实现线性增长时，该环境被视为开端式。基于此定义分析经典赌博机环境，并构造了一个满足开端式条件的赌博机环境，同时设计对应的开端式学习算法。

Result: 证明了经典赌博机环境不是开端式的，并成功构造了一个具有开端式特性的赌博机环境，且提出了在该环境中实现开端式学习的算法。

Conclusion: 信息论框架为开端式学习提供了形式化定义和理论支撑，有助于指导未来开端式AI系统的设计和分析。

Abstract: A growing body of work points to the great promise of AI systems that can continually expand their capabilities as they operate in an open-ended environment. But yet there is no coherent definition of open-endedness or theory about how an agent ought to explore an open-ended environment. We introduce an information-theoretic definition based on a new concept -- the ${\textit bit-equivalent}$ -- which quantifies the information required to attain each level of expected reward. We consider an environment to be open-ended if an agent can attain linear growth in the bit-equivalent. We establish that classical bandit environments are not open-ended and formulate a bandit environment that is. We also introduce an algorithm that achieves open-ended learning in this environment.

</details>


### [132] [Few-step Cofolding with All-Atom Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08375)
*Gianluca Scarpellini,Ron Shprints,Peter Holderrieth,Juno Nam,Pranav Murugan,Rafael Gómez-Bombarelli,Tommi Jaakola,Maruan Al-Shedivat,Nicholas Matthew Boffi,Avishek Joey Bose*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeCAF框架，通过将全原子共折叠扩散模型蒸馏为流映射，在仅需少量推理步骤的情况下生成高质量的3D生物分子复合物结构，并引入基于奖励引导搜索的推理时优化框架，在多个基准上取得更优的精度与物理有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全原子生成模型依赖昂贵的迭代扩散推理过程，导致传统部署和推理时搜索技术计算成本高昂，亟需一种能在少量推理步骤中生成高质量样本的高效方法。

Method: 基于去噪器的流映射公式，结合端点损失并支持SE(3)刚性对齐；推导变量变换使模型能在EDM风格的σ-空间噪声调度中运行，实现从预训练扩散模型的直接蒸馏；构建基于流映射前瞻的推理时奖励引导搜索框架。

Result: DeCAF-Boltz在Runs N' Poses基准上，在严格的NFE预算下，在RMSD和物理有效性评分上均优于Boltz-1x；在PoseBusters上展示更优的Pareto前沿。DeCAF-Pearl在成功率上匹配教师模型，同时仅需1/5的NFE。

Conclusion: DeCAF框架通过蒸馏策略显著降低了全原子生物分子复合物生成的计算成本，同时保持了生成质量，为高效的结构预测和推理时优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: All-atom generative modeling of 3D biomolecular complexes has emerged as the dominant paradigm for predicting the structure of proteins and protein-ligand systems. Generating structures at the atomic level of fidelity, however, typically requires expensive iterative diffusion rollouts, making both conventional deployment and inference-time search techniques computationally costly. In this paper, we introduce the Denoiser Cofolding All-Atom Flowmap (DeCAF) framework for distilling state-of-the-art all-atom cofolding models into all-atom flow maps that produce high-quality samples in only a few inference steps. We build DeCAF on a denoiser-based formulation of flow maps with endpoint losses that naturally support SE(3) rigid alignment, which we show is critical for training accurate models. We further derive a simple change of variables that lets DeCAF operate in the σ-space noise schedule of EDM-style architectures, enabling direct distillation from pretrained cofolding diffusion models. Equipped with DeCAF's flowmap lookahead, we introduce a purpose-built inference-time framework that improves sampling through reward-guided search. Empirically, DeCAF-Boltz statistically improves over Boltz-1x in both accuracy (RMSD) and physical validity scores of protein-ligand poses at strict NFE budgets on the challenging Runs N' Poses, while also showing a more optimal Pareto frontier across all inference compute budgets on PoseBusters. Distilling the state-of-the-art Pearl cofolding model, DeCAF-Pearl outperforms diffusion-based cofolding models and matches its teacher on success rate while using 5x fewer NFEs. We release our code at https://github.com/genesistherapeutics/decaf.

</details>


### [133] [RiskNet: A large-scale dataset of AI risk incidents from news with alignment and multi-dimensional annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08376)
*Leihan Zhang,Wecheng Ye,Xianlong Ma,Haochuan Liu,Yang Li,Qianyu Zhang,Jinliang Chen,Qiang Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RiskNet，一个从大规模多语言新闻源构建的AI风险事件大规模数据集。通过结构化流水线进行AI风险新闻识别、事件级报告筛选、事件对齐和多维分类，RiskNet将分散的新闻报道组织为以事件为中心的结构化记录，并提供事件分类、事件对齐和风险标注的基准数据集，旨在支持AI安全、治理和风险分析的实证研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI治理框架阐明了负责任AI的高层原则，但缺乏大规模、持续、数据驱动的实证资源来追踪和分析现实世界的AI风险事件。现有的事件收集通常依赖人工整理、规模较小，不足以支持持续监控和下游计算分析。

Method: RiskNet采用结构化流水线：从大规模多语言新闻源中识别AI风险新闻，进行事件级报告筛选，实现事件对齐，并进行多维度事件分类。该流程覆盖数亿条源记录，最终产出包含对齐事件簇和人工标注基准子集的大规模AI风险事件集合。

Result: 构建了覆盖数亿条源记录的AI风险事件数据集，包含对齐的事件簇和标注基准子集，并提供了在线浏览和探索平台。数据集支持事件分类、事件对齐和事件级风险标注等基准任务。

Conclusion: RiskNet作为结构化、可复用的实证资源，弥合了高层治理原则与AI风险事件已记录现实之间的差距，支持AI安全、治理、风险分析、基准评估以及跨源纵向分析等下游研究。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly deployed across socially consequential domains, reports of AI-related harms and failures have grown in frequency and diversity. Although existing governance frameworks articulate high-level principles for responsible AI, large-scale empirical resources for tracking and analyzing real-world AI risk incidents remain limited. Existing incident collections are often manually curated, relatively small in scale, and insufficient for continuous, data-driven monitoring and downstream computational analysis. To address this need, we present RiskNet, a large-scale dataset of AI risk incidents constructed from large-scale multilingual news sources. RiskNet applies a structured pipeline for AI risk news identification, event-level report screening, incident alignment, and multi-dimensional incident classification. The resulting resource organizes dispersed news reports into incident-centered records and provides benchmark datasets for event classification, incident alignment, and incident-level risk labeling. In its current release, RiskNet covers hundreds of millions of source records and yields a large-scale collection of AI risk-related reports, including aligned incident clusters and annotated benchmark subsets. The dataset is also accessible through an online platform for browsing and exploration. We describe the data sources, processing workflow, taxonomy design, and technical validation of the resource. RiskNet is intended to support downstream research on AI safety, governance, risk analysis, and benchmarking, as well as longitudinal and cross-source analyses of AI-related harms. By providing a structured and reusable empirical resource, RiskNet helps bridge the gap between high-level governance principles and the documented realities of AI risk incidents.

</details>


### [134] [STAR-KV: Low-Rank KV Cache Compression via Soft Thresholding for Adaptive Rank Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08382)
*Priyansh Bhatnagar,Ashkan Moradifirouzabadi,Se-Hyun Yang,SeungJae Lee,Jungwook Choi,Mingu Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: STAR-KV提出了一种自适应低秩KV缓存压缩框架，通过可微分阈值机制实现细粒度秩选择、混合低秩分解策略以及低秩感知混合精度量化，在多种LLM上实现高达75%的KV缓存压缩，结合量化可达20倍压缩，并借助Triton内核实现最高6.9倍注意力模块加速和3.1倍端到端生成吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的低秩投影方法依赖固定或启发式秩选择，难以在激进压缩下保持较低精度损失，缺乏自适应且细粒度的秩控制能力。

Method: 1) 可微分阈值机制：在注意力头和块级别实现最优秩选择；2) 混合分解策略：根据键和值投影的敏感性采用不同的低秩分解方式；3) 低秩感知混合精度量化：利用数据统计信息实现近无损的低比特量化。

Result: 多种LLM和基准测试上实现高达75% KV缓存压缩，结合量化达到20倍整体KV缓存缩减；基于Triton的自定义GPU内核实现注意力模块最高6.9倍加速，端到端生成吞吐提升3.1倍。

Conclusion: STAR-KV通过自适应细粒度秩控制和多种低秩优化技术的协同，在保持精度的同时实现了显著的KV缓存压缩和推理加速，为LLM部署提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Low-rank projection has emerged as a promising approach for compressing the KV cache by exploiting hidden-dimension redundancy. However, prior methods rely on fixed or heuristic rank selection and struggle to achieve aggressive compression with minimal accuracy degradation. We propose STAR-KV, an adaptive low-rank KV cache compression framework with fine-grained rank control. STAR-KV encompasses 1) a differentiable thresholding mechanism that enables optimal rank selection at both attention-head and block levels, 2) a hybrid decomposition strategy that applies different low-rank factorizations according to the sensitivity of key and value projections, and 3) a low-rank-aware mixed precision quantization that leverages data statistics for near lossless low-bit quantization. Evaluated across multiple LLMs and benchmarks, STAR-KV achieves up to 75% KV cache compression and up to 20x overall KV cache reduction when combined with quantization. Enabled by custom Triton-based GPU kernels, STAR-KV delivers up to 6.9x speedup for the attention module and 3.1x end-to-end generation throughput. Our code is publicly available at: https://github.com/PriyanshBhatnagar/STAR-KV.

</details>


### [135] [The Spectral Dynamics and Noise Geometry of Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08388)
*Pierfrancesco Beneventano,Mahmoud Abdelmoneum,Tomaso Poggio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon优化器（用极分解替换矩阵梯度）的优化偏差，证明其在特定条件下具有平坦谱偏向，在矩阵感知实验和小型NanoGPT预训练中验证了这一特性，并指出Muon并非普遍优越，其效果依赖于具体场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Muon优化器将梯度矩阵替换为其极因子所带来的优化偏差，探究其为何在某些场景有效而在另一些场景无效。

Method: 理论分析（熵最大化证明、欠定回归模型奇异值动力学）、矩阵感知控制实验、小规模NanoGPT和ViT对比实验。

Result: 1）理论证明极更新是单步熵最大化的选择；2）导出连续时间Muon的精确奇异值动力学，给出谱平坦化的条件；3）实验表明Muon与梯度缩放/核范数最小化行为不同，倾向平坦谱；4）NanoGPT中Muon优于AdamW，ViT中相反。

Conclusion: Muon的优化偏差是使谱平坦化，这在需要保持多奇异方向活跃的场景有益，但并非普遍优于其他优化器，其效用取决于任务特性。

Abstract: Muon replaces a matrix gradient $G=UΣV^\top$ by its polar factor $UV^\top$. This keeps the singular directions selected by the gradient, but makes the update spectrum flat. We study the optimization bias created by this operation. Under explicit alignment assumptions, we prove that the polar update is the one-step entropy-maximizing choice among bounded updates that use the gradient singular directions and do not adapt to the current weight spectrum. In an underdetermined regression model, we derive exact singular-value dynamics for continuous-time Muon and identify a measurement-dependent condition under which the normalized spectrum moves toward equal nonzero singular values. This geometry also rules out a common low-rank interpretation: at fixed Frobenius norm, Muon's distinguished state has a flat spectrum, whereas nuclear-norm minimization favors spectral concentration. Controlled matrix-sensing experiments separate the effect from simple gradient rescaling, show that norm-matched gradient descent does not reproduce Muon, and recover the predicted flattening trend across broad ablations. In small NanoGPT pretraining, Muon preserves stable rank, has a broad learning-rate plateau, and improves validation loss relative to AdamW; in a matched small-ViT control, the ranking reverses. The resulting picture is regime-dependent: Muon is not universally superior, but its flat-spectrum bias can help when many spectral directions need to remain active.

</details>


### [136] [When Are Neural Interaction Discoveries Real? Identifiability, Recoverability, and a Pre-Fit Diagnostic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08390)
*Valentina Kuskova,Dmitry Zaytsev,Michael Coppedge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究神经时序模型中变量间交互作用的可识别性问题，发现交互作用能否被可靠恢复取决于输入数据支持的几何结构，而非模型架构。通过乘性门控扩展的神经加法向量自回归模型（GNAVAR）证明：低维支持会导致不同的交互分解在观测数据上一致但在其他区域不同；提出基于联合滞后块协方差有效秩的预拟合诊断方法和两种子稳定性检查的实用操作测试。结果表明交互恢复能力依赖于支持几何结构，有效秩可作为预拟合诊断指标，独立拟合间的不稳定性是非可识别交互发现的典型特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当神经时序模型报告变量A调节变量B对目标C的影响时，这种交互作用是数据本身的属性还是模型灵活性的伪影尚不清楚。作者论证这本质上是可识别性问题，受观测输入支持的几何结构控制，而非特定的神经架构。

Method: 在乘性门控扩展的神经加法向量自回归模型（GNAVAR）中，源贡献由其他滞后变量调节。通过数学证明展示了表示容量不等于可识别性：依赖输入导致边特定交互项之间的泄漏，低维支持允许不同的交互分解在观测数据上一致但其他区域不同。提出了在显式支持条件下（包括共享调节器设置）归一化最小GNAVAR分解的群体可识别性定理，以及基于联合滞后块协方差有效秩的预拟合诊断和两种子稳定性检查。

Result: 支持条件将实证结果组织为理论预测的三种状态。结果表明：交互恢复能力取决于支持几何结构；有效秩提供实用的预拟合诊断指标；独立拟合间的不稳定性是可识别性问题的特征信号。这些现象、支持条件和不稳定性信号是模型无关的。

Conclusion: 交互作用恢复的可行性从根本上取决于数据支持的几何结构，而非模型架构。有效秩可作为实用的预拟合诊断工具，而独立拟合间的不稳定性是非可识别交互发现的典型特征。这些发现具有模型无关性，GNAVAR只是使它们可证明的工具。

Abstract: When a neural time-series model reports that one variable modulates another's effect on a target, is the discovered interaction a property of the data or an artifact of model flexibility? We argue that this is fundamentally a question of identifiability, governed by the geometry of the observed input support rather than by the specific neural architecture. We study the problem in a multiplicative-gating extension of neural additive vector autoregression (GNAVAR), in which source contributions are modulated by other lagged variables. We show that representational capacity is not identifiability: dependent inputs induce leakage between edge-specific interaction terms, and low-dimensional support permits distinct interaction decompositions that agree on the observed data while differing elsewhere. We then prove a population identifiability theorem for normalized minimal GNAVAR decompositions under explicit support conditions, including settings with shared modulators. The theory yields a simple practitioner-facing diagnostic: the effective rank of the joint lag-block covariance predicts, before fitting, whether interaction recovery is feasible for a given candidate set. When the candidate set is unknown, a two-seed stability check provides a practical operational test. The same support condition organizes empirical outcomes into the three states predicted by the theory. Our results show that interaction recoverability depends on support geometry, that effective rank provides a practical pre-fit diagnostic, and that instability across independent fits is a characteristic signature of non-identifiable interaction discovery. The identifiability phenomenon, the support condition, and the instability signature are model-agnostic; GNAVAR is the vehicle that makes them provable.

</details>


### [137] [Provably Efficient Personalized Multi-Objective Bandits with Proactive Conversational Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08410)
*Linfeng Cao,Ming Shi,Ness B. Shroff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MO-PQUCB算法，通过结合用户主动查询（如"便宜又干净的酒店"）提供的结构化偏好信号与多目标多臂老虎机的bandit反馈，利用Plackett-Luce子集选择模型建模查询，通过移位不变正则化和双探索UCB解决偏好学习与奖励探索的纠缠问题，理论证明主动查询能加速偏好估计并改善遗憾界，在查询被污染时设计了鲁棒估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅从效用反馈中推断用户偏好，将偏好学习与奖励探索纠缠在一起，忽略了用户通过主动对话式查询（如"便宜又干净的酒店"）透露的结构化偏好信号，导致学习效率低下。

Method: 提出MO-PQUCB混合算法：1）用Plackett-Luce子集选择模型对用户查询建模范式；2）通过移位不变正则化将查询偏好锚定与bandit反馈融合；3）采用双探索UCB策略平衡探索与利用；4）针对查询被污染的场景设计了鲁棒估计器。

Result: 1）证明了仅靠查询学习存在移位不变性障碍导致学习不充分；2）理论证明主动查询能加速偏好估计并改善遗憾界；3）在查询稀疏污染下达到近乎最优性能；4）实验验证了理论和实践效果。

Conclusion: 主动查询提供的结构化偏好信号能有效加速多目标多臂老虎机中的偏好学习，MO-PQUCB算法通过混合查询与bandit反馈解决了移位不变性难题，并在查询污染下保持鲁棒性。

Abstract: Personalized decision-making in multi-objective bandits requires learning user-specific trade-offs among competing objectives. Since arm utility depends on both unknown rewards and unknown preferences, existing methods infer preferences only from utility feedback, entangling preference learning with reward exploration. In practice, however, users often reveal their priorities through proactive conversational queries (e.g., "cheap and clean hotel"), yet this structured signal is not leveraged. We formalize a proactive query-based framework in which user queries provide structured preference signals. Modeling these signals via a Plackett-Luce subset choice model, we show that query-only learning is insufficient due to a fundamental shift-invariance barrier. To resolve this, we introduce MO-PQUCB, a hybrid algorithm that integrates query-based preference anchoring with bandit feedback through shift-invariant regularization and dual-exploration UCB. We prove that proactive queries accelerate preference estimation and yield improved regret scaling over prior preference-aware MO-MAB methods. Under corrupted queries, we further characterize statistical limits and design a robust estimator achieving near-optimal performance when the corruption is sparse. Experiments validate both theoretical and practical gains.

</details>


### [138] [Sparrow: Sparse Rollout for Stable and Efficient Long-context RL of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08446)
*Yang Zhou,Ranajoy Sadhukhan,Zhaofeng Sun,Zhuoming Chen,Souvik Kundu,Saket Dingliwal,Sai Muralidhar Jayanthi,Aram Galstyan,Haizhong Zheng,Beidi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）中稀疏注意力加速长上下文rollout生成的问题，发现稀疏rollout的崩溃并非由所有token均匀退化导致，而是与actor-policy不匹配的下尾统计量相关。提出动态稀疏调度保持该统计量恒定，在Qwen3系列模型上实现2.0-2.4倍加速，并引入DistillSparse通过轻量LoRA蒸馏进一步提升加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR产生极长COT导致计算成本高昂，而稀疏注意力有望加速密集rollout，但存在稳定性-效率的权衡问题：过于激进的稀疏会导致训练崩溃，过于保守则加速不足。本文旨在研究并解决这一权衡问题。

Method: 1) 观察到稀疏rollout崩溃并非由所有token均匀退化导致，多数稀疏token即使在激进稀疏下也与密集对齐良好；2) 提出假设：若每个token的actor-policy不匹配的下尾统计量在整个轨迹中保持在临界阈值以上，则稀疏rollout训练保持稳定；3) 引入动态稀疏调度，在生成过程中保持该尾统计量恒定；4) 使用成本模型在该不匹配阈值下寻找最大加速比的稀疏调度；5) 提出DistillSparse：基于轻量LoRA的蒸馏方法，使更激进的稀疏达到相同的稀疏-密集不匹配阈值。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B、Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B上分别实现2.2倍、2.4倍、2.0倍的rollout加速；阈值可泛化到更大模型Qwen3-14B以及编程领域；DistillSparse进一步提升了加速比。

Conclusion: 通过保持token级actor-policy不匹配的下尾统计量恒定，可以在稀疏rollout训练中实现稳定性与效率的良好平衡；DistillSparse通过蒸馏进一步提升了稀疏rollout的加速效果。

Abstract: Despite being powerful, reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) induces extremely long COT, making it computationally expensive. Since RLVR per-step cost is dominated by long-context rollout generation, sparse attention offers a promising way to accelerate dense rollout. However, sparse rollouts require a delicate stability-efficiency tradeoff: overly aggressive sparsity causes collapse, while overly lenient sparsity gives insufficient speedup. In this work, we study this tradeoff through sparse-to-dense actor-policy mismatch. We first observe that sparse rollout collapse is not driven by uniform degradation across tokens: most sparse tokens align perfectly with dense even under aggressive sparsity. Motivated by this, we hypothesize that sparse rollout training remains stable if the lower tail of per-token actor-policy mismatch stays above a critical threshold throughout the trajectory. We introduce a dynamic sparsity schedule that keeps this tail statistic constant during generation and validate our hypothesis. Across Qwen3 thinking-family models, keeping the tail mismatch statistic near a consistent threshold generally enables stable training. We then use a cost model to find the sparsity schedule for maximum speedup under this mismatch threshold, achieving 2.2x, 2.4x, and 2.0x rollout speedups when training Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-4B, and Qwen3-8B. Empirically, we show the thresholds generalize to a larger model (Qwen3-14B) and another RL domain (coding). Finally, our analysis naturally motivates DistillSparse: lightweight LoRA-based distillation on sparse rollout lets more aggressive sparsity reach the same sparse-to-dense mismatch threshold, yielding higher speedup.

</details>


### [139] [Not Just After One: Sleep-Inspired Replay Prevents Catastrophic Forgetting After Sequential Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08447)
*Anthony Bazhenov,Jean Erik Delanois,Giri P. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的持续学习方法，通过在多个新任务顺序训练后引入无监督的类睡眠回放阶段，可以部分恢复所有先前学习任务的性能，揭示了任务特定信息对新训练具有弹性但会逐渐衰减的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工神经网络在持续学习方面存在严重缺陷——训练新任务时往往会干扰并遗忘先前学习的任务，这与人类和动物能够连续学习、在主动学习阶段获取多个新记忆后再将其巩固到长期存储的能力形成鲜明对比。

Method: 在多个新任务上顺序训练神经网络，然后应用无监督的类睡眠回放阶段（unsupervised sleep-like replay phase），通过该回放过程部分恢复所有先前学习任务的性能。

Result: 多个新任务可以顺序训练，随后通过类睡眠回放部分恢复先前任务的性能；任务特定信息对新训练的干扰具有弹性，但会随着网络在新任务上的训练而逐渐衰减。

Conclusion: 这些发现为开发更广泛的持续学习AI解决方案提供了新的原理和方向。

Abstract: One of the critical limitations of artificial neural networks is their lack of ability to continually learn: training on new tasks often leads to interference and forgetting of the previous ones. While several algorithms have been proposed to protect old memories from interference, they are typically applied during or immediately after each new episode of training. In contrast, humans and animals can learn continuously, acquiring multiple new memories during active learning before consolidating all of them into long-term storage. Here we show that multiple new tasks can be trained sequentially before an unsupervised sleep-like replay phase is applied to partially restore performance across all previously learned tasks. Our study further suggests that task-specific information remains resilient to new training but decays gradually as network is trained on new tasks. These findings point to novel principles for developing a broad range of continual learning AI solutions.

</details>


### [140] [Theoretical Foundations of Continual Learning via Drift-Plus-Penalty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08452)
*Nazreen Shah,Govinda Arya,Bharath B. N.,Ranjitha Prasad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于控制理论的持续学习框架COLD，利用Drift-Plus-Penalty原则在优化当前任务损失的同时维护一个虚拟队列来追踪过去任务的稳定性偏差，通过可调控制参数显式调节稳定性-可塑性权衡，并提供了稳定性与收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据流非平稳且顺序到达，现有持续学习方法在适应新任务时容易发生灾难性遗忘，缺乏对遗忘过程的显式调控机制。

Method: 基于Drift-Plus-Penalty原则的持续学习框架COLD，在重放缓冲区基础上引入虚拟队列追踪长期稳定性偏差，将稳定性-可塑性权衡建模为受控动态过程。

Result: 在标准基准测试上，COLD持续优于多种最先进的持续学习方法，并通过显式调节实现了可控的遗忘行为。

Conclusion: 控制理论视角为持续学习中的稳定性-可塑性权衡提供了系统化的调节框架，COLD通过虚拟队列机制能有效平衡遗忘控制与任务适应。

Abstract: In many real-world settings, data streams are nonstationary and arrive sequentially, requiring learning systems to adapt continuously without retraining from scratch. Continual learning (CL) addresses this challenge by incorporating new tasks while mitigating catastrophic forgetting, where learning new information degrades performance on previously acquired knowledge. We introduce a control-theoretic perspective on CL that explicitly regulates the evolution of forgetting, framing adaptation as a controlled process subject to long-term stability constraints. We focus on replay-based CL, where a finite memory buffer stores representative samples from prior tasks. We propose COntinual Learning with Drift-Plus-Penalty (COLD), a continual learning framework based on the Drift-Plus-Penalty (DPP) principle from stochastic optimization. To facilitate analysis, we also consider an oracle variant, COLD-ORACLE, as a reference benchmark. At each task, both methods minimize the current task loss while maintaining a virtual queue that tracks deviations from long-term stability on previously learned tasks, capturing the stability-plasticity trade-off as a regulated dynamical process. We establish stability and convergence guarantees that characterize this trade-off through a tunable control parameter. Experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that COLD consistently outperforms a broad range of state-of-the-art CL methods while providing competitive and controllable forgetting behavior through explicit regulation of stability and plasticity.

</details>


### [141] [Beyond Linear Activation Steering: Invertible Latent Transformations for Controlling LLM Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08454)
*Tuc Nguyen,Thai Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: INNSteer提出了一种基于可逆神经网络的非线性激活操控框架，将LLM的激活映射到更易进行线性控制的潜空间，实现输入依赖的非线性干预，在多个模型和行为基准上优于现有线性及非线性操控方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活操控方法大多在原始激活空间中计算固定的线性偏移方向，将行为控制视为全局线性可加偏移。然而行为特征在激活空间中可能呈非线性变化或位于弯曲各向异性流形上，最优干预应是输入依赖的，线性方法存在根本局限性。

Method: 学习一个轻量级可逆神经网络φ，将LLM激活映射到潜空间，在该空间中行为类别更易线性分离；推理时，激活经φ映射、在潜空间进行线性操控、再经φ⁻¹逆映射回原始空间，使得简单的潜空间平移转化为原始激活空间中的非线性输入依赖干预。

Result: 在多种LLM家族、规模、行为特质和安全基准上，INNSteer在模型控制效果上持续优于线性操控、基于传输的方法及其他非线性操控基线，同时较好地保持生成流畅性。

Conclusion: 非线性、输入依赖的激活操控相比固定线性偏移更具优势，可逆潜空间变换是一种有效的实现范式。

Abstract: Activation steering provides a lightweight inference-time mechanism for controlling large language models (LLMs) by modifying their internal activation vectors toward desired behaviors. Most existing methods compute a fixed steering direction in the original activation space, typically from pairs of contrastive examples using mean differences, linear probes, or arbitrary separability criteria. While effective to a certain extent, these methods treat behavioral control as a global, linear, additive offset: the same direction is applied across inputs, and behaviors are linearly separable. This can be restrictive when behavioral features vary nonlinearly across the activation space or lie on curved and anisotropic manifolds, where the optimal intervention may be input-dependent. To address this limitation, we propose INNSteer, a nonlinear activation steering framework based on invertible latent transformations. Rather than searching for a better steering vector in the original representation space, INNSteer learns a lightweight invertible neural network $φ$ that maps an LLM's activations into a latent space where behavioral classes are more amenable to linear control. At inference time, activations are mapped through $φ$, steered in the latent space, and mapped back through the exact inverse transformation $φ^{-1}$. This makes a simple latent-space translation become a nonlinear, input-dependent intervention in the original activation space. Across experiment settings on multiple LLM families, scales, behavioral traits, and safety benchmarks, INNSteer consistently improves model control over linear, transport-based, and nonlinear steering baselines while largely preserving generation fluency.

</details>


### [142] [The Confidence Trap: Calibration Attacks for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08467)
*Cuong Dang,Jiahao Zhang,Hieu Ta Quang,Dung Le,Lu Cheng,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一图校准攻击（UGCA）框架，用于白盒场景下评估GNN校准鲁棒性，通过KL散度损失、重排序机制、混合损失和波束搜索解决图结构攻击的独特挑战，并理论证明高精度或多类别数据集上的模型更易受攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络（GNN）的置信度校准在安全关键应用中至关重要，但校准后的GNN在面对对抗性结构扰动时的鲁棒性尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出UGCA框架，包含：(1) KL散度损失以鼓励均匀预测分布； (2) 重排序机制减少标签翻转； (3) 混合损失在违反约束时恢复标签； (4) 波束搜索扩展对抗搜索空间。同时提供理论分析连接模型泛化、数据集复杂度和校准脆弱性。

Result: 大量实验表明，UGCA在保持分类准确率的同时显著增加了期望校准误差（ECE）。

Conclusion: UGCA有效揭示了GNN校准鲁棒性的脆弱面，理论结果指出高准确率模型或训练于更多类别数据集上的模型在该威胁模型下更为脆弱。

Abstract: While confidence calibration is essential for trustworthy decision-making in safety-critical applications, the robustness of calibrated GNNs to adversarial structural perturbations remains largely unexplored. However, studying calibration attacks on graphs presents unique technical challenges: (1) the discrete nature of graph structures complicates gradient-based optimization, (2) existing underconfidence objectives fail to drive predictions toward uniform distributions, and (3) GNNs are highly sensitive to edge perturbations, often causing unintended label changes that violate attack constraints. To address these challenges, we propose a \textbf{Unified Graph Calibration Attack (UGCA)} framework designed for \textbf{worst-case (white-box) analysis} of GNN calibration robustness. UGCA introduces a KL-divergence loss to encourage uniform predictive distributions, a reranking mechanism to reduce label flipping, a hybrid loss to recover labels when violations occur, and beam search to explore a broader adversarial search space. We further provide theoretical insights linking model generalization, dataset complexity, and calibration vulnerability, showing that models with higher accuracy or trained on datasets with more classes are more susceptible under this threat model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UGCA substantially increases Expected Calibration Error while preserving classification accuracy. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/CaptainCuong/Graph-Calibration-Attack.git.

</details>


### [143] [Physically Consistent Null Space Alignment for Detection of Low-Magnitude False Data Injection Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08473)
*Xin Li,Chenhan Xiao,Jonathan Cohen,Aviad Elyashar,Yang Weng,Rami Puzis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理一致零空间对齐（PCNSA）框架，通过伪零空间守恒数据预处理（PSCP）步骤保留测量数据与物理零空间的几何对应关系，从而检测隐蔽的虚假数据注入攻击（FDIA），在多个IEEE标准系统上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型驱动和数据驱动检测器难以识别低幅值但高影响的隐蔽FDIA，因为残差检验忽略了隐藏在伪零空间中的变化，而子空间学习方法捕获相关模式但缺乏物理一致性约束。

Method: 提出PCNSA框架，核心是PSCP预处理步骤：将测量数据重新表达在物理坐标框架中再进行子空间提取，保留行空间与其正交补之间的分离性，使得SVD导出的伪零子空间与物理残差空间对齐，无需显式知道系统矩阵H。

Result: 在IEEE 14、30、57和118节点系统上的实验表明，该方法能检测出逃避XGBoost、LSTM、自编码器和孤立森林基线的隐蔽攻击，在F1分数和检测准确率上更高，且在部分可观测和真实PMU噪声条件下保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: 通过保持物理零空间与测量伪零空间的几何对齐，PCNSA有效检测了传统方法无法识别的隐蔽FDIA，验证了物理一致性约束在电力系统状态估计安全中的重要性。

Abstract: False data injection attacks (FDIAs) introducing small measurement perturbations can still cause large deviations in power system state estimation when the injected signals align with the pseudo-null space of the system model. Existing model- and data-driven detectors may fail to identify such low-magnitude but high-impact attacks because residual tests ignore changes hidden in the pseudo-null space, while subspace learning methods capture correlation patterns without enforcing physical consistency. This paper proposes Physically Consistent Null Space Alignment (PCNSA), a framework that detects stealthy FDIAs by preserving, through preprocessing, the geometric correspondence between the physical null space and the measurement-derived pseudo-null space. The key point is a Pseudo-null Space Conserved data Preprocessing (PSCP) step that re-expresses measurements in the physical coordinate frame before subspace extraction. We prove that PSCP preserves the separation between row space and its orthogonal complement, a property that conventional per-feature standardization violates. This keeps the singular value decomposition (SVD)-derived pseudo-null subspace aligned with the physical residual space without explicit knowledge of H. Experiments on IEEE 14-, 30-, 57-, and 118-bus systems confirm this principle in practice: stealthy attacks that evade XTM, LSTM, AE and Isolation Forest baselines appear as clear deviations in the aligned subspace, yielding higher F1-score and detection accuracy while remaining robust under partial observability and realistic PMU noise.

</details>


### [144] [Inferring hidden forcing in a biological oscillator using Kolmogorov-Arnold networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08479)
*Julian Szereszewski,Facundo Fainstein,Leandro E. Fernandez,Gabriel B. Mindlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用Kolmogorov-Arnold网络从鸟类的气囊压力数据中重建了驱动呼吸动力学有效肌肉力的方程，揭示了隐藏的双相激活模式，并通过肌电图独立验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从部分观测数据中推断驱动动力系统的力是一个基本挑战，尤其当不同机制产生相似的可观测动力学时。本文旨在从单一压力信号中重建鸟类呼吸动力学的有效肌肉驱动力。

Method: 采用基于Kolmogorov-Arnold网络的可解释学习框架，直接从数据中推断系统的控制方程，重建有效肌肉驱动力。

Result: 成功从气囊压力测量中重建了有效肌肉力，发现了每个呼吸周期内的双相激活模式，并通过呼气肌的肌电记录独立验证了该预测。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的动力学法则重建能够揭示隐藏的物理结构并提供对未观测驱动变量的访问，为部分观测动力系统中推断潜在力建立了通用路径。
