Method: 使用相干伊辛机（CIM）结合均衡传播（Equilibrium Propagation）算法训练能量基神经网络；集成Adam优化器求解Hopfield能量网络的基态以提升收敛速度和解的精度；在更深层网络架构和卷积操作上验证可扩展性。

Result: CIM训练的神经网络性能与现有软件实现相当；Adam优化器显著提升了收敛速度和解的精度；该方法可扩展到更深层网络和卷积操作。

Conclusion: CIM动力学作为训练复杂神经网络的平台具有可扩展潜力，可通过模拟电路、光电子或集成光子学实现节能实现，为下一代AI硬件发展建立了新型物理框架。

Abstract: While Ising machines serve as advanced physical solvers for the Ising model,enabling applications in combinatorial optimization and neural network training,their scalability for large-scale neural networks remains constrained by hardware connectivity limitations and suboptimal training methodologies. In this work,we leverage a Coherent Ising Machine (CIM) to train an energy-based neural network using Equilibrium Propagation, achieving performance comparable to existing software-based implementations. We further enhance the algorithm by integrating the Adam optimizer to solve for the ground state of a Hopfield energy network, significantly improving convergence speed and solution accuracy. Additionally, we demonstrate the scalability of our approach across deeper network architectures and convolutional operations. Our results highlight the potential of CIM dynamics as a scalable platform for training complex neural networks, offering a pathway toward energy-efficient implementations via analog circuits, optoelectronics, or integrated photonics. This work establishes a novel physical framework for next-generation AI hardware development.

</details>


### [215] [Claw-R1: A Step-Level Data Middleware System for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09138)
*Daoyu Wang,Mingyue Cheng,Qingchuan Li,Shuo Yu,Jie Ouyang,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Claw-R1是一个面向智能体强化学习的中间件系统，负责管理智能体与环境交互数据的全生命周期，将交互轨迹转化为结构化的步骤级数据记录，支持可视化检查、质量筛选和训练批次配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要关注策略优化算法和训练框架，忽视了智能体-环境交互数据从生产到训练消费的完整数据生命周期管理。

Method: 通过两个核心组件实现：Gateway Server通过统一LLM API入口捕获多轮交互步骤；Data Pool将交互步骤组织为包含prompt ID、response ID、奖励和元数据的步骤级记录。

Result: 支持用户交互式检查实时轨迹、查看每一步的状态/动作/奖励、按质量和就绪程度筛选数据、为不同下游RL算法配置训练就绪批次。

Conclusion: Claw-R1将智能体交互轨迹视为受管理的数据资产而非临时运行时日志，呼吁社区重视智能体强化学习中的数据管理。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) has become an important post-training paradigm for turning LLMs from static chatbots into interactive agents, giving rise to representative applications such as OpenClaw. Existing work mainly focuses on policy optimization algorithms and training frameworks, but pays less attention to the full data lifecycle of agent-environment interactions, from data production to training consumption. To bridge this gap, we present Claw-R1, an interactive step-level data middleware system for agentic RL. Claw-R1 connects heterogeneous agent runtimes with RL training backends through two core components: a Gateway Server and a Data Pool. The Gateway Server captures multi-turn interaction steps through a unified LLM API entry point, while the Data Pool organizes them into step-level records consisting of prompt IDs, response IDs, rewards and other metadata. In our demo, users can interactively inspect live trajectories, examine the state, action, and reward of each step, curate data by quality and readiness, and configure training-ready batches for different downstream RL algorithms. Overall, Claw-R1 treats agent interaction traces as managed data assets rather than temporary runtime logs. Through this demonstration, we hope to encourage the community to recognize the importance of data management in agentic RL. Our code is available at https://github.com/AgentR1/Claw-R1 and the demonstration video can be found at link https://youtu.be/Pw47dAOw6B0.

</details>


### [216] [Improved Convergence Analysis of Topology Dependence in Decentralized SGD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09154)
*Yuki Takezawa,Anastasia Koloskova,Sebastian U. Stich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了去中心化SGD更紧的收敛性分析，证明混合矩阵的所有特征值（而非仅谱隙）都会影响收敛速率，并通过实验验证了新分析能更准确描述拓扑结构对收敛行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分析仅用谱隙刻画拓扑对去中心化SGD收敛的影响，但实验表明拓扑在异质性场景下有显著影响而在同质性场景下影响很小，这种理论与实验之间的不一致亟需更精确的理论解释。

Method: 提出了一种更紧的收敛性分析，将混合矩阵的所有特征值纳入收敛速率的刻画中，而非仅依赖谱隙这一单一指标。通过精心设计的实验系统评估去中心化SGD的收敛行为，验证新分析的有效性。

Result: 新收敛分析表明混合矩阵的所有特征值都会影响去中心化SGD的收敛速率，而不仅仅是谱隙；实验证明该分析能比现有分析更准确地描述拓扑结构对收敛速率的影响。

Conclusion: 拓扑结构对去中心化SGD收敛的影响需要从混合矩阵全谱的角度理解，仅关注谱隙是不够的；新分析弥合了理论与实验之间的差距。

Abstract: Decentralized SGD is a fundamental algorithm in decentralized learning, although the influence of an underlying network topology on its convergence behavior is not yet fully understood. Existing convergence analyses have shown that topologies with a small spectral gap significantly deteriorate the convergence rate of Decentralized SGD in both homogeneous and heterogeneous cases. However, many prior papers have reported that indeed the choice of the topology has a significant experimental impact in the heterogeneous case, but has little experimental impact on training behavior in the homogeneous case. In this paper, we present a tighter convergence analysis of Decentralized SGD, offering a more precise understanding of how topologies affect the convergence rate than the prior analysis. Specifically, unlike existing convergence analyses that used only the spectral gap as a property of the topology, our novel analysis shows that all eigenvalues of the mixing matrix affect the convergence rate. Throughout the experiments, we carefully evaluated the convergence behavior of Decentralized SGD and demonstrated that our novel convergence analysis can more accurately describe the effect of topology on the convergence rate.

</details>


### [217] [Crop Recommendation and Agricultural Query Answering System Using Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks and Hybrid Retrieval Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09160)
*Prajwal Thapa,Yagya Raj Pandeya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个集成天气预测、作物推荐和问答工具的精准农业系统，使用STGCN进行30天天气预报（MSE≈0.011），结合土壤属性生成作物推荐，并部署了基于RAG的农业问答聊天机器人，通过移动应用为农民提供实时指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展中国家（如尼泊尔）农村地区缺乏个性化农业指导，农民难以获取精准的天气预测和作物种植建议，导致决策低效、产量受限、气候适应能力不足。

Method: 1) 提出Transformer-GNN和STGCN两种深度学习模型进行30天气象预测，基于1359个地点数据；2) 将预测结果与静态土壤属性（pH、湿度、有机质）结合，通过评分算法生成作物推荐；3) 构建基于检索增强生成（RAG）的农业问答聊天机器人；4) 通过移动应用部署整个系统。

Result: STGCN优于Transformer模型（MSE 0.011 vs 0.013），能有效建模时空依赖关系；用户反馈证实系统在农村环境中的可用性和相关性。

Conclusion: 将机器学习模型与本地农业数据相结合，能够为农民提供可操作的见解，促进更明智的决策、提高作物产量并增强对气候变化的适应能力。

Abstract: This paper presents a unified system designed to support precision agriculture by integrating advanced weather prediction, crop recommendation, and a question-answering tool for farmers. We propose two deep learning models -- a Transformer-based Graph Neural Network and a Spatio-Temporal Graph Convolutional Network (STGCN) -- to forecast weather conditions for the next 30 days using data from 1,359 locations in Nepal. The STGCN outperforms the Transformer-based model in accuracy (MSE ~0.011 vs. 0.013), effectively modeling both spatial and temporal dependencies in climate data. These predictions are combined with static soil properties such as pH, moisture, and organic content to generate localized crop recommendations through a scoring algorithm that matches each crop's optimal growing conditions. Additionally, we develop a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) chatbot that leverages domain-specific agricultural documents to answer farmers' questions in natural language. The entire system is deployed via a mobile application, offering real-time suggestions and conversational support. User feedback confirms the system's usability and relevance, especially in rural settings where personalized farming guidance is limited. Overall, our approach demonstrates how combining machine learning models with local agricultural data can empower farmers with actionable insights, promoting more informed decisions, better crop yields, and increased resilience to climate variability.

</details>


### [218] [CANS: Accelerating Multiuser Collaborative Edge Inference via Cooperative Autodidactic NeuroSurgeon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09175)
*Zheshun Wu,Ziyang Zhang,Changyao Lin,Zenglin Xu,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CANS框架，通过联邦线性UCB-动态加权（FedLinUCB-DW）算法，使多用户移动边缘计算环境中各设备能自适应学习最优DNN分区策略，在未知时变系统条件下降低协同推理延迟。实验结果表明，相比非协同基线，推理延迟最高降低50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多用户协同边缘推理中，由于无线链路波动和设备能力差异等未知时变系统条件，确定每个设备的最优DNN分区策略极具挑战性。

Method: 提出CANS框架，集成FedLinUCB-DW算法：将同类型设备分组，利用本地离线早期退出推理经验进行在线探索的热启动，并推导了遗憾上界的理论保证。

Result: 在仿真环境和硬件原型系统上验证，CANS相比SOTA基线实现更低推理延迟；原型实验中，两个边缘设备上平均推理延迟相比非协同基线降低可达50%。

Conclusion: CANS框架通过共享信息反馈和联邦学习机制，有效解决多用户异构环境下的DNN协同推理优化问题，显著降低推理延迟。

Abstract: Recently, mobile edge computing (MEC)-enabled collaborative deep neural network (DNN) inference has emerged as a promising approach for delivering intelligent services to resource-constrained mobile devices. A representative scenario is multi-user collaborative edge inference, where distinct devices independently partition their DNN models and offload backend computation to a common edge server over wireless networks. However, determining the optimal DNN partition for each device is challenging due to unknown and time-varying system conditions, including fluctuating wireless links and diverse device capabilities. To address this problem, we propose Cooperative Autodidactic NeuroSurgeon (CANS), a collaborative edge inference framework that enables devices to adaptively learn optimal DNN partitions by sharing informative feedback during online inference. To handle the challenge of device heterogeneity and better leverage offline inference experience, we integrate a novel FedLinUCB-DW algorithm that groups devices of the same type and warm-starts online exploration using local offline early-exit inference experience. Furthermore, we provide theoretical guarantees for FedLinUCB-DW by deriving the regret upper bound. We also validate our method on both a simulated environment and a hardware prototype system. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that CANS achieves lower inference latency compared to state-of-the-art baselines. Especially, in prototype experiments on two edge devices, the proposed CANS reduced average inference latency by up to 50% compared to the non-cooperative baseline.

</details>


### [219] [Asymptotic Optimality of Thompson Sampling for Risk-Averse Bandits with Sub-Gaussian Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09191)
*Joel Q. L. Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明无锚非参数汤普森采样算法ρ-NPTS_SG在风险厌恶多臂赌博机问题上达到实例最优的累积遗憾界，仅需风险泛函ρ连续即可，无需Lipschitz或优势条件，覆盖CVaR、夏普比率等非Lipschitz泛函，关键技术是离散化引理和截断离散化引理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险厌恶赌博机算法要么依赖参数化奖励假设（如高斯分布），要么要求风险泛函满足Lipschitz或优势条件，无法处理夏普比率等非Lipschitz泛函，且缺乏非参数场景下的实例最优保证。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出无锚非参数汤普森采样算法ρ-NPTS_SG，利用Dirichlet聚合性质将增长字母表的Dirichlet后验投影到固定网格上，设计离散化引理（有界支撑）和截断离散化引理（次高斯尾），将多项式因子的次数固定为与样本量无关的常数，打破超指数障碍。

Result: 达到与实例相关下界匹配的领先阶log n累积遗憾，证明算法在连续风险泛函类（CVaR、均值-方差、夏普比率、扭曲风险度量等）上渐近最优，仅需密度有界和次高斯尾条件，包括高斯臂。

Conclusion: 首次为非Lipschitz风险泛函（如夏普比率）在非参数奖励假设下提供实例最优保证，所提条件（连续性）严格弱于先前参数化汤普森采样（优势条件）和UCB型算法（Lipschitz条件）的要求。

Abstract: We prove that $ρ\text{-}\mathrm{NPTS}_{\mathrm{SG}}$, an anchor-free nonparametric Thompson Sampling algorithm for risk-averse bandits, achieves regret matching the instance-dependent lower bound to leading order in $\log n$, establishing it as asymptotically optimal for any continuous risk functional $ρ$ (CVaR, mean-variance, Sharpe ratio, distortion risk measures, and more) on the class of distributions with bounded density and sub-Gaussian tails, including Gaussian arms. Both this result and its bounded-support counterpart require only continuity of $ρ$: strictly weaker than the dominance condition of prior parametric Thompson Sampling results, and strictly weaker than the Lipschitz condition of UCB-type algorithms, yielding the first instance-optimal guarantees for non-Lipschitz functionals such as the Sharpe ratio without parametric reward assumptions. The bounded-support case is developed first as a stepping stone sharing the same proof structure. The key technical contributions are a discretisation lemma (bounded support) and a truncated discretisation lemma (sub-Gaussian tails), each projecting the growing-alphabet Dirichlet posterior onto a fixed grid via the Dirichlet aggregation property, holding all polynomial prefactors at fixed degree independent of sample size and breaking the super-exponential barrier that blocked prior proofs.

</details>


### [220] [The Injection Paradox: Brand-Level Suppression in Safety-Trained LLM Recommendations via RAG Context Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09204)
*Hyunseok Paeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了RAG-based LLM推荐系统中安全训练的一个可复现失败模式——"注入悖论"（Injection Paradox）：检索文档中的提示注入会反向攻击注入者，导致目标品牌推荐率低于无注入基线。在安全训练过的Claude模型中，注入文档及同品牌未修改文档的推荐率均显著下降；Claude Opus 4.6中目标品牌从54%基线降至0。而GPT模型中同一注入反而增加了推荐，表明模型家族差异。该发现揭示了对手可通过在竞争对手文档中嵌入注入来压制其品牌的逆向攻击可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示RAG-based LLM推荐系统中安全训练存在的可复现失败模式，以及提示注入在检索文档中的意外效应——不仅没有攻击成功，反而反向压制了目标品牌，并探究不同模型家族在这一现象上的行为差异。

Method: 在RAG-based推荐系统中，向检索到的品牌文档中嵌入提示注入，在安全训练的Claude模型和GPT模型上对比测试目标品牌的推荐率变化；进行反事实实验并在多个品牌上复现；测量注入对同品牌未修改文档的溢出效应。

Result: 在Claude Opus 4.6中，目标品牌从54%基线降至零top-2推荐（50次试验均如此），即使只有1/4的品牌文档包含注入。该抑制效应从注入文档传播到同品牌未修改文档。GPT模型上同一注入反而增加推荐，呈现相反的效应方向。

Conclusion: 安全训练在RAG推荐中可能产生非预期的逆向效应——注入不仅未能攻击成功，反而通过安全敏感性行为压制了目标品牌，且该效应具有模型家族特异性。这提出了一个逆向攻击场景：攻击者可利用此机制在竞争对手文档中嵌入注入来压制对方品牌。

Abstract: We present a reproducible failure mode of safety training in RAG-based LLM recommendation -- the Injection Paradox -- in which prompt injections embedded in retrieved documents backfire against the attacker, suppressing the target brand below the injection-free baseline. In safety-trained Claude models, documents containing prompt injections suffer a sharp drop in recommendation rate, and this suppression propagates beyond the injected document to unmodified documents of the same brand. In Claude Opus 4.6, the target brand drops from a 54% baseline to zero top-2 recommendations across all 50 trials, even though only 1 of 4 brand documents in the corpus contains an injection. The directional pattern is reproduced in counterfactual experiments and across three brands. A contrasting result across the GPT models tested, where the same injection instead increases recommendations, suggests model-family differences in how injection-like context affects recommendation behavior. These findings raise the technical possibility of a reverse-attack scenario in which an adversary embeds injections in a competitor's documents to suppress the competitor's brand via safety-sensitive model behavior.

</details>


### [221] [Orange Lab: Lowering Barriers to Data Mining through Embedded Interactive Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09239)
*Matej Bevec,Aleš Erjavec,Vesna Tanko,Lena Trnovec,Lan Žagar,Ana Farič,Janez Demšar,Blaž Zupan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Orange Lab是一个基于Web的协作式可视化数据分析环境，通过组件暴露（component exposition）范式，允许用户将工作流中的选定组件嵌入任意网页上下文，实现同步交互界面，从而降低数据科学入门门槛并支持数据驱动叙事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可视化数据分析工作流系统多为独立应用，难以将分析解决方案过渡到交互式网页环境，导致数据管道难以共享、嵌入和适配为用户面向的分析工具，限制了数据科学的民主化。

Method: 提出组件暴露（component exposition）范式，允许作者将选定的工作流组件或其部分界面嵌入任意网页上下文，创建同步的交互式界面，同时隐藏底层工作流复杂性。工作流中的任何组件交互都会无缝传播，使静态管道变为动态响应式系统。

Result: 在数据素养教育场景中部署，嵌入式组件引导学生动手探索机器学习概念，无需了解底层系统即可进行交互，有效降低了入门门槛。

Conclusion: Orange Lab通过组件暴露范式有效降低了数据科学的入门壁垒，支持数据科学的民主化，使数据分析能够直接集成到在线材料中，实现定制化分析视图和叙事驱动体验。

Abstract: While visual programming of data analysis workflows has become an important vehicle for the democratization of data science, such systems remain largely confined to standalone applications and offer limited support for transitioning their visual analytics solutions into interactive web environments. As a result, data analysis pipelines are difficult to share, embed, and adapt into user-facing analytical tools. We present Orange Lab, a web-based collaborative environment for visual data analytics. At its core, Orange Lab enables users to visually construct machine learning workflows from modular components, where interactions in any component propagate seamlessly through the workflow, turning static pipelines into dynamic, reactive systems that support exploration and data-driven storytelling. Our key contribution is component exposition, a paradigm that allows authors to embed selected workflow components, or parts of their interfaces, into arbitrary web contexts, creating synchronized, interactive interfaces while hiding underlying workflow complexity. This enables the development of tailored analytical views and narrative-driven experiences that integrate data analysis directly into online materials. We demonstrate the approach through deployments in data literacy education, where embedded components guide students in hands-on exploration of machine learning concepts without requiring knowledge of the underlying system, showing that Orange Lab effectively lowers barriers to entry and supports the democratization of data science.

</details>


### [222] [BSTabDiff: Block-Subunit Diffusion Priors for High-Dimensional Tabular Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09257)
*Al Zadid Sultan Bin Habib,Md Younus Ahamed,Prashnna Gyawali,Gianfranco Doretto,Donald A. Adjeroh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向高维小样本（HDLSS）表格数据的分块-子单元生成框架BSTabDiff，通过将高维特征划分为少量潜在块，利用共享低维子单元变量生成每个块，在紧凑的块潜在空间学习全局依赖，并借助Copula、灵活边缘分布和显式缺失机制解码到全特征空间，支持扩散流等深度先验，在HDLSS场景下生成更真实稳定的合成数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HDLSS表格数据（如组学数据）存在n≪m、强局部相关组、稀疏跨组依赖、重尾非高斯边缘、异方差噪声和结构化缺失等问题，直接在高维空间进行密度学习是病态的，现有非结构化生成器无法有效处理这些挑战。

Method: 将m个观测特征划分为M个潜在块（M≪m），每个块通过共享的低维子单元变量生成；全局依赖学习在紧凑的块潜在空间ℝ^M中完成；通过Copula驱动依赖、灵活逐特征边缘分布和显式缺失机制解码到全特征空间；支持扩散模型和归一化流等深度先验作为块潜在上的分布。

Result: 在HDLSS数据上，BSTabDiff相比非结构化表格生成器产生了更真实和稳定的高维合成数据。

Conclusion: BSTabDiff通过分块-子单元结构有效解决了HDLSS表格数据的密度学习难题，实现了稳定的合成与可控基准生成。

Abstract: High-Dimensional Low-Sample Size (HDLSS) tabular domains (e.g., omics) are characterized by $n \ll m$, where $n$ = number of samples, and $m$ = number of features. Such domains often exhibit strong local correlation groups, sparse cross-group dependencies, heavy-tailed non-Gaussian marginals, heteroscedastic noise, and structured missingness, making direct density learning in $\mathbb{R}^m$ ill-conditioned since $n \ll m$. We propose BSTabDiff, a block-subunit generative framework that partitions the $m$ observed features into $M$ latent blocks ($M \ll m$) and generates each block via a shared low-dimensional subunit variable, concentrating global dependence learning in the compact block-latent space $\mathbb{R}^M$ while decoding to the full feature space with copula-driven dependence, flexible per-feature marginals, and explicit missingness mechanisms. BSTabDiff supports modern deep priors on block latents, including diffusion and normalizing flows, enabling stable synthesis and controllable benchmark generation in the HDLSS regime. Empirically, BSTabDiff produces more realistic and stable high-dimensional synthetic data when compared with unstructured tabular generators on HDLSS data.

</details>


### [223] [ERBench: A Benchmark and Testsuite for Equation Discovery Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09276)
*Paul Kahlmeyer,Henrik Voigt,Michael Habeck,Joachim Giesen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ERBench基准，用于评估符号回归算法在方程发现任务中的方程恢复能力，重点关注算法在高维、不同采样分布和噪声条件下的鲁棒性，弥补了现有基准仅关注域内预测精度和公式数少、维度单一等不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号回归评测主要依赖域内测试精度（如随机划分训练/测试集），但这无法真实反映模型发现和泛化能力；域外精度测试虽更合理，但获取具有挑战性的域外测试数据困难。因此需要一种以方程恢复为核心、系统评估算法鲁棒性的基准。

Method: 提出了Equation Recovery Benchmark (ERBench)，一个专门评估方程发现任务的评测框架，系统控制维度、采样大小、采样分布和采样域等变量，测试算法在不同条件下的鲁棒性。

Result: ERBench能够更全面地评估符号回归算法在方程恢复任务上的表现，尤其是在变化维度、噪声、采样分布和域等条件下的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 以方程恢复为目标的评估方式更能反映算法在未知方程发现中的潜力，ERBench为从业者提供了更贴近真实场景的评测工具。

Abstract: Equation discovery aims to automate the discovery of scientific models in the form of mathematical equations from data. Technically, equation discovery is implemented by symbolic regression algorithms. Performance of symbolic regression for equation discovery is measured along two dimensions: Prediction accuracy on test data, and recovery of known groundtruth formulas. For standard regression, accuracy is typically measured on in-domain test data, for instance, by splitting a data set randomly into training and test data. While this makes sense for in-domain interpolation, which is the common goal in ordinary regression, it can be a misleading proxy for true model discovery and generalization. The obvious alternative is to measure out-of-domain accuracy. However, obtaining challenging out-of-domain test data is a non-trivial problem. Therefore, we focus on equation recovery for evaluating symbolic regression algorithms for equation discovery. The rationale is that symbolic regression algorithms that perform well in recovering known groundtruth formulas are good candidates to perform well in unknown equation discovery. Existing benchmarks for symbolic regression include equation recovery tasks, however, with only a small number of groundtruth formulas that are publicly known. Moreover, these benchmarks place less emphasis on evaluating the robustness of algorithms in terms of their behavior under changing dimensionality, sampling size, sampling distribution and sampling domain. This, however, is of central importance to practitioners wanting to discover equations for modeling natural phenomena, since data is almost certainly noisy and comes from diverse domains, distributions, and sample sizes. To fill this gap, we introduce the Equation Recovery Benchmark (ERBench), a new evaluation framework designed to rigorously assess algorithms explicitly targeting the task of equation discovery.

</details>


### [224] [Internalizing Geometric Law: Learning from Solver Residuals for Precision-Critical Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09278)
*Rafael Cabral,Pang Zixi,Ziyi Shou,Shen Xin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PyGeoX（可编程几何DSL）和PyGeoX-Bench（300个分层几何问题基准），发现全局范数奖励下的"异常梯度遮蔽"问题，并提出饱和加性奖励（SAR）机制，将奖励分解为有界逐约束项，在硬层级上相比MSE基线提升2.3倍求解率，8B模型性能与更大规模前沿系统相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在精度关键领域（如技术制图、机械设计）频繁产生幻觉，输出必须满足严格的几何约束。现有方法难以处理开放式的、需同时满足数十个相互作用约束的几何合成问题。

Method: 1) 提出PyGeoX，一个可编程几何DSL，将声明式约束编译为可微损失函数；2) 构建PyGeoX-Bench，含300个分层难度问题及可验证奖励；3) 识别异常梯度遮蔽（Outlier Gradient Masking）问题；4) 提出饱和加性奖励（SAR），将奖励分解为有界逐约束项。

Result: SAR相比MSE基线在硬层级上提升2.3倍求解率，基于8B参数模型的性能与更大规模前沿系统竞争。

Conclusion: 饱和加性奖励有效解决了异常梯度遮蔽问题，使较小模型也能在复杂几何约束求解任务中达到竞争力水平。

Abstract: Large Language Models frequently hallucinate in precision-critical domains such as technical diagramming and mechanical design, where outputs must satisfy strict geometric constraints. We study open-ended geometric synthesis from natural language: translating free-form descriptions into precise constructions whose entities must simultaneously satisfy dozens of interacting constraints. To make this tractable, we release PyGeoX, a programmable geometric DSL that compiles declarative constraints into a differentiable loss, and PyGeoX-Bench, a stratified suite of 300 problems with per-constraint verifiable rewards. Using PyGeoX as a verifier, we identify a failure mode we call Outlier Gradient Masking: under global-norm rewards (any scheme that aggregates residuals through a single norm, for example, $\exp(-\mathrm{MSE})$), a single outlier constraint can nullify the learning signal across all others. To address this, we propose Saturating Additive Rewards (SAR), which decompose the reward into bounded per-constraint terms, preserving partial progress and ensuring consistent gradients even under severe violations. Against MSE-based rewards, the natural baseline for geometry solvers, SAR improves the hard-tier solving rate by $2.3\times$, and the resulting 8B model is competitive with much larger frontier systems on this benchmark. We release the engine, benchmark, and data at https://github.com/Huawei-AI4Math/PyGeoX.

</details>


### [225] [Trajectory Geometry of Transformer Representations Across Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09287)
*Vishal Pandey,Gopal Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将Transformer前向传播视为高维表征流形上的离散轨迹，利用五类几何度量（轨迹长度、曲率、语义收敛指数、层间余弦相似度、表征稳定性）分析跨层演化规律。在三个模型家族与五种提示族中发现：语义相关提示在中后期收敛（趋同于吸引子动态）、推理任务曲率大于词汇变化（计算复杂度编码）、歧义token轨迹分叉、层间余弦相似度揭示三阶段通用结构（编码-精化-输出准备），全部效应在随机对照中消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性研究多关注模型编码了什么（如探针法），但对其表征如何随层数演化知之甚少。本文旨在利用计算神经科学的几何工具，建立一种无探针、模型无关的表征轨迹分析框架。

Method: 将Transformer前向传播定义为离散种群轨迹，在环境空间中直接计算五类几何度量：轨迹长度、曲率、语义收敛指数（CI）、层间余弦相似度、表征稳定性。在GPT-2、TinyLlama、Qwen2.5三个模型上，对五种控制提示族进行系统实验，并设置洗牌层与随机嵌入对照。

Result: 1) 语义相关提示在中后期显著收敛（峰值CI=0.41-0.58, p<0.001），符合吸引子动态；2) 推理任务曲率（0.71-0.83 rad）显著高于词汇变化（0.27-0.31 rad）；3) 歧义token最终层表征分离可达5.6倍；4) 层间余弦相似度揭示编码-精化-输出准备三阶段结构，跨架构一致。

Conclusion: 轨迹几何提供了一种原则性的、无探针的机械可解释性视角，所有效应在对照实验中消失，验证了方法有效性。作者发布了开源、模型无关的分析管线。

Abstract: Understanding how transformer representations evolve across layers, not merely what they encode, remains an open problem in mechanistic interpretability. We recast the transformer forward pass as a discrete population trajectory through a high-dimensional representation manifold, drawing on geometric tools from computational neuroscience. Rather than probing for pre-specified features, we characterize trajectory geometry using five metrics computed directly in the ambient space: trajectory length, curvature, a semantic convergence index, layerwise cosine similarity, and representational stability. Across three model families (GPT-2, TinyLlama, Qwen2.5) and five controlled prompt families, we report four findings. First, semantically related prompts converge significantly in middle-to-late layers (peak CI 0.41--0.58, p<0.001, Mann-Whitney U), consistent with attractor-like dynamics. Second, reasoning tasks produce trajectories of greater curvature than lexical variations (0.71--0.83 rad vs. 0.27--0.31 rad), suggesting curvature encodes computational complexity. Third, ambiguous tokens exhibit trajectory bifurcation with up to 5.6x representational separation by the final layer, absent in unambiguous controls. Fourth, layerwise cosine similarity reveals a universal three-phase structure: encoding, elaboration, and output preparation, consistent across all three architectures. All four effects vanish under shuffled-layer and random-embedding controls. We release a fully open-source, model-agnostic pipeline and argue that trajectory geometry constitutes a principled, probe-free lens for mechanistic interpretability.

</details>


### [226] [Intention Driven Identification of In-Possession Match Phases in Association Football through Temporal Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09289)
*Yuesen Li,Daniel Link*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时空追踪数据的足球控球阶段识别框架，采用层次化阶段模型（3种战术意图、6个阶段）和时序图注意力网络（T-GAN），在德甲数据集上取得了优秀的帧级和序列级识别表现，并验证了时序建模和图关系建模对阶段分割的不同贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足球比赛中控球阶段难以直接观察，且由战术意图驱动而非仅凭空间模式定义，现有方法缺乏对控球阶段的有效识别手段。

Method: 构建了包含3种战术意图（入侵对手空间、保持控球、射门）和6个阶段（构建、推进、反击、维持、持续威胁、终结）的层次化阶段模型；开发了时序图注意力网络（T-GAN），融合帧级球员交互图、上下文特征和Transformer时序建模。

Result: 在7场德甲比赛数据上，T-GAN在意图级别宏平均F1达0.87，入侵相关阶段F1为0.76，射门阶段F1为0.79；序列级IoT-D F1经后处理从0.68提升至0.79（意图）和从0.61提升至0.71（阶段）。时序建模是分割质量的主要驱动因素，图关系建模对反击识别贡献显著。

Conclusion: 该框架能够将连续追踪数据转化为战术可解释的控球阶段表示，可应用于自动化比赛标注、战术分析和打法特征刻画。

Abstract: Understanding tactical organisation of association football, hereafter referred to as football, requires identifying distinct match phases. Yet in-possession phases are rarely directly observable and are shaped by evolving tactical intentions, rather than spatial patterns alone. This study proposes a data-driven framework for identifying in-possession match phases from spatiotemporal tracking data. Seven German Bundesliga matches recorded at 25 Hz with TRACAB were analysed. A hierarchical phase model was defined with three tactical intentions (Invade Opponent Space, Keep Possession, Scoring) and six phases (Build Up, Progression, Counter Attack, Maintenance, Sustained Threat, Finishing). A Temporal Graph Attention Network (T-GAN) was developed to combine frame-level player-interaction graphs, contextual features, and Transformer-based temporal modelling. Performance was evaluated using frame-level F1 and a sequence-aware Intersection over Truth-Dominance (IoT-D) metric. T-GAN achieved macro-average frame-level F1 scores of 0.87 at the intention level, 0.76 for invasion-related phases, and 0.79 for scoring phases. At the sequence level, mean diagonal IoT-D F1 increased from 0.68 to 0.79 for intentions and from 0.61 to 0.71 for phases after post-processing, indicating improved temporal coherence. Model comparisons showed that sequence modelling was the main driver of segmentation quality, while graph-based relational modelling was particularly beneficial for Counter Attack recognition. Exploratory player attention analysis further suggested that wide and midfield positional groups contributed strongly to phase discrimination. Overall, the framework translates continuous tracking data into tactically interpretable in-possession phase representations, with potential applications in automated match annotation, tactical analysis, and playing-style profiling.

</details>


### [227] [PRISM: Topology-Aware Cross-Modal Imputation for Modality-Deficient Federated Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09301)
*Zekai Chen,Miao Zhang,Jiayang Xing,Xunkai Li,Xun Wu,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态联邦图学习（MM-FGL）中客户端层面模态缺失（如部分客户端仅有图像交互图而无文本，反之亦然）的现实问题，提出PRISM框架，通过拓扑感知的联邦跨模态补全方法，从联邦中主动检索并补全缺失模态语义，并在拓扑控制下注入本地图传播，避免补全误差被图结构放大。在六个多模态图数据集上，PRISM平均优于SOTA基线4.48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦图学习方法假设所有客户端共享相同模态基础，但实际场景中不同客户端可能缺失完整文本或图像模态（客户端级模态缺失）。不同于随机实例缺失，这种缺失导致客户端缺乏重建缺失模态所需的局部语义基础；更重要的是，在图学习中，不完整表示初始化消息传递，补全误差可能被接收拓扑过滤、混合和放大，而现有方法未考虑这一问题。

Method: 提出PRISM（Proactive Retrieval and Imputation via Structural Meta-prompting），一种拓扑感知的联邦跨模态补全框架。核心思想是从联邦中主动检索缺失模态语义，而非仅依赖局部观测重建；然后通过拓扑感知控制将补全的语义注入本地图传播过程，防止误差被图结构放大。

Result: 在六个多模态图数据集上，覆盖图中心任务和模态中心任务，PRISM一致提升了模态缺失客户端的性能，平均超越SOTA基线4.48%。

Conclusion: PRISM有效解决了客户端级模态缺失下的多模态联邦图学习问题，通过主动检索和拓扑感知补全策略，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Multimodal federated graph learning (MM-FGL) aims to collaboratively learn from decentralized graphs with text and images. However, real-world clients may not share a common modality basis: a visual-search client may contain image--interaction graphs but no seller descriptions, while a catalog client may provide text but no product images. We refer to this practical setting as client-level modality deficiency. Unlike random instance-wise missingness, a deficient client lacks the local semantic basis needed to reconstruct the absent modality. More importantly, in graph learning, incomplete representations initialize message passing, so imputation errors can be filtered, mixed, and amplified by the receiving topology. To address this gap, we propose \textbf{PRISM} (\textbf{P}roactive \textbf{R}etrieval and \textbf{I}mputation via \textbf{S}tructural \textbf{M}eta-prompting), a topology-aware federated cross-modal imputation framework. Rather than reconstructing the missing modality solely from local observations, PRISM recovers missing-modality semantics from the federation and introduces them into local graph propagation under topology-aware control. Experiments on six multimodal graph datasets across graph-centric and modality-centric tasks show that PRISM consistently improves modality-deficient clients, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines by \textbf{4.48}\% on average.

</details>


### [228] [Toward Compiler World Models: Learning Latent Dynamics for Efficient Tensor Program Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09312)
*Haolin Pan,Lianghong Huang,Xvlin Zhou,Mingjie Xing,Yanjun Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种受世界模型启发的张量程序优化评估器，通过动作条件潜在动力学建模调度轨迹，在相同搜索预算下显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动调度器依赖成本模型评估静态代码快照，忽略了产生该代码的调度轨迹，导致对动作依赖关系不敏感且易受表面代码变化影响。

Method: 将调度评估建模为动作条件的潜在动力学过程：从初始程序开始，在连续潜在空间中使用轻量级过渡模型滚动执行调度动作，避免昂贵的AST变异和重复编码，最终将动态表示与动作及硬件特征结合进行候选排名。

Result: 在TVM AutoScheduler中实现，64次试验预算下代表性子图延迟相比Ansor在GPU上提升1.37倍，CPU上提升1.54倍；使用10倍更少测量量即可在2.2%几何平均内匹配Ansor-10K性能；全模型推理比PyTorch/PyTorch-opt(cuDNN)加速4.61倍/3.67倍。

Conclusion: 通过建模调度轨迹而非静态快照，世界模型启发的方法能更高效地探索搜索空间，显著减少测量成本并提升优化效果。

Abstract: Tensor program optimization is essential for modern machine learning systems, but its search space is enormous. Existing auto-schedulers reduce measurement cost with learned cost models, yet they usually evaluate each candidate as a static code snapshot, ignoring the schedule trajectory that produced it. This makes them insensitive to action dependencies and vulnerable to superficial code variations. We propose a \emph{world-model-inspired} evaluator that models schedule evaluation as action-conditioned latent dynamics over program states. Starting from the initial program, it rolls out scheduling actions in a continuous latent space with a lightweight transition model, avoiding expensive AST mutation and repeated code encoding. The final dynamic representation is combined with action and hardware features to rank candidates. Implemented in TVM AutoScheduler, our method improves representative-subgraph latency over Ansor by 1.37$\times$ on GPU and 1.54$\times$ on CPU under the same 64-trial budget. It also matches Ansor-10K within 2.2% geometric mean using 10$\times$ fewer measurements, and accelerates full-model inference over PyTorch/PyTorch-opt(cuDNN) by 4.61$\times$/3.67$\times$ geometric mean.

</details>


### [229] [Machine-Learning Emulation of Satellite Greenhouse Gas Retrievals: Stability over Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09313)
*Nugzar Gognadze,Motonobu Kanagawa,Yu Someya,Hisashi Yashiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨机器学习模型作为温室气体反演算法仿真器的时效稳定性，发现预测精度随时间推移而下降，将时间作为输入特征可改善XCH4预测，且简单Lasso模型在性能和稳定性上优于神经网络等复杂方法，在TCCON验证中时间增强Lasso的误差与GOSAT和TCCON之间的差异相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅在同一时间段评估机器学习仿真器，缺乏对其时间稳定性的考察，无法判断模型在时间推移后是否仍保持预测精度。

Method: 使用GOSAT卫星数据，对比Lasso和神经网络等模型在不同时间段的预测性能，引入时间作为输入特征，并使用TCCON地面观测网络进行外部验证。

Result: 预测精度随测试期远离训练期而下降；时间特征显著提升Lasso和神经网络对XCH4的预测；Lasso模型在性能和稳定性上优于神经网络；时间增强Lasso在TCCON验证中误差与GOSAT-TCCON间差异相当。

Conclusion: 机器学习温室气体反演仿真器存在时间漂移问题，时间特征能部分缓解，但简单模型（Lasso）在时间稳定性上更具优势，适合实际部署。

Abstract: Retrieval algorithms are used to estimate atmospheric concentrations of greenhouse gases (GHGs), such as carbon dioxide (CO2) and methane (CH4), by solving inverse problems from high-spectral-resolution satellite radiance measurements. However, these algorithms are computationally expensive, which makes real-time estimation at scale difficult. Machine-learning models have therefore been proposed as fast emulators of retrieval algorithms. Most existing studies, however, evaluate them only on test data from the same period as the training data.
  We study the stability over time of such emulators using data from the Greenhouse Gases Observing SATellite (GOSAT). We show that prediction accuracy generally deteriorates when the test period moves away from the training period. We also show that including time as an input feature substantially improves XCH4 prediction for Lasso and neural-network models. Among the methods considered, a simple Lasso model performs as well as or better than more complex methods such as neural networks, and yields more stable predictions over time. We further validate the results using the Total Carbon Column Observing Network (TCCON), a ground-based observation network. On the TCCON-matched dataset, the time-augmented Lasso achieves errors against TCCON that are comparable to the disagreement between GOSAT and TCCON for both XCO2 and XCH4.

</details>


### [230] [A Universal Dense Football Event Representation Based on TabTransformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09327)
*Weiran Yang,Daniel Memmert,Maximilian Klemp-Weins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Transformer的模型，通过将足球事件数据中的类别特征编码为密集嵌入向量，学习动作语义的潜在依赖关系，在动作价值评估和风格识别等下游任务中实现了优于基线方法的概率校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多采用独热编码或序数嵌入处理足球事件数据中的类别特征，忽略了动作描述符的内在语义信息，需要更有效的表示学习方法。

Method: 基于Transformer架构，将类别事件特征编码为可学习的嵌入向量，通过自注意力机制捕获类别特征间的潜在依赖关系，在大规模预训练中学习运动特定的动作语义。

Result: 在下游预测任务中，嵌入表示在Brier分数上优于任务特定基线方法，展现出更优的概率校准性能。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的密集表示能够有效捕获足球事件中类别特征的语义信息，为动作价值估计和比赛风格识别等下游任务提供更好的特征表示。

Abstract: Football event data constitute a rich spatiotemporal source for quantitative analysis of player actions in team sports. These datasets contain heterogeneous features, combining continuous location coordinates with categorical variables such as action type, action outcome, and body part. Such data have been applied in sports analytics for match outcome forecasting, player evaluation, and tactical pattern recognition. However, existing approaches predominantly encode categorical features using one-hot or ordinal embedding representations, overlooking the intrinsic semantics of action descriptors. The Transformer is a deep neural network architecture based on self-attention that captures dependencies between input features at arbitrary positions. We propose and implement a Transformer-based model to learn latent dependencies among categorical event features and produce dense representations of football events. By encoding categorical features as learned embedding vectors, sport-specific action semantics are captured during pretraining, enabling the representations to support downstream tasks such as action value estimation and play style recognition. Empirical evaluation shows that the embedding representations yield superior probability calibration over task-specific baselines on the downstream prediction tasks, as measured by Brier score.

</details>


### [231] [Thresholded Local Hyper-Flow Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09340)
*Meher Chaitanya,Sebastian Dalleiger,Luana Ruiz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TL-HFD是一种保持局部性的阈值化超图扩散方法，通过限制活跃区域并采用阈值化边界激活，在理论上证明局部更新等价于全局更新，并给出有限时间对偶次优性保证，实验中在保持或提升分割质量的同时减少了激活体积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有局部超图扩散(HFD)求解器在每次迭代中未能保持计算的局部性，导致在噪声场景下扩散会吸收非目标顶点，降低了分割的准确性和效率。

Method: 提出TL-HFD：一阶方法，维护种子周围的活跃区域，在该区域及其直接边界上执行投影次梯度更新，并通过阈值化(top-k)边界激活进行扩展。理论上证明活跃区域上的度预处理投影次梯度步与全局更新等价，将阈值化更新视为带有显式跳过边界误差的非精确投影次梯度步。

Result: 建立了精确更新和阈值化更新的有限时间对偶次优性；推导了受局部次梯度范数和最小边界推动控制的加性激活体积界；将带有局部化支持的近似对偶最优性转化为早期停止迭代的鲁棒扫描割保证。实验表明TL-HFD通常匹配或优于HFD，同时激活更少体积，在噪声实例上提升最大。

Conclusion: TL-HFD通过阈值化策略有效保持了扩散的局部性，在理论上保证了收敛性和分割质量，在实践中对噪声场景具有更强的鲁棒性和效率优势。

Abstract: Local Hyper-Flow Diffusion (HFD) gives an edge-size-independent Cheeger-type guarantee for seeded clustering in general submodular hypergraphs, but existing HFD solvers do not keep intermediate computation local at every iteration. We introduce Thresholded Local HFD (TL-HFD), a first-order method that maintains an active region around the seeds, performs projected subgradient updates on that region and its immediate boundary, and expands via thresholded (top-k) boundary activation. We prove that the local update is exact: the degree-preconditioned projected subgradient step restricted to the active region and its boundary coincides with the unrestricted global update. We establish finite-time dual suboptimality for both exact and thresholded updates, treating the latter as inexact projected subgradient steps with explicit skipped-boundary error. We further derive an additive activated-volume bound controlled by realized local subgradient norms and the minimum boundary-push among newly activated vertices, and translate approximate dual optimality with localized support into a robust sweep-cut guarantee for early-stopped iterates. For general submodular cut-costs, each iteration is local in the scanned region and oracle-sensitive in the hyperedge primitive. Empirically, TL-HFD often matches or improves over HFD while activating less volume, with the largest gains on noisy instances where diffusion tends to absorb non-target vertices.

</details>


### [232] [PBSD: Privileged Bayesian Self-Distillation for Long-Horizon Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09348)
*Yang Tian,Rui Wang,Xumeng Wen,Junjie Li,Shizhao Sun,Lei Song,Jiang Bian,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PBSD提出了一种基于贝叶斯自蒸馏的方法，通过后验/先验概率比将稀疏的最终奖励转化为每个回合的细粒度信用赋值信号，从而有效解决长程智能体任务中的信用分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体任务中，基于结果的强化学习面临严重的信用分配难题：轨迹级奖励只能验证最终正确性，却无法指导中间推理步骤或工具交互对结果的贡献程度。这一问题在多轮搜索智能体中尤为突出。

Method: PBSD通过验证答案的后验与先验概率比衡量轨迹质量，利用贝叶斯法则将难以直接估计的答案侧比率转化为可解的学生模型与特权答案条件教师模型之间的似然比。通过自回归分解该贝叶斯证据分数，获得每个回合级别的信号，识别中间回合是否支持或削弱验证结果。

Result: 实验表明，PBSD在领域内和领域外场景中均能持续提升性能，并能有效将短上下文训练的知识迁移到长上下文推理中。

Conclusion: PBSD提供了一种原则性强且优雅的重新加权方案，将稀疏的最终监督转化为贝叶斯校准的回合级信用信号，同时与标准策略优化完全兼容。其细粒度信用分配机制有助于更有效的策略学习并提升泛化能力。

Abstract: Long-horizon agentic tasks pose a fundamental credit assignment challenge for outcome-base reinforcement learning: trajectory-level rewards verify final correctness but provide limited guidance on which intermediate reasoning steps or tool interactions contribute to the outcome. The difficulty is especially pronounced in multi-turn search agents, where successful trajectories may contain misleading actions and failed trajectories may contain valuable evidence-gathering steps. We propose PBSD (Privileged Bayesian Self-Distillation), a Bayes-calibrated self-distillation method for fine-grained credit assignment under sparse final rewards. PBSD measures trajectory quality through the posterior-to-prior probability ratio of the verified answer and applies Bayes' rule to convert this hard-to-estimate answer-side ratio into a tractable likelihood ratio between a standard student model and a privileged answer-conditioned teacher model. Autoregressive decomposition of this Bayesian evidence score yields turn-level signals that identify whether each intermediate turn supports or undermines the verified outcome. Consequently, PBSD provides a principled and elegant reweighting scheme that transforms sparse outcome supervision into Bayes-calibrated turn-level credit signals, while remaining fully compatible with standard policy optimization. Experiments demonstrate that PBSD consistently enhances performance across both in-domain and out-of-domain settings, and effectively transfers knowledge from short-context training to long-context inference, suggesting that its fine-grained credit assignment mechanism facilitates more effective policy learning and yields improved generalization.

</details>


### [233] [Scaling Neural Network Verification with Tensor Parallelism and Fully Sharded Data Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09377)
*Sergei Vorobyov,Eugene Ilyushin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对形式化神经网络验证中GPU内存瓶颈问题，将张量并行（TP）和全分片数据并行（FSDP）两种并行技术适配到auto_LiRPA/α,β-CROWN验证框架中。TP通过分片权重和A矩阵减少约2倍峰值内存，但边界紧度随分片区数量增加而下降；FSDP仅分片权重矩阵，产生的边界与单GPU基准比特级一致，内存降低80-90%，峰值内存降低34-39%，并成功应用于完整验证（β-CROWN+Branch-and-Bound）和卷积层。实验表明，α-CROWN+BaB模式下的内存瓶颈是逐神经元alpha张量而非权重矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化神经网络验证中，标准边界传播算法（IBP、CROWN、α-CROWN）受限于GPU内存，权重和松弛系数矩阵必须完全驻留在单个加速器上，限制了可验证网络的规模。

Method: 将张量并行（TP）和全分片数据并行（FSDP）两种大规模模型训练中的并行技术适配到auto_LiRPA/α,β-CROWN验证框架中。TP同时对权重矩阵和A矩阵进行分片；FSDP仅对权重矩阵分片，每层使用AllGather操作。

Result: TP在P=2时实现约2倍峰值内存降低，但边界紧度因分片区内的IBP替换而下降。FSDP产生的边界与单GPU基准比特级一致，基线内存降低80-90%，峰值内存降低34-39%。FSDP成功应用于β-CROWN+Branch-and-Bound完整验证和卷积层，在CIFAR-100 ResNet-large上获得完整unsat结果。

Conclusion: FSDP更优，能保持边界紧度且显著降低内存消耗；α-CROWN+BaB模式下的主要内存瓶颈是逐神经元alpha张量而非权重矩阵，为未来工作指明了方向。

Abstract: Formal neural network verification -- proving that a network satisfies safety properties for \emph{all} inputs in a specified domain -- is bounded in practice by GPU memory: standard implementations of bound-propagation algorithms (IBP, CROWN, $α$-CROWN) require weight and relaxation-coefficient matrices to reside entirely on one accelerator. We adapt two parallelism techniques originally developed for large-scale model training to the \texttt{auto\_LiRPA}\,/\,$α,β$-CROWN verification framework. \textbf{Tensor Parallelism (TP)} shards both weight and $A$-matrices across GPUs, achieving ${\approx}2\times$ peak-memory reduction at $P{=}2$; soundness is confirmed on VNN-COMP 2022 MNIST-FC benchmarks, though bound tightness degrades with the number of sharded zones due to forced IBP substitution for intermediate bounds inside sharded zones. \textbf{Fully Sharded Data Parallelism (FSDP)} shards only weight matrices with a per-layer \texttt{AllGather}, producing bounds that are \emph{bitwise identical} to the single-GPU baseline: baseline memory drops by 80--90\%, peak memory by 34--39\% on wide MLPs. FSDP integrates cleanly with complete verification ($β$-CROWN + Branch-and-Bound) and with convolutional layers (\texttt{BoundConv}); a complete \emph{unsat} result is obtained for CIFAR-100 ResNet-large (VNN-COMP 2024) under FSDP. Across all experiments the memory bottleneck in $α$-CROWN+BaB mode proves to be per-neuron alpha tensors, not weight matrices, pointing to the key direction for future work.

</details>


### [234] [Reasoning Arena: Trace Tournaments When Verifiable Rewards Fall Short](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09380)
*Han Zhou,Adam X. Yang,Laurence Aitchison,Anna Korhonen,Albert Q. Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对强化学习中使用可验证奖励时出现的"零优势样本"问题（同一提示下所有轨迹获得相同奖励），本文提出Reasoning Arena框架，通过构建轨迹锦标赛将组内推理质量差异转化为相对奖励信号，并利用Bradley-Terry模型高效整合，在数学和编程基准上平均提升7.6%，加速训练27%-41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RLVR范式中，可验证奖励在组级别经常变得无信息：当同一提示的所有采样轨迹获得相同奖励时，组相对优势估计无法提供梯度信号，即使这些轨迹在推理质量上存在显著差异，导致大量样本被浪费。

Method: 提出Reasoning Arena框架：1）将无多样性的奖励组路由至裁判系统；2）构建轨迹锦标赛，将推理轨迹进行两两比较以揭示组内细粒度偏好；3）为提升效率，每条新轨迹仅与动态更新的少量锚定轨迹进行比较；4）在不完全比较图上拟合Bradley-Terry模型，实现可扩展的强化学习集成。

Result: 在竞赛数学和编程基准上，Reasoning Arena相比RLVR基线平均提升7.6%；训练加速27%-41%；节省近50%的生成计算量；有效将原本被浪费的零优势样本转化为有用的梯度更新。

Conclusion: 通过将无信息奖励组中的推理质量差异转化为相对奖励信号，Reasoning Arena不仅提升了推理性能，还大幅提高了训练效率和计算资源利用率。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a leading paradigm for improving the reasoning ability of large language models through outcome-based supervision. However, verifiable rewards frequently become uninformative at the group level: when all sampled traces of a given prompt receive identical rewards, group-relative advantage estimation provides no gradient signal, even though the traces may differ substantially in reasoning quality. We propose Reasoning Arena, an adaptive training framework that routes such non-diverse reward groups to a judge system instead of discarding them. Beyond examining the final answer, Reasoning Arena constructs trace tournaments, where reasoning traces are compared head-to-head to expose finer-grained preferences within the group, converting reasoning quality into rich relative reward signals. To make reward estimation efficient, rather than exhaustively comparing every pair, each new trace is evaluated against a small, dynamically updated pool of previously generated traces as anchors to efficiently establish a relative ranking. We then fit a Bradley-Terry model on the incomplete comparison graph, enabling scalable RL integration without quadratic pairwise comparisons. Empirical results demonstrate that Reasoning Arena consistently outperforms the RLVR baseline by 7.6% on average in competition mathematics and coding benchmarks. By converting otherwise wasted zero-advantage samples into useful gradient updates, our method accelerates training by 27% to 41%, saving nearly 50% of generation compute, and substantially improves overall reasoning performance.

</details>


### [235] [Distilling Safe LLM Systems via Soft Prompts for On Device Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09388)
*Motasem Alfarra,Cristina Pinneri,Dana Kianfar,Mohammed Almousa,Christos Louizos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对资源受限的边缘设备上部署安全大语言模型（LLM）的挑战，系统研究了参数高效的安全对齐方法。通过跨多种LLM架构、训练目标和参数高效微调方法的系统评估，发现软提示（soft prompts）结合基于蒸馏的训练方法始终优于其他方案。提出了基于总变差和KL散度的蒸馏框架，有效将防护模型的安全行为迁移到学习的软提示中。实验表明，该方法在安全性-有用性权衡上优于LoRA适配器、引导向量和直接优化方法，且推理时仅需极少的额外内存和计算。研究确立了软提示蒸馏作为边缘设备LLM安全对齐的首选方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的边缘设备上部署安全LLM面临关键挑战：虽然结合LLM和防护模型的双系统能提供有效安全保证，但其巨大的内存和计算需求使得设备端部署代价过高。现有方法缺乏针对资源受限场景的全面研究，亟需参数高效的安全对齐方案。

Method: 1) 系统评估多种LLM架构、训练目标和参数高效微调方法；2) 提出基于总变差（Total Variation）和KL散度的蒸馏框架；3) 将防护模型的安全行为通过蒸馏迁移到可学习的软提示中。

Result: 软提示结合蒸馏训练在安全性-有用性权衡上显著优于LoRA适配器、引导向量和直接优化方法，且在推理时仅需极少量额外内存和计算资源。

Conclusion: 软提示蒸馏被确立为资源受限边缘设备上LLM安全对齐的首选方法，为实现安全且高效的设备端LLM部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Deploying safe large language models (LLMs) on resource-constrained edge devices presents a critical challenge: while dual-model systems combining LLMs with guard models provide effective safety guarantees, their substantial memory and computational demands make them prohibitively expensive for on-device deployment. This paper presents a comprehensive study of parameter-efficient safety alignment methods for resource-constrained settings. Through systematic evaluation across multiple LLM architectures, training objectives, and parameter-efficient fine-tuning approaches, we identify that soft prompts combined with distillation-based training consistently outperform alternative methods. We introduce distillation frameworks based on total variation and KL divergence that effectively transfer safety behaviors from guard models into learned soft prompts. Our evaluations on various benchmarks demonstrate that this combination achieves superior safety-usefulness trade-offs compared to LoRA adapters, steering vectors, and direct optimization methods, while requiring minimal additional memory and compute at inference time. These findings establish soft prompt distillation as the preferred approach for safety alignment in on-device LLM deployment.

</details>


### [236] [Benchmarking Empirical Privacy Protection for Adaptations of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09401)
*Bartłomiej Marek,Lorenzo Rossi,Vincent Hanke,Xun Wang,Michael Backes,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了差分隐私（DP）适配大语言模型（LLM）时的实际隐私风险。通过在不同数据分布（完全重叠、同分布、分布外）下使用最强攻击方法进行基准测试，发现数据分布偏移显著影响隐私脆弱性：适配数据越接近预训练分布，实际隐私风险越高。参数高效微调方法（如LoRA）对分布外数据提供了最高的经验隐私保护。最后提出了一个涵盖预训练-适配全管线的结构化隐私评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管差分隐私（DP）在理论上为LLM适配提供了隐私保障，但其实际有效性尚不明确。主要原因在于LLM预训练数据与适配数据之间存在重叠和相互依赖，这可能会在DP努力下仍然破坏隐私。现有研究缺乏对这一问题在实际场景中的系统分析。

Method: 使用最先进的攻击方法（如鲁棒成员推断和Canary数据提取）对DP适配下的LLM进行隐私风险评估。系统性地改变适配数据分布（精确重叠→同分布→完全分布外），并评估不同适配方法和不同隐私预算对脆弱性的影响。

Result: 分布偏移强烈影响隐私脆弱性：适配数据越接近预训练分布，实际隐私风险越高，即使没有直接数据重叠。参数高效微调方法（如LoRA）对分布外数据实现了最高的经验隐私保护。

Conclusion: 研究识别了实现DP LLM适配中实际隐私的关键因素，为在敏感场景中部署定制模型提供了可操作的见解。并提出了一个超越适配隐私的全结构化框架，用于识别和评估LLM预训练-适配全管线的隐私风险。

Abstract: Recent work has applied differential privacy (DP) to adapt large language models (LLMs) for sensitive applications, offering theoretical guarantees. However, its practical effectiveness remains unclear, partly due to LLM pretraining, where overlaps and interdependencies with adaptation data can undermine privacy despite DP efforts. To analyze this issue in practice, we investigate privacy risks under DP adaptations in LLMs using state-of-the-art attacks such as robust membership inference and canary data extraction. We benchmark these risks by systematically varying the adaptation data distribution, from exact overlaps with pretraining data, through in-distribution (IID) cases, to entirely out-of-distribution (OOD) examples. Additionally, we evaluate how different adaptation methods and different privacy regimes impact the vulnerability. Our results show that distribution shifts strongly influence privacy vulnerability: the closer the adaptation data is to the pretraining distribution, the higher the practical privacy risk at the same theoretical guarantee, even without direct data overlap. We find that parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods, such as LoRA, achieve the highest empirical privacy protection for OOD data. Our benchmark identifies key factors for achieving practical privacy in DP LLM adaptation, providing actionable insights for deploying customized models in sensitive settings. Looking forward, we propose a structured framework for holistic privacy assessment beyond adaptation privacy, to identify and evaluate risks across the full pretrain-adapt pipeline of LLMs.

</details>


### [237] [LargeMonitor: Monitoring Online Task-Free Continual Learning via Large Pretrained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09430)
*Mingqi Yuan,Xiaoquan Sun,Shihao Luo,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LargeMonitor框架，利用预训练大模型（LVM用于零样本漂移检测，LMM用于语义诊断）实现解耦的、无需训练耦合的在线无任务持续学习，能够自主检测数据流分布漂移并诊断其语义病因（如新类出现或环境域偏移），从而动态部署适应性的优化策略，显著提升现有TFCL算法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线TFCL方法（如提示调优、动态结构扩展）严重依赖训练耦合的优化动态（如损失波动、潜在距离）来驱动适应，无法区分数据流变化的根本结构来源，对所有分布漂移都机械地施以固定策略，缺乏针对性的自适应能力。

Method: LargeMonitor包含两个解耦模块：(1) 检测模块——利用冻结的大视觉模型(LVM)的稳定表示空间实现零样本漂移检测，无需训练依赖或阈值调优；(2) 诊断模块——通过大多模态模型(LMM)解释流变化的精确语义病因（如新类涌现 vs. 环境域偏移）。基于此双阶段能力，动态部署漂移类型特定的优化策略。

Result: 在多个TFCL设置和基准上的大量实验表明，LargeMonitor实现了对复杂数据流的精确、鲁棒的检测与诊断，并持续提升了现有在线TFCL算法的性能。

Conclusion: 利用预训练基础模型解耦漂移检测与诊断，能够克服训练耦合范式的固有限制，为在线TFCL提供更具结构感知能力的自动化连续适应方案。

Abstract: Online task-free continual learning (TFCL) requires intelligent agents to sequentially accumulate knowledge from an unbounded, non-stationary data stream under strict single-pass constraints and without any explicit task identifiers. Existing online TFCL paradigms primarily rely on parameter-efficient prompt tuning or dynamic structure expansion driven by training-coupled optimization dynamics, such as empirical loss fluctuations or evolving latent distances. As a result, these training-coupled solvers remain agnostic to the structural origins of distribution drift, mechanically enforcing a fixed strategy across fundamentally distinct streaming variations. To address this gap, we propose LargeMonitor, a framework that leverages large pretrained foundation models to autonomously orchestrate task-free continuous adaptation. Specifically, LargeMonitor introduces a decoupled detection module utilizing the frozen, stable representation space of large vision models (LVMs) to achieve robust, zero-shot drift detection without training-dependent interference or brittle threshold tuning. Upon a confirmed drift, the framework activates a context-aware diagnostic module driven by large multimodal models (LMMs) to interpret the precise semantic etiologies of the stream variation (e.g., novel class emergence vs. environmental domain shift). This dual-stage capability empowers the continuous learner to dynamically deploy adaptive and shift-specific optimization strategies. Extensive experiments across multiple TFCL settings and benchmarks demonstrate that LargeMonitor achieves precise, robust detection and diagnosis of complex data streams while consistently improving the performance of existing online TFCL algorithms.

</details>


### [238] [Graph Mamba Operator: A Latent Simulator for Interacting Particle Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09432)
*Karn Tiwari,Niladri Dutta,N M Anoop Krishnan,Prathosh A P*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出图曼巴算子(GraMO)，一种将状态空间模型与图交互学习集成的潜在空间模拟器，通过单一循环耦合图交互和时间状态更新，实现长程预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN方法自回归展开导致误差累积，且时空分离处理难以捕捉多跳依赖和全局结构，缺乏长程时域建模能力。

Method: GraMO在单一循环中融合图基交互与时间状态更新，状态更新线性且系数依赖输入自适应变化，区别于现有按节点排序或分阶段时空更新的做法。

Result: 在N体系统、运动捕捉和机器人数据集上取得最低误差，尤其在长程预测中增益最大。

Conclusion: GraMO通过在潜在空间中耦合图交互与时间演化，有效解决了长程预测中的误差累积问题，优于现有方法。

Abstract: Modeling interacting dynamical systems requires capturing spatial interactions alongside long-range temporal dependencies. Graph neural networks (GNNs) provide a natural representation but typically rely on autoregressive rollouts and treat spatial and temporal dynamics separately, leading to error accumulation over long horizons. Existing approaches also focus on local interactions and short temporal contexts, limiting their ability to capture multi-hop dependencies and global structure. We introduce the Graph Mamba Operator (GraMO), a latent-space simulator that integrates state-space models with graph-based interaction learning. In contrast to prior work that sequences nodes or applies spatial and temporal updates in separate stages, GraMO couples graph-based interactions and temporal state updates within a single recurrence. The update is linear in the latent state, with input-dependent coefficients that adapt across regimes. We evaluate GraMO on N-body systems, motion capture, and robotics datasets, achieving the lowest error across benchmarks and the largest gains in long-horizon prediction.

</details>


### [239] [Operator learning for solving Fokker-Planck equations with various initial conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09434)
*Li Zeng,Xiaoliang Wan,Yaobin Wang,Fabio Nobile,Tao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于条件归一化流的物理信息神经网络（PINN）框架，用于高效逼近福克-普朗克方程在多种初始条件下的解算子。通过Chapman-Kolmogorov方程将问题重构为转移概率密度逼近，利用线性化SDE的PDF作为归一化流的基础分布，并引入时间加权损失函数来缓解小时间尺度下的数值不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FPE在随机动力学中至关重要，但现有数值方法针对不同初始条件需重复求解，缺乏高效统一的解算子逼近方法。

Method: 1) 利用Chapman-Kolmogorov方程将FPE求解转化为转移PDF逼近问题；2) 使用线性化SDE的PDF作为归一化流的基础分布，避免Dirac delta初始分布的奇异性；3) 引入时间加权损失函数平衡因果关系与训练难度。

Result: 多种数值实验表明该方法在逼近FPE解算子方面具有有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够高效处理多种初始条件下的FPE求解，通过归一化流和时间加权策略有效解决了Dirac初值奇异性和小时间尺度数值不稳定性问题。

Abstract: The Fokker-Planck equation (FPE) plays a pivotal role in describing the time evolution of probability density functions (PDFs) for systems governed by stochastic dynamics. In this work, we propose a conditional normalizing flow-based physics-informed neural network (PINN) framework for efficiently approximating the solution operator of the FPE for a whole range of initial conditions. Leveraging the Chapman-Kolmogorov equation for Markovian stochastic processes, the problem is reformulated into approximating a transition PDF starting at initial time from a Dirac mass centered at an arbitrary point. The PDF of an associated linearized stochastic differential equation (SDE) is employed as the base distribution for the normalizing flow, providing a good approximation of the target PDF, especially for small times, and thereby avoiding the singularity of the map associated with the Dirac delta initial distribution. Furthermore, a time-weighted loss function is introduced to mitigate numerical instabilities arising at small times, achieving a balance between causality and training difficulty as time progresses. A variety of numerical experiments are presented to illustrate the effectiveness and robustness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [240] [Breaking the Tokenizer Barrier: On-Policy Distillation across Model Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09456)
*Yifan Niu,Han Xiao,Dongyi Liu,Zelong Wang,Dihong Gong,Yasheng Wang,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨分词器的同策略蒸馏方法，通过精确的token映射算法，使得教师模型与学生模型无需共享同一分词器即可进行标准同策略蒸馏，实验证明该方法在计算效率上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同策略蒸馏方法要求教师和学生模型共享相同的分词器，这限制了OPD在模型系列之间的适用性；而当前跨分词器的主流做法是使用SFT在教师生成的回答上进行蒸馏，无法捕获教师概率分布中蕴含的丰富知识。

Method: 提出了一种跨分词器的同策略蒸馏方法，通过精确的token映射算法，使得高保真的token级信号能够跨不同分词器传播，从而在模型家族之间实现标准的同策略蒸馏。

Result: 大量实验表明，跨分词器OPD在各种基准测试中比基线方法显著更高效，在计算效率上具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 该方法解锁了更广泛的教师-学生模型对用于同策略蒸馏，为LLMs之间的适应和交互增强开辟了新的途径。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) has become a core technique in the post-training of Large Language Models (LLMs) for transferring knowledge from domain experts to student models. However, existing OPD distillation methods require teacher and student models to share the same tokenizer, restricting the applicability of OPD within the model series. Current mainstream practice typically employs Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) on teacher-generated responses for cross-tokenizer distillation, which fails to capture the rich knowledge embedded in the teacher's probability distribution. In this work, we enable the standard on-policy distillation method to operate across model families, ensuring that high-fidelity token-level signals can propagate across different tokenizers with a precise token-mapping algorithm. Extensive experiments show that cross-tokenizer OPD is significantly more compute-efficient than baselines on various benchmarks. Our results unlock a broader range of teacher-student pairs for OPD, opening up new avenues for adapting and enhancing interactions between LLMs.

</details>


### [241] [Escaping the KL Agreement Trap in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09471)
*Haoran Xin,Anhao Zhao,Ying Sun,Jin Li,Xiaoyu Shen,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中存在“低KL散度一致陷阱”（low-KL agreement trap），即当学生模型产生不可恢复的前缀时，教师模型仍会局部认可退化状态，导致提供低反向KL但缺乏纠正信号的无效监督。为此，提出KAT（KL Agreement Trap Termination）方法，通过动态自适应阈值检测并终止此类持久陷阱，过滤弱监督信号。在四个数学基准上，KAT使avg@k提升2.66%，pass@k提升3.43%，同时平均生成长度减少59.73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中，当学生模型漂移到不可恢复的前缀时，教师模型可能局部认可退化状态，产生低反向KL但缺乏纠正训练信号的监督，形成“低KL一致陷阱”，影响蒸馏效果。

Method: 提出KAT（KL Agreement Trap Termination），一种在线OPD终止规则，通过动态训练自适应阈值检测并终止持续的低KL一致状态，从而过滤退化一致产生的弱监督信号。

Result: 在四个数学基准上，KAT使avg@k准确率提升2.66%，pass@k提升3.43%，同时平均rollout长度减少59.73%。

Conclusion: 低KL一致陷阱是OPD中导致无效监督的关键问题，KAT通过动态检测和终止机制能有效提升蒸馏质量和计算效率。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense token-level supervision by asking a teacher to score student-generated rollouts. However, when the student drifts into an unrecoverable prefix, the teacher may locally agree with the degraded state, producing low reverse KL but little corrective training signal. We identify this persistent regime as a low-KL agreement trap. Further analyses show that tokens during and after such traps produce less useful supervision signals. We propose KAT (KL Agreement Trap Termination), an online OPD termination rule that detects persistent low-KL agreement with a dynamic training-adaptive threshold. By filtering weak supervision from degenerate agreement, KAT improves avg@k accuracy by 2.66% and pass@k by 3.43% across four mathematical benchmarks, while reducing average rollout length by 59.73%.

</details>


### [242] [Loss-Guided Adaptive Scale Refinement for Molecular Force Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09480)
*Limin Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种损失引导的自适应尺度细化框架，用于分子力预测。该框架将预定义的尺度作为初始锚点，通过插值、路由、可微尺度更新和尺度池精炼来发现任务有效的分辨率。在NaCl水溶液离子体系上的实验表明，该方法能自动生成中间尺度，显著降低力预测误差（MAE从399.65降至381.23），尤其在近接触区域效果明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子系统涉及从局部配位到长程静电等多种空间尺度的相互作用，但现有分子表示学习方法大多依赖人工预定义的固定尺度，而任务最优的建模尺度可能与这些固定水平不一致，因此需要一种能够自适应发现任务有效尺度的方法。

Method: 提出损失引导的自适应尺度细化框架，包括：(1) 将预定义尺度作为初始锚点；(2) 通过插值、路由、可微尺度更新和尺度池精炼四种操作发现任务有效分辨率；(3) 构建短尺度和长程力预测分支并分析其互补性。

Result: 在NaCl水溶液离子体系上：Oracle硬路由将整体力MAE从399.65降至382.67，连续Oracle插值进一步降至380.96；近接触区域MAE从327.22降至260.51；从端点锚点{0,1}出发，损失引导更新自动生成中间尺度，最终尺度池{0,0.125,0.25,0.375,0.5,0.75,1}达到整体MAE 381.23。

Conclusion: 自适应尺度细化是分子表示学习的一个有前景方向，尤其是在固定尺度建模不足的情况下，该方法能自动发现任务最优的尺度组合，显著提升分子力预测性能。

Abstract: Molecular systems involve interactions across multiple spatial scales, from local coordination and short-range perturbations to long-range electrostatic and solvent-mediated effects. However, most molecular representation learning methods rely on manually predefined scales, and the task-optimal modeling scale may not coincide with these fixed levels. This study introduces a loss-guided adaptive scale refinement framework for molecular force prediction, treating predefined scales as initial anchors and discovering task-effective resolutions through interpolation, routing, differentiable scale updates, and scale pool refinement.
  Using a NaCl aqueous ionic system as a minimal testbed, this study constructs short-scale and long-range force prediction branches and analyzes their complementarity. Oracle hard routing reduces the overall force MAE from 399.65 to 382.67, while continuous oracle interpolation further reduces it to 380.96. In close-contact regimes with nearest-ion distance below 0.6 nm, the close-contact MAE decreases from 327.22 to 260.51. A minimal scale pool update experiment shows that starting from endpoint anchors {0,1}, loss-guided updates automatically generate intermediate scales and recover most of the continuous oracle performance. The final updated scale pool {0,0.125,0.25,0.375,0.5,0.75,1} achieves an overall MAE of 381.23.
  These results support adaptive scale refinement as a promising direction for molecular representation learning, especially when fixed-scale modeling is insufficient.

</details>


### [243] [BUDDY: BUdget-Driven DYnamic Depth Routing for Adaptive Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09514)
*Yuhua Zhou,Shaoqi Yu,Shichao Weng,Changhai Zhou,Mingze Yin,Fei Yang,Aimin Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Buddy是一个预算驱动的动态深度路由框架，通过轻量级决策模块根据输入选择top-k层执行，复用首层KV缓存实现解码时自适应路由，并支持输入依赖的预算预测，在Llama和Qwen模型上实现了严格的预算控制与精度-计算权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度剪枝方法要么无法在用户指定的计算预算下灵活控制，要么固定路由路径无法随解码过程上下文增长而自适应调整，导致推理效率低。

Method: 提出Buddy框架：(1) 轻量级决策模块根据输入对中间层评分，确定性地执行top-k层以满足预算；(2) 复用首层KV缓存作为低开销的全局上下文源，与最新token表征池化后用于路由决策，实现解码时自适应；(3) 可选预算预测器估计输入相关的计算量以平衡质量与效率。

Result: 在Llama系列和Qwen模型上的实验表明，Buddy与强静态剪枝基线竞争力相当，通常能改善精度-计算权衡，并且独特地支持严格预算控制、解码时重路由以及单一训练模型内的多预算配置。

Conclusion: Buddy作为一种动态深度路由方法，在保持模型质量的同时实现了灵活的计算预算控制和解码时自适应，优于静态剪枝方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) incur high inference cost due to their depth and parameter scale. Depth pruning can reduce latency by skipping redundant Transformer blocks, but existing methods (i) provide limited control under user-specific compute budgets and (ii) typically fix the routing path, failing to adapt as the context grows during decoding. We propose Buddy, a budget-driven dynamic depth routing framework. Buddy uses a lightweight Decision Module to score intermediate layers conditioned on the input and deterministically executes the top-k layers to satisfy a given budget. To support decode-time adaptation, Buddy reuses the first-layer KV cache as a low-overhead global context source and pools it together with the newest token representation before each routing decision. When no explicit budget is provided, an optional Budget Predictor estimates an input-dependent compute level to balance quality and efficiency. Experiments on Llama-family and Qwen models show that Buddy is competitive with strong static pruning baselines and often improves the accuracy-compute trade-off, while uniquely supporting strict budget control, decode-time rerouting, and multiple budgets within a single trained model.

</details>


### [244] [Investigating Calibration Challenges in Probabilistic Electricity Price Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09517)
*Jan Niklas Lettner,Hadeer El Ashhab,Benjamin Schäfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当前概率电价预测中，评分规则优先考虑锐度而忽视校准性，导致过自信和统计不可靠的预测，呼吁未来研究转向校准感知的目标和架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生能源整合增加了市场波动性，概率电价预测对风险管理至关重要，但现有评分规则存在校准性不足的问题。

Method: 通过理论分析指出现有评分规则（如CRPS）优先优化锐度（sharpness）而忽视校准性（calibration），导致模型沦为确定性预测的代理。

Result: 发现当前评分规则会导致过自信且统计不可靠的不确定性估计，模型在忽视校准性时丧失概率预测的分布完整性。

Conclusion: 未来研究应转向校准感知的目标函数和网络架构，以确保能源市场预测的分布完整性。

Abstract: As renewable energy integration increases market volatility, probabilistic electricity price forecasting has become essential for effective risk management. However, current-proper-scoring rules often prioritize forecast sharpness at the expense of calibration, leading to overconfident and statistically unreliable uncertainty estimates. This work highlights the critical gap between theoretical scoring and practical calibration, demonstrating that models can become mere proxies for deterministic forecasts when reliability is neglected. We conclude that future research must shift toward calibration-aware objectives and architectures to ensure the distributional integrity of energy market forecasts.

</details>


### [245] [Efficient Traffic Prediction at Scale: A Systematic Study of STGCN Architectural Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09539)
*Soban Nasir Lone,Mohamed Abouelela,Taeyoung Yu,Jiwon Kim,Constantinos Antoniou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地研究了STGCN（时空图卷积网络）是否存在过参数化问题。通过在四个交通数据集上的实验发现，单块（1-block）STGCN在短期预测（10分钟）中在三个数据集上达到最优性能，长时预测误差仅边际增加（≤1.8%），而标准双块结构带来61%的CPU推理延迟增加和37%的吞吐量下降，三块结构更是成本翻倍而收益不足0.5%。这表明默认的双块STGCN在许多应用场景下是过参数化的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STGNNs计算开销大，虽有高效替代方案被提出，但一个根本性问题尚未被探索：这些架构本身是否过参数化？本研究旨在系统性地检验这一假设，为实际部署和效率优化提供指导。

Method: 以STGCN为研究对象，在四个多样化交通数据集上系统性地对比1-block、2-block（标准）和3-block三种变体的预测性能与计算效率，评估不同块数对短期和长期预测精度、CPU推理延迟和吞吐量的影响。

Result: 单块架构在三个数据集的短期预测（10分钟）中达到最优；长时预测误差仅增加≤1.8%。双块结构相较单块增加61% CPU推理延迟、降低37%吞吐量。三块结构计算成本翻倍但性能提升不足0.5%。

Conclusion: 标准双块STGCN在多数应用场景下存在过参数化问题，单块架构在精度与效率间取得了更好的平衡。这一发现对实际部署和效率导向的基准研究均有重要启示。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal graph neural networks (STGNNs) have become the dominant approach for traffic prediction, yet their computational requirements pose challenges for practical deployment in intelligent transportation systems (ITS). While recent work has proposed efficient alternatives to STGNNs, a fundamental question remains unexplored: are these architectures themselves over-parameterised? We examine this question using the Spatio-Temporal Graph Convolutional Network (STGCN), one of the most widely adopted models in this domain. Through systematic experiments across four diverse traffic datasets, we compare 1-block, 2-block (standard), and 3-block STGCN variants. Our findings reveal that the single-block architecture achieves optimal performance for short-term prediction (10 mins) on three of four datasets, while incurring only marginal degradation ($\leq$1.8% relative error) at longer horizons. Crucially, the 2-block variant incurs 61% higher CPU inference latency and 37% lower throughput relative to 1-block -- substantial overhead for resource-constrained ITS deployment. The 3-block architecture offers no favourable tradeoff, more than doubling computational cost for $<$0.5% relative improvement. These results suggest that the default 2-block STGCN may be over-parameterised for many applications, with implications for both practitioners deploying traffic prediction systems and researchers benchmarking efficiency-focused methods.

</details>


### [246] [Safe-RULE: Safe Reinforcement UnLEarning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09559)
*Shixiong Jiang,Taozheng Zhu,Fanxin Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出安全强化遗忘（Safe-RULE）框架，作为离线安全强化学习中对数据投毒攻击的防御方法，无需重新训练即可消除中毒数据影响，实验证明能有效提升安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线安全强化学习依赖静态数据集，易遭受数据投毒攻击，攻击者注入恶意样本会破坏安全性并导致不安全策略行为。现有方法缺乏有效的防御机制。

Method: 提出Safe-RULE（安全强化遗忘）学习范式，将强化遗忘扩展到离线安全强化学习，在遗忘过程中同时考虑任务性能和安全约束，无需从头重新训练或访问原始训练环境。

Result: 在基准安全强化学习任务上的实验表明，该方法有效提升了针对数据投毒攻击的安全性表现。

Conclusion: Safe-RULE为离线安全强化学习提供了一种可行的防御框架，能够消除中毒数据影响，平衡任务性能与安全约束。

Abstract: Offline safe reinforcement learning (Safe RL) enables policy learning without online interactions, making it suitable for safety-critical systems such as robotics systems. However, its reliance on static datasets exposes offline Safe RL to data poisoning attacks, where adversaries inject malicious samples that compromise safety and induce unsafe policy behavior. In this work, we propose a new learning paradigm, named safe reinforcement unlearning (Safe-RULE), used as a defense framework to remove the influence of poisoned data without retraining from scratch or requiring access to the original training environment. We further extend reinforcement unlearning to offline Safe RL by explicitly accounting for both task performance and safety constraints during the unlearning process. Experiments across benchmark Safe RL tasks demonstrate that our approach effectively enhances safety performance against data poisoning attacks.

</details>


### [247] [On Choosing the $μ$ Parameter in Gaussian Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09582)
*Bogdan Kulynych,Antti Honkela*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究从纯-DP ε到GDP μ的映射，通过三种攻击指标匹配强对手的成员推断攻击最坏情况，推荐μ≈ε/5作为保守转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作主张使用高斯差分隐私（GDP）来报告隐私保护机器学习中的隐私保证，但缺乏从纯差分隐私（ε）到GDP（μ）的原则性映射方法。

Method: 通过匹配强对手成员推断攻击在最坏情况下的三种指标（固定FPR下的乘法优势、固定召回率下的精度、标准隐私分布）来建立从纯-DP ε到GDP μ的映射。

Result: 给出了不同参数范围内μ的取值表，并推荐μ≈ε/5作为保守的通用转换公式。

Conclusion: 提供了从纯-DP到GDP的原则性映射方法，为隐私保护机器学习中的隐私度量报告提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Recent work argues for using Gaussian differential privacy (GDP) to report the privacy guarantees in privacy-preserving machine learning. We provide principled mappings from pure-DP $\varepsilon$ to GDP $μ$ by matching the worst-case success of a strong-adversary membership inference attack in terms of three metrics: multiplicative advantage at fixed FPR, precision at fixed recall, and the standard privacy profile. We tabulate $μ$ values across a useful range of parameters and recommend $μ\approx \varepsilon/5$ as a conservative general-purpose conversion.

</details>


### [248] [Assessing Sample Quality in Conditional Generation under Compositional Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09601)
*Berker Demirel,Valentino Maiorca,Marco Fumero,Theofanis Karaletsos,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种后验、逐样本的可信度评分方法，用于评估条件生成模型在外推性生成场景下的样本质量，该评分结合了全局真实性和属性忠实度两个可估计量，能够在训练分布覆盖条件下实现有效的过滤、排序和生成中止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件生成模型在科学领域常被用于探索罕见或未观测到的属性组合，但标准评估指标需要参考目标分布，而在外推性生成场景中该分布天然不可用，形成评估循环问题。

Method: 提出一种后验逐样本可信度评分，包含两个分量：(1) 全局真实性——衡量样本与真实数据流形的兼容性；(2) 属性忠实度——衡量样本更接近请求属性还是其他合理替代属性。在训练分布覆盖条件下，该评分可恢复外推生成样本间的有意义的比较。

Result: 在生物成像中，筛选出的样本更好地保留了真实形态结构并提升了下游预测性能；在受控视觉基准上也观察到类似增益。评分还可应用于生成过程中，在完全解码前实现中止。

Conclusion: 该方法为外推性条件生成场景提供了一种无需参考分布的评估手段，支持现成预训练模型的后验评估，并能有效提升生成质量和下游任务性能。

Abstract: Conditional generators provide a natural tool for controllable generation, including settings where the desired condition is a new composition of observed attributes or experimental factors. In many applications, especially in scientific domains, such models are attractive to explore conditions for which real samples are rare, expensive, or not yet observed. However, this creates a circularity for evaluation: standard conditional quality metrics require a reference target distribution, but in the extrapolative regime that distribution is unavailable by definition. We address this problem with a post-hoc, per-sample trust score for assessing conditional samples using only the training distribution. The score combines two estimable quantities: global realism, measuring compatibility with the real data manifold, and attribute-wise faithfulness, measuring whether a sample is closer to the requested attributes than to plausible alternatives. We show that the score can recover meaningful comparisons across extrapolated generations, under a mild coverage condition on the observed attributes. These comparisons enable effective filtering, ranking, and abstention of generations and can be used directly on off-the-shelf pretrained models. In biological imaging, selected samples preserve real morphological structure better and improve downstream predictive performance, while similar gains are observed on controlled vision benchmarks. Finally, we show how the score can be applied during generation, enabling abstention before full decoding. Code is available at https://github.com/berkerdemirel/faithful-cond-gen.

</details>


### [249] [Closure-Validated Circuit Discovery in Attention Heads: Co-activation Proposes, Ablation Disposes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09607)
*Yongzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究是否可以通过注意力头的共激活聚类获得有意义的电路结构。在稠密模型中，聚类社区通过了闭合测试（消融后损伤与随机对照组相当），但在混合专家模型中，路由条件聚类虽然统计学显著却未通过闭合测试——消融反而降低了损失。研究表明，廉价信号只是电路提议，而非确认的电路，闭合测试是区分二者的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性研究越来越将组件群体而非单个单元作为基本分析对象，通常通过聚类共激活统计来发现这些群体。本文质疑这种廉价信号是否真的能够识别出注意力头电路。

Method: 采用稀疏自编码器聚类方法适配到注意力头，通过因果消融而非重构来验证。对聚类发现的社区执行闭合测试：消融发现的社区，将每个样本的损伤与随机对照匹配组进行比较。在两种稠密模型（Pythia 1B, OLMo 1B）和混合专家模型（OLMoE-1B-7B）上实验，并在训练过程中扩展闭合测试。

Result: 稠密模型中聚类社区通过了闭合测试；混合专家模型中路由条件聚类恢复了统计学显著信号，但未通过闭合测试——消融反而改善了损失，方向错误。训练过程中，注意力目标选择性和参与比率与功能在双向解耦。

Conclusion: 廉价信号只是电路提议（circuit proposal），而非确认的电路（confirmed circuit）；闭合测试是区分二者的关键。

Abstract: Interpretability increasingly treats groups of components, not individual units, as the basic object, and proposes to find them by clustering co-activation statistics. We ask whether such a cheap signal actually identifies an attention-head circuit. Adapting a sparse-autoencoder clustering recipe to attention heads -- but validating by causal ablation rather than reconstruction -- we cluster heads and then run a closure test: ablate the discovered community and compare per-example damage to matched-random controls. Across two dense 1B-scale models (Pythia 1B, OLMo 1B) and two input distributions, the communities pass closure. In a Mixture-of-Experts model (OLMoE-1B-7B), route-conditional clustering recovers a statistically real signal that nonetheless does not survive closure -- ablation improves loss, the wrong direction. Extending closure across training, attention-target selectivity and participation ratio decouple from function in both directions. We conclude that a cheap signal is a circuit proposal, not a confirmed circuit; closure is what separates them.

</details>


### [250] [Constrained user-item allocation for e-commerce marketing campaigns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09623)
*Maja Lindström,Natalija Glisovic,Jan von Pichowski,Tommy Löfstedt,Martin Rosvall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自动定向(auto-targeting)方法，联合选择用户和物品构建多个不相交的营销活动，通过约束谱双聚类、贪心局部搜索和多臂老虎机三种策略解决该组合优化问题。实验表明双聚类在活动质量、提升度和公平性上表现最佳，而基于老虎机的方法在超大规模数据上更具可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有营销活动方法要么假设预定义活动结构，要么将物品选择与用户分配解耦，无法从联合交互模式中发现活动分组。因此需要一种能同时选择用户和物品、构建多个不相交活动的新形式化方法。

Method: 形式化自动定向问题，提出三种互补策略：(i) 约束谱双聚类，发现用户-物品亲和矩阵中的稠密区域；(ii) 带成对交换的贪心局部搜索，进行组合优化；(iii) 多臂老虎机框架，通过探索逃逸局部最优。在合成数据、Amazon Reviews基准和大规模商业数据上与模拟退火基线对比。

Result: 双聚类在活动质量、提升度和公平性上始终取得最高分数，在小规模数据集上运行高效；老虎机方法在超大规模数据上提供了可扩展的替代方案。

Conclusion: 自动定向方法能有效从联合交互模式中构建营销活动，双聚类适用于中小规模场景，老虎机方法适用于大规模场景，为零售商提供了根据数据规模选择合适策略的指导。

Abstract: When running marketing campaigns, retailers must decide which products to promote and which users to target. These decisions are inherently coupled: effective campaigns match users and items with strong mutual affinity into non-overlapping groups of predefined sizes. However, existing approaches assume predefined campaign structure or decouple item selection from user assignment, and cannot discover campaign groupings directly from joint interaction patterns. We therefore formalize this campaign problem as auto-targeting: jointly selecting users and items to construct multiple disjoint campaigns. To solve this combinatorial problem, we propose three complementary strategies: (i) constrained spectral biclustering to find dense regions in the user-item affinity matrix, (ii) greedy local search with pairwise swaps for combinatorial refinement, and (iii) a multi-armed bandit framework to escape local optima through exploration. We evaluate these methods on a synthetic dataset, the Amazon Reviews benchmarks, and large-scale proprietary commercial data, and compare the results to simulated annealing as a baseline. The results show that biclustering consistently achieves the highest campaign quality, lift, and fairness scores. While biclustering runs efficiently on smaller datasets, its runtime increases substantially on very large ones, where bandit-based methods instead offer a scalable alternative.

</details>


### [251] [A Unifying Framework for Concept-Based Representational Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09653)
*Grégoire Dhimoïla,Victor Boutin,Agustin Martin Picard,Thomas Fel,Thomas Serre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了概念对齐的统一框架，将其分解为表示vs概念、实例级vs分布级两个维度，并引入干预基准InterVenchA和耦合稀疏自编码器CoSAE，证明概念对齐本质上是多目标优化问题，仅0.1%配对数据即可恢复实例级对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念对齐方法在相同术语下优化不同目标，导致实际对齐的内容不明确，缺乏统一的定义和评估框架。

Method: 提出双轴分解框架（表示vs概念、实例级vs分布级），定义四种对齐属性；构建干预基准InterVenchA分别测量提取质量、翻译质量和概念一致性；提出Coupled Sparse Autoencoder (CoSAE)联合优化互补对齐目标。

Result: 常用假设（对齐目标等价）在实践中不成立；纯无监督目标无法恢复有意义的实例级对齐；CoSAE能实现强对齐，仅需0.1%配对数据即可通过锚定分布目标恢复实例级对齐。

Conclusion: 概念对齐本质上是多目标优化问题，必须同时定义、测量和优化多个对齐目标。
