Abstract: Learned representations across models and modalities often exhibit striking structural similarities, suggesting shared underlying concept decompositions. However, concept alignment remains poorly defined: existing approaches optimize different objectives under the same terminology, obscuring what is actually aligned.
  We propose a unifying framework that decomposes alignment along two axes: what is aligned (representations vs. concepts) and at what level (instance-wise vs. distributional). This induces four corresponding properties -- instance-wise and distributional variants of translation and concept consistency -- and reveals precisely which of these guarantees existing methods provide. We further introduce \InterVenchA, an intervention-based benchmark that separately measures extraction quality, translation quality, and concept consistency. Through theory and experiments, we show that commonly assumed equivalences between alignment objectives fail in practice: optimizing one property does not reliably recover the others, and purely unsupervised objectives fail to recover meaningful instance-level alignment. We then propose the Coupled Sparse Autoencoder (CoSAE), which jointly enforces complementary alignment objectives. Strong alignment emerges only in this regime. Surprisingly, as little as 0.1\% paired data is sufficient to recover instance-level alignment when anchoring distributional objectives.
  Overall, our results show that concept alignment is fundamentally multi-objective: it must be defined, measured, and optimized as such.

</details>


### [252] [Muon Learns More Robust and Transferable Features than Adam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09658)
*Tianyu Ruan,Fengzhuo Zhang,Shuche Wang,Shihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon优化器在预训练大规模语言模型和视觉分类器时的特征学习优势，通过实验证明Muon学习到的特征在鲁棒性和迁移性上均优于Adam和SGD，并通过理论分析支持了其实验发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管Muon已被证明在预训练效率上优于Adam和SGD，但其在特征学习方面的优势尚不明确，本文旨在从鲁棒性和迁移性角度揭示Muon的特征学习优势。

Method: 通过在损坏图像和文本上评估预训练模型的鲁棒性，使用逐层探针分析logit边际，在线性分类器训练和全模型微调中评估迁移性，测量隐藏状态的有效秩，以及通过多分量特征分类问题进行理论证明。

Result: Muon学习到的特征在多种架构（包括Transformer和CNN）上均比Adam和SGD更鲁棒，鲁棒性优势体现在更大的逐层logit边际上；Muon特征的迁移性更优，这由其更高的隐藏状态有效秩支持；理论证明Muon能获得更大的边际和更高的有效秩。

Conclusion: Muon不仅在预训练效率上优于Adam和SGD，在特征学习的鲁棒性和迁移性方面也具有显著优势，理论和实验共同支持了这一结论。

Abstract: Muon has recently emerged as a state-of-the-art optimizer for pretraining Large Language Models (LLMs) and vision classifiers. Despite its efficiency advantage over Adam and SGD, the feature-learning advantage of Muon remains unclear. This paper investigates Muon's feature-learning advantage through the lens of robustness and transferability. First, by evaluating pretrained models on corrupted images and texts, we show that features learned by Muon are consistently more robust than those learned by Adam and SGD across different architectures, including transformers and Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs). Using trained layer-wise probes, we further show that this robustness advantage is reflected in larger logit margins across layers. Second, by training linear classifiers or fine-tuning full models from pretrained parameters on downstream tasks, we demonstrate that Muon-learned features transfer more effectively than those learned by Adam and SGD. This transferability advantage is further supported by the diversity of hidden states across layers, as measured by effective rank. Finally, in a representative classification problem with multi-component features, we prove that Muon attains larger margins and higher effective rank than Adam and SGD, providing theoretical support for our empirical findings.

</details>


### [253] [In-Context Learning for Latent Space Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09664)
*Tuan A. Vu,Harri Lähdesmäki,Julien Martinelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对潜空间贝叶斯优化（LSBO）中表格基础模型作为代理模型时存在的预训练分布不匹配问题，提出通过在分子VAE潜空间上生成的合成优化任务对表格基础模型进行继续预训练，并加入正则化项保持原模型先验，从而显著提升了分子优化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LSBO中使用的表格基础模型（如TabPFN、TabICL）是在标准回归任务上预训练的，但LSBO中从潜编码到目标值的映射与这些回归任务分布存在显著不匹配，导致代理模型性能下降。

Method: 在表格基础模型代理的预训练阶段基础上，补充在分子VAE潜空间上定义的合成优化任务进行继续预训练，并在训练目标中加入正则化项以锚定原始检查点，保持广泛回归先验的同时避免过度特化。

Result: 在留出的分子优化基准测试上，所提出模型取得了强性能，验证了针对LSBO特定适配对上下文代理模型的有效性。

Conclusion: LSBO特定适配对于上下文代理模型是重要的，通过潜空间合成任务继续预训练能有效解决分布不匹配问题，提升分子优化性能。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) is a central tool for sample-efficient design, and latent-space Bayesian optimization (LSBO) extends it to structured objects such as molecules and proteins. In parallel, tabular foundation models such as TabPFN and TabICL now achieve state-of-the-art regression performance and are increasingly used as BO surrogates. Because their Bayesian behavior is induced by large synthetic pretraining collections, the composition of this pretraining distribution is crucial. LSBO creates a distinctive mismatch: the induced map from latent code to objective value differs markedly from the regression tasks used to train current in-context models. We address this mismatch by complementing the pretraining stage of tabular foundation model surrogates with synthetic optimization tasks defined on the latent space of a molecular VAE. The continued-pretraining objective features a regularizer that anchors the model to the original checkpoint, preserving its broad regression prior while avoiding overspecialization to the adaptation tasks. On held-out molecular optimization benchmarks, the resulting model achieves strong performance, supporting the relevance of LSBO-specific adaptation for in-context surrogates.

</details>


### [254] [Algorithm for Contextual Queueing Bandits with Rate-Optimal Queue Length Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09668)
*Seoungbin Bae,Dabeen Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CQB-η-2算法，通过仅在截止轮前引入随机探索的三阶段策略，将上下文队列博弈的队列长度遗憾从Õ(T^{-1/4})改进到Õ(T^{-1/2})，并证明了匹配的minimax下界Ω(T^{-1/2})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文队列博弈算法在随机上下文下只能达到Õ(T^{-1/4})的队列长度遗憾率，本文旨在提升该速率至最优的Õ(T^{-1/2})。

Method: 提出三阶段算法CQB-η-2：第一阶段纯随机探索构建初始估计器；第二阶段结合η-随机探索与UCB规则，在学习的同时维持负漂移；第三阶段在探索截止后纯用UCB。遗憾分解以截止轮为界，截止前利用负漂移压制次优选择导致的队列长度差异，截止后利用前两阶段积累的随机探索样本保证UCB决策的离去率差较小。

Result: 队列长度遗憾达到Õ(T^{-1/2})，并证明minimax下界为Ω(T^{-1/2})，二者匹配至对数因子。

Conclusion: 上下界共同刻画了队列长度遗憾随水平线T的minimax依赖关系（仅差对数因子），证明了所提算法的最优性。

Abstract: Contextual queueing bandits provide a framework for learning to schedule heterogeneous jobs under unknown context-dependent service rates. Under stochastic contexts, existing algorithms achieve $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/4})$ queue length regret, defined as the expected difference between the learner's and oracle's queue lengths at horizon $T$. In this paper, we improve this rate to $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2})$. The key observation is that random exploration is needed only up to a carefully chosen cutoff round, rather than throughout the entire horizon. We propose CQB-$η$-2, a three-phase algorithm: (i) pure random exploration to construct an initial estimator, (ii) $η$-random exploration combined with a UCB rule to continue learning while maintaining negative drift, and (iii) pure UCB after the exploration cutoff. Our proof decomposes the queue length regret at the cutoff round. Before the cutoff, negative drift suppresses queue length differences caused by suboptimal choices. After the cutoff, the first two phases provide sufficient random exploration samples, ensuring that UCB decisions incur small departure-rate gaps. Combining these two bounds yields queue length regret of order $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2})$. We further prove a minimax lower bound of order $Ω(T^{-1/2})$. The proof constructs two hard instances that are statistically indistinguishable up to the final service decision, and uses a queue-specific coupling argument to convert the resulting testing error into queue length regret. Together, our upper and lower bounds characterize the minimax dependence on the horizon $T$ up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [255] [Transition-Based Digital Twin Modelling for Alzheimer's Disease under Sparse Longitudinal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09671)
*Yinyu Huang,Yilin Zhang,Sofia Michopoulou,Christopher Kipps,Rahman Attar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于个性化数字孪生框架的阿尔茨海默病进展预测方法，整合多模态纵向数据，通过过渡建模和序列建模两种互补策略实现认知状态预测、诊断分类、不确定性量化及患者特异性"what-if"轨迹分析。在ADNI数据上评估表明，局部过渡建模比序列建模在稀疏不规则数据上更具数据效率和预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AD预测方法多聚焦于静态分类或群体水平风险估计，缺乏针对个体的建模能力和不确定性感知推理，难以应对AD进展的高度异质性和纵向数据稀疏不规则的特点。

Method: 构建个性化数字孪生框架，集成过渡建模（相邻访视间状态转移）和序列建模（完整时序依赖）两种互补策略，利用ADNI多模态数据（认知评估、临床变量、MRI表型），实现认知状态评分预测、诊断分类、不确定性量化和患者特异性情景分析。

Result: 在无泄露的受试者层级分割评估中，评分预测和诊断分类均表现良好；过渡建模在稀疏不规则ADNI设置下取得了比序列建模更高的预测准确度，表明局部过渡建模更具数据效率。

Conclusion: 时序建模策略需与临床数据结构相匹配，过渡式数字孪生方案在神经退行性疾病的个性化预测中是一种实用且可解释的方法；序列模型在不确定性感知轨迹预测中仍有价值，但过渡建模在数据效率和稳健性上更具优势。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease (AD) progression is highly heterogeneous and is typically observed through sparse and irregular longitudinal data, posing challenges for prediction and personalised monitoring. Existing machine learning approaches have improved AD prediction using multimodal data, yet often focus on static classification or cohort-level risk estimation, providing limited support for subject-specific modelling and uncertainty-aware reasoning. To address these limitations, we present a personalised digital twin framework for AD prediction and scenario-based analysis using multimodal longitudinal data. The proposed approach integrates complementary modelling strategies to capture clinical transitions and temporal dependencies across visits. Using data from the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI), including cognitive assessments, clinical variables, and MRI-derived phenotypes, the framework predicts cognitive status and diagnostic categories while quantifying predictive uncertainty and enabling patient-specific what-if trajectory analysis. Evaluation on leak-free subject-level splits demonstrates strong performance in score forecasting and diagnosis classification. In this sparse and irregular ADNI setting, transition-based modelling of adjacent visits achieved higher predictive accuracy than the sequence-based branch, suggesting that local transition modelling may be more data-efficient. While sequence models remain valuable for uncertainty-aware trajectory forecasting, local transition modelling offers a more data-efficient and robust predictive strategy. These findings highlight the importance of aligning temporal modelling strategies with clinical data structure and suggest that transition-based digital twin formulations may provide a practical and interpretable approach for personalised disease forecasting in neurodegenerative disorders.

</details>


### [256] [AutoMegaKernel: A Statically-Checked Agent Harness for Self-Retargeting Megakernel Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09682)
*Jaber Jaber,Osama Jaber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoMegaKernel (AMK) 是一个将 HuggingFace Llama 系列模型编译为单个持久协作 CUDA 内核的系统，只需一次launch即可完成整个前向传播，无需手动编写每模型的 CUDA 代码。它通过静态图检查（非机械化证明）的冻结调度-IR 验证器，在 7,160 个对抗性调度中零假接受、全接受 360 个真实 lowerings。系统自动为 10/10 个支持模型生成正确 megakernel，在 SmolLM2-135M 上逐 token 复现 HuggingFace 贪婪解码（困惑度匹配 2.5e-7）。搜索发现的 int8 (W8A16) megakernel 在批量-1 解码中超越 CUDA-graphed cuBLAS bf16：L4 最高 1.33x，L40S 1.25-1.27x，A10G 最高 1.08x，RTX 5090 1.19-1.23x；但在高带宽训练类 A100/H100 上落后 cuBLAS。代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大模型推理通常需要多个 CUDA kernel 启动，带来调用开销和效率损失。手动编写融合 kernel (megakernel) 虽能提升性能，但需要大量人力针对每个模型进行手写 CUDA 代码优化，缺乏通用性和自动化。作者希望构建一个自动化系统，能够从 HuggingFace 模型出发，自动编译为单个持久协作 CUDA 内核，同时保证正确性和性能。

Method: AMK 的核心方法包括：(1) 将模型编译为单个持久协作 CUDA kernel，一次 launch 完成整个前向传播；(2) 使用冻结调度-IR 验证器，通过静态图检查（非机械化证明）在 launch 前认证无死锁、无竞争——测试 7,160 个对抗性调度（其中 6,091 个不安全）实现零假接受，同时接受全部 360 个真实 lowerings；(3) 同一代码源可重定向到 sm_80/sm_90/sm_120 架构；(4) 自动化搜索发现最优 int8 (W8A16) 量化策略。

Result: (1) 自动生成正确的 megakernel 覆盖 10/10 个支持模型；(2) 在 SmolLM2-135M 上逐 token 复现 HuggingFace 贪婪解码，困惑度匹配误差 2.5e-7；(3) 无监督自改进循环在基线上实现 1.25-1.72x 加速；(4) int8 (W8A16) megakernel 在批量-1 解码中超越 CUDA-graphed cuBLAS bf16：L4 最高 1.33x，L40S 1.25-1.27x，A10G 最高 1.08x，RTX 5090 1.19-1.23x；(5) 性能排序不是带宽的简单函数（864 GB/s L40S 胜过 600 GB/s A10G），分水岭在于推理类 vs 训练类 GPU；(6) 在高带宽训练类 A100/H100 上落后 cuBLAS，作者识别出跨 SM 同步瓶颈。

Conclusion: AMK 证明了自动化生成正确且高性能的大模型融合 kernel 是可行的，无需针对每个模型手动编写 CUDA 代码。在推理类 GPU 上 int8 megakernel 能超越 cuBLAS bf16，但在高带宽训练类 GPU 上受限于跨 SM 同步瓶颈。作者坦诚报告了这一局限性，并开源了代码与测试框架以促进后续研究。

Abstract: AutoMegaKernel (AMK) compiles a HuggingFace Llama-family model into a single persistent cooperative CUDA kernel that runs the whole forward pass in one launch, with no per-model hand-written CUDA. The contribution is the system, not raw speed.
  A frozen schedule-IR validator statically certifies deadlock-freedom and race-freedom via static graph checks (not a mechanized proof), so an unsafe agent-proposed schedule is rejected before launch: across 7,160 adversarial schedules (6,091 unsafe) it had zero false-accepts and accepted all 360 real lowerings. The same source retargets sm_80/sm_90/sm_120 from one codebase, auto-generates correct megakernels for 10 of 10 supported models, and on a real SmolLM2-135M checkpoint reproduces HuggingFace greedy decode token-for-token (perplexity match 2.5e-7). An unattended, agent-drivable autoresearch loop self-improves the megakernel over its own baseline (1.25-1.72x).
  A search-found int8 (W8A16) megakernel beats CUDA-graphed cuBLAS bf16 at batch-1 decode across NVIDIA's datacenter inference fleet: L4 up to 1.33x, the current-gen L40S 1.25-1.27x, A10G up to 1.08x at scale, and the consumer RTX 5090 1.19-1.23x. The ordering is not a clean function of bandwidth (the 864 GB/s L40S beats the 600 GB/s A10G); the divide is inference-class vs training-class. AMK trails cuBLAS on the high-bandwidth training-class A100/H100, where the harness localizes the cross-SM-sync bottleneck; we report the gap plainly. This is a precision-asymmetric (W8A16 vs bf16) comparison at decode position 0; the largest real checkpoint is TinyLlama-1.1B. Code and the harness: https://github.com/RightNow-AI/AutoMegaKernel

</details>


### [257] [When Do Local Score Models Extrapolate Across Size? A Diagnostic Theory and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09705)
*Wenjie Xi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究生成模型中的尺寸迁移问题，发现仅靠架构局部性无法保证稳定外推，而高斯平滑分数的准局部性才是关键。通过Tweedie公式揭示远距离扰动通过后验协方差影响局部分数成分，提出FDLF基准验证了空间混合、平滑分数准局部性与模型感受野之间的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决生成建模中模型在小系统上训练后能否稳定迁移到大系统的问题，揭示仅靠平移不变架构和局部性不足以保证尺寸外推的稳定性，需要从理论层面理解控制稳定外推的核心机制。

Method: 通过Tweedie公式理论分析平滑分数受远距离扰动的机制，提出准局部性概念；证明局部边缘在反向扩散下的尺寸一致比较定理；设计FDLF（有限深度局部流）白盒诊断基准，提供精确分数、密度和可控响应范围。

Result: 在空间混合条件下，平滑分数相对于感受野保持准局部性，可实现稳定外推；当空间混合减弱时，分数局部性迅速退化，尺寸迁移失败。理论分析与FDLF基准上的实验验证一致。

Conclusion: 生成模型的尺寸稳定外推取决于高斯平滑分数的准局部性而非单纯架构局部性，空间混合强度是决定迁移成功与否的关键因素。FDLF可作为诊断和验证该机制的有效工具。

Abstract: Scientific generative modeling often requires size transfer, where models trained on small systems are evaluated on larger ones. While translation-invariant architectures enable this evaluation, we show that architectural locality alone does not guarantee stable size extrapolation. Instead, stable extrapolation is governed by the quasi-locality of the Gaussian-smoothed score. Through Tweedie's formula, far-away perturbations can influence local score components via posterior covariance, meaning a local model succeeds only if its receptive field covers the smoothed score's response range. We formalize this mechanism, proving a size-uniform comparison theorem for local marginals under reverse diffusion. We also introduce Finite-Depth Local Flow (FDLF), a white-box diagnostic benchmark with exact scores, densities, and controllable response ranges. Empirically, we validate the interplay between spatial mixing, smoothed-score quasi-locality, and model receptive fields. Under spatial mixing, the smoothed score remains quasi-local relative to the receptive field, enabling stable extrapolation. Conversely, when spatial mixing weakens, the score's locality rapidly degrades, causing size transfer to fail.

</details>


### [258] [BrainSurgery: Reproducible and Reliable Declarative Weight Manipulations for Model Editing and Upcycling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09707)
*Gianluca Barmina,Annemette Broch Pirchert,Andrea Blasi Núñez,Lukas Galke Poech,Peter Schneider-Kamp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BrainSurgery 是一个通过声明式 YAML 方案对神经网络检查点进行鲁棒且可复现的"张量手术"的工具，支持结构修改、数学变换和张量重塑，并内置断言验证以防止静默错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度学习模型规模扩大，管理和修改大型检查点（如层重构、精度转换、低秩分解、架构调试等）日益困难，而现有工作流依赖脆弱的临时 Python 脚本，缺乏鲁棒性和可复现性。

Method: 通过抽象存储格式和内存管理，使用声明式 YAML 方案执行复杂变换；支持基于正则表达式和结构定位的结构修改、数学变换和张量重塑；内置断言验证张量形状、数据类型和数值以防范静默错误。

Result: 提供了四个示例和三个案例研究（从模型升级到 LoRA 提取），展示了 BrainSurgery 在多种检查点操作场景中的有效性和可复现性。

Conclusion: BrainSurgery 通过可复现且经过验证的操作，为未来深度学习研究中的检查点管理提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: As deep learning models scale, managing, inspecting, and modifying large checkpoints has become increasingly challenging. Researchers often need to alter model weights for layer restructuring, precision casting, low-rank factorization, and architectural debugging, yet these workflows often rely on fragile ad-hoc Python scripts. Here, we introduce BrainSurgery, a tool for robust and reproducible "tensor surgery" on neural network checkpoints, and provide a system demonstration covering four examples and three case studies from model upcycling to LoRA extraction. By abstracting storage formats and memory management, BrainSurgery executes complex transformations through declarative YAML plans. It supports structural modifications, mathematical transformations, and tensor reshaping through expressive regex and structural targeting, while built-in assertions validate tensor shapes, data types, and values to prevent silent errors. We envision that BrainSurgery will provide a strong foundation for future research through its reproducible and validated operations.

</details>


### [259] [Evaluating the Representation Space of Diffusion Models via Self-Supervised Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09718)
*Xiao Li,Yixuan Jia,Zekai Zhang,Xiang Li,Lianghe Shi,Jinxin Zhou,Zhihui Zhu,Liyue Shen,Qing Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Fisher信息的不变污染比（ICR）指标，统一评估扩散模型的表征与生成能力，发现中间噪声水平下特征不变性最优、分类性能最佳，且ICR可作为训练中记忆化开始的敏感指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在生成和自监督表征学习上均表现优异，但两者之间的内在联系尚未被充分探索，缺乏统一的评估框架。

Method: 将特征分解为不变分量和残差分量，基于Fisher信息推导不变污染比（ICR），从表征和生成两个维度分析扩散模型行为。

Result: 表征方面，中间噪声水平下特征不变性最强且下游分类性能最优；生成方面，ICR可作为训练早期记忆化开始的敏感指示器，无需外部评估器或测试集即可检测。

Conclusion: 扩散模型可通过其学习表征的几何结构从自监督视角进行监控，ICR为统一理解扩散模型的表征与生成能力提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Diffusion models have demonstrated remarkable generative capabilities and have also emerged as powerful self-supervised representation learners, yet the connection between these two abilities remains less explored. Drawing inspiration from self-supervised learning (SSL), we introduce a framework for jointly evaluating the representation and generation capabilities of diffusion models. Specifically, we decompose features into invariant and residual components and derive the Invariant Contamination Ratio (ICR), a Fisher-based metric that quantifies how residual variation contaminates invariant signal in feature space. We use this framework to analyze both discriminative and generative behavior of diffusion models. On the representation side, we find that invariance peaks at intermediate noise levels, which also yield the best downstream classification performance. On the generative side, we study how training transitions from genuine generalization to memorization in data-limited regimes, and show that ICR serves as a sensitive training-time indicator of early learning: increasing residual energy along Fisher directions marks the onset of memorization, detectable from training features alone without external evaluators or held-out test sets. Overall, our results show that diffusion models can be monitored from a self-supervised perspective through the geometry of their learned representations.

</details>


### [260] [Disentanglement with Holographic Reduced Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09725)
*Jhonny J. Velasquez Olivera,Christo K. Thomas,Walid Saad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于全息简化表示（HRR）的无监督解耦学习方法，利用HRR解绑定操作作为归纳偏置，将解耦表示视为符号结构而非连续表示，并通过信息论分析证明了每个槽位的容量界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解耦方法多依赖连续表示（如VAE、GAN），难以捕捉概念间的组合关系；而学习离散符号结构又面临可微性问题。因此需要一种既能保持可微性又能利用符号结构优势的解耦方法。

Method: 使用全息简化表示（HRR）作为潜在表示，通过HRR的解绑定操作提供归纳偏置以分离因子；将解耦表示视为向量求和形式的符号结构，而非标量维度的低维潜在向量。

Result: 在潜在遍历和解耦指标上达到与基线相当的竞争力；HRR表示对噪声具有更强的鲁棒性，在多种SNR下保持重建质量。

Conclusion: 通过信息论分析证明解绑定操作能诱导出近似独立的符号-值对，并导出每个槽位的容量界限，量化了可靠编码的独特符号概念数量，从理论上解释了HRR对解耦的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Disentanglement, the separation of factors of variation in data using neural networks, remains a long-standing challenge in machine learning. Prior work has addressed this problem with variational autoencoders and generative adversarial networks that incorporate ideas from variational inference and information-theoretic constraints. In contrast to methods that rely on continuous representations, we propose a design that treats disentangled representations as symbolic structures, motivated by the compositional relationships among the concepts that make up samples from a distribution. However, learning discrete symbolic structures with neural networks while maintaining differentiability is difficult and often requires complex architectures. To address this, we introduce an unsupervised learning algorithm that uses holographic reduced representations (HRR) for neural disentanglement. We show that the HRR unbinding operation provides an inductive bias for separating factors and yields competitive results against baselines, as measured by latent traversals and disentanglement metrics. We complement these empirical findings with an information-theoretic analysis of the HRR unbinding channel. We prove that unbinding induces approximately independent symbol-value pairs and derive a per-slot capacity bound that quantifies how many distinct symbolic concepts can be reliably encoded, giving a quantitative account of the inductive bias toward disentanglement. The resulting representations differ from standard autoencoder-based models, in that their latent units are vectors that are summed together, rather than scalar dimensions of a low-dimensional latent vector. We show that this HRR representation is more robust to noise than other disentangled representations and maintains reconstruction quality across a range of SNRs.

</details>


### [261] [Tight Sample Complexity of Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09731)
*Chenxiao Yang,Nathan Srebro,Zhiyuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文严格刻画了深度为L、参数总量为W的Transformer在输入序列长度T下的VC维，上界为O(L W log(T W))，下界为Ω(L W log(T W / L))；同时刻画了思维链学习的样本复杂度，Teacher forcing方法的样本复杂度为O(L W log((T+T')W))，下界为Ω(L W log((T+T')W / L))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了从理论层面理解Transformer的表达能力与泛化能力，特别是其VC维和思维链学习的样本复杂度，填补该领域理论分析的空白。

Method: 通过VC维的上下界分析，分别证明了Transformer的上界O(L W log(T W))和近匹配的下界Ω(L W log(T W / L))；对于思维链学习，分析了Teacher forcing策略的样本复杂度，并给出匹配的下界。

Result: 建立了Transformer VC维的严格上下界，以及思维链学习的样本复杂度上下界，结果近乎匹配。

Conclusion: Transformer的VC维和思维链学习的样本复杂度与参数数量L、W以及序列长度T、T'的对数相关，为理解其泛化性能提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We tightly characterize the VC dimension of depth-$L$ Transformers with a total of $W$ parameters, mapping an input sequence of length $T$ to a single output, establishing an upper bound of $O(L W \log (T W))$ and a nearly matching lower bound of $Ω(L W \log (T W / L))$. We further tightly characterize the sample complexity of chain-of-thought learning using such a Transformer, showing teacher forcing (i.e. selecting a predictor consistent with the entire chain-of-thought on training data) learns with sample complexity $O\left(L W \log \left(\left(T+T^{\prime}\right) W\right)\right)$ and that any learning rule that uses chain-of-thought data requires at least $Ω\left(L W \log \left(\left(T+T^{\prime}\right) W / L\right)\right)$ examples, where $T$ is the input length and $T^{\prime}$ is the number of autoregressive steps.

</details>


### [262] [Learning Dynamics Reveal a Hierarchy of Weight-Induced Layerwise Gram Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09744)
*Claudio Nordio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究前馈ReLU网络在固定读出和二次损失下的梯度下降动力学，将其重写为训练集空间上场的集体动力学。对于单隐层，可消除权重变量得到残差的封闭方程；对于深层网络，残差动力学保持层状核结构，但从第三层开始需要权重诱导的Gram算子层级来实现跨层信息传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将梯度下降动力学从权重空间重新表述为训练集空间上场的集体动力学，以揭示神经网络的内部工作机制和信号传播规律。

Method: 采用前馈ReLU网络，固定读出层并使用二次损失函数。通过数学推导，将权重变量从激活动力学中消除，得到关于残差的封闭方程。对于单隐层，推导出因子化为输入几何矩阵和动态共激活矩阵的集体核；对于深层网络，分析层状核结构及Gram算子层级。

Result: 单隐层网络可实现权重变量的完全消除，得到残差的封闭动力学方程；深层网络（从第三层开始）需要引入权重诱导的Gram算子层级来闭合动力学方程。

Conclusion: 梯度下降动力学可以在训练集空间上表示为场的集体动力学，其核结构随网络深度增加而变得复杂，需要层级化的算子来维持闭合形式。

Abstract: We study feed-forward ReLU networks with fixed readout and quadratic loss. The aim is to rewrite gradient descent not primarily as a dynamics in weight space, but as a collective dynamics closed in terms of fields defined on the training-set space. For a single hidden layer, the weight variables can be eliminated from the activation dynamics, yielding a closed equation for the residuals governed by a collective kernel that factorizes into an input-geometric matrix and a dynamical co-activation matrix. For deeper networks, the residual dynamics retains a clean layer-wise kernel structure. However, from depth three onward, closure requires a hierarchy of weight-induced Gram operators that mediate information transport across layers.

</details>


### [263] [Perturbative Contrastive Physical Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09756)
*Kyungeun Kim,Amanuel Anteneh,Israel Klich,Olivier Pfister,J. M. Schwarz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出扰动对比物理学习框架(PCPL)，通过比较物理系统在不同条件下的响应差异来实现学习，统一并扩展了平衡传播和频率传播等方法，并在弹簧网络和光子电路平台上验证了分类和模拟乘法任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理系统对扰动的响应是理解系统的关键，通过对比不同条件下系统响应的差异提供了一种学习机制，无需外部处理器或显式反向传播即可实现学习行为。

Method: PCPL框架通过可控改变输入、边界条件、参数或解释函数产生物理状态对比，从中提取更新信号。更新可反映局部敏感性或全局逆问题结构，但无需集中梯度计算，学习几何由系统自身物理响应隐式涌现。

Result: 在弹簧网络（通过测量位移和力更新键刚度）和连续变量光子电路（通过x正交测量和雅可比矩阵有限差分估计训练）两个平台上成功学习了分类任务；光子电路还实现了模拟乘法。

Conclusion: PCPL提供了一种通用物理学习框架，统一了现有方法，展示了不依赖显式反向传播的自主物理学习系统的可行性。

Abstract: Responses to perturbations are key to understanding physical systems. The ability to contrast such responses by comparing how a system reacts under slightly different conditions provides a mechanism for learning. Here, we introduce Perturbative Contrastive Physical Learning (PCPL), a general framework in which learning emerges from measurable contrasts between physical states produced by controlled changes to inputs, boundary conditions, parameters, or interpreter functions. PCPL unifies and extends prior approaches: Equilibrium Propagation is rooted in contrasts between free and nudged equilibria in energy-based systems, while Frequency Propagation corresponds to contrasts extracted from sinusoidally driven, frequency-demodulated responses. We show that contrast-driven updates can reflect either local sensitivities or global inverse-problem structure, yet do not require centralized gradient computation. Instead, effective learning geometry emerges implicitly from the system's own physical response, allowing learning behavior to arise without an external processor or explicit backpropagation. We demonstrate PCPL in two platforms: (i) spring networks that update bond stiffness using measured displacements and forces, and (ii) continuous-variable photonic circuits trained via x quadrature measurements and finite-difference estimates of the Jacobian. Both platforms successfully learn classification tasks. We further show that a continuous-variable photonic circuit can be trained to implement analog multiplication, illustrating a step toward more autonomous physical learning systems.

</details>


### [264] [Preserving Plasticity in Continual Learning via Dynamical Isometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09762)
*Andries Rosseau,Robert Müller,Ann Nowé*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究深度神经网络在持续学习中的可塑性丧失问题，通过神经切线核与动态等距理论，提出保持网络可塑性的新方法（AdamO优化器及等距正则化），并在监督学习和强化学习基准中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在非平稳环境下的持续训练会导致可塑性逐渐丧失，限制进一步学习能力。现有方法对可塑性丧失的机制理解不足，缺乏系统性的理论分析和有效的保持策略。

Method: 首先将可塑性与经验神经切线核（NTK）关联，识别出动态等距（层间Jacobian奇异值接近1）是关键机制。提出（1）等距促进正则化方案，可重新激活休眠ReLU单元；（2）AdamO优化器，解耦等距正则化与梯度更新（类似AdamW）；（3）对现有可塑性保持方法进行动态等距视角的重新解释。

Result: 在监督学习和强化学习的持续学习基准中，所提方法一致达到或超越现有方法的性能，有效保持可塑性。

Conclusion: 动态等距是保持持续学习中网络可塑性的关键机制，通过等距正则化和AdamO优化器可以有效实现。同时证明近动态等距与表达性非线性表示是兼容的。

Abstract: Continual training of deep neural networks under non-stationarity often leads to a progressive loss of plasticity, eventually limiting further learning. We relate plasticity to the empirical Neural Tangent Kernel, and identify dynamical isometry (the condition that layer-wise Jacobian singular values remain close to one) as a key mechanism for preserving plasticity in continual learning. We revisit a class of networks that are almost-everywhere isometric while remaining universal Lipschitz function approximators, demonstrating that near-dynamical isometry is compatible with expressive nonlinear representations. For general architectures, we propose an efficient isometry-promoting regularization scheme and identify a novel mechanism by which it can reactivate dormant ReLU units. Building on this, we introduce AdamO, an Adam-style adaptive optimizer that decouples isometry regularization from gradient updates, analogous to AdamW. We further reinterpret prior plasticity-preserving approaches through the lens of dynamical isometry, showing that they target only a partial measure of isometry. Across supervised and reinforcement-learning continual-learning benchmarks designed to induce plasticity loss, our methods consistently match or outperform existing approaches.

</details>


### [265] [iOSWorld: A Benchmark for Personally Intelligent Phone Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09764)
*Lawrence Keunho Jang,Mareks Woodside,Geronimo Carom,Andrew Keunwoo Jang,Jing Yu Koh,Ruslan Salakhutdinov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: iOSWorld是一个基于原生iOS模拟器的交互式基准测试，包含26个内置应用和133个任务，用于评估手机智能体在个性化、多应用协同和记忆推理方面的能力。最佳模型在视觉+XML设置下达到52%的整体准确率，但在多应用任务上仅为37%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动智能体基准测试缺乏个性化能力，无法评估智能体利用用户身份、历史记录和偏好进行推理的能力。作者希望构建一个具有持久用户身份和跨应用关联数据的基准测试，推动手机的个性化智能体发展。

Method: 构建了26个全新iOS应用及其中相互关联的数据（交易、消息、旅行记录、社交关系、财务活动等），设计了133个任务，分为单应用任务（27个）、多应用任务（60个，跨2-8个应用）和记忆与个性化任务（46个，需从个人数据中推断模式）。在纯视觉和视觉+XML两种设置下评估了前沿和开源计算机使用模型。

Result: 最佳配置整体准确率52%，多应用任务仅37%。视觉+XML特权访问使前沿模型提升高达26个百分点，但小模型未从附加的辅助功能树输入中获益。

Conclusion: 多应用协同和个性化推理仍是当前手机智能体的主要瓶颈，开源了iOSWorld作为包含完整应用、数据、任务、评分标准和评估代码的基准测试。

Abstract: A useful phone agent needs to be personally intelligent. It should reason over a user's identity, history, and preferences as they exist on the device, not just follow isolated instructions in an impersonal sandbox. Existing mobile agent benchmarks lack this kind of personalization. We introduce iOSWorld, the first interactive native iOS simulator benchmark built around a persistent user identity spanning 26 newly built iOS apps. These apps contain connected data such as transactions, messages, travel records, social relationships, and financial activity. iOSWorld includes 133 tasks across three increasingly difficult categories. Single-app tasks (27) test one app, multi-app tasks (60) span 2 to 8 apps, and memory and personalization tasks (46) require agents to infer patterns from personal data. We evaluate frontier and open-source computer-use models in both vision-only and privileged vision+XML settings. The best configuration reaches 52\% overall but only 37\% on multi-app tasks. Privileged vision+XML access improves frontier models by up to 26 percentage points, while smaller models do not benefit from added accessibility-tree input. We release iOSWorld as an open-source benchmark with all apps, seeded data, tasks, rubrics, and evaluation code.

</details>


### [266] [Zero Touch Predictive Orchestration: Automating Time-Series Models for the Cloud-Edge Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09787)
*Abd Elghani Meliani,Arora Sagar,Adlen Ksentini,Raymond Knopp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向云边连续体（CEC）中冷启动问题的自动时间序列预测架构，通过轻量级资源暴露器（RE）动态发现节点并采集遥测数据，再将稀疏的本地样本与公开高分辨率数据集TimeTrack融合，经神经架构搜索（NAS）引擎自动生成高精度基线模型，实验表明该方法能有效缓解冷启动问题，提升预测精度并加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云边连续体（CEC）中边缘节点高度波动，需零接触主动管理，但新发现节点缺乏历史数据（冷启动问题），而通用模型无法捕捉节点特有的硬件与微服务行为，导致预测精度差。

Method: 提出全自动预测架构：(1) 轻量级、技术无关的资源暴露器（RE）动态发现节点并采集可定制遥测数据；(2) 将稀疏的本地样本与公开高分辨率数据集TimeTrack（45秒间隔）自动融合，利用TimeTrack的基础高频时间模式与本地数据的精确校准；(3) 经神经架构搜索（NAS）引擎自动生成基线预测模型。

Result: 融合目标数据与TimeTrack在MSE、MAE、MAPE指标上显著提升预测精度，并加速收敛，优于仅用稀疏本地样本、仅用通用数据集或用其他标准数据集混合的方案。

Conclusion: 所提数据混合方法能有效缓解云边连续体中的冷启动问题，为持续MLOps部署提供稳健基础。

Abstract: The Cloud-Edge Continuum (CEC) enables latency-critical applications by distributing resources to the far edge, but its extreme volatility makes proactive Zero Touch Management via time-series forecasting essential. However, orchestrators face a severe "cold start" problem: newly discovered nodes lack the historical data required to train localized predictive models, while generalized models fail to capture unique hardware and microservice behaviors. To solve this, we propose a fully automated time-series prediction architecture driven by a novel data-mixing methodology. At the infrastructure level, we introduce a lightweight, technology-agnostic Resource Exposer (RE) that dynamically discovers nodes and continuously collects customizable telemetry (e.g., compute, network, energy). To overcome the sparsity of these initial local samples, our framework automatically merges them with TimeTrack, our publicly available, high-resolution dataset collected at 45-second intervals. This synergizes TimeTrack's foundational, high-frequency temporal patterns with the precise calibration of the local node data. Processed through a Neural Architecture Search (NAS) engine, the system automatically generates highly accurate baseline models. Experimental results demonstrate that merging the target data with TimeTrack effectively mitigates the cold start challenge. This integration significantly improves forecasting accuracy measured in Mean Squared Error (MSE), Mean Absolute Error (MAE), and Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE) and accelerates convergence compared to training on the sparse local samples alone, training solely on generic datasets, or mixing the target data with standard alternative datasets, establishing a robust foundation for continuous MLOps deployment.

</details>


### [267] [Bandits for Efficient Experimentation: Adapting to Control Group, Preferences, and Context Drifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09802)
*Udvas Das,Waris Radji,Debabrota Basu,Odalric-Ambrym Maillard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究动态上下文分布下的保守线性上下文随机多臂老虎机问题，提出Dri-MED算法，在非平稳异方差噪声下实现了约束感知的实例依赖后悔界$\tilde{\mathcal O}\left(\frac{\kappa}{\tilde{\Delta}}d^2\log T\right)$和$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d)$的期望约束违反次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性上下文老虎机工作大多假设上下文分布静态或忽略用户群体偏好差异，且缺乏对基线策略约束的保障。实际场景中（如推荐系统）上下文分布随时间漂移，且学习者需要确保每次决策不低于基线策略的期望奖励。

Method: 将问题归约为均值平稳但噪声为异方差非平稳的线性老虎机；基于线性MED策略设计Dri-MED算法，通过异方差回归精心处理方差感知乘性项$\kappa$，并融入约束感知的次优性间隙$\tilde{\Delta}$。

Result: 实例依赖后悔界为$\tilde{\mathcal O}\left(\frac{\kappa}{\tilde{\Delta}}d^2\log T\right)$，期望约束违反次数为$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d)$。数值实验表明Dri-MED显著优于忽略漂移和偏好结构的保守基线方法。

Conclusion: Dri-MED算法在动态上下文分布和约束保障条件下实现了高效的 regret-约束权衡，验证了异方差非平稳噪声建模的必要性和算法的实用性。

Abstract: We consider a variant of the linear contextual stochastic multi-armed bandits, where the learner must provide recommendations to a group of users, each having its personalized preference vector, and in the presence of context distributions that are drifting over time. Under practitioner-friendly assumptions, we reduce this setting to linear bandit with stationary mean but heteroskedastic and non-stationary noise. We further study the case when the learner must ensure the mean reward of each decision must exceed that of a baseline strategy $\boldsymbolπ_0$ at each decision step. We introduce Dri-MED, an algorithm inspired from the linear version of the MED strategy, and carefully adapted to handle the non-stationary heteroskedastic noise. We show that the instance-dependent regret scales as $\tilde{\mathcal O}\left(\fracκ{\tildeΔ}d^2(\log(T)\right)$, where $\tildeΔ$ is the constraint-aware sub-optimality gap subject to policy $π_0$, with variance-aware multiplicative term $κ$ that we carefully handle using heteroskedastic regression. We further show Dri-MED enjoys $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(d)$ expected constraint violations. Our numerical results suggest that Dri-MED significantly outperforms conservative baselines that ignores the drift and preference structure.

</details>


### [268] [Topological Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09806)
*Lennart Bastian,Samuel Leventhal,Mustafa Hajij,Tolga Birdal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出拓扑神经算子（TNOs），一种在胞腔复形上进行算子学习的框架，通过离散外微积分实现跨维度特征耦合，并进一步提出层级TNOs（HTNOs）以传播长程和拓扑依赖信息。该框架统一了现有神经算子，在多个PDE基准上提升了准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子（NOs）仅在点和/或边上定义函数，缺乏对拓扑结构和高维单元（如面、体）上物理量的显式建模能力，无法自然地处理梯度、旋度、散度等微分算子所对应的跨维度耦合关系，且难以保证物理量的几何支撑与守恒兼容性。

Method: TNOs将数据表示为定义在不同维度胞腔上的特征，利用离散外微积分（DEC）通过梯度型、旋度型和散度型算子实现显式的跨维度耦合。其核心设计原则是解耦信息流路径（由固定拓扑算子决定）与信息变换方式（由学习模块决定）。HTNOs进一步引入学习得到的粗化胞腔复形，以实现长程和拓扑依赖信息的传播。

Result: 在多个PDE基准测试（包括不规则几何流动问题）上，TNOs和HTNOs相比现有方法提升了准确率；控制实验进一步分离并验证了原生高阶秩和拓扑结构的收益。

Conclusion: TNOs为算子学习提供了统一的视角，能够天然地融合拓扑与几何结构，提升物理模拟的精度和可解释性，并可作为现有神经算子的泛化形式。

Abstract: We introduce Topological Neural Operators (TNOs), a principled framework for operator learning on cell complexes that lifts neural operators (NOs) from functions on points and/or edges to topological domains. TNOs represent data as features defined on cells of varying dimension and model their interactions through Discrete Exterior Calculus, enabling explicit cross-dimensional coupling via gradient-, curl-, and divergence-type operators. The key design principle is to decouple where information flows, as governed by fixed topological operators, from how it is transformed (which is learned), yielding models that respect the geometric support of physical quantities and expose conservation and compatibility structure. We further propose Hierarchical TNOs (HTNOs), which incorporate learned coarse complexes to propagate long-range and topology-dependent information. Our framework subsumes existing NOs as a special case, providing a unified perspective on operator learning across discretizations. Across a range of PDE benchmarks, including irregular-geometry flow problems, TNOs and HTNOs improve accuracy; controlled studies further isolate the benefits of native higher-rank and topological structure. Project page: https://circle-group.github.io/research/TNO

</details>


### [269] [Rethinking the Divergence Regularization in LLM RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09821)
*Jiarui Yao,Xiangxin Zhou,Penghui Qi,Wee Sun Lee,Liefeng Bo,Tianyu Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRPO提出用平滑的、优势加权二次正则化项替代DPPO中的硬掩码机制，在保持相同信任区域几何结构的同时，对超出边界的策略偏移提供连续梯度权重和修正信号，提升了LLM RL训练的稳定性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PPO/GRPO使用比率裁剪来近似信任区域控制，但重要性比率在长尾词汇上难以准确反映分布偏移；DPPO改用基于散度的硬掩码，但一旦token跨越信任区域边界，梯度被直接丢弃而非修正，导致信息损失。

Method: 提出Divergence Regularized Policy Optimization (DRPO)，用平滑的优势加权二次正则化项替代硬掩码，对策略偏移施加连续有界的梯度权重，在边界外仍提供修正信号。

Result: 在不同模型规模、架构和精度设置下的实验表明，DRPO相比基线方法提升了LLM RL训练的稳定性和效率。

Conclusion: DRPO通过连续正则化替代离散掩码，在保持信任区域约束的同时避免了梯度丢弃，实现了更稳定高效的策略优化。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a key component of post-training large language models (LLMs). In practice, LLM RL is often off-policy because of training-inference mismatch and policy staleness, making trust-region control essential for stable optimization. Mainstream methods such as PPO and GRPO approximate this control with a ratio-clipping mechanism, but the importance ratio can be a poor proxy for distributional shift in long-tailed vocabularies. Recent work such as DPPO addresses this mismatch by replacing ratio-based clipping with a divergence-based mask, yielding a trust region defined by the sampled token's absolute probability shift. However, DPPO still relies on a hard mask: once a token crosses the trust-region boundary in a harmful direction, its gradient is discarded rather than corrected. To address this, we propose Divergence Regularized Policy Optimization (DRPO), which replaces the hard mask with a smooth advantage-weighted quadratic regularizer on policy shift. DRPO preserves the same trust-region geometry as DPPO while inducing bounded, continuous gradient weights that attenuate diverging updates and provide corrective signals beyond the boundary. Experiments across model scales, architectures, and precision settings show that DRPO improves the stability and efficiency of LLM RL training.

</details>


### [270] [An Agency-Transferring Model-Free Policy Enhancement Technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09825)
*Anton Bolychev,Georgiy Malaniya,Sinan Ibrahim,Pavel Osinenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将已有的次优基线策略嵌入强化学习训练过程的方法，通过仲裁机制在基线策略与学习策略之间动态切换，初始依赖基线策略、逐步转移控制权，最终得到无需基线支持的独立学习策略，在保证高目标到达率的同时提升训练效率并超越基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从零训练RL策略成本高昂，需要精心设计奖励与环境、大量调参和计算资源；而许多控制问题已有功能正常但次优的基线策略可用。本文旨在利用现有基线策略提升RL训练效率并产生优于基线的最终策略。

Method: 提出一个仲裁机制，在每个步骤决定由基线策略还是可训练的学习策略控制智能体。训练初期强依赖基线策略，随后逐步将控制权转移给学习策略，最终学习策略成为独立神经网络。该方法形式化定义了基线策略的"功能性"（能高概率到达并停留在目标集），并利用该性质在训练全程保持高目标到达率。

Result: 在连续控制基准测试上，该方法取得了与竞争方法相当或更优的回报，同时在训练全程（包括最终无基线支持的阶段）保持了最高的目标到达率。

Conclusion: 理论分析在给定假设下提供了行为的形式化解释，推导了独立学习策略目标到达概率的下界。实验验证了该方法在训练效率和性能上的优势，证明了利用基线策略引导训练的有效性。

Abstract: Training reinforcement learning (RL) policies from scratch is
  costly: it requires careful reward and environment design,
  extensive tuning, and substantial computation.
  Yet many control problems already have a functional but
  suboptimal policy available as a baseline.
  This paper proposes a method for embedding such a baseline into
  the RL training process, simultaneously improving training
  efficiency relative to from-scratch methods and producing a
  learning policy that outperforms the baseline.
  At each step, the method arbitrates between the baseline policy
  and a trainable learning policy, initially relying strongly on
  the baseline policy and then progressively transferring agency to
  the learning policy.
  By the end of training, the learning policy is a standalone
  neural network that operates without baseline policy support.
  The paper formalizes what it means for the baseline policy to be
  functional: under this policy, the agent reaches a goal set and
  remains there with high probability.
  The proposed arbitration mechanism is designed to exploit this
  property during training, yielding high goal-reaching rates right
  from the beginning of training.
  A theoretical analysis provides a formal interpretation of this
  behavior under stated assumptions and extends it to the final
  baseline-free regime, where explicit lower bounds are derived for
  the goal-reaching probability of the standalone learning policy.
  Empirical results on continuous-control benchmarks show that the
  proposed method achieves returns that match or exceed those of
  competitive approaches, while maintaining the highest
  goal-reaching rates throughout training among the compared
  methods -- including in the final stage, where the learning policy
  operates without any baseline support.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [271] [Hardware-aware Low-latency Quantum Compilation with Data-driven Lightweight Error Detection for Early Fault-Tolerant Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07666)
*Sumit Chongder*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种硬件感知编译与数据驱动量子纠错（QED）的联合优化框架，通过噪声加权代价函数和学习型多目标调度器协同优化量子比特映射、SWAP插入和综合征调度。在VQE、相位估计和Grover基准测试中（6-20量子比特，深度10-160），联合协同设计相比SABRE在8量子比特VQE实例中可将算法成功概率提升高达68%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编译和纠错工具链将两者孤立处理，缺乏在延迟约束下平衡纠错开销与成功概率的原则性方法。

Method: 提出硬件感知编译与数据驱动QED联合框架，通过噪声加权代价函数和学习型多目标调度器，协同优化量子比特映射、SWAP插入和综合征调度。

Result: 在HPC集群上使用GPU加速密度矩阵仿真，在VQE、相位估计和Grover基准测试中，联合协同设计将算法成功概率提升高达68%（95% CI: 60%-76%），优于SABRE基线。

Conclusion: 早期容错NISQ处理器中，通过将编译与纠错联合协同设计，可以在有限资源下显著提升算法成功率，为NISQ时代的量子计算提供了一种有效的折中方案。

Abstract: Noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) processors are entering an early fault-tolerance regime where full quantum error correction carries prohibitive resource costs, yet lightweight error detection can meaningfully improve algorithmic success rates. Existing compilation and error-detection toolchains treat these concerns in isolation, with no principled way to balance detection overhead against success probability under latency constraints. We present an integrated hardware-aware compilation and data-driven quantum error-detection (QED) framework that jointly optimises qubit mapping, SWAP insertion, and syndrome-schedule placement via a noise-weighted cost function and a learned multi-objective scheduler. Simulation experiments on an HPC cluster using GPU-accelerated density-matrix simulation (NVIDIA cuQuantum SDK) across VQE, phase-estimation, and Grover benchmarks, three noise profiles, and circuit sizes of 6-20 qubits (depths 10-160), show that joint co-design raises algorithmic success probability by up to 68 percent (95 percent CI: 60 percent to 76 percent) over SABRE on an 8-qubit VQE instance with post-selection.

</details>


### [272] [Visual-to-Code Authoring, Tensor-Network Debugging, and Quantum-Circuit Inspection Tools in Python](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08760)
*Alejandro Mata Ali*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出三个互补的可视化包——Tensor-Network-Visualization、Tensor-Network-Editor和Quantum Circuit Drawer，用于张量网络和量子电路的视觉调试、结构化检查、可视化编程、后端代码生成及电路渲染，旨在弥补现有Python生态中结构错误难以发现的问题，而非提供仿真功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量网络和量子电路通常更易于通过可视化而非代码进行推理，但在Python中它们常通过后端特定对象或紧凑符号表达式构建、变换和检查，导致结构错误在开发、调试和交流中难以被察觉。

Method: 三个互补包：Tensor-Network-Visualization用于视觉调试和结构检查；Tensor-Network-Editor实现可视化到代码的创作、后端代码生成、JSON保存、导出及设计级分析；Quantum Circuit Drawer用于清晰的电路渲染、检查和电路或分布结果的比较。它们作为现有张量网络库、科学Python工作流和量子SDK之上的可视化创作与检查层。

Result: 使结构构件可视化、可编辑、可检查、可比较、可导出且可重现，但不实现新的收缩算法、不执行量子电路、不保证跨任意后端的完全语义等价。

Conclusion: 三个包构成了围绕现有生态系统的可视化工件层，解决了结构错误的可见性问题，提升了开发与交流效率。

Abstract: Tensor networks and quantum circuits are structural objects whose meaning depends on connectivity, indices, contraction order, gate placement, measurements, and related design choices. They are often easier to reason about visually than as code, yet in Python they are frequently constructed, transformed, and checked through backend-specific objects or compact symbolic expressions. This can make structural mistakes hard to notice during development, debugging, and communication. This paper presents three complementary packages: Tensor-Network-Visualization for visual debugging and structural inspection of supported tensor-network and traced einsum workflows; Tensor-Network-Editor for visual-to-code authoring, backend code generation, JSON preservation, export, and design-level analysis; and Quantum Circuit Drawer for clear circuit rendering, inspection, and complementary comparison of circuits or documented result distributions. The packages form a visual authoring and inspection layer around existing tensor-network libraries, array-based scientific Python workflows, and quantum SDKs. They are not simulators: they do not implement new contraction algorithms, execute quantum circuits, or guarantee full semantic equivalence across arbitrary backends. Their contribution is to make structural artifacts visible, editable, inspectable, comparable, exportable, and reproducible within those ecosystems.

</details>


### [273] [Correlation-Assisted Odd-Parity Encoded Gates in Coupled Fluxonium Qubits under Non-Markovian TLS Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07699)
*Chenghong Ji,Chaoying Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在耦合 fluxonium 量子比特的奇宇称双量子比特子空间中，利用交换耦合和失谐驱动逻辑门，并分析相关纵向 TLS 噪声对逻辑门保真度的影响，发现正空间相关性可抑制差分噪声从而提升保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索将相关纵向噪声部分转换为共模涨落的机制，并在奇宇称双量子比特子空间中实现噪声自适应的逻辑编码操作，以提高量子门保真度。

Method: 将交换耦合的两量子比特哈密顿量投影到奇宇称子空间得到有效逻辑哈密顿量（交换驱动 XL 旋转，失谐驱动 ZL 旋转）；用有限记忆时间的纵向随机过程建模相关 TLS 噪声；通过平均门保真度评估编码门性能。

Result: 正的噪声空间相关性可抑制差分涨落，从而提高编码逻辑门的保真度；比较了 OU、马尔可夫和随机电报三种噪声模型，并分析了逻辑动力学解耦的作用。

Conclusion: 识别出耦合 fluxonium 器件中奇宇称编码操作的噪声自适应控制机制，并启示未来需开展包含泄漏和脉冲约束的多能级仿真。

Abstract: Correlated longitudinal noise can be partially converted into common-mode fluctuations in an oddparity two-qubit subspace. We analyze an encoded logical qubit formed by the states in two coupled fluxonium qubits. Projecting the exchange-coupled two-qubit Hamiltonian onto this subspace yields an effective logical Hamiltonian in which the exchange interaction drives XL rotations and the qubit detuning drives ZL rotations. We model correlated two-levelsystem (TLS) noise by using longitudinal stochastic processes with finite memory time and evaluate encoded-gate performance through the average gate fidelity. Within the projected model, positive spatial noise correlation suppresses the differential fluctuation and thereby improves the fidelity of encoded logical gates. We further compare Gaussian Ornstein-Uhlenbeck, Markovian, and randomtelegraph noise models and examine the role of logical dynamical decoupling. These results identify a noise-adapted control mechanism for odd-parity encoded operations in coupled fluxonium devices and motivate future multilevel simulations including leakage and pulse-level constraints.

</details>


### [274] [Benchmarking Quantum Algorithmic Resilience for CVaR Portfolio Optimization: The Expressibility-Coherence Trade-off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07727)
*Prashik N. Somkuwar,K. Srinivasan,G. Raghavan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了HE-VQNN和WS-QAOA两种量子算法在NIFTY 50指数16个资产组合优化上的表现，发现WS-QAOA因非局部门开销导致硬件退相干，而HE-VQNN虽保持硬件相干性但无法捕捉尾部风险相关性，揭示了NISQ设备上金融优化的根本性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子组合优化在复杂金融建模中具有理论优势，但NISQ设备的硬件拓扑严重限制了物理实现，因此需要系统评估不同量子算法在真实硬件上的可行性。

Method: 提出一种经典-量子混合代理矩阵绕过CVaR辅助量子比特瓶颈，将NIFTY 50指数的16个资产映射到IBM重六边形处理器上，系统量化了算法对路由过程中"SWAP税"的弹性，对比了HE-VQNN和WS-QAOA两种算法。

Result: WS-QAOA提供精确理论映射但因指数级非局部门开销遭受灾难性硬件退相干；HE-VQNN保持硬件相干性但缺乏捕捉密集尾部风险资产相关性的数学表达能力。

Conclusion: 当前NISQ架构上密集金融优化面临算法不可表达性与硬件退相干之间的非可行选择，揭示了缺乏全连接性的NISQ计算机的根本局限性。

Abstract: Quantum combinatorial optimization offers theoretical advantages for complex financial modeling, but physical implementation on Noisy Intermediate Scale Quantum (NISQ) devices is severely constrained by hardware topology. This study presents a hardware benchmarking analysis between a Hardware Efficient Variational Quantum Neural Network (HE-VQNN) and the Warm Start Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (WS-QAOA) for a hybrid Mean Variance and Conditional Value at Risk (CVaR) portfolio objective. By implementing a novel classical quantum hybrid proxy matrix to bypass the CVaR auxiliary qubit bottleneck, we map up to 16 assets from the NIFTY 50 index onto an IBM heavy hex processor. We systematically quantify algorithmic resilience to the "SWAP tax" incurred during routing. Empirical results reveal a critical operational trade-off: WS-QAOA provides exact theoretical mapping but suffers catastrophic hardware decoherence due to exponential nonlocal gate overhead. Conversely, HE-VQNN preserves hardware coherence but lacks the mathematical expressibility to capture dense tail risk asset correlations. This study exposes the limitations of dense financial optimization on current architectures forces an nonviable choice between algorithmic inexpressibility and hardware decoherence. This is indicative of a deeper limitation as to what can and cannot be done with NISQ computers lacking in all-to-all connectivity.

</details>


### [275] [Exploring the landscape of compact magic-state distillation factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07734)
*Hugo Jacinto,Xavier Valcarce,Victor Barizien,Élie Gouzien,Nicolas Sangouard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用经典纠错码框架来推导魔法态蒸馏协议，并通过SAT求解器得出了一系列关于量子比特数、协议深度、工厂距离和输出错误率等关键指标的不可行性定理，同时给出了新的最小量子比特数的蒸馏协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在容错量子计算中，以最少的物理量子比特和操作来生产高保真度魔法态是实现大规模量子计算的关键挑战。尽管出现了如培育法等替代方案，魔法态蒸馏在实现极低错误率方面仍然不可或缺。

Method: 利用经典纠错码框架来推导蒸馏协议，通过SAT求解器系统性地研究固定量子比特数下的蒸馏协议，推导关键指标之间的不可行性定理。

Result: 证明了任何少于8个量子比特的T-to-T态蒸馏协议最多只能检测3个错误，任何少于8个量子比特的T-to-CCZ态蒸馏协议最多只能检测2个错误。给出了距离4和5的T-to-T态蒸馏协议（分别使用10和11个量子比特），以及距离3和4的T-to-CCZ态蒸馏协议（分别使用9和10个量子比特）。

Conclusion: 经典纠错码为魔法态蒸馏协议提供了更简单的推导框架，特别适合固定量子比特数下的系统研究。新提出的蒸馏协议达到了文献中给定距离下的最小量子比特数。

Abstract: Producing high-fidelity magic states using the smallest possible amount of physical qubits and operations stands as a very important challenge to achieve fault-tolerant quantum computation at scale. Besides emerging proposals for alternative methods such as cultivation, magic state distillation remains essential for achieving very low error rates. Known distillation protocols are usually built through quantum codes derived from triorthogonal matrices. Here, exploiting the specific noise structure present in magic state distillation protocols, we show that classical error-correcting codes offer a simpler framework for deriving these protocols. This formulation is particularly well suited to systematic numerical and analytical studies of distillation protocols involving a fixed number of qubits. Specifically, we use a SAT solver to derive a series of no-go theorems that relate key figures of merit, including the number of qubits, the protocol depth, the factory distance, and the prefactor in the output error rate. For instance, we prove that any $T$-to-$T$ state distillation protocol using fewer than eight qubits can detect at most three errors, while any $T$-to-$\mathrm{CC}Z$ state distillation protocol using fewer than eight qubits can detect at most two errors. Our results also include new distillation protocols with the smallest number of qubits for a given distance in the literature, namely distance 4 and 5 $T$-to-$T$ state protocols supported on 10 and 11 qubits, as well as distance 3 and 4 $T$-to-$\mathrm{CC}Z$ state distillation protocols supported on 9 and 10 qubits.

</details>


### [276] [Exact metastability in a class of driven-dissipative quantum many-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07736)
*David D. Noachtar,Aashish A. Clerk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类具有隐藏时间反演对称性（量子细致平衡形式）的驱动-耗散多体开放量子系统，提出在耗散一级相变附近，可以利用非平衡稳态的特殊纯化方法解析预测慢时间尺度，并通过耗散横向场伊辛模型和非线性腔模型验证了该猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体量子系统的亚稳态及其指数级长时间尺度是近期研究热点，但现有半经典或路径积分瞬子方法在某些情况下难以处理，需要发展新的解析预测方法。

Method: 利用具有隐藏时间反演对称性的Lindblad主方程描述一类驱动-耗散开放量子系统，提出通过非平衡稳态的特殊纯化来解析预测耗散一级相变附近的慢时间尺度，并结合数值研究进行验证。

Result: 在耗散横向场伊辛模型（集体和局域衰减）以及驱动-耗散非线性腔模型中，该猜想被证实准确，能够定量预测亚稳态和慢动力学行为。

Conclusion: 该方法为包括半经典或路径积分瞬子方法难以处理的系统提供了定量理解亚稳态和慢动力学的途径。

Abstract: Metastability in many-body quantum systems and its associated exponentially-long timescales have been the subject of considerable recent interest. Here, we focus on a class of driven-dissipative many-body open quantum systems described by a Lindbladian having hidden time-reversal symmetry (a form of quantum detailed balance). Examples include boundary-driven interacting spin chains, bosonic lattice models and driven-dissipative collective spin models. We suggest that for such systems, slow timescales in the vicinity of a dissipative first-order phase transition can be analytically predicted using a special purification of the non-equilibrium steady state. We show the accuracy of our conjecture through detailed studies of a dissipative transverse-field Ising model with collective and local decay, and a driven-dissipative nonlinear cavity model. Our results allow quantitative insights into metastability and slow dynamics for a range of systems, including cases where semiclassical or path-integral instanton approaches are intractable.

</details>


### [277] [$100\pmΔt$ Years of Quantum Uncertainty: From Origins to Modern Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07747)
*Lorcan O. Conlon,Biveen Shajilal,Jie Zhao,Tim C. Ralph,Gerd Leuchs,Ulrik L. Andersen,Syed M. Assad,Ping Koy Lam,Yunlong Xiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了海森堡不确定性原理从最初的思想实验到现代数学形式体系的百年发展历程，重点介绍了其在量子度量学、多参数估计和压缩态等领域的核心应用，并展望了未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定性原理作为量子力学与经典物理的根本分水岭，其百年发展已从简单思想实验演变为丰富的研究领域，需要对这一发展脉络进行全面梳理，以深化对量子理论的理解并推动实际应用。

Method: 通过综述性分析，追溯不确定性原理从早期思想实验到多种数学表述（不确定性关系）的演进过程，考察各表述间的相互关联，并系统评估其在量子度量学等领域的应用。

Result: 揭示了不确定性原理在量子度量学中支撑信息提取策略的核心作用，建立了其与多参数估计和压缩态理论的深层联系，展示了该原理从理论到实践的全方位影响。

Conclusion: 不确定性原理在百年发展后仍持续推动量子理论的深化和实际技术突破，未来一个世纪有望迎来更多变革性发现。

Abstract: Heisenberg's uncertainty principle is a cornerstone of quantum mechanics, marking a decisive departure from classical physics. Conceived almost a century ago through a thought experiment showing that measuring an electron's position inevitably disturbs its momentum, it began as a deceptively simple idea that sparked countless studies and grew into the rich research field it is today. This review traces its development into a spectrum of mathematical formulations -- known as uncertainty relations -- and explores their interconnections and wide-ranging applications. We highlight its central role in quantum metrology, where it underpins strategies for extracting information from quantum systems with ever-increasing precision, and its links to multiparameter estimation and squeezed states. This review, dedicated to the centenary of the uncertainty principle, reflects on how it has deepened our understanding of quantum theory and driven practical advances, and looks ahead to a century poised for further surprising and transformative discoveries.

</details>


### [278] [Floquet Entanglement Generation in Parametrically Driven Coupled Superconducting Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07797)
*Gustavo M. Meneses A.,Daniel Dominguez,María José Sánchez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了两个超导量子比特通过参数驱动纵向相互作用产生纠缠的动力学机制，发现了一种不同于传统共振激发的纠缠产生方式，并提出了“相干纠缠破坏”现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超导量子比特系统中通过参数驱动实现纠缠产生的非传统机制，特别是区别于标准共振激发到纠缠本征态的途径。

Method: 利用Floquet理论和精确数值模拟分析时间演化；采用广义Van Vleck近简并微扰理论推导有效解析描述。

Result: 发现纠缠源于周期性驱动下多光子共振条件对两个可分离本征态的混合；纠缠度可通过驱动振幅有效调控，特定振幅下纠缠完全抑制（相干纠缠破坏）。

Conclusion: 揭示了参数驱动超导量子比特系统中纠缠产生的新机制，该效应无法在标准旋波近似下捕捉，为量子信息处理中的纠缠调控提供了新途径。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamical generation of entanglement in a system of two superconducting qubits coupled through a parametrically driven longitudinal interaction. Using Floquet theory and exact numerical simulations, we analyze the time evolution of the system initialized in a separable ground state. Our results reveal a nontrivial mechanism for entanglement generation, fundamentally distinct from the conventional resonant excitation to an entangled eigenstate. We show that this mechanism emerges when two initially separable eigenstates are mixed by the periodic driving under multiphoton resonance conditions. Since the effect cannot be captured within a standard rotating-wave approximation, we employ generalized Van Vleck near-degenerate perturbation theory to derive an effective analytical description. Within this framework, we demonstrate that the sustained entanglement originates from the hybridization of the dominant Floquet states, namely those with the largest overlap with the initial ground state. Furthermore, the degree of entanglement can be efficiently controlled through the driving amplitude. In particular, for specific amplitudes, the entanglement is fully suppressed. We term this phenomenon as coherent destruction of entanglement.

</details>


### [279] [Near-deterministic single-atom loading on a photonic integrated circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07800)
*Xinchao Zhou,Ahreum Lee,Dipanjan Das,Saivirinchi Prabandhakavi,Chen-Lung Hung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实现了在光子集成电路（PIC）的微环谐振器上近乎确定性地装载单个中性原子，通过光学传送带技术将原子输运至芯片上，达到了强耦合腔量子电动力学所需的单原子协同参数C>1，并展示了基于实时反馈的确定性原子转移（单原子转移概率82%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将全同量子发射体（中性原子）与光子集成电路耦合是实现可扩展量子科学与信息处理的关键步骤，但迄今尚未实现高效原子捕获与强单原子-光子耦合的实验方案。

Method: 利用光镊中的移动光晶格形成精密光学传送带，将捕获的原子稳定输运至PIC微环谐振器上；通过连续监测穿过电路的探测光子透射率（对微环附近单原子位置敏感），检测平均占据数；基于实时反馈，将输运来的原子确定性地转移至微环上的静态陷阱中。

Result: 实现了平均1.5个原子占据数（来自70个占据格点），传送带位置重现性4 nm；确定性转移实验中获得82%的单原子转移概率和18%的双原子转移概率；达到单原子协同参数C>1，满足强耦合条件。

Conclusion: 该技术可扩展至确定性、高效的原子阵列组装，为中性原子与复杂功能光子集成电路的集成提供可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Coupling identical quantum emitters to a photonic integrated circuit (PIC) is a key step for scaling up emitter-photon interfaces for quantum science and information processing. Neutral atoms are attractive candidates due to their indistinguishability and controllability. However, experimental realizations of efficient atom trapping on a PIC while achieving strong single atom-photon coupling has so-far remained elusive. Here, we demonstrate near-deterministic single-atom loading on a microring resonator circuit, reaching single-atom cooperativity parameter C > 1 for strong coupling in cavity quantum electrodynamics. We utilize a precision optical conveyor belt, formed by a moving optical lattice in an optical tweezer, to steadily deliver trapped atoms onto a PIC. By continuously monitoring the transmission of probe photons through the circuit, which is sensitive to the proximity of single atoms near a microring resonator, we detect mean occupancy of 1.5 from 70 occupied lattice sites in a conveyor-belt transport of 4 nm position reproducibility. Based upon real-time feedback, we deterministically transfer the delivered atoms into a stationary trap on the microring, achieving 82% (18%) probability of single-(two-)atom transfer. Our technique can be extended to deterministic, highly efficient atom array assembly, providing a scalable route for neutral atom integration with PICs of complex functionalities.

</details>


### [280] [Hamiltonian-Guided Leverage Embedding: Robust Subspace Compression for Efficient QAOA Parameter Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07814)
*Sumanta Mukherjee,Kalyan Dasgupta,Surya Shravan Kumar Sajja,Kameshwaran Sampath,Abhishek Singh,Dhriti Verma,Dzung Phan,Jayant Kalagnanam*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出HGLE算法，利用QAOA测量样本的低秩结构，通过加权Ising特征矩阵和杠杆分数行采样实现降维，从而高效鲁棒地估计QAOA变分参数(γ, β)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAOA中变分参数(γ, β)的经典估计需要在被采样噪声污染的高维非凸景观上优化，计算瓶颈严重。

Method: 提出Hamiltonian-Guided Leverage Embedding (HGLE)算法：将低能量子样本编码为加权Ising特征矩阵，通过杠杆分数行采样压缩矩阵并保持秩-r子空间几何，再驱动经典置信域循环进行参数估计。

Result: 提供了秩保持和能量逼近误差的理论保证，在Max-Cut和最大独立集问题及不同密度图拓扑上验证了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 利用QAOA测量样本的显式低秩结构可显著降低变分参数估计的计算成本，同时保持噪声鲁棒性。

Abstract: The Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) is a hybrid quantum-classical framework for combinatorial optimization on near-term quantum devices. A central bottleneck is the classical estimation of its variational parameters γ and β, which must be optimized over a high-dimensional, non-convex landscape corrupted by sampling noise. We observe that the classical feature matrices constructed from QAOA measurement samples exhibit pronounced low-rank structure, and exploit this property for noise-robust, reduced-dimension parameter search. We present the Hamiltonian-Guided Leverage Embedding (HGLE) algorithm - a hybrid pipeline that encodes low-energy quantum samples into a weighted Ising feature matrix and compresses it via leverage-score row sampling, provably preserving the dominant rank-rsubspace geometry. The compressed representation drives a classical trust-region loop for (γ, β) estimation at a fraction of the original cost. We provide formal guarantees for rank preservation and energy approximation error, and demonstrate robustness across problem types (Max-Cut, Maximum Independent Set) and graph topologies of varying density.

</details>


### [281] [Projected Inverse Iteration: An Eigenvalue Approach to Ground-State Computation with Neural Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07825)
*Hang Zhang,Victor Armegioiu,Juan Carrasquilla,Siddhartha Mishra,Johannes Müller,Jannes Nys,Marius Zeinhofer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出投影逆迭代（PII）方法，将基态搜索重新框架为特征值问题，实现快速、谱隙无关的收敛，并在强阻挫自旋系统中优于现有优化技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经网络波函数的量子多体问题优化方法（如自然梯度下降和随机重整化）存在依赖于谱隙的收敛瓶颈，在处理具有竞争序和近简并基态的系统（如阻挫磁体和强关联电子材料）时效果有限。

Method: 将基态搜索重新框架为特征值问题，提出投影逆迭代（PII）方法，在保持随机重整化多项式计算复杂度的同时实现快速且对谱隙不敏感的收敛。

Result: 在具有挑战性的二维自旋系统（包括高度阻挫的J₁-J₂模型）上，PII优于标准优化技术，在小谱隙存在的情况下能有效发现复杂量子态。

Conclusion: PII可被解释为一种专为特征值问题定制的自然梯度方法，有望推广应用于深度学习中的相关挑战。

Abstract: Deep learning offers a powerful approach to quantum many-body problems via neural network wavefunctions, but their optimization remains a severe bottleneck. Existing optimization methods, including natural gradient descent and stochastic reconfiguration, suffer from spectral gap-dependent convergence that limits their effectiveness on systems fraught with competing orders and nearly degenerate ground states, such as frustrated magnets and strongly correlated electron materials. Here, we introduce Projected Inverse Iteration (PII) by re-framing the ground-state search as an eigenvalue problem. PII achieves rapid, gap-insensitive convergence while preserving the favorable polynomial computational scaling of stochastic reconfiguration. Demonstrated on challenging two-dimensional spin systems, including the highly frustrated $J_1$-$J_2$ model, PII outperforms standard optimization techniques and presents a promising algorithmic strategy for discovering complex quantum states in the presence of small spectral gaps. More broadly, PII can be interpreted as a novel natural gradient method tailored for eigenvalue problems, opening up its application to related challenges within deep learning.

</details>


### [282] [Affine Filtering Measurements and Their Applications to Quantum Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07852)
*Avijit Mandal,Noah Shutty,Henry D. Pfister,Stephen P. Jordan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于仿射滤波测量的无歧义状态区分（USD）方法，用于在纯态经典-量子信道上解码线性码。通过将最优测量设计转化为半定规划（可进一步简化为线性规划），并构建了针对局部码的量子解码框架。仿真表明，在规则LDPC码上，该方法优于符号级USD和PGM解码方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有USD测量用于量子解码时，往往忽略码字间的结构信息。本文旨在利用线性码的代数结构，设计一种能够输出仿射子空间（而非单个符号）的USD测量，从而提升解码性能。

Method: 1) 将仿射滤波测量问题建模为半定规划；2) 利用群协变索引和特征对角化将其简化为线性规划；3) 基于该最优测量构建适用于局部码的量子解码框架；4) 在规则LDPC码（Gallager ensembles，单校验局部约束）上进行仿真验证。

Result: 在i.i.d.纯态信道上，基于仿射滤波的解码方法在规则LDPC码上优于符号级无歧义状态区分（USD）和符号级最优测量（PGM）方法。

Conclusion: 仿射滤波测量作为一种细粒度的USD方法，能够有效利用码结构信息，在量子解码中展现出优于传统符号级方法的性能。研究同时指出，与Buzet和Chailloux的并发工作相比，本文侧重于码感知（code-aware）的构造与解码，而非码无关（code-agnostic）场景。

Abstract: Unambiguous state discrimination (USD) measurements are attractive because outcomes are either marked as conclusive (i.e., error free) or inconclusive (i.e., erased). We study affine filtering measurements, a structured variant of USD for decoding classical linear codes over pure-state classical-quantum channels, where a conclusive outcome identifies an affine subspace containing the transmitted codeword and an inconclusive outcome is treated as an erasure. For a group-covariant indexing of pure-state codewords, we show that the optimal design of affine filtering measurements is a semidefinite program that can be reduced to a linear program via character-based diagonalization. We use the resulting measurement to build a quantum decoding framework for local codes, and we demonstrate (via simulations on regular LDPC codes from Gallager ensembles using single parity check local constraints) that affine filtering based decoding can outperform symbol-wise USD and symbol-wise pretty good measurement based decoding methods on i.i.d. pure-state channels. In an independent and concurrent work, Buzet and Chailloux study similar fine-grained USD measurements for symmetric families of states. Their focus is on the code-agnostic setting whereas our focus is on code-aware constructions and decoding.

</details>


### [283] [Defining Unique, Redundant, and Synergistic Quantum Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07880)
*Sean Ericson,Hailin Wang,S. J. van Enk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将经典的部分信息分解（PID）框架扩展到量子领域，定义了量子信息的独特、冗余和协同分量，并展示了独特信息在量子纠错码中的核心作用（任何可擦除纠错的编码子集必须包含零独特信息），以及协同信息与逻辑操作的关系。此外，冗余量子信息被证明是Zurek等人提出的退相干机制中经典世界涌现的关键特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典信息论中的部分信息分解（PID）框架推广到量子领域，以量化量子信息中的独特、冗余和协同分量，从而更深入地理解量子纠错、量子逻辑操作以及量子-经典过渡中的信息结构。

Method: 通过扩展经典PID框架至量子域，定义量子信息的独特、冗余和协同分量；将这些分量应用于量子纠错码分析（检验编码子集的擦除可纠正性）和退相干机制（Zurek的量子达尔文主义解释）的理论分析。

Result: 1) 独特信息在量子纠错中起核心作用：任何可擦除纠错的编码子集必须包含零独特信息。2) 协同信息出现在两个不相交编码子集之间，当逻辑操作支持在整个集合上而非子集上单独支持时。3) 冗余量子信息是Zurek等人提出的退相干机制中经典世界如何从量子世界涌现的关键特征。

Conclusion: 量子PID框架为理解量子信息结构提供了新的理论工具，独特、冗余和协同信息分别对应量子纠错、经典世界涌现和量子逻辑操作等不同物理场景中的关键信息角色。

Abstract: We extend the classical ideas of the Partial Information Decomposition (PID) to the quantum domain and quantify unique, redundant, and synergistic quantum information. We show that unique information plays the central role in quantum error correction codes: any erasure-correctable subset of encoding qubits must contain zero unique information. Synergistic information between two disjoint subsets of encoding qubits appears when a logical operation is supported on the whole set but not on the subsets separately. In a different application of our PID, we show that redundant quantum information is the crucial feature of the decoherence mechanism proposed by Zurek \textit{et al.} to explain how the classical world emerges out of the quantum world.

</details>


### [284] [All-Optical Wide-Field Magnetometry with Van Der Waals Quantum Sensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07899)
*Feifei Zhou,Peiyan Ma,Jiajun Li,Ke Jing,Shihao Ru,Hongwei Chen,Ying Dong,Xinqing Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验演示了一种基于六方氮化硼中带负电硼空位（$V_B^-$）中心的全光宽场磁力测量策略，利用基态能级反交叉（GSLAC）实现无微波的磁场传感，在约42×21 μm²区域内实现近场直流磁场分布成像，单像素灵敏度达67.1 μT/√Hz，空间分辨率约1 μm/像素，较ODMR方法提升约三倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $V_B^-$中心具有范德华晶格结构和原位量子传感潜力，但现有方法依赖微波调控，限制了其在极端条件下的应用。本文旨在开发一种无需微波的全光磁测量方案，拓展$V_B^-$中心在量子传感中的适用性。

Method: 利用$V_B^-$中心对磁场敏感的基态能级反交叉（GSLAC）效应，该效应在特定磁场下诱发$m_S=0$与$m_S=-1$之间的强电子自旋跃迁；通过监测GSLAC特征峰的偏移来精确测定外磁场，实现全光学宽场磁成像。

Result: 在约42×21 μm²区域内实现载流电路的近场直流磁场分布成像；单像素光子散粒噪声极限灵敏度为67.1 μT/√Hz，较传统ODMR方法提升约三倍；空间分辨率约1 μm/像素。

Conclusion: 该全光策略无需微波驱动，简化了实验装置，提升了灵敏度，为$V_B^-$中心在极端条件下实现鲁棒且便捷的量子磁测量铺平了道路。

Abstract: Negatively charged boron vacancy ($V_B^-$) centers in hexagonal boron nitride ($h$-BN) have attracted wide-range interests owing to their van der Waals lattice and their potentials for $in$-$situ$ quantum sensing. Here we propose and experimentally demonstrate an all-optical strategy for wide-field magnetometry based on $V_B^-$ centers. This strategy exploits the magnetically sensitive ground-state level anti-crossing (GSLAC) of $V_B^-$ centers, which induces a strong electron spin transition between $m_S = 0$ and $m_S = -1$ states, enabling microwave-free magnetic field measurement. By monitoring the shift of GSLAC feature, the external magnetic field can be precisely determined. Using this technique, we demonstrate all-optical wide-field imaging of near-field DC magnetic field distribution from current-carrying circuits over an area of around 42 $\times$ 21 $μ$m$^2$. An estimated photon shot-noise-limited sensitivity of 67.1 $μ$T/$\sqrt{\text{Hz}}$ is achieved for a single pixel, which is an approximately threefold improvement over the ODMR method, along with a spatial resolution of about 1 $μ$m per pixel. Our approach expands the applicability of $V_B^-$ centers in quantum sensing, paving the way for robust and convenient magnetometry under extreme conditions.

</details>


### [285] [Repair Before Veto, When Repair Is Hidden: Quantum-Accessible Features for Repair-Augmented Constraint Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08020)
*Yifan Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-RACL（量子修复增强约束学习）框架，将硬约束决策系统的"否决"改为"先修复再否决"。通过构造一个基于离散对数（DLP）隐藏的修复类别问题，证明标准经典策略无法有效访问该特征，而量子智能体利用Shor算法/傅里叶结构可以访问，从而显著降低错误否决率（<1.1%），展示了量子AI在特定决策语义下提供经典方法无法获取的关键特征访问能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统硬约束决策系统在遇到不可行候选时直接否决，但当系统有能力进行低成本修复时，这种否决过于僵化，会产生"错误否决"（false veto）而非简单的排序错误。作者希望建立一种"先修复再否决"的决策语义，并在其中识别出量子计算可以发挥独特作用的关键推理环节。

Method: 1) 提出RACL决策语义：通过顺序修复计划恢复可行性和偏好，否则返回结构化拒绝信用；2) 识别关键硬链接为"修复可行性推理"——即从观测候选和上下文中推断哪个修复类别能恢复可行性；3) 构造基于离散对数隐藏的RACL族：修复类别是潜指数a=log_g(x)中的移位区间规则，学习者仅观测x=g^a mod p；4) 利用标准DLP的学习分离假设，证明该特征经典策略无法有效访问，而量子智能体可通过Shor/傅里叶结构访问。

Result: 跨6个素数和10个种子，有界原始输入经典策略和错误原始傅里叶编码策略接近随机水平，而Q-DLP策略的错误否决率低于1.1%，在所有配对种子中胜出，QNI_cond指标达到0.9777至0.9972。经典DL预言机版本与其匹配，证明差异来自特征访问而非分类器容量。

Conclusion: 量子AI在此并非作为通用的模型升级，而是针对该DLP隐藏的修复家族，提供了缺失的关键特征访问能力，从而闭环了"先修复再否决"的决策流程。这明确了量子计算在决策系统中一个独特且具理论支撑的应用定位。

Abstract: Hard-constraint decision systems usually veto infeasible candidates. This is too rigid when the system can act: if a known affordable repair would make an infeasible candidate feasible and valuable, rejection is a false veto rather than a ranking error. We introduce Q-RACL (Quantum Repair-Augmented Constraint Learning), a repair-before-veto framework that first defines RACL decision semantics and then identifies the single inference link where quantum feature access can be load-bearing. RACL accepts a candidate when a sequential repair plan restores feasibility and preference; otherwise it returns structured rejection credit. The hard link is repair-feasibility inference: which repair class restores feasibility from an observed candidate and context. We construct a discrete-logarithm-hidden RACL family where the repair class is a shifted interval rule in the latent exponent a = log_g(x), while the learner observes only x = g^a mod p. Under standard DLP-based learning separation, this coordinate is inaccessible to efficient raw-input classical policies but accessible to a quantum agent through Shor/Fourier structure. Across six primes and ten seeds, bounded raw-input classical policies and a wrong raw-Fourier encoding remain near chance, whereas the Q-DLP policy keeps false-veto rate below 1.1%, wins all paired seeds, and yields QNI_cond = 0.9777 to 0.9972. A classical DLog oracle matches it, isolating feature access rather than classifier capacity. Thus quantum AI is not added as a generic model upgrade; for this DLP-hidden repair family, it supplies the missing feature that closes the repair-before-veto loop.

</details>


### [286] [Numerical solution of the nonlinear Dirac equation by a splitting variational quantum algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08053)
*Qian Zuo,Ying He,Xiaofei Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Dirac-sVQA的算子分裂变分量子算法，用于模拟非线性狄拉克方程（NLDE）。该算法将NLDE演化分解为线性狄拉克子步（通过自旋子-傅里叶狄拉克传播子实现）和非线性变分修正（通过测量基变分更新实现），数值实验表明该算法能准确捕捉总密度和分量自旋子动力学，且误差随时间稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性狄拉克方程中的状态依赖非线性相互作用导致时间离散更新依赖于中间自旋子态，无法作为固定的状态无关幺正电路实现，因此需要一种新的量子算法来克服这一困难。

Method: 将NLDE演化分解为线性狄拉克子步和非线性变分修正两部分。线性子步使用自旋子-傅里叶狄拉克传播子在联合位置-自旋寄存器上实现，保持狄拉克算子的自旋-动量耦合和质量诱导自旋演化；非线性修正通过少量重叠、自通道和交叉通道可观测量重构为基于测量的变分更新。

Result: 数值实验表明，Dirac-sVQA在多种非线性机制下能准确捕捉总密度和分量自旋子动力学，与经典傅里叶伪谱分裂解高度吻合，且误差随时间稳定。

Conclusion: 这些结果为算子分裂变分量子模拟非线性相对论波动方程的可行性提供了数值证据。

Abstract: In this work, we propose an operator-splitting variational quantum algorithm, termed Dirac-sVQA, for simulating the nonlinear Dirac equation (NLDE). The main difficulty arises from the state-dependent nonlinear interaction, its time-discrete update depends explicitly on the intermediate spinor state and, in general, cannot be implemented as a fixed state-independent unitary circuit. To address this difficulty, we decompose the NLDE evolution into a structured linear Dirac substep and a nonlinear variational correction. The linear substep is implemented by a spinor-Fourier Dirac propagator on a joint position-spin register, preserving the spin-momentum coupling and mass-induced spin evolution of the Dirac operator. The nonlinear correction is reformulated as a measurement-based variational update through a small set of overlap, self-channel, and cross-channel observables. We provide the corresponding quantum circuits and derive measurement-aware resource and complexity estimates. Numerical experiments in several nonlinear regimes show that Dirac-sVQA accurately captures both the total density and the componentwise spinor dynamics, agrees well with classical Fourier pseudospectral splitting solutions, and exhibits stable error behavior over time. These results provide numerical evidence for the feasibility of operator-splitting variational quantum simulation for nonlinear relativistic wave equations.

</details>


### [287] [Robust applicability of continuous dynamical decoupling to decoherence reduction in longitudinal and transverse-noise settings: The role of anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08114)
*S. Afonso,J. M. Gomez Llorente,J. Plata*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于连续动态解耦（CDD）理论，将其从仅处理纵向噪声的局限推广至同时存在横向噪声和各向异性噪声的通用量子比特场景，通过分析驱动场线性斜升（噪声Landau-Zener跃迁）对量子态修饰的影响，利用连续幺正变换将噪声转化为依赖于驱动参数的有效随机项，并基于此提出通过调控有效噪声谱特性来抑制退相干的控制策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CDD理论主要聚焦于抑制纵向涨落引起的纯退相，但实际量子比特系统中普遍存在横向涨落和各向异性噪声，缺乏对这些复杂噪声场景下CDD效率的系统理论评估。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用连续幺正变换序列方法，将进入系统的噪声转化为依赖于驱动参数的有效随机过程；将驱动场通过线性斜升引入的态修饰描述为含噪Landau-Zener跃迁；通过分析有效噪声的谱特性，设计通过受控改变驱动参数来抑制各类噪声的策略。

Result: 通过适当选择控制参数，CDD在推广后的通用场景下（同时存在纵向噪声、横向噪声及各向异性噪声）仍表现出显著的鲁棒性；有效噪声的谱特性依赖驱动参数，为噪声缓解提供了可控手段。

Conclusion: 将CDD理论推广至包含横向和各向异性噪声的通用量子比特环境是可行的，通过优化驱动参数可以显著提升CDD对各种噪声的鲁棒性，为实际量子比特系统中的退相干抑制提供了更普适的理论指导。

Abstract: We analytically evaluate the efficiency of continuous dynamical decoupling (CDD) to curb decoherence in generic qubit setups where diverse sources of noise can be present. Previous theoretical approaches to CDD have mainly focused on its potential to cope with longitudinal fluctuations. Here, the basic scenario tackled with CDD is generalized. Apart from dealing with pure dephasing induced by diagonal noise, we consider the impact of transverse fluctuations, usually present in the practical arrangements. In particular, the implications of anisotropic noisy inputs are studied. Additionally, we analyze the role of the fluctuations in the dressing of the qubit by the CDD field of control: since the driving field is usually switched on through linear ramps of its characteristic parameters, the associated dressing of the original states can be described in terms of noisy Landau-Zener transitions. In our approach, based on a sequence of unitary transformations, the noise entering the system is cast into effective stochastic terms whose spectral characteristics are dependent on the driving parameters. This description allows the design of strategies to mitigate the impact of the fluctuations using controlled changes in the effective-noise properties. Significant robustness of CDD against the generalization of the basic scenario can be achieved through an appropriate choice of the parameters of control.

</details>


### [288] [Markovian dynamics of single-rebit open quantum systems with applications to colour perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08117)
*Michel Berthier,Gabriel Niebel,Edoardo Provenzi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了实数域上开放二态量子系统（rebit）的马尔可夫动力学，完成了rebit量子信道的完全分类，并将其应用于颜色感知建模，展示了如何通过马尔可夫rebit信道模拟非中性光源引起的色度畸变和色觉缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对实数域上开放二态量子系统（rebit）的马尔可夫动力学缺乏系统性研究，且尚未有工作将这类量子信道应用于颜色感知建模中。

Method: 通过GKSL方程对rebit量子信道进行完全分类，识别对应的Lindblad生成元并推导完全正性条件；利用Lüders测量模型将rebit态空间与颜色感知关联，用马尔可夫rebit信道模拟非中性光源引起的颜色畸变。

Result: 获得了rebit马尔可夫CPTP映射的完整分类，并展示了该分类在颜色感知建模中的可行性，通过数字图像仿真验证了模型效果。

Conclusion: rebit马尔可夫动力学不仅具有完整的数学分类，还能有效应用于颜色视觉建模，为研究色觉缺陷和色度畸变提供了新的量子信息学视角。

Abstract: This paper investigates the Markovian dynamics of open two-state quantum systems defined over the real numbers (rebits). Two main objectives are pursued.
