First, we present a comprehensive classification of Markovian rebit quantum channels, i.e. one-parameter semigroups of completely positive, trace-preserving (CPTP) maps acting on the rebit state space. We show that a full characterisation of their action can be achieved and that describing these channels as solutions of the GKSL equation allows us to explicitly identify the associated Lindblad generators and conditions for complete positivity. Second, we present an original application of this classification to colour perception. Using a recent model in which perceived colours arise from Lüders measurements on the rebit state space, we show how chromatic distortion induced by a non-neutral illuminant can be modelled by a Markovian rebit channel that progressively diminishes colour distinguishability. Other types of channels could be used to study colour vision deficiencies. These phenomena are illustrated by simulations on digital images, highlighting the relevance of rebit Markovian dynamics in modelling colour vision.

</details>


### [289] [Every Rank-Two Entangled State is Projectively Steerable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08189)
*Yu-Xuan Zhang,Jing-Ling Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明所有秩为2的两体纠缠态在任意有限局域维度下都是投影可导引的（至少单向），当有效局域维度相等时则是双向投影可导引的。证明基于边界定理而非不等式优化或两量子比特约化，揭示了支撑-核几何结构可作为低秩纠缠态投影可导引性的实用判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 秩2是纠缠与EPR导引可能发生分歧的第一个真正混合秩，该分歧是否发生是开放问题。本文旨在确定秩2两体纠缠态在投影测量限制下是否必然具有可导引性。

Method: 采用边界定理方法：利用维度-秩障碍迫使较大局域方的投影结果抵达信任态锥的纯边界点；通过支撑-核切触块同时作为NPT子式和投影导引证书；对于退化接触情形，利用秩2约束关闭该分支并迫使产生主动接触。

Result: 所有秩2两体纠缠态在任意有限局域维度下都是投影可导引的；当有效局域维度相等时是双向投影可导引的。整个第一个混合态层次（秩2）构成完整的投影导引扇区。

Conclusion: 纠缠与EPR导引在秩2层次不存在分歧；支撑-核几何结构提供了实用的低秩纠缠态投影可导引性判据。

Abstract: Pure entangled states are already steerable by suitable projective measurements within their Schmidt supports, whereas rank two is the first genuinely mixed rank at which entanglement and Einstein--Podolsky--Rosen steering could bifurcate. We prove that this bifurcation does not occur even under the restricted measurement class of projective measurements: every rank-two bipartite entangled state in arbitrary finite local dimensions is projectively steerable in at least one direction, and is two-way projectively steerable when the effective local dimensions are equal. The proof is a boundary theorem rather than a steering-inequality optimization or a two-qubit reduction. A dimension--rank obstruction forces a projective outcome on the larger effective party to hit a pure boundary point of the trusted state cone. At such a contact, a nonzero support--kernel tangent block is simultaneously an NPT minor and a projective-steering certificate; if the first contact is degenerate, the rank-two constraint closes that branch and forces a second, active contact. Thus the entire first mixed-state stratum is a complete projective-steering sector, and the same support--kernel geometry gives a practical low-rank certificate whenever the relevant boundary contact is nondegenerate.

</details>


### [290] [Entanglement in the Quantum Volunteer's Dilemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08227)
*Noah Dane Hebdon,Dax Enshan Koh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Eisert-Wilkens-Lewenstein量子博弈框架下，将经典志愿者困境推广到可调纠缠参数γ的量子版本，研究了对称纳什均衡存在的条件，推导出纠缠阈值与玩家数目的解析关系，发现最大纠缠并非必要，均衡在纠缠度超过阈值时即可维持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子志愿者困境分析仅考虑最大纠缠情形，但实际量子设备中纠缠资源有限，需要理解纠缠程度对博弈均衡行为的影响。

Method: 在Eisert-Wilkens-Lewenstein量子博弈框架中引入可调纠缠参数γ，推导对称纳什均衡存在的显式条件，分析两种经典策略轮廓，利用解析方法计算纠缠阈值。

Result: 最大纠缠并非维持对称均衡的必要条件；存在一个纠缠阈值，高于该阈值时均衡行为持续存在；该阈值与系统规模（玩家人数）直接相关；分别给出了2≤n≤9和偶数n两种情况下的阈值解析表达式。

Conclusion: 该纠缠阈值的刻画对资源受限量子设备上的实现具有直接参考价值，表明即使纠缠资源有限，量子志愿者困境仍可维持合作行为。

Abstract: A well-known model in game theory, the Volunteer's Dilemma describes a group of $n$ players who decide whether to volunteer for a collective benefit at a personal cost, or to abstain and risk forfeiting the benefit altogether. A quantum version of this dilemma, developed within the Eisert-Wilkens-Lewenstein framework, allows each player to manipulate one qubit of a shared entangled state, leading to symmetric Nash equilibria with higher expected payoffs than in the classical game. Existing analyses, however, assume maximal entanglement. Within the same framework, we introduce a generalized Quantum Volunteer's Dilemma with a tunable entanglement parameter $γ$ and study the extent to which equilibrium behavior depends on the level of entanglement. We derive explicit conditions relating $γ$, the number of players, and the players' strategies under which symmetric Nash equilibria exist, focusing on two canonical strategy profiles: one for $2\leq n\leq 9$, and one for even $n$. We find that maximal entanglement is not required to sustain symmetric equilibria. Instead, equilibrium behavior persists above a threshold value, which we compute analytically in both cases. We also demonstrate that the threshold value directly depends on system size. This characterization is directly relevant for implementations on resource-constrained quantum devices, where entanglement is inherently limited.

</details>


### [291] [QnRL: Quantum-Native Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08276)
*Alexander DeRieux,Walid Saad*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为量子原生强化学习（QnRL）的框架，利用量子系统的叠加和纠缠特性直接在希尔伯特空间中学习条件分布，以建模随机环境中的决策策略。通过提出的量子振幅回踢（QuAK）算法，QnRL能够在量子态中蒸馏条件动作策略分布，相比基线方法，评估分数最高提升82.9%，参数减少最多94.3%，并更准确地估计未知观测的期望回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子强化学习（QRL）架构通过估计期望值间接近似环境行为，表达能力有限且自适应潜力不足。需要一种能够直接利用量子计算机分布特性建模环境随机变量的新型QRL方法。

Method: 提出QnRL框架，它是一个分布强化学习框架，通过叠加和纠缠量子态在希尔伯特空间中学习条件分布。核心是提出的量子振幅回踢（QuAK）算法，能够在多个叠加分布之间比较第m个矩的n次幂，从而在希尔伯特空间内从量子生成模型的矩中蒸馏出条件动作策略分布。

Result: 在多种环境下的实验结果显示，与基线方法相比，QnRL的评估分数最高提升82.9%，平均参数减少最多94.3%，对未见观测的期望回报估计更准确，且能更好地适应变化的随机条件。

Conclusion: QnRL作为一种量子原生分布强化学习框架，通过直接建模环境分布而非估计期望值，在表达能力和自适应性能上显著优于现有方法。其复杂分布组合还能表达纯经典和经典采样量子分布模型无法捕捉的环境相关性。

Abstract: Quantum reinforcement learning (QRL) is a promising approach to learn effective decision strategies across several applications with stochastic environments. Instead of directly modeling the random variables that govern these environments, existing QRL architectures indirectly approximate environment behavior by estimating expected outcomes, which limits their expressive power and adaptive potential. Overcoming such challenges requires a novel QRL approach that exploits the distributional nature of quantum computers to directly model environment random variables as quantum state distributions. Hence, in this paper, a novel framework dubbed quantum-native reinforcement learning (QnRL) is proposed. QnRL is a distributional RL framework that learns conditional distributions naturally in Hilbert space via superimposed and entangled quantum states. Thus, QnRL can directly model the behavior of stochastic learning environments via the natural properties of quantum systems. QnRL accomplishes this via a novel, proposed quantum amplitude kickback (QuAK) algorithm that enables comparing the $n$-th power of the $m$-th moment of multiple superimposed distributions. It is theoretically proven that a conditional action policy distribution is distilled from the moments of a quantum generative model entirely within Hilbert space via QuAK, and optimized via QnRL. This complex distribution composition is also shown to provide extra dimensions for expressing environment correlations that are unknown to purely classical and classically-sampled quantum distributional models. Experimental results across diverse environments show that QnRL achieves up to $82.9\%$ higher evaluation scores, with up to $94.3\%$ fewer parameters on average, more accurately estimates the expected return for unseen observations, and better adapts to varying stochastic conditions compared to the baseline.

</details>


### [292] [On solutions of the Schrödinger equation for some molecular potentials: Power-series method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08290)
*Francisco M. Fernández*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明标准幂级数方法比Ikhdair和Sever提出的波函数方法更简单、更强大，并以伪谐振子和Kratzer-Fues势为例进行了说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对Ikhdair和Sever在文献[Cent. Eur. J. Phys. 6, 697 (2008)]中提出的波函数方法，本文旨在展示标准幂级数方法在求解量子力学问题时的优越性。

Method: 采用标准幂级数方法（即教材中常见的级数解法），与Ikhdair和Sever的波函数方法进行对比分析，以伪谐振子势和Kratzer-Fues势作为检验案例。

Result: 标准幂级数方法在数学形式上更简洁，在求解范围和效率上更具优势，能够更有效地处理所选的势能问题。

Conclusion: 标准幂级数方法优于Ikhdair和Sever提出的波函数方法，是求解此类量子力学问题的更优选择。

Abstract: We show that the standard power-series method described in many textbooks of quantum-mechanics and quantum-chemistry is simpler and more powerful than the wavefunction approach proposed by Ikhdair and Sever [Cent. Eur. J. Phys. 6, 697 (2008)]. As illustrative examples we choose the pseudoharmonic and Kratzer-Fues potentials already treated by those authors.

</details>


### [293] [Quantum algorithms for stochastic nonlinear differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08349)
*Sergey Bravyi,Adam Byrne,Mykhaylo Zayats,Sergiy Zhuk*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子算法，用于求解一类具有耗散和二次漂移的高维随机微分方程，该算法适用于强非线性全连接系统，在保持范数的漂移条件下，利用辅助量子谐振子系统实现与系统维度对数相关、与演化时间线性相关的计算代价，并证明了该方法可以应用于湍流等强非线性随机微分方程的量子模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程和计算物理中的许多模型涉及随机非线性动力学，但高维随机微分方程的精确模拟仍然具有挑战性。已有量子算法仅适用于弱非线性和稀疏系统，无法处理强非线性和全连接相互作用系统。

Method: 提出一种量子算法，用于求解具有耗散和二次漂移的N维随机微分方程。主要技术突破是设计了一个用于模拟N个相互作用的量子谐振子辅助系统的子程序，其计算代价与N呈多对数关系。

Result: 对于保持范数的漂移（流体动力学离散化的关键条件），该方法能够以严格的误差界近似低阶相关函数的期望值，计算代价与log(N)呈多项式关系、与演化时间呈线性关系。

Conclusion: 该算法拓展了量子算法处理强非线性随机微分方程的能力，可将Navier-Stokes方程和阻尼Euler方程等湍流模型纳入该框架，为湍流和非线性波动动力学的强非线性SDE量子模拟开辟了道路。

Abstract: Stochastic nonlinear dynamics underlie many models in engineering and computational physics, yet accurate high-dimensional simulation remains challenging. We present a quantum algorithm for a broad class of $N$-dimensional stochastic differential equations with dissipation and quadratic drift. The algorithm applies to strongly nonlinear systems with all-to-all interactions, thereby extending the scope of previously known quantum algorithms that were limited to weak nonlinearity and sparse systems. For norm-preserving drifts, a condition satisfied by key fluid dynamics discretizations, our method approximates expectation values of low-order correlation functions with rigorous error bounds at a cost polynomial in $\log{(N)}$ and linear in the evolution time. Our main technical advance is a subroutine for simulating an auxiliary system of $N$ interacting quantum harmonic oscillators with cost polylogarithmic in $N$. Finally, we formulate turbulence models, including Navier-Stokes and damped Euler equations, within this framework, opening a route to quantum simulation of strongly nonlinear SDEs governing turbulence and nonlinear wave dynamics.

</details>


### [294] [A century of coherent states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08361)
*Dusan Popov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于对角算子排序技术（DOOT）和广义超几何函数的非线性相干态构造方法，通过定义阶梯算子（产生和湮灭算符）使其正规序积等于量子系统的无量纲哈密顿量，从而为任意已知本征能量的非谐振子构建广义相干态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管相干态（包括线性和非线性）已存在一个世纪并发展出多种理论或实验构造方案，但现有方法依赖于阶梯算子的选择及其结构常数。本文旨在提出一种更通用的构造方法，适用于非谐振子，且不依赖于特定的结构常数选择。

Method: 采用对角算子排序技术（DOOT）应用于广义超几何函数，定义一对阶梯算子（产生和湮灭算符），使其正规序积等于量子系统的无量纲哈密顿量。利用已知的无量纲能量本征值，即可简便地确定这些算符的作用。

Result: 成功构建了非谐振子的广义相干态，该方案仅需已知系统的无量纲能量本征值表达式即可确定阶梯算符的作用，且阶梯算符的正规序积恰好等于系统的无量纲哈密顿量。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种通用、简洁的广义相干态构造方法，特别适用于非谐振子系统，拓宽了相干态理论的应用范围。

Abstract: During the century of existence of the notion of coherent states, either linear or nonlinear, several schemes for their construction, theoretical or experimental, have been developed. Generally, the mathematical structure of coherent states depends on the choice of ladder operators, and consequently on the structure constants. In this paper, we propose a way to construct generalized coherent states for anharmonic oscillators that is based on a diagonal operator ordering technique (DOOT) applied to generalized hypergeometric functions, that is, on some of the most general special functions. These states are generated by the action of a pair of ladder operators, the creation and the annihilation, whose ordered normal product is equal to the dimensionless Hamiltonian of the quantum system. In addition, the action of these operators is easy to find if the expression for the dimensionless energy eigenvalues is known.

</details>


### [295] [A K-band Kinetic Inductance Parametric Amplifier Near the Quantum Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08406)
*Chaofan Wang,Shihan Liu,Yufeng Wu,Danqing Wang,Manuel C. C. Pace,Xiangzheng Li,Hong X. Tang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种基于氮化铌（NbN）薄膜的无结动能电感参量放大器，工作于微波K波段23 GHz，实现了高达40 dB增益、100 MHz增益-带宽积、23 dB增益下-85 dBm的1 dB饱和输入功率，以及不超过1.4量子的附加噪声。该架构利用NbN的大超导能隙，可扩展至更高频率，支持毫米波超导量子比特的高保真读取和轴子搜索等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将超导量子器件推向更高工作频率可拓展其功能性并支持在更高温度下运行，但需要超越几GHz范围的近量子极限放大器，现有器件在此频段表现不足。

Method: 采用无结结构、基于NbN薄膜的动能电感参量放大器设计，工作于23 GHz微波K波段。

Result: 增益高达40 dB，100 MHz增益-带宽积，23 dB增益下1 dB饱和输入功率为-85 dBm，相位保持放大下附加噪声不超过1.4个量子。

Conclusion: 基于NbN大超导能隙的架构可扩展至更高频率，为毫米波超导量子比特高保真读取和扩大质量窗口的轴子搜索等应用提供支持。

Abstract: Advancing superconducting quantum devices to higher operating frequencies broadens their functionality and enables operation at elevated temperatures, but it also requires near-quantum-limited amplifiers beyond the few-gigahertz regime. Here we present a junction-free, kinetic-inductance parametric amplifier based on thin-film niobium nitride (NbN) operating at 23 GHz in the microwave K-band, achieving a gain up to 40 dB, a 100 MHz gain-bandwidth product, a 1 dB saturation input power of -85 dBm with 23 dB gain, and added noise no greater than 1.4 quanta for phase-preserving amplification. Leveraging the large superconducting gap of NbN, this architecture can be extended to even higher frequencies, supporting applications such as high-fidelity readout of millimeter-wave superconducting qubits and axion searches over an expanded mass window.

</details>


### [296] [Quantum Kravchuk Transform using $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ fast-forwarding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08443)
*Chaowen Guan,Akshit Katiyar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种量子Kravchuk变换算法，其复杂度在维度和误差参数的倒数上呈对数规模，通过将Kravchuk变换映射到su(2)李代数并利用快速前推模拟方法实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现Kravchuk变换的高效量子算法，使其复杂度在维度和误差参数上达到对数级缩放。

Method: 首先建立计算基下Kravchuk变换到Fock基下su(2)李代数的映射，然后利用振荡子表示中su(2)算子的快速前推模拟方法实现高效的量子Kravchuk变换。

Result: 提出了一个量子Kravchuk变换算法，其时间复杂度在维度和误差参数的倒数上呈对数级增长。

Conclusion: 通过结合Kravchuk变换与su(2)李代数的结构关系以及快速前推模拟技术，成功实现了高效的对数级量子Kravchuk变换算法。

Abstract: We present a quantum algorithm for the Kravchuk transform that scales logarithmically in both the dimension and the inverse of the error parameter. The quantum Kravchuk transform maps computational basis states to states with amplitudes proportional to Kravchuk functions. We achieve this by combining two key techniques: the structural relationship between the Kravchuk transform and the Lie algebras $\mathfrak{su}(2)$, and a recent fast-forwarding simulation method for $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ operators in the oscillator representation. More precisely, we first establish the map from Kravchuk transform in computational basis to $\mathfrak{su}(2)$ in Fock basis. Then built on this connection, we apply the fast-forwarding to achieve an efficient quantum Kravchuk transform.

</details>


### [297] [A Dual Metastable-State Encoding Architecture for Quantum Processing with $^{171}\mathrm{Yb}$ Atom Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08453)
*Chun-Wei Liu,Saiwei Nie,Eesha Banerjee,Micah Davidson,Nick Reynolds,Alyssa L. Miller,Alex P. Burgers*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于$^{171}\mathrm{Yb}$原子的双亚稳态量子比特编码方案，利用${}^3\mathrm{P}_0$和${}^3\mathrm{P}_2$两个独立能级流形分别实现核自旋量子比特（用于存储和算术操作）和超精细自旋量子比特（用于快速拉曼操作和直接态选择性成像），通过相干转移连接两个子空间，实现了单平台内的中电路测量和快速量子操作，为中性原子容错量子计算提供了可行框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子阵列在量子信息处理中具有可扩展性、长相干时间和强Rydberg相互作用等优势，但物理错误率仍是挑战，且容错量子纠错需要在中路进行测量和重置辅助量子比特而不干扰邻近数据量子比特，这带来了巨大的控制和架构开销，因此量子比特编码成为重要的架构决策。

Method: 利用$^{171}\mathrm{Yb}$原子的两个亚稳态流形：${}^3\mathrm{P}_0$流形提供长相干核自旋量子比特用于存储和算术运算，${}^3\mathrm{P}_2$流形提供超精细分裂$Δ_{\mathrm{HF}} = 2π\times 6.7~\mathrm{GHz}$的超精细自旋量子比特用于快速拉曼操作和直接态选择性成像，通过两个亚稳态流形间的相干转移连接两个量子比特子空间。

Result: 在${}^3\mathrm{P}_2$中模拟了单量子比特和双量子比特门保真度，以及HF和NS量子比特子空间之间的相干转移，并将这些物理层面的估计整合到架构资源估计和逻辑层面仿真中。

Conclusion: 该方法将中电路测量和快速量子比特操作集成在单物种平台内，为未来基于中性原子量子比特的容错量子计算提供了多功能框架。

Abstract: Neutral-atom arrays combine scalable qubit registers, long coherence times, flexible optical control, and strong Rydberg-mediated entangling interactions, making them a promising platform for quantum information processing. However, physical error rates remain a challenge, and fault-tolerant quantum error correction (QEC) requires repeated mid-circuit measurement and reset of ancilla qubits without disturbing nearby data qubits. This requirement introduces significant control and architectural overhead, making qubit encoding an important architectural decision. Here, we propose a dual metastable-state qubit encoding for $^{171}\mathrm{Yb}$ atoms that utilizes two independent qubit subspaces in the $(6s6p)\,{}^3\mathrm{P}_0$ and $(6s6p)\,{}^3\mathrm{P}_2$ manifolds. The ${}^3\mathrm{P}_0$ manifold provides a long-coherence nuclear-spin (NS) qubit suitable for storage and arithmetic operations, while the ${}^3\mathrm{P}_2$ manifold provides a hyperfine-spin (HF) qubit, with $Δ_{\mathrm{HF}} = 2π\times 6.7~\mathrm{GHz}$, that enables fast Raman operations and direct state-selective imaging. Coherent shelving between the two metastable manifolds connects the qubit subspaces, allowing operations to be assigned to spectrally distinct processor zones. We simulate single-qubit and two-qubit gate fidelities in ${}^3\mathrm{P}_2$, as well as coherent shelving between the HF and NS qubit subspaces. We incorporate these physical-level estimates into an architectural resource estimation and logical-level simulation. Our approach integrates mid-circuit measurements and fast qubit operations within a single-species platform, providing a versatile framework for future fault-tolerant quantum computing with neutral-atom qubits.

</details>


### [298] [Impact of spontaneous emission on spin-squeezed quantum sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08478)
*Jinyang Li,Selim M. Shahriar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了原子传感器中自发辐射（SE）对量子传感方案的影响，发现广义回波压缩协议（GESP）比传统回波压缩协议（CESP）更抗SE，且在Sr-88同位素中，SE导致的信号对比度降低与无压缩的Ramsey协议相同，且量子噪声被SE抑制，这是一个出乎意料的有利结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子传感器中，单轴扭转压缩（OATS）操作中的自发辐射（SE）是一个重要的不完美因素，但研究SE极其困难，因为SE会使原子转移到所有塞曼子能级，导致相关集体态数量过大。本文旨在通过研究相对简单的同位素Sr-88来克服这一困难，并评估SE对回波压缩协议的影响。

Method: 聚焦于仅有单个塞曼子能级的Sr-88同位素，通过解析推导和数值模拟，比较广义回波压缩协议（GESP）和传统回波压缩协议（CESP）对自发辐射的鲁棒性，并分析SE对信号对比度和量子噪声的影响。

Result: GESP比CESP更抗SE；对于采用Sr-88的GESP，SE导致的信号对比度降低与无压缩的Ramsey协议相同；量子噪声被SE抑制。

Conclusion: GESP比CESP对自发辐射更具抵抗力，这是GESP一个此前未被认识到的优势。SE对量子噪声的抑制是一个此前未发现的新效应，挑战了先前的结论和直觉预期。

Abstract: The echo squeezing protocols (ESPs) are techniques that amplify the phase shift in a quantum sensor with one-axis-twist squeezing (OATS). For atomic sensors, spontaneous emission (SE) in the OATS operations is an important imperfection, which, however, is prohibitively difficult to study. SE can transfer atoms to all the Zeeman substates, making the number of relevant collective states excessively large. In this paper, we focus on a relatively simple isotope, namely Sr-88, which only has one Zeeman substate in each of the two ground states. Nevertheless, studying the effect of SE is still challenging because SE will populate all collective states, either symmetric or asymmetric, thus putting the ensemble into a mixed state. Based on analytical derivation and numerical simulations, we conclude that the GESP is more resistant to SE than the conventional echo squeezing protocol (CESP). This is another advantage of the GESP that has not been realized formerly. We also find that for the GESP employing Sr-88, the SE-induced reduction in the signal contrast is the same as that for a Ramsey protocol without squeezing and the quantum noise is suppressed by SE. This is an unexpectedly favorable result. The SE-induced suppression of quantum noise constitutes a previously unrecognized effect that challenges both earlier conclusions and intuitive expectations.

</details>


### [299] [Is Exact Markovianity Fundamental Once Time Is Relational?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08595)
*Partha Nandi,Francesco Petruccione*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过有限分辨率量子时钟的关系性框架，将相对论性开放量子动力学与时空的协变性统一，推导出时空超曲面上的协变主方程，证明精确马尔可夫演化只是有效而非基础的，并在锐钟极限下还原为引力退相干方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决开放量子理论中演化依赖外部经典时间参数与相对论物理学中不存在根本性优选时间概念之间的张力。

Method: 采用Schwinger-Tomonaga形式化方法，通过有限分辨率量子时钟建立关系性描述，在时空超曲面上推导协变主方程。

Result: 推导出时空超曲面上的协变主方程；证明即便局域相互作用，约化动力学也普遍是非马尔可夫的；环境关联与时钟涨落共同生成记忆核；精确的GKLS马尔可夫结构只在关系性粗粒化后出现；锐钟极限下还原为Anastopoulos-Hu引力退相干方程。

Conclusion: 精确马尔可夫演化是有效而非基础的；相对论性开放量子动力学本质上是非马尔可夫的，其记忆效应源于环境关联与时钟有限分辨率的联合作用。

Abstract: Markovian open quantum theory assumes evolution with respect to an external classical time parameter, yet no preferred notion of time exists fundamentally in relativistic physics. We resolve this tension by formulating relativistic open quantum dynamics relationally through finite-resolution quantum clocks. Using the Schwinger-Tomonaga formalism, we derive a covariant master equation directly on spacetime hypersurfaces and show that the resulting reduced dynamics is generically non-Markovian even for local interactions. Environmental correlations and clock fluctuations jointly generate the memory kernel, while the Gorini-Kossakowski-Lindblad-Sudarshan (GKLS) structure emerges only after relational coarse-graining, implying that exact Markovian evolution is effective rather than fundamental. In the sharp-clock limit, the formalism reduces to the Anastopoulos-Hu gravitational decoherence equation.

</details>


### [300] [Modified One-Axis-Twist Squeezing for Deterministic Orientation Control of Schrödinger Cat States with Unknown Atom-Number Parity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08482)
*Jinyang Li,Selim M. Shahriar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的一轴扭曲压缩（MOATS）操作，可以生成与原子数奇偶性无关的薛定谔猫态，解决了传统SCSP中因奇偶性未知而无法达到海森堡极限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统薛定谔猫态协议（SCSP）在原子数N较大时，由于N的奇偶性未知，无法确定SC态的取向，导致无法达到海森堡极限灵敏度。作者旨在解决这一奇偶性依赖问题。

Method: 提出了一种改进的一轴扭曲压缩（MOATS）操作，该操作能够生成方向确定的SC态，不依赖于原子数N的奇偶性。

Result: MOATS操作生成的SC态方向固定，消除了对N奇偶性的依赖，使得SCSP在N未知或可变的情况下仍能实现海森堡极限灵敏度。

Conclusion: MOATS方案克服了传统SCSP的奇偶性障碍，使得大原子数系统的量子增强传感变得可行，更具实用价值。

Abstract: The Schrödinger cat state protocol (SCSP) makes use of a Schrödinger cat (SC) state generated with one-axis-twist squeezing (OATS) to enhance the sensitivity. In principle, the SCSP can magnify the phase shift by a factor N, the number of atoms under interrogation, accompanied by a quantum noise amplification by a factor of root-N, enabling an atomic sensor to reach the Heisenberg limit. However, the current SCSP can reach this benchmark only if the parity of N is known, which is almost impossible for a large ensemble. The reason is the orientation of the SC state generated with the conventional OATS depends on the parity of N. In this paper, we propose a modified OATS (MOATS) operation that generates the SC state aligned in a certain direction regardless of the parity of N.

</details>


### [301] [Quantum Advantage over Wirings of Nonsignaling Boxes in Multipartite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08488)
*Peter Bierhorst,Arkaprabha Ghosal,Soumyadip Patra*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在多方量子网络中，即使允许所有参与者共享双向量子非经典资源，量子纠缠测量仍然比基于局部布线的无信号非局域资源测量具有优势，并通过显式的四方行为及一般化到K+2方的论证支持这一结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明纠缠测量能在无需预先共享非局域资源时展现优势，但当允许所有参与者共享双向量子非局域资源时，这一优势是否仍能保持尚未明确。本文旨在从资源理论视角回答这一问题。

Method: 采用资源理论框架，将双向量子非经典资源视为免费共享资源，构造显式的四方行为，将其与仅使用局部布线的无信号非局域盒子所能实现的行为进行对比，并推广到K+2方的一般情形。同时考察了星型网络配置及无全局共享经典随机性的场景。

Result: 明确展示了一种四方行为：使用双向量子资源结合纠缠测量可以达成，而即便允许更一般的无信号非局域盒子并辅以全局共享经典随机性，只要测量限于局部布线，就无法实现。该分离可推广至任意K>2的K+2方情形。

Conclusion: 纠缠测量在多参与者量子网络中具有超越经典布线的根本性优势，这种优势在资源理论视角下依然成立，且具有普适性。

Abstract: Quantum-entangled measurements are known to enable multi-party behaviors that are impossible with unentangled measurements on nonlocal resources, even those that are super-quantum and bound only by the no-signaling principle. This advantage can be witnessed by the entanglement swapping protocol, along with corresponding impossibility results for "nonlocality swapping". However, the advantage assumes the absence of pre-existing nonlocal resources shared by the swapped-to parties; it no longer holds if all pairs of parties are allowed to share bipartite nonlocal resources. Here, we consider a resource-theoretic perspective in which bipartite nonclassical resources are free resources that can be shared by any pair of parties in a multipartite network, and ask whether quantum entangled measurements can still provide an advantage over certain basic measurements, known as wirings, of nonsignaling nonlocal resources. We resolve this question in the affirmative by demonstrating an explicit four-party behavior that can be achieved with bipartite quantum resources subject to entangled measurements, and cannot be achieved if the bipartite resources are allowed to be more general nonsignaling nonlocal "boxes" so long as the measurements are restricted to local wirings, even also allowing for globally shared classical randomness. Furthermore, the argument generalizes: the same separation can be witnessed for K+2 parties with access to K-partite nonlocal resources for any K > 2. We also examine these results in different contexts, such as the star network configurations and scenarios not admitting globally shared classical randomness, further enhancing understanding of the capabilities of entangled measurements in multi-party configurations.

</details>


### [302] [Pure and mixed Dicke state ansatz for equality and inequality constraints in variational quantum eigensolver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08504)
*J. V. S Scursulim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出首个保可行性混合Dicke态量子线路架构，通过密度矩阵形式将汉明重量约束（等式与不等式）直接编码进量子电路，消除惩罚项需求。在投资组合优化问题中，该架构相比受限可行子空间内的随机搜索，在高质量解所需目标函数调用次数上展现出明显优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子算法中，设计能充分探索可行子空间的拟设以及调优惩罚项中的拉格朗日乘子以保障解质量，是两大核心挑战。

Method: 提出混合Dicke态拟设，利用密度矩阵形式结构性地将等式/不等式约束直接编码进量子电路；纯Dicke态拟设作为等式约束的特例被恢复；多约束组通过单个纯/混合Dicke态的张量积推广。采用CMA-ES优化器，在三个复杂度递增的投资组合优化场景中验证。

Result: 随着可行搜索空间增大，提出的拟设在识别高质量解所需目标函数调用次数上显著优于受限随机搜索。IBM NISQ硬件实验表明噪声缓解和电路编译优化仍是实际部署的开放挑战。

Conclusion: 该框架具有通用性，可直接应用于其他汉明重量约束的组合优化问题。

Abstract: Combinatorial optimization can be addressed with quantum computing through variational quantum algorithms, but a central challenge in this approach is to design an ansatz expressive enough to explore the feasible subspace of the Hilbert space where the optimal solution lies. Another major challenge is tuning the Lagrange multipliers in penalty terms to enforce feasibility and guarantee solution quality. To address both challenges, we propose the first feasibility-preserving mixed Dicke state ansatz for Hamming weight constrained combinatorial optimization, extending the density matrix formalism to structurally encode equality and inequality constraints directly into the quantum circuit, thereby eliminating the need for penalty terms in the objective function. The proposed framework handles both constraint types, with the pure Dicke state ansatz recovered as a special case corresponding to equality constraints, and generalizes to multiple constraint groups via tensor products of individual pure or mixed Dicke states. We validate the proposed approach in the context of combinatorial portfolio optimization across three experimental scenarios with increasing constraint complexity, using the CMA-ES optimizer and comparing its performance against random search with replacement restricted to the feasible subspace. As the feasible search space grows, the proposed ansatz demonstrates a clear advantage over random search in terms of the number of objective function calls required to identify high-quality solutions. Hardware experiments on IBM NISQ processors confirm that noise mitigation and circuit transpilation optimizations remain open challenges for practical deployment. The framework is general and directly applicable to other combinatorial optimization problems with Hamming weight constraints.

</details>


### [303] [Cascaded Rydberg antiblockade: Multi-atom excitation dynamics and entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08536)
*Jin-Lei Wu,Jun Wu,Pei-Yao Song,Yan Wang,Ya Gao,Xue-Ke Song,Shi-Lei Su*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出通过Floquet调制在四个全连接相互作用原子中实现级联Rydberg反阻塞（RAB）机制，在集体自旋激发空间中构建了一种新的合成维度——Dicke态晶格（DSL）。通过全局周期性驱动，合成有效哈密顿量，实现了五站点DSL上的完美态转移，具备从逐步最近邻跳跃到单步跃迁的多条可编程路径。该DSL平台还可模拟动态Su-Schrieffer-Heeger模型，通过软量子控制实现拓扑启发的全RAB $|0000\rangle \to |1111\rangle$，并增强对无序的鲁棒性。此外，结合绝热捷径技术，在亚微秒时间尺度内生成了高保真度的纠缠双Fock态和GHZ态，超越了传统绝热协议的速度极限。该工作展示了Rydberg原子阵列中用于量子模拟和多体纠缠工程的灵活可编程合成维度，为量子信息处理的发展铺平了道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Rydberg原子阵列中构建灵活可编程的合成维度平台，以实现高效的量子模拟和多体纠缠工程，克服传统方案在速度和鲁棒性方面的限制。

Method: 利用Floquet调制在四个全连接Rydberg原子中实现级联RAB机制，构建Dicke态晶格（DSL）作为合成维度；通过全局周期性驱动设计有效哈密顿量；结合软量子控制模拟动态SSH模型；引入绝热捷径技术加速纠缠态制备。

Result: 实现了五站点DSL上从逐步最近邻跳跃到单步跃迁的多路径完美态转移；实现了拓扑启发的全RAB态转移并增强了对无序的鲁棒性；在亚微秒时间尺度内生成了高保真度的纠缠双Fock态和GHZ态，速度超越传统绝热协议。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种在Rydberg原子阵列中灵活可编程的合成维度方案，可用于量子模拟和多体纠缠工程，为量子信息处理提供了新途径。

Abstract: We propose a cascaded Rydberg antiblockade (RAB) regime via a Floquet modulation in four fully connected interacting atoms, which establishes a new synthetic dimension, Dicke-state lattice (DSL), in the space of collective spin excitations. By applying a global periodic driving, we synthesize an effective Hamiltonian that enables perfect state transfer across the five-site DSL with multiple programmable pathways from stepwise nearest-neighbor jumps to a single-step transition. This DSL platform further allows us to simulate a dynamic Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model, where soft quantum control is employed to achieve topologically inspired full RAB $|0000\rangle \to |1111\rangle$ with enhanced robustness against disorder. Moreover, by incorporating the shortcut to adiabaticity technique, we generate high-fidelity entangled twin-Fock and Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger states on the four atoms within sub-microsecond timescales, outperforming the speed limits of conventional adiabatic protocols. Our work demonstrates a flexible and programmable synthetic dimension for quantum simulation and multipartite entanglement engineering in Rydberg atom arrays, paving the way for the future development of quantum information processing.

</details>


### [304] [Dissipative Channels Determine Open Electromagnetic Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08557)
*Hyunwoo Choi,Junwoo Gim,Thomas E. Roth,Weng Cho Chew,Dong-Yeop Na*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对任意无源边界条件的开放电磁系统的量子化方案，通过麦克斯韦算子的耗散几何结构识别 reservoirs，将涨落通道分为材料吸收辅助 reservoirs 和开放边界辅助 reservoirs，并推导了格林函数输入-输出关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开放电磁系统量子化方案通常需要现象学地指定 reservoirs，缺乏从系统本身耗散结构出发的统一框架。本文旨在建立一种基于麦克斯韦算子耗散几何的通用量子化方法。

Method: 通过分解麦克斯韦算子的虚部实现场算子的玻色子化，识别出两类涨落通道：材料吸收导致的介质辅助 reservoirs 和开放边界导致的边界辅助 reservoirs。后者根据边界条件可具体化为自由空间辐射模式、阻抗负载通道、导波端口模式等。随后推导格林函数输入-输出关系。

Result: 得到了频率相关的散射核和噪声核，无需马尔可夫或单模假设。在损耗结构（混合阻抗和出射边界）以及光子集成电路结构（波导端口边界）中进行了应用示例。

Conclusion: 该方法为处理具有任意无源边界条件的开放电磁系统提供了一种通用量子化框架，能自然导出非马尔可夫输入-输出关系，适用于复杂光子集成电路等实际场景。

Abstract: We formulate a quantization scheme for open electromagnetic systems with arbitrary passive boundary conditions. Rather than specifying reservoirs phenomenologically, the method identifies them from the dissipation geometry of the Maxwell operator. Factoring the imaginary part of the Maxwell operator gives a bosonic realization of the field operator and separates the fluctuation channels into medium-assisted reservoirs from material absorption and boundary-assisted reservoirs from exchange through the open boundary. Depending on the boundary condition, the latter become free-space radiation modes, impedance-load channels, guided port modes, or more general boundary channels. Green-function input-output relations then follow as an application, yielding frequency-dependent scattering and noise kernels without Markov or single-mode assumptions. To illustrate the practical application, we consider a lossy structure with mixed impedance and outgoing boundaries, and photonic integrated circuit configurations with waveguide port boundaries.

</details>


### [305] [A five-qubit 1-resistant graph state and stabilizer marginal certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08561)
*Zicheng Han,Wanchen Zhang,Xiande Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在稳定子与图态框架下研究抗粒子丢失纠缠，解决了五量子比特1-抗性纯态的存在性问题，证明了五环图态|C₅>即为该态。同时发展了稳定子子群方法用于验证图态的m-抗性，并对五、六、七顶点图态进行了局部Clifford等价分类，发现六量子比特存在三类2-抗性稳定子态，而七量子比特不存在任何非平凡m-抗性稳定子态，且证明N≥7的环图态无m-抗性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子信息处理中，粒子丢失是主要的退相干机制之一，研究在粒子丢失后仍能保持纠缠的量子态对于量子通信和量子计算具有重要的理论意义和应用价值。此前五量子比特1-抗性纯态的存在性问题是尚未解决的最小案例。

Method: 提出了稳定子子群方法：利用局部稳定子证明完全可分性，使用精确负部分转置(NPT)见证者证明纠缠性。将方法应用于五、六、七顶点所有非同构图态，进行了局部Clifford等价分类。

Result: (1) 解决了五量子比特1-抗性纯态的存在性，确认为五环图态|C₅>的局部Clifford类；(2) 六量子比特存在三个不同局部Clifford类的2-抗性稳定子态；(3) 七量子比特不存在任何非零可容许m的m-抗性稳定子态；(4) N≥7的环图态|C_N>无m-抗性。

Conclusion: 完整刻画了小规模图态和稳定子态的m-抗性分类，为抗粒子丢失纠缠态的研究提供了系统方法学框架和关键构造。

Abstract: We study particle-loss resistant entanglement within the framework of stabilizer and graph states. A pure state is \(m\)-resistant if it remains entangled after the loss of any \(m\) particles and becomes fully separable after the loss of any \(m+1\) particles. The smallest previously unresolved qubit case was the existence of a five-qubit \(1\)-resistant pure state, which is resolved here by the five-cycle graph state \(\ket{C_5}\). A stabilizer-subgroup method is also developed for verifying \(m\)-resistance in graph states, using local stabilizers to certify full separability and exact negative partial transpose~(NPT) witnesses to certify entanglement.
  Applying this to all graph states associated with non-isomorphic graphs on five, six, and seven vertices, we obtain a graph state classification up to local Clifford equivalence, which also classifies stabilizer states
  up to local Clifford equivalence. Thus, the five-qubit \(1\)-resistant stabilizer states are exactly the local Clifford class of \(C_5\). Six-qubit \(2\)-resistant stabilizer states exist in three distinct local Clifford classes, whereas no seven-qubit stabilizer state is \(m\)-resistant for any nonzero admissible \(m\). Finally, we prove that the cycle graph states \(\ket{C_N}\) with \(N\ge 7\) are not \(m\)-resistant for any \(0\le m\le N-2\).

</details>


### [306] [Another Legacy of Andrzej Kossakowski: A Self-Contained Derivation of the GKLS Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08579)
*Gen Kimura*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为纪念GKLS方程50周年而作，重构了从马尔可夫性、完全正性、迹保持性和无穷小结构逻辑推导出GKLS生成元的过程，并包含作者对Kossakowski的个人回忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为OSID特刊撰写纪念GKLS方程50周年的文章，旨在从逻辑路径而非历史综述的角度重构GKLS生成元的推导过程，并表达对导师Kossakowski的敬意。

Method: 采用自洽的逻辑重构方式，聚焦于Kossakowski的结构性洞见：将马尔可夫性、完全正性、迹保持性以及量子动力学半群的无穷小结构结合，推导出GKLS生成元的标准形式。

Result: 还原了一条从基本原理到GKLS生成元形式的逻辑路线，并提供了Kossakowski的个人学术回忆片段。

Conclusion: 强调Kossakowski在GKLS方程形式化推导中的关键结构性贡献，而非单纯的历史回顾。

Abstract: This note is written for the special issue of OSID dedicated to the 50th anniversary of the Gorini--Kossakowski--Lindblad--Sudarshan equation. Its purpose is not to give a comprehensive historical review, but rather to reconstruct, in a self-contained way, one logical route leading to the GKLS generator. The emphasis is placed on Kossakowski's structural insight: the combination of Markovianity, complete positivity, trace preservation, and the infinitesimal structure of quantum dynamical semigroups naturally leads to the celebrated form of the generator. I also include a few personal recollections of Andrzej Kossakowski, under whose guidance I spent one year as a postdoctoral researcher in 2003--2004.

</details>


### [307] [Hidden Conformal Boundary Data in Finite-Temperature Stabilizer Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08606)
*Reyhaneh Khasseh,M. A. Rajabpour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了开放临界量子自旋链在有限温度下的稳定子Rényi熵（Rényi指数为1/2），发现该可观测量可表示为热关联矩阵所有平方子式绝对值的和，并严格简化为单个Pfaffian。该Pfaffian具有块Toeplitz-Hankel结构，揭示了不同热区间的标度行为。在温度与系统尺寸成反比的交叉窗口，稳定子熵分解为饱和广延贡献与通用有限尺寸标度函数，该标度函数为level-8 eta商，不同于普通自由边界马约拉纳热因子，在高温度交叉区给出Pauli-弦期望权重谱的Cardy类渐近行为。结果表明有限温度稳定子熵揭示了常规热力学探针无法观测的隐藏缺陷类共形数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索有限温度下临界量子自旋链中稳定子Rényi熵的行为，揭示该可观测量能否提供超越传统热力学量的共形场论信息。

Method: 以横向场伊辛链为例，将稳定子Rényi熵（Rényi指数1/2）表示为热关联矩阵所有平方子式绝对值的和，并通过Pfaffian表示严格简化该指数级求和，进而利用块Toeplitz-Hankel结构分析大尺寸标度行为。

Result: 在逆温度与系统尺寸成正比的交叉窗口，稳定子熵可因子化为饱和广延部分与通用标度函数，该标度函数为level-8 eta商，与普通自由边界马约拉纳热因子不同；低温下该偏差指数级隐藏，高温下控制交叉行为，给出Pauli-弦期望权重谱的Cardy类渐近形式。

Conclusion: 有限温度稳定子熵能够揭示常规热力学探针无法探测的隐藏缺陷类共形数据，是一种新型的量子信息热力学探针。

Abstract: We study the finite-temperature stabilizer Rényi entropy of the open critical quantum spin chains. At Rényi index one half, this observable probes the distribution of thermal Pauli-string expectation values and can be written as a sum over absolute values of all square minors of a finite-temperature correlation matrix for the transverse-field Ising chain. We show that this exponentially large sum is exactly reducible to a single Pfaffian. The Pfaffian representation reveals a block Toeplitz--Hankel structure and allows us to extract the large-size scaling in several thermal regimes. In the crossover window where the inverse temperature is proportional to the system size, the stabilizer entropy factorizes into a saturated extensive contribution and a universal finite-size scaling function. We find that this scaling function is a level-eight eta quotient, rather than the ordinary free-boundary Majorana thermal factor. The deviation is exponentially hidden at low temperature but controls the high-temperature crossover, where it gives a Cardy-like asymptotic for the Pauli-string expectation-weight spectrum. These results show that finite-temperature stabilizer entropy reveals hidden defect-like conformal data invisible to ordinary thermodynamic probes.

</details>


### [308] [Nonlocal Teams and Information Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08645)
*Drishti Baruah,Sachin Teli,Ankur A. Kulkarni*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从随机团队信息结构的角度研究贝尔不等式，通过CHSH博弈及其动态变体，分析经典、投影和量子策略在团队理论解概念下的表现，发现投影策略在标准CHSH中具有重要性质，但在动态版本中不成立，揭示了量子策略中信息结构的微妙相互作用以及某些已知思想在信息结构变化下的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从信息结构的视角重新审视贝尔不等式，探究不同策略类型（经典、投影、量子）在团队理论框架下的行为特性，特别是信息结构变化对已知结果的影响。

Method: 采用CHSH博弈及其动态变体作为分析框架，比较经典策略、投影测量策略和量子策略在团队理论解概念下的表现差异。

Result: 投影策略在标准CHSH博弈中具有重要性质，但这些性质在动态版本的CHSH博弈中不再成立。

Conclusion: 量子策略中信息结构的影响是微妙且复杂的，某些已知的量子信息理论结果对信息结构的变化非常敏感，改变博弈的信息结构可能导致原有性质失效。

Abstract: We look at Bell inequalities from the lens of information structures in stochastic teams. We consider the usual CHSH game and a dynamic variant of the same to study how various classes of strategies, classical, projective and quantum, behave under team theoretic solution concepts. We find that projective strategies (where each player performs projective measurements) enjoy important properties in the usual CHSH game, but they do not carry over to its dynamic version. These results shed light on the delicate interplay of information structure in quantum strategies and the fragility of some well known ideas under changes of information structure.

</details>


### [309] [High Precision Qubit-Efficient Variational Continuous Optimization via Amplitude Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08690)
*Parth Danve*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于单量子比特振幅编码的变分量子优化框架，将连续决策变量编码为单量子比特的测量概率，避免传统二进制离散化所需的对数量子比特开销，并通过振幅估计而非朴素采样来提升连续值恢复精度，从而在标准门模型量子计算机上实现更高效的连续优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统QAOA等变分方法需将连续变量离散化为二进制表示，导致量子比特需求随精度对数增长，且受限于固定分辨率编码。本文旨在消除显式离散化，在标准量子电路模型下实现连续变量的高效编码与优化。

Method: 将每个连续决策变量编码为单量子比特态的振幅平方（测量概率），替代二进制离散化；使用振幅估计（而非朴素采样或断层重建）来读取编码变量；分析振幅估计误差向决策变量误差及目标函数误差的传播规律。

Result: 建立了宽度-精度权衡（width-versus-precision tradeoff），表明该框架以每决策变量恒定1量子比特的代价取代了二进制精度所需的量子比特对数增长，将精度要求转移到估计过程而非量子比特数量。

Conclusion: 该方法为在标准量子比特架构上实现连续优化提供了有前景的新方向，相比传统离散化变分公式具有独特优势。

Abstract: Optimization of continuous-variable objectives on standard gate-based quantum computers via variational algorithms such as QAOA is typically approached by first discretizing each decision variable into a finite binary representation. This increases qubit requirements and restricts solution precision through fixed-resolution encodings. We propose a qubit-efficient variational framework for continuous optimization that instead encodes each decision variable into the squared amplitude or equivalently, the measurement probability of a single qubit state. This removes explicit discretization from the variable representation while remaining entirely within the standard qubit circuit model unlike methods like CV-QAOA employing qumode based hardware to achieve the same. To read out encoded variables, we propose using amplitude estimation rather than naive sampling or tomographic reconstruction, with the goal of improving precision scaling for continuous-value recovery. We outline how amplitude-estimation error propagates to decision-variable error and then to objective-value error under standard regularity assumptions, suggesting a distinct width-versus-precision tradeoff relative to discretized approaches. In particular, the framework replaces the logarithmic increase in qubits needed for finer binary precision with a constant cost of one qubit per decision variable, while shifting accuracy requirements into the estimation procedure. We position this approach relative to traditional discretized variational formulations, and argue that it provides a promising new direction for continuous optimization on standard qubit architectures.

</details>


### [310] [Chiral Quantum Entanglement Transfer with Giant Atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08695)
*Peng-Fei Wang,Lei Huang,Yi-Long-Yue Guo,Jing Wang,Han-Xiao Zhang,Hong Yang,Dong Yan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了多巨原子波导系统中的纠缠转移，通过调控手性自发辐射和暗态动力学，实现了完美的单向顺序或选择性量子态及其纠缠转移，纠缠长度可动态调整，在非马尔可夫条件下仍能保持稳定纠缠，为量子网络中的长距离纠缠传输和鲁棒状态交换提供了可扩展方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多巨原子波导系统中纠缠转移的物理机制，解决传统纠缠传输中的传播延迟和空间限制问题，实现可扩展的连续长距离纠缠传输。

Method: 利用手性自发辐射调控和暗态动力学，通过周期性分段函数调制附加相位，在非马尔可夫条件下实现巨原子间的稳定状态交换和纠缠保持。

Result: 实现了完美的单向顺序或选择性纠缠转移；纠缠长度可动态调整，支持长短程纠缠的鲁棒转换；系统自然收敛到暗态保证高保真度传输；在非马尔可夫条件下仍能保持稳定纠缠和近乎无损耗的状态交换。

Conclusion: 该方案为量子网络中连续长距离纠缠传输和鲁棒状态交换提供了可扩展路径，克服了传统编织架构的传播延迟和空间限制问题。

Abstract: We investigate entanglement transfer in a multi-giant-atom waveguide system. By tailoring chiral spontaneous emission and exploiting dark-state dynamics, the setup enables perfect, unidirectional sequential or selective transfer of quantum states and their associated entanglement. The distance between two entangled atoms, i.e., the entanglement length, can be dynamically adjusted, allowing robust conversion between long-range and short-range entanglement during propagation. The system inherently converges to a dark state, guaranteeing high-fidelity directional transfer. When the additional phase is modulated as a periodic piecewise function, spatially separated giant atoms exhibit stable, nearly lossless state exchange and maintain steady entanglement even under non-Markovian conditions. This behavior mimics conventional braided architectures without suffering from propagation delays or spatial restrictions. Our proposal offers a scalable pathway for continuous long-distance entanglement transport and resilient state exchange in quantum networks.

</details>


### [311] [Regularised Arbitrary Gauge non-Relativistic QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08704)
*Alex Chivers-White,Adam Stokes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发展了带正则化的任意规范非相对论量子电动力学形式体系，比较了Coulomb规范与多极规范（含Lorentz形状因子），分析了正则化对微扰论、哈密顿量划分及电偶极近似极限的影响。正则化多极规范在相互作用强度与物质局域性之间存在截断依赖的权衡，抑制了直接原子间相互作用，并对Dicke临界性等短程现象具有重要含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非相对论QED在处理短程现象时存在规范依赖和发散问题，需要建立正则化框架来统一比较不同规范描述，并理解截断效应对物理结果的影响。

Method: 发展了含Lorentz形状因子的正则化任意规范非相对论QED形式体系；对比了Coulomb规范与多极规范；通过微扰论分析了哈密顿量不同划分方式和电偶极近似极限。

Result: 正则化多极规范下，相互作用强度与物质子系统局域性之间存在截断依赖的权衡关系，直接原子间相互作用被抑制。

Conclusion: 该正则化框架对Dicke临界性等短程现象有重要理论启示，为规范依赖问题的处理提供了系统方案。

Abstract: We develop a regularised arbitrary-gauge formulation of nonrelativistic quantum electrodynamics and use it to compare Coulomb and multipolar descriptions with a Lorentzian form factor. We analyse the effect of regularisation in perturbation theory, including alternative partitions of the Hamiltonian into free and interaction parts, and the limits of the electric dipole approximation. The regularised multipolar gauge exhibits a cut-off-dependent trade-off between the strength of individual interaction terms and the localisation of material subsystems that suppresses direct inter-atomic interactions. We discuss the implications of the framework for short-range phenomena, including Dicke criticality.

</details>


### [312] [Simulating quantum circuits with a neural statebank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08707)
*Taige Wang,Liang Fu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出神经状态库（neural statebank），利用自回归Transformer检查点沿电路轨迹学习量子波函数，以紧凑的神经网络表示实现高保真度模拟，在34量子比特电路中以30万参数达到约10^{-2}的保真度损失，性能优于其他近似模拟器且内存占用远少于精确态矢量演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证量子处理器的核心瓶颈在于预测量子电路的输出，因为通用波函数随系统规模呈指数增长，现有方法在计算资源和精度之间难以取得平衡。

Method: 提出神经状态库，每一层存储为从上一检查点经局部门更新训练得到的自回归Transformer检查点，形成紧凑的神经网络表示，能够评估振幅并生成独立样本。

Result: 在34量子比特的长程电路中，30万参数的状态库达到约10^{-2}的保真度损失，优于其他测试的近似模拟器，同时内存使用远少于精确态矢量演化。该架构还能准确模拟量子近似优化、Clifford+T和Clifford电路。

Conclusion: 神经状态库以极少的参数实现了对大规模量子电路的高效近似模拟，在保真度和内存效率上优于现有方法，为量子电路验证提供了新的可行途径。

Abstract: Predicting the output of quantum circuits is a central bottleneck for verifying quantum processors because a generic wavefunction grows exponentially with system size. We introduce a neural statebank that learns this wavefunction along the circuit trajectory. Each layer is stored as an autoregressive Transformer checkpoint trained from local gate updates to the preceding checkpoint, producing a compact neural representation that can evaluate amplitudes and generate independent samples. On long-range circuits combining entanglement, magic, and non-diagonal branching, a 0.3-million-parameter statebank reaches $\sim 10^{-2}$ infidelity at 34 qubits, outperforming the other tested approximate simulators while using far less memory than exact state-vector evolution. The same architecture accurately simulates quantum approximate optimization, Clifford+$T$, and Clifford circuits.

</details>


### [313] [Quantum resource localizability transitions in deep thermalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08756)
*Xiaozhou Feng,Chang Liu,Zihan Cheng,Wen Wei Ho,Matteo Ippoliti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子资源约束如何影响深度热化现象，提出了一个统一框架将量子资源理论分为两类：平滑局域化(SL)和阈值局域化(TL)。SL类中局部资源随全局资源密度连续变化，TL类中则存在临界阈值导致局部资源从最小突变为接近最大。该理论由块锐化机制解释，并在零率量子纠错码中发现新的魔法相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子资源约束（如非稳定子性、相干性、不对称性、虚数性、非高斯性等）对深度热化现象的影响，理解全局量子资源丰度如何重塑从部分测量中涌现的局域波函数分布。

Method: 开发了基于广义量子资源理论的深度热化统一框架，通过将每种QRT视为希尔伯特空间中块之间的相干性，引入"块锐化"信息论机制，将QRT分为SL和TL两类进行解析分析。

Result: 发现QRT可分为两类：SL类（局部资源连续变化）和TL类（存在临界阈值导致突变的深度遍历破缺相变）。理论定量预测了所有研究QRT的相边界，并通过大量数值模拟验证。在零率量子纠错码中发现新的魔法相变，并对量子资源认证协议产生新启示。

Conclusion: 量子资源约束会对深度热化产生的局域波函数分布产生根本性影响，SL-TL二分法源于块锐化机制。该发现挑战了零率量子纠错码中魔法相变仅发生在有限率的传统认知，并为基于测量后态系综的量子资源认证提供了新方向。

Abstract: We investigate how quantum resource constraints affect deep thermalization, the emergence of universal local wavefunction distributions from partial measurements of a quantum many-body state. Quantum resources, such as non-stabilizerness (magic), coherence, asymmetry, imaginarity, and non-Gaussianity, are essential for quantum information processing, and constraints on their global abundance can reshape these emergent distributions. To address this question, we develop a unified framework for deep thermalization within general quantum resource theories (QRTs). Our central result is that QRTs fall into two classes: ``smoothly localizable'' (SL) QRTs, where the resource content of local post-measurement states changes continuously with the global resource density, set by the initial state and measurement basis, yielding continuously tunable wavefunction distributions; and ``threshold localizable'' (TL) QRTs, where the local resource content jumps discontinuously from minimal to near-maximal past a critical global resource threshold, producing a sharp transition between a resourceless, ``deep-ergodicity breaking'' distribution and a resourceful, maximally random one. We trace this SL-TL dichotomy to an information-theoretic mechanism, block sharpening: by viewing each QRT as coherence between blocks in Hilbert space, we show that the local resource content depends on the measurement's ability to collapse an initial superposition into a single resourceless block. Our theory is analytically tractable and quantitatively predicts the phase boundaries across all studied QRTs, which we validate with extensive numerical simulations. Finally, we highlight two consequences: a novel magic transition in zero-rate quantum error-correcting codes--previously believed to occur only at finite rates--and new implications for quantum resource certification protocols based on post-measurement state ensembles.

</details>


### [314] [Neural network decoder confidence as a learned proxy for the logical gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08758)
*David Dentelski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文测试了图神经网络（GNN）解码器的软输出能否作为最小权重完美匹配（MWPM）逻辑间隙的代理。在旋转表面码电路级噪声下，基于GNN logit的后选择比基于MWPM间隙的后选择实现了更低的逻辑错误率，且GNN置信度更接近理想后验对数似然比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错码解码器在输出硬判决的同时，还需要提供软信息来估计判决的可靠性。MWPM解码器的常用置信度指标是互补间隙（逻辑间隙），但该指标在某些场景下不可用、计算成本高或与噪声模型不匹配，因此需要寻找替代方案。

Method: 使用预训练的GNN解码器（在旋转表面码均匀电路级噪声下训练），将其软输出（logit）与MWPM互补间隙在相同采样症候群上进行对比。通过后选择实验比较两者的逻辑错误率，并分析置信度分布的相似性与差异。

Result: 基于GNN logit的后选择产生的逻辑错误率低于基于MWPM间隙的后选择。逐样本分析显示，GNN置信度在低值和中间值区域与MWPM间隙的符号分布相似，但对许多正确解码的样本赋予更高置信度。两种分数都近似后验对数似然比，但GNN置信度幅度更接近理想值。

Conclusion: 仅基于症候群和逻辑标签训练的神经网络解码器不仅学会了类似间隙的判别能力，还学会了定量的置信度标度。在MWPM间隙估计不可用、成本高昂或与噪声模型不匹配时，GNN置信度可有效用于基于置信度的后选择。

Abstract: To utilize quantum error-correcting codes, a decoder must infer the logical sector from the measured syndrome. Beyond producing a hard logical decision, some decoders provide soft information that estimates the reliability of that decision. For minimum-weight perfect matching (MWPM), a common confidence measure is the complementary, or logical, gap. Here we test whether the logit of a graph neural network (GNN) decoder can act as a learned proxy for the logical gap. Using a pretrained GNN for the rotated surface code under uniform circuit-level noise [Physical Review Research, 7(2):023181, 2025], we compare its soft output with the MWPM complementary gap on the same sampled syndromes. We find that post-selection based on the GNN logit yields a lower logical error rate than one based on the MWPM gap. Shot-by-shot, the signed GNN confidence distribution resembles the signed MWPM gap at low and intermediate values, but assigns higher confidence to many correctly decoded shots. While both scores approximate the posterior log-likelihood ratio, the GNN confidence magnitude is closer to its ideal value. These results show that a neural-network decoder trained only on syndromes and logical labels learns both gap-like discrimination and a quantitative confidence scale, enabling confidence-based post-selection when MWPM gap estimates are unavailable, costly, or poorly matched to the noise model.

</details>


### [315] [Algebra of Bivariate-Bicycle Surface Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08771)
*Renyu Wang,Leonid P. Pryadko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了BBS码（由有限域上双变量多项式对构造）的性质与其公共根在扩域中的数量和位置之间的关系。根的代数重数决定码的维数，该维数在Laurent多项式环的单射自同构下保持不变。零或无穷坐标的根对应边界处的特殊生成元需求，且可在单射变换下出现或消失，揭示了倾斜边界的结构。基于此，提出了一种适用于矩形、对角线和任意倾斜边界的BBS码构造方法，其关键优势在于满足特定方向边缘条件时无需角点修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立BBS码的性质与双变量多项式公共根在扩域中位置及数量之间的内在联系，并基于此开发一种无需角点修正、适用于多种边界形状（矩形、对角线、任意倾斜）的通用BBS码构造方法。

Method: 通过分析有限域上双变量多项式对的公共根在扩域中的分布（有限非零坐标根的代数重数决定维数，零/无穷坐标根对应边界特殊生成元），研究其在单射自同构下的不变性及变换行为，从而制定满足特定方向边缘条件的BBS码构造规则。

Result: 建立了码维数与根的代数重数之间的确定关系；证明了维数在Laurent多项式环单射自同构下的不变性；揭示了零/无穷坐标根对应边界结构及其在单射变换下的动态行为；提出了适用于矩形、对角线和任意倾斜边界的BBS码构造方案，且该方案无需角点修正。

Conclusion: 成功建立了BBS码的代数结构与其多项式公共根几何分布之间的深刻联系，并据此开发了一种灵活、无需角点修正的BBS码构造框架，可适应多种边界形状，显著简化了码的设计流程。

Abstract: We relate the properties of bivariate-bicycle-surface (BBS) codes, constructed from a pair of bivariate polynomials over a finite field, to the number and location of their common roots in the extension field. The number of roots $(x,y)$ with finite, non-zero coordinates -- counted with algebraic multiplicity -- determines the dimension of the codes. This dimension is invariant under monomial automorphisms of the Laurent polynomial ring. Conversely, roots with zero or infinite $x$- or $y$-coordinates indicate that specialized generators are required near the corresponding boundary (e.g., the left or right boundary for a root where $x$ is zero or infinite, respectively). These roots can appear or disappear under monomial transformations, which reveals the structure of tilted boundaries. Based on these results, we formulate a prescription for constructing BBS codes that works for regions with rectangular, diagonal, and arbitrarily tilted boundaries. A key advantage of this approach is that no corner corrections are needed, provided the polynomials satisfy orientation-specific edge conditions.

</details>


### [316] [Randomized simulation of quantum channels using small ancilla](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08784)
*Marcin Kotowski,Michał Kotowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究利用小尺寸辅助系统（ancilla）、经典随机化和输出失效标志的后选择来概率性地精确模拟量子信道。证明了任意d维系统上的幺正信道可以用辅助维度k和成功概率Ω(k/log d)模拟；对于n量子比特系统，仅需O(log n)个辅助量子比特即可达到常数成功概率。同时证明了该权衡最优，并发现高度非交换信道（含随机信道）仅需单个辅助量子比特即可实现常数成功概率模拟。此外，强非幺正信道在该模型下必然失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何用尽可能小的辅助资源（尤其是辅助维度）来模拟一般量子信道，在量子计算和量子信息处理中，资源优化是核心问题之一。现有模拟方法往往需要较大辅助空间，本文旨在量化辅助维度与成功概率之间的最优权衡。

Method: 采用基于划分的协议（partition-based protocol）和矩阵浓度不等式，特别是Bandeira、Boedihardjo和van Handel近期改进的非交换Khintchine不等式，对幺正信道进行概率性精确模拟分析。通过构造信道族证明下界最优性，并区分高度非交换信道与强非幺正信道的不同表现。

Result: 幺正信道可用辅助维度k和成功概率Ω(k/log d)模拟；n量子比特系统仅需O(log n)辅助量子比特即可常数概率成功；该权衡最优；高度非交换信道（如随机信道）仅需单辅助量子比特即可常数成功概率；强非幺正信道在该模型下必然失败。

Conclusion: 建立了量子信道概率性精确模拟中辅助维度与成功概率之间的最优权衡，揭示了不同信道类别在该模拟模型下的本质差异，并为未来研究（如引入自适应扩展）提供了方向。

Abstract: We study the problem of implementing a quantum channel using a small ancilla with classical randomization and postselection on an output failure flag. The simulation is probabilistic, but exact conditioned on success. We prove that any unital channel on a $d$-dimensional system can be simulated with ancilla dimension $k$ and success probability $Ω(\frac{k}{\log d})$. Equivalently, every unital channel on $n$ qubits can be simulated with constant success probability using only $O(\log n)$ ancillary qubits. We show that this tradeoff is best possible by constructing a family of channels which cannot be simulated with success probability better than $O(\frac{k}{\log d})$. We also show that the class of \textit{highly noncommutative} channels, which includes random channels, admits constant-success simulation with just a single ancillary qubit. We further show that this model of simulation necessarily fails for strongly non-unital channels and discuss possible extensions involving adaptivity. On the technical side we rely on a partition-based protocol and matrix concentration inequalities, including the recent refinement of noncommutative Khintchine inequalities due to Bandeira, Boedihardjo and van Handel.

</details>


### [317] [Graph Neural Networks for Fast Operator Selection in Adaptive VQE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08794)
*Javad Vahedi,Hadi H. Arefi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出GNN-VQE方法，用图神经网络替代ADAPT-VQE中的全池梯度扫描来预测下一个纠缠算子，在保持能量精度的同时大幅降低经典计算开销，并展现出向分子体系迁移的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ADAPT-VQE虽能构建紧凑量子电路，但每次迭代需扫描整个算子池，经典计算成本与池大小线性增长，对于长程相互作用或大算子集系统成为瓶颈。

Method: 将自适应算子选择建模为基于图的决策问题，训练图神经网络从相互作用图和状态相关可观测量中直接预测下一个纠缠算子。训练数据来自无序长程自旋链的精确模拟，以梯度幅度作为监督信号。进一步将GNN作为候选算子生成器，仅对少量GNN推荐候选进行精确重评分。

Result: GNN策略准确复现了贪婪梯度选择规则的主导结构，显著优于仅基于相互作用强度的启发式方法。GNN-VQE达到接近标准ADAPT-VQE的能量误差，同时大幅减少全池梯度评估次数。在LiH和BeH₂分子测试中，对GNN少量候选进行精确重评分可恢复近最优行为，仅搜索了池中极小部分。

Conclusion: 自适应电路构建中存在可学习的结构，可通过图神经网络加以利用以加速变分量子算法，为提升VQE实际可扩展性提供了新途径。

Abstract: Adaptive variational quantum algorithms like ADAPT-VQE construct tailored ansätze by iteratively selecting operators from a pool using gradient-based criteria. While this avoids oversized parameter spaces, repeatedly scanning the full pool incurs a classical cost that scales linearly with pool size-a major bottleneck for systems with long-range interactions or large operator sets. Here, we reformulate adaptive operator selection as a graph-based decision problem and introduce a graph neural network (GNN) policy that predicts the next entangling operator directly from the interaction graph and state-dependent observables. Training data are generated from exact simulations of disordered long-range spin chains, using gradient magnitudes as supervision signals. The learned policy accurately reproduces the dominant structure of the greedy gradient-based selection rule, significantly outperforming heuristics based solely on interaction strength. Integrated into a variational quantum eigensolver (VQE) workflow, this GNN-VQE approach achieves energy errors close to standard ADAPT-VQE while drastically reducing full-pool gradient evaluations. To test transferability beyond spin models, we evaluate the policy on small active-space molecular benchmarks (LiH and BeH_$2$). We find the GNN is highly effective as a shortlist generator: exact rescoring over just a few GNN-proposed candidates recovers near-oracle rollout behavior while searching only a small fraction of the pool. These results demonstrate that adaptive circuit construction contains learnable structure that can be exploited to accelerate variational quantum algorithms.

</details>


### [318] [Energy-Efficient Satellite Wake-Up via Bosonic Identification: The Role of Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08845)
*Gökhan Elmas,Janis Nötzel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本工作在卫星通信场景下研究显式同步约束对确定性识别的影响，发现识别性能与同步精度之间存在根本性权衡，同步能量需求远超识别信号，并引出了玻色子码在抖动下的纠错能力研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知信息论最优识别码通常假设完美同步，但在实际低接收能量场景（如卫星通信）中同步约束不可忽略，需研究同步对识别性能的影响。

Method: 采用确定性识别框架，假设相位编码相干态时钟方案（离散时间索引由单位圆上的等距相位旋转表示），在显式同步约束下分析卫星广播场景——卫星向多个地面站广播特定用户设备签名以建立通信。

Result: 发现根本性不对称：任意发射功率下，识别性能随块长增加而提升，但同步精度随之下降；卫星时钟到地面站的传输能量可能比识别信号高出数个数量级。

Conclusion: 同步精度与识别性能存在根本性不对称：增加块长可提升识别性能，但会降低同步精度；卫星时钟同步所需能量可能比识别信号高出数个数量级，表明同步严重制约识别性能，并引发了对抖动下玻色子码纠错能力的研究动机。

Abstract: The information-theoretic concept of identification describes a sender-receiver architecture in which the receiver only checks whether a particular message was sent or not, thereby promising a low-energy receiver design. In low received-energy regimes, quantum receivers are a promising tool for studying the system limits. However, the known information-theoretically optimal identification codes typically assume perfect synchronization. In this work, we study deterministic identification in a satellite setting under explicit synchronization constraints, where a satellite broadcasts the signature of a specific User Equipment (UE) which it assumes to be attached to one out of several possible Ground Station (GS), with the goal of establishing communication with the target UE. Within the proposed design, and assuming a specific phase-encoded coherent-state clock scheme in which the discrete time index is represented by equidistant phase rotations on the unit circle, our results reveal a fundamental asymmetry: At any transmission power, identification performance improves with blocklength, whereas synchronization accuracy degrades. In particular, the energy needed for transmitting the satellite clock to the GS can be several orders of magnitude higher than the one needed for the identification signal. This indicates that synchronization strongly impacts identification performance and motivates the investigation of the error-correcting capabilities of bosonic codes under jitter.

</details>


### [319] [SCOPE: A Syndrome-Driven Control Plane for QEC-Enabled Quantum Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08873)
*Xiaojie Fan,Zian Wang,Ashutosh Tiwari,Himanshu Gupta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: SCOPE是一种基于被动遥测（利用QEC解码器自然产生的错误综合征）的网络层架构，通过联合路由和编码优化，在量子网络中降低逻辑错误率30-65%，相比传统主动层析成像方法减少60%以上的估计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子网络从实验测试台向容错系统演进时，控制平面仍依赖拓扑或标量保真度指标进行路由决策，未能与量子纠错策略耦合，且主动层析成像因吞吐量崩溃和服务中断而不可行。

Method: 提出SCOPE架构，包含：(1) 被动遥测模块，从QEC解码器自然产生的错误综合征中提取信息；(2) 推理引擎，聚合信号重建时变误差图；(3) 决策引擎，主动推送最优路由和编码配置到源节点。

Result: NetSquid和IBM校准仿真显示：估计误差相比EM基线降低60%以上；在大规模网络中，逻辑错误率相比拓扑感知基线降低30-35%（最高达65%）。

Conclusion: SCOPE证明了利用被动遥测实现联合路由与编码优化的可行性，为量子网络控制平面从物理层保真度向端到端逻辑错误率的性能范式转变提供了有效方案。

Abstract: As quantum networks evolve from experimental testbeds to fault-tolerant systems, the primary performance metric shifts from physical link fidelity to end-to-end logical error rate. However, current control planes remain ill-equipped for this transition: routing decisions are typically decoupled from Quantum Error Correction (QEC) strategies, relying on topology or scalar fidelity metrics that fail to predict how specific physical noise structures interact with logical codes. Optimizing this coupled route-and-code performance requires precise, real-time visibility into network error biases, yet traditional active tomography is operationally prohibitive due to throughput collapse and service interruption.
  We present SCOPE (Syndrome-based COntrol PlanE), a network-layer architecture that enables joint routing and coding optimization using purely passive telemetry. Instead of injecting probes, SCOPE harvests error syndromes -- the parity-check outcomes naturally generated by QEC decoders during user service. By aggregating these signals, SCOPE's inference engine reconstructs the network's time-varying error map, capturing complex, context-dependent noise correlations. This visibility drives a decision engine that proactively pushes optimal route-and-code configurations to source nodes. NetSquid and IBM-calibrated simulations show that SCOPE reduces estimation error by more than 60% relative to a standard EM baseline. In large-scale networks, this precision reduces logical error rates by 30-35% (up to 65%) against topology-aware baselines.

</details>


### [320] [Impact of gate-voltage noise on silicon spin-qubit variational quantum eigensolvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08874)
*Xinning Wang,Bohdan Khromets,Zachery Merino,Jonathan Baugh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种硅量子点处理器的硬件-算法协同仿真框架，将3D静电学与有效g因子和交换耦合联系起来，并通过VQE用于H₂基态能量估计作为测试平台，研究了静态电压校准误差和随机电报噪声对量子门保真度和算法精度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅自旋量子比特受硬件缺陷（电荷噪声引起的势涨落和栅极电压静态失校准）限制，导致量子门保真度和算法精度下降，亟需理解这些噪声对混合量子算法性能的影响。

Method: 构建硬件-算法协同仿真框架，将3D静电学模拟与有效g因子和交换耦合计算相结合，通过真实控制脉冲传播电压级噪声；在门级使用量子过程层析成像和Kraus算子分析区分相干与非相干误差；在电路级以VQE用于H₂基态能量估计为测试平台，研究静态缩放/偏移误差和随机电报噪声的影响。

Result: 基于交换的双量子比特门对这些噪声的敏感度比ESR驱动的单量子比特旋转高约一个数量级；识别出与化学精度能量估计兼容的失校准强度和噪声切换时间区间；讨论了基于含噪能量估计完整分布的统计后处理方法。

Conclusion: 该协同仿真框架能够量化硅量子点处理器的硬件噪声对VQE算法性能的影响，并指出通过统计后处理可进一步提高准确性，为近量子硅基量子计算平台的噪声缓解策略提供了指导。

Abstract: Quantum computers offer a route to outperform classical methods in tasks such as molecular simulation, motivating hybrid algorithms like the Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) for near-term devices. Silicon spin qubits are a promising platform for scalable quantum computation, but their performance is limited by hardware imperfections -- most notably charge-noise-induced potential fluctuations and static miscalibration of gate-electrode voltages -- which degrade quantum gate fidelities and, ultimately, algorithmic accuracy. Here we develop a hardware-algorithm co-simulation framework for silicon quantum-dot processors that links 3D electrostatics to effective $g$-factors and exchange couplings, and propagates voltage-level noise through realistic control pulses. Using VQE for $\mathrm{H}_2$ ground-state energy estimation as a circuit-level testbed, we study both static scaling/offset errors on the gate-electrode voltages and stochastic fluctuations modeled as random-telegraph noise with tunable amplitudes and switching times. At the gate level, we show that exchange-based two-qubit gates are roughly an order of magnitude more sensitive to these types of noise than ESR-driven single-qubit rotations. Quantum process tomography and Kraus-operator analysis further distinguish coherent and incoherent contributions and quantify the fraction of error that is, in principle, correctable by a compensating unitary. Embedding these noise models into the VQE circuit, we identify regimes of miscalibration strength and noise switching time compatible with chemically accurate energy estimates, and discuss how statistical post-processing based on the full distribution of noisy energy estimates could further improve accuracy.

</details>


### [321] [Impact of the Unruh effect on the estimation precision of Gaussian channel parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08905)
*Shoukang Chang,Yawen Tang,Wei Ye,Shao-Ming Fei,Zunlue Zhu,Xingdong Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Unruh效应对高斯通道（热衰减器和热放大器）参数估计精度的影响，发现Unruh效应显著降低了单参数和双参数估计的精度，并指出外差测量在高加速度或大热平均数下接近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往高斯通道参数估计研究主要关注惯性系，非惯性系（尤其是Unruh效应）下的情形尚未被探索。

Method: 分析了Unruh效应对热衰减器和热放大器两种高斯通道参数估计精度的影响，分别考虑了输入相干态和压缩真空态，并利用量子Cramér-Rao界分析双参数估计的精度极限。

Result: Unruh效应对相干态和压缩真空态下的单参数估计精度均有显著降低；双参数估计中量子Cramér-Rao界是渐近可达的精度极限，且Unruh效应同样产生负面影响；外差测量在高加速度或大热平均数下接近最优。

Conclusion: 为相对论背景下量子参数估计提供了重要的理论见解和实践指导。

Abstract: Gaussian quantum channels constitute a pivotal physical framework for characterizing the dynamics of Gaussian quantum states. Extensive scholarly attention has been devoted to the estimation of parameters associated with Gaussian channels. However, while previous research has predominantly focused on parameter estimation within inertial frames, the noninertial scenario, particularly in the context of the Unruh effect, remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we analyze the impact of the Unruh effect on the estimation precision of Gaussian channel parameters, with a specific focus on thermal attenuator and thermal amplifier channels. Our findings reveal that the Unruh effect significantly degrades the precision of single-parameter estimation for Gaussian channel parameters when employing both the input coherent state and squeezed vacuum state. For the two-parameter estimation, we further demonstrate that the quantum Cramér-Rao bound serves as an asymptotically achievable precision limit. Consistent with the single-parameter case, the Unruh effect exerts a detrimental impact on the precision of two-parameter estimation. Notably, heterodyne measurement is near-optimal for both single- and two-parameter estimation in the limit of high acceleration or large thermal mean numbers. These results provide crucial theoretical insights and practical guidance for advancing quantum parameter estimation in a relativistic context.

</details>


### [322] [Quantum Mechanical Studies of Photodissociation Dynamics on Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08929)
*Zikun Zhuang,Chengdong Yang,Yuchen Wang,Dong H. Zhang,Bin Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在量子计算机上研究光解离动力学的量子算法，以NOCl分子为基准测试对象。该算法利用分步算符法、量子傅里叶变换和酉变换矩阵进行波函数传播，通过膨胀方案实现非酉吸收势传播子以施加出射边界条件，并使用Hadamard测试提取自相关函数计算光解离截面。结果表明，在理想条件下量子计算结果与基准一致，且算法对噪声和统计采样误差具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论量子动力学计算随系统规模呈深度缩放，经典计算难以处理复杂体系。虽然量子计算提供了自然解决方案，但其在核量子动力学中的应用仍然稀少，因此需要开发适用于量子计算机的核量子动力学算法。

Method: 采用分步算符法传播波函数，利用量子傅里叶变换(QFT)和酉变换矩阵在不同表示之间切换；通过膨胀方案实现非酉吸收势传播子，以在截断网格上施加出射边界条件；使用Hadamard测试从量子线路中提取自相关函数，进而计算光解离截面。

Result: 在理想条件下，量子计算结果与基准值吻合良好；该算法对噪声和统计采样误差具有鲁棒性，表明在有噪声的中等规模量子(NISQ)设备上具有应用潜力。

Conclusion: 所提出的量子算法能够有效模拟光解离动力学，且对噪声鲁棒，证明了量子计算在核量子动力学研究中的可行性，为在近期量子设备上开展复杂量子动力学研究铺平了道路。

Abstract: Theoretical quantum dynamics calculations scale deeply with system size, rendering classical calculations intractable for complex systems. While quantum computing offers a natural solution, its application to nuclear quantum dynamics remains scarce. Here, we present a quantum algorithm to study photodissociation dynamics on quantum computers, benchmarked on the NOCl molecule. The wavefunction is propagated via a split-operator method, utilizing the Quantum Fourier Transform and unitary transformation matrix to switch representations. To impose outgoing boundary conditions on a truncated grid, we use a non-unitary absorbing potential propagator, implemented through a dilation scheme. The photodissociation cross section is calculated from the auto-correlation function, which is extracted using the Hadamard test. Our quantum computing results agree well with benchmarks under ideal conditions, and we further demonstrate that the algorithm is robust to noise and statistical sampling errors, indicating the promising application of noisy devices to quantum dynamics studies.

</details>


### [323] [Controlling multiparameter quantum estimation in exciton-optomechanics system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08949)
*Hamza Harraf,Mohamed Amazioug,Rachid Ahl Laamara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究混合激子-光力机械（EOM）系统中的多参数量子估计，通过对称对数导数（SLD）和右对数导数（RLD）方法计算量子Fisher信息矩阵，分析激子-光子耦合强度与激子衰减率的联合估计精度，发现强耦合与低温可显著提升估计性能，外差探测可接近最优量子精度极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数量子估计是量子计量学的核心任务，本文旨在探索混合激子-光力机械系统中多参数联合估计的量子极限及影响因素。

Method: 采用高斯态形式化方法推导稳态量子涨落的协方差矩阵，利用SLD和RLD两种方法计算量子Fisher信息矩阵，分析量子Cramér-Rao界，并比较外差与零差探测方案的性能。

Result: 强混合相互作用与低温可显著提升估计精度；热涨落与耗散过程会劣化计量性能；外差探测优于零差探测，在合适参数下可接近最优量子精度极限。

Conclusion: 证实了混合EOM系统在多参数量子估计中的潜力，外差探测是逼近量子极限的有效实验方案。

Abstract: Multiparameter quantum estimation has emerged as a central task in quantum metrology. In this work, we investigate multiparameter quantum estimation in a hybrid exciton--optomechanical (EOM) system. The system consists of a semiconductor quantum well embedded inside a driven optomechanical microcavity, where the excitonic, optical, and mechanical modes interact coherently through exciton--photon and radiation-pressure couplings. Using the Gaussian-state formalism, we derive the covariance matrix of the steady-state quantum fluctuations and employ both the symmetric logarithmic derivative (SLD) and right logarithmic derivative (RLD) approaches to evaluate the quantum Fisher information matrix associated with the simultaneous estimation of the exciton--photon coupling strength $g$ and the excitonic decay rate $k_x$. We analyze the corresponding quantum Cramér--Rao bounds and determine the most informative precision limit governing the attainable estimation accuracy. The influence of several experimentally relevant parameters, including temperature, driving power, optomechanical coupling strength, and dissipation rates, is investigated in detail. Our results show that strong hybrid interactions and low-temperature regimes significantly enhance the estimation precision, whereas thermal fluctuations and dissipation processes deteriorate the metrological performance. Furthermore, we compare the ultimate quantum limits with experimentally feasible Gaussian measurement strategies based on homodyne and heterodyne detection. We show that heterodyne detection provides better estimation performance than homodyne schemes and can approach the optimal quantum precision limit in suitable parameter regimes.

</details>


### [324] [Relativistic Effects in Spin Correlations Induced by QED Scattering and Wigner Rotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08995)
*Juan D. Fonseca,B. Hiller,I. G. da Paz,M. Sampaio*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Møller散射及扩展过程中电子间自旋关联的相对论性起源，发现非相对论近似下偶极-偶极和电流-偶极相互作用产生关联，并通过von Neumann熵变化和Wigner旋转中的量子相干性加以验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Møller散射中电子自旋关联的相对论性本质，并在Wigner旋转框架下理解其产生机制，同时解释非弹性过程$e^{-}e^{+}\rightarrowμ^{-}μ^{+}$中已有研究的分歧。

Method: 在质心系和洛伦兹助推系中分析$e^{-}e^{-}\rightarrow e^{-}e^{-}$和$e^{-}e^{-}C\rightarrow e^{-}e^{-}C$过程的散射振幅，通过非相对论近似提取有效相互作用，利用von Neumann熵和Wigner旋转下的量子相干性表征自旋关联。

Result: 偶极-偶极和电流-偶极相互作用是产生自旋关联的根源；Wigner旋转下熵在局域幺正变换下保持不变，但大快度下密度矩阵出现量子相干性；最终粒子的自旋期望值编码了散射过程信息。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了相对论性散射中自旋关联的理解，并为解释$e^{-}e^{+}\rightarrowμ^{-}μ^{+}$过程中的分歧结果提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study the relativistic nature of the interactions that, at tree level, generate spin correlations between two electrons in Møller scattering, as well as in an extended process involving a witness particle $C$. The corresponding processes, $e^{-}e^{-}\rightarrow e^{-}e^{-}$ and $e^{-}e^{-}C\rightarrow e^{-}e^{-}C$, are analyzed both in the center-of-mass frame and, for the former process, in a Lorentz-boosted frame where Wigner rotations arise. It is found that, through a nonrelativistic approximation of the scattering amplitudes, dipole-dipole and current-dipole interactions are responsible for the emergence of these correlations. This is evidenced by the variation of the von Neumann entropy of one electron for initially separable states, and of $C$ for an initially prepared three-particle entangled W-state. In Wigner rotations, the invariance of entropy under local unitary transformations is maintained at the expense of the emergence of quantum coherence in the density matrix at large rapidities. As a consequence, the final states of both particles are evaluated and shown to encode information about the scattering process through their spin expectation values. This framework is then used to comment on the correlations in the inelastic process $e^{-}e^{+}\rightarrowμ^{-}μ^{+}$, for which some research has reported differing results.

</details>


### [325] [The Transformation-Response Framework: An Operational Reformulation of Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09000)
*Meng-Jun Hu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于变换-响应框架的量子力学操作化重构，将量子态定义为系统对所有物理变换的响应集合（特征函数），仅通过正定性公设推导出整个标准量子力学形式体系，包括希尔伯特空间、玻恩规则、薛定谔方程和费曼路径积分，并揭示了新的物理约束——乘积序正定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子力学形式体系依赖希尔伯特空间等数学结构，缺乏操作化、背景独立且经济的公理化基础。作者旨在通过单一正定性公设重构量子理论，使其根植于操作原语，并揭示新的物理约束。

Method: 提出变换-响应框架：将量子态定义为特征函数χ(g)，即系统对所有局部群G中物理变换g的干涉响应；唯一公设是χ的正定性（确保变换叠加不产生负概率）；利用GNS构造导出希尔伯特空间，利用博赫纳定理导出玻恩规则，利用群自同构导出薛定谔方程，利用Trotter极限导出费曼路径积分。

Result: 成功从单一正定性公设推导出量子力学的全部标准形式体系；框架具有背景无关和时间中性特性，时间被理解为G中单参数子群的坐标；发现新的乘积序正定性约束，可能具有可检验的预言。

Conclusion: 该框架为量子理论提供了一个统一、经济且可证伪的操作化基础，并可能产生超越标准量子力学的可检验新物理预言。

Abstract: We present the transformation-response framework, an operational reformulation of quantum mechanics. A quantum state is not a Hilbert space object but the catalog of a system's responses to all physical transformations: for each operation $g$ from the system's local group $G$, an interference experiment gives a complex value $χ(g)$. The collection $\{χ(g): g\in G \}$ is the characteristic function and defines the state. The only postulate is that $χ$ is positive-definite, encoding the requirement that no superposition of transformations yields negative probability. From this single assumption, the entire standard formalism is derived: Hilbert space via GNS construction, Born rule via Bochner theorem, Schrödinger equation from group automorphisms, and especially Feynman path integral as a Trotter limit. The framework is background-independent and time-neutral: time is a coordinate along a one-parameter subgroup of $G$. It also reveals a new physical constraint, product order positivity, which may lead to testable predictions. The framework provides a unified, economical, and falsifiable foundation for quantum theory rooted in operational primitives.

</details>


### [326] [Satellite-Based Quantum Communication: Performance Evaluation of Discrete-Variable Quantum Key Distribution Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09217)
*Muskan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本论文对卫星量子密钥分发（QKD）协议进行了全面的性能分析，在考虑大气效应和操作条件的情况下，比较了BB84、B92、BBM92和E91等四种协议，并进一步研究了高维QKD协议（HD-BB84和HD-Extended B92），发现HD-BB84在密钥率、噪声容限和概率分布方面均优于HD-Extended B92。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服光纤QKD系统的距离限制，利用卫星平台实现远距离量子通信，同时评估和优化不同QKD协议在真实大气条件下的性能表现。

Method: 采用圆形光束传播模型（包含衍射、湍流、衰减和指向误差）对BB84、B92、BBM92、E91协议进行数值仿真比较；采用椭圆光束近似对高维HD-BB84和HD-Extended B92协议进行建模，并在不同系统维度、天气条件和天顶角下进行仿真分析。

Result: 协议性能受信道不对称性、光束传播特性和噪声的显著影响；HD-BB84相比HD-Extended B92实现了更高的密钥率、更优的噪声容限和更有利的密钥率概率分布，证明了高维编码在卫星QKD中的优势。

Conclusion: 高维编码（特别是HD-BB84）是实现鲁棒卫星QKD的有前景方案，研究结果为低地球轨道卫星链路的协议选择提供了指导。

Abstract: Quantum Key Distribution (QKD) has emerged as a fundamentally secure approach to communication in the era of quantum computing, offering protection against threats posed to classical cryptographic schemes such as RSA and Diffie-Hellman. This thesis presents a comprehensive performance analysis of satellite-based QKD protocols, focusing on both prepare-and-measure and entanglement-based schemes under realistic atmospheric and operational conditions. The study begins by introducing the theoretical foundations of quantum communication, including qubits, entanglement, and quantum entropy, and motivates the need for satellite-based QKD to overcome the distance limitations of fiber-based systems. Subsequently, the thesis evaluates four prominent QKD protocols-BB84, B92, BBM92, and E91-using a circular beam propagation model that incorporates atmospheric effects such as diffraction, turbulence, attenuation, and pointing errors, along with environmental noise contributions for uplink and downlink. Comparative numerical simulations reveal that protocol performance is strongly influenced by channel asymmetries, beam propagation characteristics, and noise, providing guidance on optimal protocol selection for low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite links. The research further investigates high-dimensional (HD) QKD protocols, specifically HD-BB84 and HD-Extended B92, using the elliptic-beam approximation to account for turbulence-induced distortions for both uplink and downlink. Simulations under vary ing system dimensions, weather conditions, and zenith angles demonstrate that HD-BB84 achieves higher key rates, superior noise tolerance, and more favorable probability distributions of the key rate compared to HD-Extended B92, highlighting the advantages of high-dimensional encoding for robust satellite-based QKD.

</details>


### [327] [Range-controlled entanglement in Lindbladian skin states of monitored fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09306)
*Gianluca Passarelli,Angelo Russomanno,Davide Rossini,Procolo Lucignano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在监测费米子链中，通过定向粒子守恒耗散实现林德布拉德皮肤态，并分析了不同跳跃范围下皮肤积累与量子纠缠的有限尺寸标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究耗散工程如何稳定传统幺正动力学无法实现的状态，特别是通过定向粒子守恒耗散产生林德布拉德皮肤态，并理解耗散与相干跳跃如何共同调控皮肤局域化与量子纠缠之间的内在关联。

Method: 在具有可调跳跃范围的监测费米子链中，采用高斯轨迹近似方法，研究短程跳跃与长程跳跃两种情形下的有限尺寸标度行为。

Result: 短程跳跃表现出完全的皮肤积累和面积律纠缠熵；足够长程的跳跃则产生有限体相尾部和有效的次体积律纠缠熵（代数标度）。

Conclusion: 耗散与相干跳跃的联合作用共同决定了皮肤局域化强度和量子纠缠性质，两者之间存在紧密的相互关联。

Abstract: Reservoir engineering can stabilize states inaccessible to unitary dynamics. Directed particle-conserving dissipation creates Lindbladian skin states, where Pauli exclusion turns edge accumulation into a many-body density imbalance. In a monitored fermion chain with tunable hopping range, we identify, within a Gaussian trajectory approximation, two finite-size scaling regimes: short-range hopping is consistent with complete skin accumulation and area-law entanglement, whereas sufficiently long-range hopping produces a finite bulk tail and effective algebraic sub-volume-law entanglement.
