Dissipation and coherent hopping thus jointly control skin localization and quantum entanglement, highlighting their close interconnection.

</details>


### [328] [Energy Transport in Randomly Coupled Quantum Systems: A Perturbative Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09308)
*Tingfei Li,Runyu Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过高斯随机矩阵模拟耦合，在大量子数极限下推导出二阶能量传递率和热导率的显式表达式，并针对不同态密度进行了计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究两个通过随机相互作用耦合的量子系统间的能量传输机制，建立一种简单且系统的微扰展开方法。

Method: 将耦合建模为高斯随机矩阵，利用谱方法和图展开，在大量子数极限下进行二阶微扰展开。

Result: 得到了能量传递率和热导率的前导阶和次前导阶贡献的显式表达式，并针对高斯型、常数型、半圆型和伽马型态密度进行了具体计算。

Conclusion: 所提出的随机矩阵方法能够系统且简洁地描述量子系统间的能量传输，二阶微扰展开在大量子数极限下有效。

Abstract: We study energy transport between two quantum systems coupled through a random interaction. The central feature of our approach is to model the coupling as a Gaussian random matrix, which enables a simple and systematic perturbative expansion. In the large-$N$ limit, we derive explicit expressions for the energy transfer rate and heat conductance up to second order in the coupling strength. Using spectral methods and diagrammatic expansions, we obtain the leading- and next-to-leading-order contributions to the energy transfer rate. We illustrate our results through explicit calculations for Gaussian, constant, semicircular, and Gamma densities of states.

</details>


### [329] [Entanglement-assisted continuous-variable concatenated codes for encoding qubits or oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09379)
*Nihar Ranjan Dash,Sanjoy Dutta,R. Srikanth,Subhashish Banerjee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种纠缠辅助（EA）版本的级联编码方案：一是将EA稳定子（外）码与GKP（内）码级联的量子比特-到-振荡器方案（以三量子比特EA重复码+GKP为例）；二是将GKP（外）码与EA稳定子（内）码级联的振荡器-到-振荡器方案（以GKP码+三量子比特EA重复码为例，并使用两个最大纠缠模压制数据模的位置和动量正交误差），并推广到n量子比特EA重复码族，误差方差压制因子为1/n。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将纠缠辅助（EA）稳定子码的码率优势与GKP码的纠错能力结合，探索通过级联编码进一步提升纠错性能的可能性，特别是在振荡器系统中实现更优的误差压制。

Method: 提出两种EA级联编码结构：（1）EA稳定子码作为外码、GKP码作为内码的量子比特-到-振荡器级联；（2）GKP码作为外码、EA稳定子码作为内码的振荡器-到-振荡器级联。通过具体示例（三量子比特EA重复码与GKP码的相互级联）及推广到n量子比特情形进行验证。

Result: 对于振荡器-到-振荡器级联方案，给出了GKP码与三量子比特EA重复码（使用2个最大纠缠模）级联的示例，并推广到n量子比特EA重复码族，实现了数据模位置和动量正交误差方差以因子1/n被压制。

Conclusion: 纠缠辅助技术可以扩展到级联编码领域，通过将EA稳定子码与GKP码进行不同层次的级联，能够有效压制振荡器系统中数据模的误差，为量子纠错提供新的设计思路。

Abstract: Entanglement-assisted (EA) stabilizer codes enhance the rate of error correction in relation to codes with no pre-shared entanglement. Meanwhile, bosonic error-correcting codes, such as the Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) code, can be concatenated with qubit stabilizer codes to significantly reduce the logical failure probability of those stabilizer codes. First, we combine the above two concepts to propose an EA version of the qubit-into-oscillators concatenated code that chains an EA-stabilizer (outer) code with a GKP (inner) code. As an example we present a three-qubit EA-repetition concatenated with a GKP code. Second, we propose an EA version of the non-Gaussian oscillator-into-oscillators concatenated code that chains a GKP (outer) code with an EA-stabilizer (inner) code. As an example we present a GKP code concatenated with a three-qubit EA repetition code that uses two maximally entangled modes (emodes) and suppresses the variances of both position and momentum quadrature errors of a data mode. Furthermore, we generalize the latter example to a family of GKP code concatenated with a $n$-qubit EA repetition code that uses ${n-1}$ emodes and suppresses the variances of both position and momentum quadrature errors of a data mode by a factor ${1/n}$.

</details>


### [330] [Classical Stochasticity Using Quantum Computers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09384)
*Diego Campos,Narasimha Reddy Gosala,Arundhati Dasgupta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用量子算法的测量随机性来建模经典随机模拟，通过Python随机数生成器求解经典Lorenz系统，并比较其经典随机性与量子算法测量的输出结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子算法中固有的测量随机性是否可以有效建模经典随机模拟，从而为随机系统提供新的计算范式。

Method: 使用Python随机数生成器模拟经典Lorenz系统的随机行为，同时采用量子算法（利用其测量过程的随机性）求解同一系统，并对两者的输出进行对比分析。

Result: 论文对比了经典Lorenz系统的随机性与量子算法测量输出之间的异同，具体量化结果未在摘要中给出。

Conclusion: 量子算法的测量随机性可以作为经典随机模拟的一种替代或补充方法，但需要进一步评估其在具体随机系统中的有效性和精确性。

Abstract: We suggest that quantum algorithms can be used to model classical stochastic simulations as the measurement process is inherently random. To illustrate, we solve the classical Lorenz system with stochastic behavior using a Python random number generator. We compare the classical stochasticity of the Lorenz system with the measured output of the system obtained using quantum algorithms.

</details>


### [331] [Hardware-Aware QAOA for Honeypot Traffic Partitioning on 100+ Qubit IBM Quantum Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09469)
*Cameron V. Cogburn,Casimer DeCusatis,Evan Spillane*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了基于变分量子算法解决DoS/DDoS缓解中通过加权MaxCut映射蜜罐流量划分问题的前期工作，构建了从蜜罐事件到QUBO的可复现流水线，在IBM量子硬件上执行QAOA，并与经典启发式基线及无噪声矩阵乘积态参考进行比较。最大实例为110节点、181边，运行于三个IBM后端。实验表明浅层QAOA可执行实际流量划分任务，但后端架构和路由开销影响目标质量、安全指标和运行时间。当前经典启发式可解决标记基准图，因此不声称量子优势。本文建立了硬件可行性与架构基准框架，并指出MaxCut成本、安全质量、路由开销和运行时间必须作为网络安全相关量子优化的独立指标报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DoS/DDoS缓解方案需要区分恶意流量与良性流量，已有工作提出将蜜罐流量划分映射为加权MaxCut问题并用变分量子算法求解。本文旨在扩展这一原理验证方向，建立可复现的端到端流水线，并在真实量子硬件上评估硬件可行性、架构影响及性能指标，为网络安全领域的量子优化提供基准框架。

Method: 构建了从蜜罐事件级数据到QUBO的可复现流水线；生成了包含16、32、66、110个事件节点的带标签时间二分基准图；在IBM量子硬件上执行QAOA（浅层固定深度）；使用经典启发式基线（如Goemans-Williamson算法）和无噪声矩阵乘积态（MPS）参考进行比较；跨不同量子处理器架构进行路由开销分析。

Result: 最大的基准实例为110节点、181边，在三个IBM后端上成功执行。浅层QAOA能够在实用规模上执行真实流量划分任务，但后端架构和路由开销显著影响目标函数质量、安全度量指标和实际运行时间。简单经典启发式算法能够求解当前标记基准图。

Conclusion: 本研究不声称量子优势，但建立了硬件可行性验证和架构基准框架。MaxCut成本、安全质量、路由开销和运行时间必须作为网络安全相关量子优化的独立指标分别报告。该工作为未来更大规模、更复杂的量子网络安全应用提供了系统性的评估方法论和基准数据集。

Abstract: Denial-of-service (DoS) and distributed denial-of-service (DDoS) mitigation requires separating malicious traffic from benign traffic while minimizing disruption to legitimate users. Prior work proposed mapping honeypot traffic partitioning to a weighted MaxCut problem and solving the resulting graphs with variational quantum algorithms. We extend this proof of principle direction with a reproducible event-level honeypot-to-QUBO pipeline, labeled temporal bipartite benchmark graphs with 16, 32, 66, and 110 event nodes, QAOA executions on IBM quantum hardware, classical heuristic baselines, a noiseless matrix product state reference, and a routing overhead analysis across quantum processor architectures. The largest benchmark is a 110-node, 181-edge instance executed on three IBM backends. Our results show that a shallow QAOA can execute real traffic partitioning workloads at the utility scale, while backend architecture and routing overhead affect objective quality, security metrics, and observed runtime. Because simple classical heuristics can solve the current labeled benchmark graphs, these experiments are not a quantum advantage claim. Instead, we deliberately use a fixed, shallow QAOA implementation to enable controlled comparisons across problem sizes and hardware architectures. This work establishes a hardware feasibility and architecture benchmark framework, and demonstrates that MaxCut cost, security quality, routing overhead, and runtime must be reported as separate metrics for cybersecurity relevant quantum optimization.

</details>


### [332] [Algebraic Kolmogorov--Arnold representation theorem for quantum measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09584)
*Sviatoslav V. Dzhenzher*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了量子Kolmogorov-Arnold（QKA）表示定理，将经典KA定理推广到量子信息论中，证明了对无纠缠多量子比特乘积态的任何目标物理性质，均可用有限、固定的局域内观测量和浅层单变量多项式架构精确分解，并且该表示在对抗性扰动下具有显著稳定性，与经典KA定理的病态不稳定性形成鲜明对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Kolmogorov-Arnold表示定理存在病态不稳定性和严重的重参数化敏感性，限制了其实际应用。本文旨在将KA定理推广到量子信息论中，建立代数化、有界次多项式版本的量子KA表示，以克服经典版本的稳定性缺陷，为量子信息处理提供更鲁棒的理论框架。

Method: 首先引入并证明了经典KA定理的代数化、有界次多项式版本；然后将其推广到量子信息论中，建立QKA表示定理；最后通过Heisenberg图像分析该表示在对抗性扰动下的稳定性，包括对内部测量算符的有界物理扰动和对输入状态的量子信道攻击。

Result: QKA表示能够对无纠缠多量子比特乘积态的目标物理性质进行精确分解，且该表示在两种类型的对抗性扰动下均保持稳定：(1) 作用于内部测量算符的有界物理扰动；(2) 作用于输入状态的对抗性量子信道攻击。

Conclusion: 量子化的Kolmogorov-Arnold表示克服了经典版本的不稳定性问题，展现出了显著的鲁棒性，为量子信息理论中涉及多体系统和测量的问题提供了新的理论工具和稳定框架。

Abstract: We establish an operational framework connecting the classical Kolmogorov--Arnold (KA) representation theorem to quantum information theory. By introducing and proving an algebraic, bounded-degree polynomial version of the theorem, we demonstrate that any target physical property of an unentangled multi-qubit product state can be exactly decomposed using a finite, fixed set of local <<inner>> observables and a shallow architecture of univariate polynomials.
  We further analyze the stability of this Quantum Kolmogorov--Arnold (QKA) representation under adversarial perturbations. In stark contrast to the pathological instabilities and severe reparameterization sensitivities inherent to the classical Kolmogorov--Arnold representation theorem, our algebraic quantum framework exhibits remarkable resilience. We prove that the representation remains stable against bounded physical perturbations acting on the inner measurement operators, and show via the Heisenberg picture that it is inherently immune to adversarial quantum channel attacks acting on the input states.

</details>


### [333] [Entanglement Generation through Coherent and Non-Coherent Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09599)
*Marco Enriquez,Francisco Delgado*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过相干路径叠加局域幺正操作和受控Pauli通道的随机实现两种方式，从可分离态确定性生成Bell、GHZ和W类纠缠态，并分析了噪声场景下路径叠加和不定因果序配置中的纠缠生成、成功概率及纠缠-纯度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠的可控生成是量子信息科学的核心挑战，本文旨在探索从完全可分离态出发，利用相干路径叠加和随机Pauli通道等控制范式实现纠缠的确定性生成，并扩展到噪声场景下的分析。

Method: 采用两种控制范式：1）相干路径叠加局域幺正操作；2）受控Pauli通道的随机实现。推导了局域算子生成纠缠的条件，分析了路径叠加和不定因果序配置下噪声混合态通过Pauli通道对的演化，得到输出态闭式表达式，并用concurrence量化纠缠。

Result: 实现了从完全可分离输入态确定性生成Bell、GHZ和W类纠缠态，所得态局域幺正等价于标准多体纠缠态。在噪声场景中，识别了随机纠缠出现的参数区间，确定了成功概率，刻画了纠缠与纯度之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 相干路径叠加局域操作和受控Pauli通道两种范式均能有效从可分离态生成多体纠缠，且在噪声环境下仍可观测到随机纠缠，但存在纠缠-纯度权衡。

Abstract: The controlled generation of quantum entanglement from separable states remains a central challenge in quantum information science. Here, we investigate entanglement generation using two related control paradigms: coherent path superposition of local unitary operations and stochastic implementations of Pauli channels under coherent control. We show that entangled states belonging to the Bell, GHZ and W classes, can be deterministically generated from fully separable inputs by coherently superposing alternative sets of local unitary transformations. Conditions on the local operators for entanglement generation are derived, and the resulting states are shown to be locally unitary equivalent to standard multipartite entangled states. We further extend the analysis to noisy scenarios, where separable mixed states evolve through pairs of Pauli channels arranged in path-superposition and indefinite causal order configurations. Closed-form expressions for the output states are obtained, and entanglement is quantified using concurrence. By exploring representative channel families across their parameter space, we identify regimes where stochastic entanglement emerges, determine the associated success probabilities, and characterize trade-offs between entanglement and purity.

</details>


### [334] [Zermelo's navigation problem through the lens of quantum annealing: How the Landau-Zener approximation leads to an efficient classical solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09602)
*Sølve Selstø,Tor Kristian Dahle,Sergiy Denysov,Yves-Laurent Ariel Rezus,Leiv Øyehaug*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将经典Zermelo导航问题（河流穿越问题）表述为绝热量子计算问题的方法，使用量子三能级系统（qutrit）进行编码，并通过Landau-Zener公式分析演化保真度。令人惊讶的是，该量子公式揭示了问题的一种经典结构，使得计算复杂度仅与问题规模呈二次方关系，而非指数关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 河流穿越问题（Zermelo导航问题）是一个具有实际意义的经典优化问题，其复杂度可调节。作者希望通过量子计算框架来求解该问题，探索其潜在的指数级加速可能性。

Method: 将Zermelo问题编码为绝热量子计算问题，使用qutrit（量子三能级系统）表示路径段。构造包含惩罚项（强制执行边界条件）和探索项（允许系统在中间构型间移动）的哈密顿量。利用绝热演化过程，系统通过一系列避免交叉点演化，并使用Landau-Zener公式估计最终保真度。

Result: 发现Landau-Zener近似有效的区域提供了一种确定性的方式识别正确解，且计算开销仅与问题规模呈二次方关系。量子公式揭示了问题潜在的经典结构，可以被高效利用。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一个实际优化问题如何被转化为量子退火框架，并通过薛定谔方程、避免交叉和Landau-Zener理论进行分析。虽然最初因问题的指数复杂度而引入量子公式，但最终揭示了一种可高效利用的经典结构，为量子计算与经典优化之间的关联提供了教学性示例。

Abstract: The river-crossing problem, also known as Zermelo's navigation problem, is a classic example of an optimization problem with practical relevance and a scalable degree of complexity. It asks for the optimal trajectory of a vessel moving through a water flow field and provides a setting in which physics, variational methods, and optimization are naturally intertwined. We state a version of Zermelo's problem and then solve it as formulate it as an adiabatic quantum-computing problem using quantum trits, or qutrits for short. The construction includes a penalty term that enforces the prescribed boundary conditions and an exploration term that allows the system to move through intermediate configurations toward the optimal feasible path. In the adiabatic description, the evolution proceeds through a sequence of avoided crossings, so that the resulting fidelity can be estimated using the Landau-Zener formula. Remarkably, the regime in which this approximation is valid also provides a deterministic way to identify the correct solution with computational effort that scales only quadratically with the problem size. Thus, a quantum formulation initially motivated by the apparent exponential complexity of the problem reveals an underlying classical structure that can be exploited efficiently. Our approach also provides a pedagogical illustration of how a real-world optimization problem can be cast into a quantum-annealing framework and then analyzed using the Schrödinger equation, avoided crossings, and Landau-Zener theory.

</details>


### [335] [Quantum Algorithms for Modulated Circulant Matrix Vector Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09618)
*Kimy Agudelo,Aldo Quelopana Cristina Manzaneda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文定义了一种名为“调制量子傅里叶变换（MQFT）”的量子原语，专门针对调制循环矩阵（一类特殊的N参数循环矩阵）设计，该矩阵家族具有基于范德蒙德型基的结构化谱分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期文献引入了调制循环矩阵这一特殊类别的N参数循环矩阵，其具有基于范德蒙德型基的结构化谱分解特性。受此定义启发，作者希望为这类矩阵族设计一个专用的量子原语。

Method: 基于调制循环矩阵的数学定义和谱分解结构，提出了调制量子傅里叶变换（MQFT）作为量子原语。

Result: 成功定义了MQFT这一量子原语，使其与调制循环矩阵家族相匹配。

Conclusion: MQFT是针对调制循环矩阵族专门设计的量子原语，有望在相关量子计算任务中发挥重要作用。

Abstract: Modulated circulant matrices form a special class of N-parametric circulant matrices, recently introduced in the literature, with a structured spectral decomposition based on a Vandermonde type basis. Motivated by this definition, in this work we define the Modulated Quantum Fourier Transform (MQFT), a quantum primitive tailored to this matrix family.

</details>


### [336] [Phase-only control of GRAPE shaped pulses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09624)
*Andrew J. Baldwin,Jonathan A. Jones*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文比较了GRAPE算法中纯相位控制与相位-幅度控制两种脉冲设计方法，发现纯相位控制具有显著优势。通过Trotter化可用相位调制模拟幅度调制，表明两种方法本质等价。纯相位优化速度更快更简单，且生成的脉冲可转换为相位-幅度形式以获得更鲁棒的实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较GRAPE算法中纯相位控制与相位-幅度控制两种脉冲设计方法的性能差异，并解释纯相位控制为何具有优势。

Method: 使用GRAPE算法分别进行纯相位控制和相位-幅度控制来设计成形脉冲，利用Trotter化证明相位调制可模拟幅度调制。

Result: 纯相位控制比相位-幅度控制具有显著优势；两种方法本质等价，但纯相位优化速度更快、更简单；两种方法设计的脉冲可相互转换。

Conclusion: 纯相位控制是更优的脉冲设计选择，其生成的脉冲可转换为相位-幅度形式以获得更鲁棒的实现。

Abstract: We compare phase-only control with phase-and-amplitude control when designing shaped pulses using the GRAPE algorithm, and explain why phase-only control has significant advantages. Trotterisation can be used to simulate amplitude modulation with phase modulation, indicating that the two approaches are fundamentally equivalent. Pulses designed by either method can be interconverted, but phase-only optimization is faster and simpler. The resulting pulses can then be converted into phase-and-amplitude form for more robust implementation if desired.

</details>


### [337] [Parahydrogen Cooling of Nuclear Spin Chains at Hypogeomagnetic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09649)
*Alexey Kiryutin,Danil Markelov,Ivan Zhukov,Erik Van Dyke,Alexandra Yurkovskaya,Danila Barskiy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用对氢诱导的超极化技术（SABRE）在近零磁场下对化学设计的12自旋链进行超极化，实现了百分级的13C和15N极化，使系统熵显著降低（S/k从8.318降至8.274），并展示了超越单自旋极化的多自旋关联有序，为量子模拟、量子控制和哈密顿量学习提供了实验基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 溶液态分子核自旋网络具有化学可编程、可精确测量及室温相干等优点，是理想的量子模拟器，但其主要限制在于热Boltzmann极化导致的高度混合、高熵初始态，无法满足量子信息科学对低熵初始化的要求。

Method: 采用对氢基可逆交换信号放大（SABRE）技术，在次地磁场（0.52 μT）下对化学工程的12自旋链[U-13C,15N]-丁腈进行超极化，并通过快速场循环至9.4 T进行位点分辨NMR读出。

Result: SABRE产生百分级13C和15N极化，制备了非平衡多自旋有序；von Neumann熵分析显示最优转移场0.52 μT下全自旋系统S/k=8.274，相比未极化参考的8.318降低0.043；15N和13C子系综分别达到52 mK和257 mK的核自旋温度；全网络的熵赤字大于各子系统的熵赤字之和，表明存在关联多自旋有序。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了利用SABRE超极化可有效降低核自旋网络的熵，实现远超热平衡的低熵初始化，为量子模拟、量子控制和哈密顿量学习等量子信息科学应用提供了可实验基准的平台。

Abstract: Solution-state molecular nuclear spin networks are promising quantum simulators because their scalar-coupling Hamiltonians are chemically programmable, precisely measurable, and coherent at room temperature. Their main limitation for quantum information science is initialization: thermal Boltzmann polarization produces highly mixed, high-entropy states. Here, we use parahydrogen-based Signal Amplification by Reversible Exchange (SABRE) at hypogeomagnetic fields (i.e., magnetic fields below Earth field) to hyperpolarize the chemically engineered 12-spin chain [U-13C,15N]-butyronitrile. SABRE generates percent-level 13C and 15N polarization and prepares non-equilibrium multi-spin orders across the network. A von Neumann entropy analysis of such a hyperpolarized system shows that, at the optimal transfer field of 0.52 uT, the full spin system could reach S/k = 8.274, compared with S/k = 8.318 for the unpolarized reference, giving (S-Sth)/k = -0.043. Experimentally, nuclear spin temperatures of 52 mK and 257 mK are achieved for 15N and 13C subensembles, respectively. The larger entropy deficit of the full network than of individual subsystems indicates correlated multi-spin order beyond single-spin polarizations. Rapid field cycling to 9.4 T enables site-resolved NMR readout, while the precisely determined coupling network provides an experimentally benchmarked Hamiltonian for testing quantum-simulation, quantum-control, and Hamiltonian-learning protocols.

</details>


### [338] [The dynamic 4.8.8 Floquet code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09678)
*Aliki A. Capatos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对4.8.8 Floquet码提出了动态量子纠错电路方案，在保持空间距离完整的前提下实现了更高的阈值和更低的量子比特开销，其中无复位动态变体在电路级退极化噪声下达到0.512%（BP+matching为0.574%）的最高阈值，显著优于标准辅助位电路的0.228%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Floquet码中动态电路（无辅助位）能提升容错量子存储的阈值并降低开销，但6.6.6蜂窝结构存在空间距离减半的问题。对于4.8.8晶格布局，此前仅推测其动态构造能保持完整距离，本文旨在确认这一猜想并给出具体实现方案。

Method: 构造了4.8.8 Floquet码的四种电路级实现（两种动态变体：含/不含中间复位，标准辅助位电路，流水线辅助位电路），在环面上进行电路级退极化噪声下的数值基准测试，使用MWPM和BP+matching两种解码器。

Result: 复位动态电路每轮阈值0.463%（MWPM）/0.490%（BP+matching）；无复位动态电路达0.512%/0.574%，为四者最高；标准辅助位电路仅0.228%/0.240%；流水线辅助位电路达0.478%/0.489%。复位动态电路的时间距离增长更快（渐近2≤d_t/n_qec≤3），在快复位场景下时空体积最小，无复位变体在慢复位场景下最优。

Conclusion: 4.8.8动态电路在保持完整空间距离的同时获得了预期的阈值增益和开销降低，验证了Floquet码中动态综合征提取的优势。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum memories depend on the syndrome extraction circuit as much as on the underlying code. Ancilla-free or dynamic circuits are an effective way to improve this circuit layer. For the 6.6.6 honeycomb Floquet code, making the circuit dynamic raises the threshold and lowers the qubit overhead, but at the cost of halving the spatial code distance. A dynamic construction for the 4.8.8 lattice layout was conjectured to preserve full distance. I confirm this and give a dynamic measurement circuit for the CSS 4.8.8 Floquet code. To benchmark it, I construct and compare four circuit-level implementations on a torus, including two dynamic variants (with and without mid-circuit resets), the standard ancilla-based circuit, and a pipelined ancilla-based circuit. Under circuit-level depolarising noise, the reset dynamic circuit reaches a per-round threshold of $0.463\%$ $(0.490\%)$ with MWPM (BP+matching), while the no-reset variant reaches the highest threshold of all four circuits at $0.512\%$ $(0.574\%)$. The standard ancilla-based circuit only achieves $0.228\%$ $(0.240\%)$, but the pipelined schedule reaches $0.478\%$ $(0.489\%)$. The reset dynamic circuit also has a faster-growing timelike distance, with $2\le d_t/n_{\mathrm{qec}}\le 3$ asymptotically against a tight $3/2$ for the other three, and running it for fewer rounds gives the smallest spacetime volume in the fast-reset regime, while the no-reset variant is smallest in the slow-reset regime. The 4.8.8 dynamic circuits therefore see the expected threshold gain and overhead reduction without the spatial-distance cost, demonstrating the advantage of dynamic syndrome extraction in Floquet codes.

</details>


### [339] [Leveraging Landau-Zener-Stückelberg interference for accelerating diabatic quantum annealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09706)
*Matthias Werner,Matías Jonsson,Artur García-Sáez,Arnau Riera,Tameem Albash*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了变分量子退火加速的物理机制是Landau-Zener-Stückelberg干涉，并基于此提出了一种参数更少的变分调度方案，证明了经典优化可在多项式时间内完成，论证了相干性是该机制的关键资源，并在包括MAXCUT在内的难题实例上取得了优于绝热量子退火的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解变分量子退火相对于传统绝热量子退火实现指数级加速的底层物理机制，并基于该机制设计更高效的变分调度方案。

Method: 识别出Landau-Zener-Stückelberg干涉是变分退火加速的根本机制；基于此洞察提出参数更少的变分调度ansatz；理论上证明了该调度参数的经典优化可在多项式时间内完成；通过数值实验验证了相干性的关键作用以及所提方法在包括MAXCUT基准实例上的性能。

Result: 所提出的简化变分调度ansatz在经典优化上具有多项式时间复杂度；相干性被证明是该机制不可或缺的资源；数值实验表明该方法在挑战性问题上显著优于绝热量子退火，并在MAXCUT基准实例上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 明确利用物理机制（如Landau-Zener-Stückelberg干涉）可以设计出更有效的变分退火算法，为量子退火的加速提供了理论依据和实用方案。

Abstract: Diabatic quantum annealing with variationally optimized schedules can exhibit exponential speedups over conventional adiabatic quantum annealing, as was demonstrated numerically for a frustrated Ising ring model by C{ô}t{é} et al. Here we identify Landau-Zener-St{ü}ckelberg interference as the underlying mechanism for this speedup, and based on this insight we propose a variational schedule ansatz with far fewer parameters. This simplified ansatz allows us to show analytically that the classical optimization of the schedule parameters can be done in polynomial time and discuss conditions when we expect this type of mechanism to provide speedups over adiabatic annealing. Furthermore, we provide an analytical argument that coherence is an essential resource for this mechanism, which we verify numerically. We perform extensive numerical tests of the proposed ansatz and observe substantial improvements over adiabatic annealing and competitive performance in particularly challenging problem instances, including a well-studied MAXCUT instance commonly used for benchmarking. Our work shows that explicitly leveraging physical mechanisms can lead to more effective designs of variational annealing algorithms.

</details>


### [340] [Optomechanically controlled response amplification for enhanced quantum sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09716)
*Javid Naikoo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明腔光力学系统中的强放大动力学响应可用于增强量子传感。通过将光力学相互作用调谐至增强敏感度区域，弱扰动会产生不成比例的大响应，从而显著提高估计精度。量子Fisher信息在扰动减弱时呈现发散标度，且外差探测可实现与量子Fisher信息相同的渐近标度，表明标准测量即可获得增强灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索腔光力学系统中强放大动力学响应是否能够作为增强量子传感的物理资源，突破传统传感方法的精度极限。

Method: 将光力学相互作用调谐至增强敏感度区域，利用高斯估计理论分析量子Fisher信息的标度行为，并通过外差探测验证经典Fisher信息的可达性。

Result: 量子Fisher信息在扰动强度趋近于零时呈现发散标度，意味着估计误差被有效抑制；外差探测得到的经典Fisher信息与量子Fisher信息具有相同的渐近标度。

Conclusion: 放大的光力学动力学是一种可控的量子增强传感与计量资源，标准外差测量即可实现理论预测的增强灵敏度。

Abstract: We show that strongly amplified dynamical responses in cavity optomechanical systems can be harnessed for enhanced quantum sensing. By tuning the optomechanical interaction to a regime of enhanced susceptibility, weak perturbations produce disproportionately large changes in the system response, leading to substantially improved estimation precision. Using Gaussian estimation theory, we demonstrate that the quantum Fisher information exhibits a divergent scaling as the perturbation strength decreases, implying a corresponding suppression of the estimation error. We further show that heterodyne detection of the output cavity field yields the classical Fisher information with the same asymptotic scaling as the quantum Fisher information, demonstrating that the enhanced sensitivity is accessible with a standard measurement protocol. These results identify amplified optomechanical dynamics as a controllable resource for quantum enhanced sensing and metrology.

</details>


### [341] [A Bell-State Extension of Loop-Back Quantum Key Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09723)
*Luis Adrián Lizama-Pérez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Bell态的环路量子密钥分发架构扩展，通过隐藏参考Bell态和改进的检测机制，解决了双向QKD协议中的经典披露、信号空间受限和可检测性不足的问题，实现了约3/4的固有检测概率和最高50%的有效吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双向QKD协议长期面临经典披露、信号空间受限以及替代攻击或纠缠交换攻击下可检测性不足等挑战。

Method: 采用纠缠Bell态与远程节点上的确定性局域Pauli编码；Alice私下准备并知晓初始Bell态作为隐藏参考；利用家族内和家族间Bell态跃迁扩展信号空间；Alice执行Bell态测量确定性地推断Bob的操作，无需基筛选。

Result: 替代策略下固有检测概率约3/4每轮；有效吞吐量仅受Bell态测量成功率限制，在线性光学实现中可达50%。

Conclusion: 该方案适用于移动或边缘QKD场景，其中被动远程节点需在高损耗和有限交互时间下运行。

Abstract: Bidirectional quantum key distribution (QKD) protocols face persistent challenges related to classical disclosure, confinement of the signal space to predictable subspaces, and limited detectability under substitution or entanglement-swapping attacks. In this work, we present a Bell-state extension of the Loop-Back QKD architecture that improves efficiency and detectability while preserving its defining feature of a simplified, measurement-free remote terminal. The protocol employs entangled Bell states together with deterministic local Pauli encoding at the remote node. A central element is that Alice privately prepares and knows the initial Bell state, which serves as a hidden reference enabling her to interpret the Bell-state transition induced by Bob, while preventing an adversary from reconstructing the encoding without access to this reference. By exploiting both intra- and inter-family Bell transitions, the scheme expands the effective signal space beyond the subspace restrictions of earlier two-way protocols. Alice performs a Bell-state measurement to deterministically infer Bob's operation without any basis sifting. Although the traveling subsystem remains locally maximally mixed, concealing the initial Bell family amplifies disturbance under separable substitution strategies, yielding an intrinsic detection probability of approximately 3/4 per round. From an efficiency perspective, the protocol lifts the intrinsic post-selection limitation of single-qubit Loop-Back schemes: the effective throughput is bounded only by the Bell-state measurement success probability, reaching up to 50% in linear-optical implementations. These features make the proposed scheme particularly suitable for mobile or edge-based QKD scenarios, where passive remote nodes must operate under high loss and limited interaction times.

</details>


### [342] [Sambe Approach to Floquet-Lindblad Open Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09727)
*Andriani Keliri,Marco Schirò*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Floquet理论在扩展Sambe-Liouville空间中构建了时间无关的Floquet Lindbladian，将含时的开放量子系统问题转化为静态非厄米本征值问题，并引入矩阵连分数方法非微扰地求和多光子过程，应用于耗散二能级系统和参量驱动量子点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在含时周期驱动的开放量子系统中，由于演化的非幺正性，传统Floquet工程中有效时间无关哈密顿量的构造方法不再适用，因此需要发展适用于耗散系统的有效时间无关Floquet Lindbladian理论。

Method: 基于Floquet理论在扩展Sambe-Liouville空间中构造时间无关的Floquet Lindbladian；对于谐波驱动，引入矩阵连分数方法非微扰地求和多光子过程；利用 resolvent 形式论得到开放量子系统关联函数的谱Floquet表示。

Result: 成功构建了有效Floquet Lindbladian，相比其他高频展开方法具有一次性给出整个无穷级数展开的优势；应用于耗散二能级系统计算了共振荧光谱，应用于参量驱动量子点计算了谱函数和电流-电压特性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一套处理周期驱动开放量子系统的完整理论框架，为Floquet工程在耗散系统中的推广提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We study driven and open quantum systems described by a time-periodic Lindblad master equation. In closed systems, the stroboscopic dynamics can always be described by an effective time-independent Floquet Hamiltonian; this idea is the basis of Floquet engineering. However, in the presence of dissipation, the existence of an effective time-independent Floquet Lindbladian is not guaranteed due to the non-unitary nature of the evolution. Using Floquet theory, we construct a well-defined time-independent Floquet Lindbladian in an extended Sambe-Liouville space, transforming the initial time-dependent problem to a static and non-Hermitian eigenvalue problem. For harmonic driving, we introduce a matrix continued fraction method to nonperturbatively resum multiphoton processes and construct an effective Floquet Lindbladian acting only on the physical Liouville space. Compared to other high-frequency expansions, this method has the advantage of providing the whole infinite series expansion at once. Using a resolvent formalism, we show how to obtain a spectral Floquet representation of correlation functions of an open quantum system. As an application, we consider a dissipating two-level system in a linearly polarized field and calculate its resonance fluorescence spectrum. Furthermore, we consider a parametrically driven quantum dot with pump and loss for which we calculate its spectral function and current-voltage characteristics.

</details>


### [343] [Quantum Cut Sparsifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09728)
*Arpon Basu,Joshua Brakensiek,Pravesh K. Kothari,Aaron Putterman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究哈密顿量的稀疏化问题，主要贡献是证明任何n量子比特的量子割哈密顿量可被稀疏化为Õ(n/ε²)个项，同时保持所有态的能量在1±ε因子内。该方法通过将矩阵作用分解为不变子空间，结合Alon-Kozma算子值不等式和扩展图分解技术实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续Basu、Brakensiek和Putterman [2026]关于哈密顿量稀疏化的研究路线，特别关注广泛研究的量子割哈密顿量的稀疏化可行性。

Method: 1) 将矩阵作用分解为不变子空间；2) 利用Alon和Kozma的算子值不等式（基于Caputo、Liggett和Richthammer的"八爪鱼不等式"）将稀疏化技术推广到所有扩展图；3) 通过扩展图分解进一步推广到所有图。

Result: 任何n量子比特的QC哈密顿量可稀疏化为Õ(n/ε²)个项，同时能量保持1±ε因子。该方法同时对所有ℓ的Kikuchi图有效。

Conclusion: 提出了一个重要性采样方案，使采样图的Kikuchi图在任意水平ℓ上都是原图Kikuchi图的谱近似，且相同的采样方案对所有ℓ同时有效。

Abstract: In this paper, we continue a line of research initiated by Basu, Brakensiek, and Putterman [2026] studying the sparsifiability of Hamiltonians. We focus particularly on the sparsifiability of the widely-studied Quantum Cut (QC) Hamiltonians. Our main result is that in an $n$-qubit system, any $n$-qubit QC Hamiltonian can be sparsified to $\widetilde{O}(n /\varepsilon^2)$ many terms while preserving the energy of every state up to a factor of $1 \pm \varepsilon$. Our result can be interpreted as giving an importance sampling scheme for the edges of an arbitrary graph $G$ such that the \emph{Kikuchi} graph at level $\ell$ of the sampled graph is a spectral approximation to the Kikuchi graph of $G$. Importantly, the \emph{same} sampling scheme works simultaneously for all $\ell$.
  The natural approach of leverage score sampling, analyzed via matrix concentration inequalities, yields a polynomially worse bound in our setting because the underlying matrices have dimension $\sim 2^n$. Instead, our approach relies on decomposing the action of these matrices into invariant subspaces. Then, by using an operator-valued inequality of Alon and Kozma [Ann. Henri Poincaré, 2020], itself building on an \emph{octopus inequality} of Caputo, Liggett, and Richthammer [J. AMS, 2010], we extend our sparsification technique to all expander graphs. We then invoke expander decomposition to extend our sparsifier to all graphs.

</details>


### [344] [Adaptive directional gradients for parameterised quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09734)
*Brian Coyle,Snehal Raj,Virag Umathe,El Amine Cherrat,Elham Kashefi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于前向自动微分的量子电路梯度估计框架（Forward Gradient），可无偏估计参数移位梯度，并推导出自适应优化器QUIVER，在多达60量子比特、1770参数的哈密顿量保持正交量子神经网络上，以数量级效率超越传统参数移位法则，并在QAOA和VQE任务中优于iCANS/gCANS等测量节俭型优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化量子电路在量子硬件上训练时，梯度估计的测量开销是主要瓶颈。参数移位法则的测量成本随可训练参数数量线性增长，在大规模训练中占据了总采样预算的绝大部分，亟需更高效的梯度估计方法。

Method: 提出量子前向梯度估计框架：基于自动微分前向模式，通过平均可自由调整数量的随机方向导数获得无偏梯度估计，涵盖SPSA、随机坐标下降和参数移位法则作为极限情况；并在此框架下推导QUIVER自适应优化器，其更新规则来自封闭形式的最小测量成本分配。

Result: 在ECG5000和MNIST数据集上，使用多达60量子比特、1770参数的哈密顿量保持正交量子神经网络，前向梯度训练效率比参数移位法则高出数个数量级；在QAOA和VQE优化任务中，QUIVER优化器优于iCANS和gCANS测量节俭型优化器。

Conclusion: 量子前向梯度估计框架在保证无偏性的前提下大幅降低了梯度估计的测量成本，QUIVER自适应优化器进一步优化了测量资源分配，为大规模量子电路训练提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Training parameterised quantum circuits (PQCs) on quantum hardware is bottlenecked by the measurement cost of gradient estimation, which under the parameter-shift rule scales linearly in the number of trainable parameters and dominates the total shot budget of training at scale. In this work, we propose a framework of forward gradient estimators for PQCs, based on the forward mode of automatic differentiation, that yields an unbiased estimator of the gradient by averaging a freely tunable number of random directional derivatives and recovers SPSA, random coordinate descent, and the parameter-shift rule as limiting cases, with no ancilla qubits or controlled-gate overhead. We prove that stochastic quantum forward gradient descent converges under standard assumptions, with an explicit second-moment expansion that interpolates between the single-direction extreme of SPSA and the full-gradient extreme of parameter-shift. Within this framework we derive QUIVER (Quantum Iterative V-adaptive Estimator Rule), an adaptive optimiser for parameterised circuits whose update rule follows from a closed-form minimum measurement-cost allocation. We show numerically that forward gradients train Hamming-weight-preserving orthogonal quantum neural networks with up to 60 qubits and 1770 parameters on the ECG5000 and MNIST datasets orders of magnitude more efficiently than the parameter-shift rule. We also demonstrate that our proposed QUIVER optimiser can outperform iCANS and gCANS measurement-frugal optimisers on optimisation problems using the quantum approximate optimisation algorithm and quantum simulation with the variational quantum eigensolver.

</details>


### [345] [Complexity-driven transitions in quantum observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09765)
*Zhenyu Du,Siyuan Cheng,Han Ye,Junjie Chen,Xiao Yuan,Xiongfeng Ma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了量子观测中信息可读性与测量复杂度（量子电路深度）之间的基本关系，发现了由测量复杂度驱动的隐藏-可见相变：低于临界深度阈值时，读出能力随系统规模指数衰减，信息本质上不可访问；超过阈值后，随机测量可恢复量子Fisher信息的恒定比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子观测中，破坏性测量将量子态不可逆地投影为经典数据，必然导致信息损失。作者希望探究支配这种信息损失的物理原理，并构建最优测量以最大化读出效率。

Method: 建立了读出能力（可访问经典Fisher信息与总量子Fisher信息之比）与测量复杂度（投影前所需的量子电路深度）之间的内在关系；严格证明了临界深度阈值的存在（δ维架构为Θ((log n)^{1/δ})，全连接架构为Θ(log log n)）；证明了使用近似酉3-设计（optimal-depth电路构造）的随机测量可普遍恢复QFI的恒定比例。

Result: 发现了由测量复杂度驱动的隐藏-可见相变：低于临界深度时读出能力随系统规模指数衰减；超过阈值后立即进入可见区域，可恢复恒定比例的QFI。

Conclusion: 揭示了支配量子观测的基本标度律和相变，为量子学习、态认证和量子计量学划定了明确的资源边界。

Abstract: Observing the physical world is a foundational pursuit in science. In the quantum realm, however, observation necessitates a fundamental quantum-to-classical conversion: destructive measurements irreversibly project quantum states into classical data, inevitably incurring a loss of information. What physical principles govern this information loss, and how can we construct optimal measurements to maximize the readout? Here, we address these questions by establishing an intrinsic relationship between readout capability--quantified by the ratio of accessible classical Fisher information to the total quantum Fisher information (QFI), and measurement complexity--defined as the quantum circuit depth required prior to projection. Remarkably, we uncover a sudden emergence of observability: a sharp hidden-to-visible transition driven entirely by measurement complexity. We rigorously prove that below critical depth thresholds--$Θ((\log n)^{1/δ})$ for $δ$-dimensional architectures and $Θ(\log\log n)$ for all-to-all connectivity--readout capability decays exponentially with system size $n$, rendering the quantum information fundamentally inaccessible. Surprisingly, immediately above this threshold, the system enters a visible regime: we demonstrate that randomized measurements universally recover a constant fraction of the QFI using approximate unitary 3-designs, for which we explicitly develop optimal-depth circuit constructions tailored to finite-dimensional architectures. By unveiling the fundamental scaling laws and transitions that govern quantum observation, our results delineate definitive resource boundaries for quantum learning, state certification, and quantum metrology.

</details>


### [346] [From Pauli Strings to Quantum Dynamics: A Unified Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09773)
*Roberto Gargiulo,Paul Herringer,Robert Zeier*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过保利字符串与克利福德子群（由transvection生成）之间的辛几何联系，提出了基于不变量的方法（保利轨道、对称性、不变子空间）来高效识别保利李代数及其轨道，并证明这些克利福德子群构成相应保利李群的3-设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统的动力学性质（如可达性和计算能力）对量子计算、量子控制和多体物理至关重要，但现有工具（如表示论和李理论）对于任意生成集难以精确或高效计算。作者希望利用保利字符串的优良性质，建立更高效的可达性分析框架。

Method: 利用保利字符串的辛几何性质，建立保利李代数与由transvection生成的克利福德子群之间的深层联系；提出基于不变量的视角（保利轨道、对称性、不变子空间）来刻画这些对象及其可达性；设计高效算法用于识别李代数和轨道；通过初等方式证明transvection生成的克利福德子群提供相应保利李群的3-设计。

Result: 给出了高效识别保利李代数和轨道的算法；建立了保利李代数与transvection生成的克利福德子群之间的结构对应关系；证明了该子群构成保利李群的3-设计；通过变分量子算法、受限量子计算、多体系统和随机电路等结构化示例验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于不变量的方法为分析保利生成集的可达性和李代数结构提供了高效且简洁的框架，并揭示了保利李代数与克利福德群之间的深层辛几何联系。

Abstract: Understanding the dynamical properties of quantum systems is an essential task in quantum computing, quantum control, and many-body physics. Tools such as representation theory and Lie theory provide crucial information on reachability and computational power. However, this information can be difficult to access exactly or compute efficiently for arbitrary generating sets. Here we focus on the setting of Pauli strings, which satisfy numerous exceptional properties that simplify the problem. We find deep connections between Pauli Lie algebras and certain subgroups of the Clifford group generated by transvections, through the symplectic properties of the Pauli strings. This allows us to give an invariant-based perspective on these objects and their reachability, in the language of Pauli orbits, symmetries, and invariant subspaces. The invariant-based approach provides efficient algorithms for identifying Lie algebras and orbits, as well as a simple framework for analyzing structured Pauli generating sets. We also show in an elementary way that Clifford subgroups generated by transvections provide 3-designs for the corresponding Pauli Lie groups. We illustrate the framework through structured examples from variational quantum algorithms, restricted quantum computation, many-body systems, and random circuits.

</details>


### [347] [Who Earns the Safety? Intervention-Aware Quantum Predictive Control with Safety Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09778)
*Yifan Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了干预感知变分量子可微预测控制(IA-VQC-DPC)方法，通过原-对偶干预预算训练VQC策略，并引入安全归属协议来分解安全层贡献。在BOPTEST建筑控制仿真中，量子策略在相同参数量下比经典策略更安全、更舒适，且干预感知训练显著降低策略原始违规和防护层依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有硬安全过滤器可以保证约束满足，但可能掩盖策略本身的安全能力——过滤器默默修复不称职的上游策略，导致后过滤成功度量的是过滤器而非策略本身。因此需要问：安全是由策略本身赢得的，还是由保护层提供的？

Method: (i) 在可微控制障碍函数(CBF)投影下，采用原-对偶干预预算训练紧凑变分量子电路(VQC)策略，惩罚对安全层的依赖；(ii) 提出安全归属协议，将执行轨迹的修正分解为CBF项和部署运行时防护项，并通过关闭防护的评估来压力测试策略。

Result: 在BOPTEST建筑控制仿真（5个种子，每种方法60个回合）中，干预感知训练显著降低了量子策略的原始预过滤违规和总安全层依赖（均p<0.0001），且无显著能量回归。在约400参数预算下，量子策略比匹配的经典策略更安全、更舒适。关闭防护评估确认了改进是策略层面的，并揭示了一个有价值的负面结果：学习的可微能量头只有与分布感知运行时防护配对时才是安全的。

Conclusion: 安全归属协议是通用的，不限于量子策略和建筑领域。干预感知训练能有效提升策略自身的安全能力，而非依赖外部过滤器。

Abstract: Hard safety filters are increasingly placed downstream of learned controllers to guarantee constraint satisfaction at run time. Yet a filtered controller that never violates a constraint may still have learned nothing about safety: the filter can silently repair an incompetent upstream policy, so that post-filter success measures the filter, not the policy. We argue that safe policy learning should ask who earns the safety - the policy or its protective layers - and we make this question measurable. We introduce Intervention-Aware Variational Quantum Differentiable Predictive Control (IA-VQC-DPC), which (i) trains a compact variational quantum circuit (VQC) policy under a primal-dual intervention budget that penalizes reliance on a differentiable Control-Barrier-Function (CBF) projection, and (ii) is evaluated with a safety-attribution protocol that decomposes the executed-trajectory correction into a CBF term and a deployment runtime-guard term, and stress-tests the policy with guard-off evaluation. On closed-loop, high-fidelity BOPTEST building-control emulators (5 seeds, 60 episodes per method), intervention-aware training significantly lowers the quantum policy's raw pre-filter violation and total safety-layer reliance (both p < 10^-4) with no significant energy regression; at an equal approximately 400-parameter budget the quantum policy is significantly safer and more comfortable than a matched classical policy. Guard-off evaluation confirms the improvement is policy-level and exposes a valuable negative result: a learned differentiable energy head is only safe when paired with a distribution-aware runtime guard. The attribution protocol is general beyond quantum policies and buildings.

</details>


### [348] [Operation Mpemba effect: Breakdown of resource-Markovianity of free dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09790)
*Tian-Ren Jin,Yu-Ran Zhang,Heng Fan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出资源-马尔可夫性概念，将Mpemba效应（初始远离平衡态的系统反而更快弛豫）操作化地定义为弛豫操作对资源-马尔可夫性的破坏，从而在一般资源理论中实现了与度量无关的操作性表征，并揭示了量子资源、非马尔可夫性与Mpemba效应之间的深层关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Mpemba效应的刻画往往依赖于特定的距离或资源度量，缺乏与度量无关的操作性定义。作者希望从量子资源理论的角度，给出一个普适的操作性表征框架。

Method: 引入资源-马尔可夫性（resource-Markovianity）这一概念，将量子马尔可夫性扩展到量子资源理论中，并将Mpemba效应定义为弛豫操作破坏资源-马尔可夫性的现象，同时提出基于资源-非马尔可夫性度量的定量刻画方法。

Result: 建立了与度量无关的Mpemba效应操作性表征框架；以态的可区分性Mpemba效应（关联量子马尔可夫性）和热majorization Mpemba效应为例进行了验证。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了量子资源、非马尔可夫性与Mpemba效应之间的深层关联。

Abstract: The Mpemba effect refers to faster relaxation of states that are initially farther from equilibrium, yet its characterization is often tied to a chosen distance or resource measure. We introduce resource-Markovianity, an extended concept of quantum Markovianity to quantum resource theories, and formulate the resource Mpemba effect operationally as the breaking of resource-Markovianity by a relaxation operation. This yields a measure-independent operational characterization of resource Mpemba effects in general resource theories, together with quantitative characterizations based on resource-non-Markovianity measures. We illustrate the framework with the Mpemba effect for distinguishability of states, due to its relation to quantum Markovianity, and with the thermomajorization Mpemba effect from an operational perspective. These results reveal a deep interplay between quantum resources, non-Markovianity, and the Mpemba effect.

</details>


### [349] [On the viability of Transatlantic Quantum Entanglement Distribution using Combined Satellite and Stratospheric Relay Nodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09805)
*Kimia Mohammadi,Paul J. Godin,Thomas Jennewein*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了跨大西洋6500公里地面距离的量子纠缠分发链路架构，发现由低轨卫星（LEO）上的纠缠光子源和两个高空气球平台（HAPs）上的无源光学中继组成的混合架构，在无需量子存储器和中继器的条件下，能实现最高的纠缠分发速率（每年约5×10^6个安全密钥比特），比单颗中轨卫星（MEO）配1米口径地面接收机的方案高出近两个数量级，并降低了系统复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索建立全球量子网络的可行路径，研究跨大西洋（6500公里）量子纠缠分发的链路架构，寻找无需量子存储器和中继器的当前技术条件下的最优方案。

Method: 定义了多种自由空间链路配置（涉及卫星和HAPs），基于链路预算、空间辐射、轨道特性和系统复杂度进行分析比较，评估了混合架构（LEO卫星+HAPs光学中继）与单颗MEO卫星方案的性能差异。

Result: 混合架构（LEO卫星纠缠源+HAPs双无源光学中继）提供最高纠缠分发速率，搭配30厘米口径地面接收机可实现每年约5×10^6个安全密钥比特，比单颗MEO卫星配1米口径地面接收机方案高出近两个数量级。

Conclusion: 混合卫星-HAP架构在降低系统复杂度的同时，实现了可扩展且更易接入的长距离量子通信网络，具有有效载荷设计和发射要求的实际优势，并能通过HAP的机动性降低天气相关的链路中断。

Abstract: To explore the pathways toward establishing a global quantum network, we investigate several link architectures for transatlantic quantum entanglement distribution over a 6,500 km ground distance. We define free-space link configurations involving satellites and stratospheric high altitude platforms (HAPs), using today's technology and without relying on quantum memories and repeaters. Considering link budgets, space radiation, orbital characteristics, and system complexity we find that a hybrid architecture consisting of an entangled photon source located on a low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite supported by two passive optical relays located on HAPs provides the overall highest entanglement distribution rate. In addition, the satellite HAP architecture offers practical advantages in payload design and launch requirements, and the ability to lower the weather-related link interruptions assuming some maneuverability of HAPs. Overall, this hybrid configuration yields on the order of 5X10^6 secure key bits per year using 30 cm aperture ground receivers, nearly two orders of magnitude higher than achievable with a single MEO satellite and 1 m aperture ground receivers. Our results highlight the major benefits of hybrid satellite HAP architectures by reducing system complexity while enabling scalable and more accessible long-range quantum communication networks.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [350] [A Landscape of Cosmological Decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07663)
*S. Shajidul Haque,Bret Underwood*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，用来评估早期宇宙的量子到经典转变。作者发现，受当前观测约束的绝热、近高斯、近标度不变的原始微扰，其混合态仍有未受约束的自由度（纯度和动量方差），这些参数揭示了一个统一的混合态几何景观。研究发现：穿越Glauber-Sudarshan P函数正定阈值需要环境主动向系统注入动量；增强的动量方差在辐射时期动态激发引力势的衰减模，该衰减模虽在CMB时间相干性上消失足够快，但其初始振幅对退相干模型施加了严格的引力非线性约束——完全排除了退相干热态，而长波长发散将振幅对角线退相干模型限制在70个e-fold以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前观测对原始微扰的约束（绝热、近高斯、近标度不变）并不足以唯一确定其量子态，混合态中存在尚未被观测约束的自由度（纯度和动量方差）。作者旨在探索这些未被约束的自由度所允许的参数空间，统一不同退相干模型及其指针基，并对这些模型施加理论和观测约束。

Method: 采用Glauber-Sudarshan P函数作为分析工具，将混合态的参数空间（纯度、动量方差）映射到一个统一的几何景观中。通过分析P函数的正定性阈值，区分环境对系统的不同作用方式（动量注入 vs. 动量抑制）。研究动量方差增强对引力势衰减模的动力学影响，并结合宇宙微波背景时间相干性和引力非线性条件推导约束。

Result: (1) P函数正定性的阈值条件要求环境必须主动向系统注入动量而非抑制动量；(2) 增强的动量方差在辐射时期动态激发了引力势的衰减模；(3) 衰减模在CMB时间尺度上消失足够快，但其初始振幅受到引力非线性的严格约束；(4) 退相干热态被完全排除；(5) 振幅对角线退相干模型被限制在少于70个e-fold的膨胀内。

Conclusion: 建立了一个评估早期宇宙量子-经典转变的统一框架，能够映射和关联不同的退相干模型及其指针基，并对这些模型施加了严格的引力非线性约束和膨胀e-fold数限制。

Abstract: Current observations constrain primordial perturbations to be adiabatic, approximately Gaussian, and nearly-scale invariant. However, a generic mixed state satisfying these constraints has additional unconstrained degrees of freedom, which can be parameterized by the purity of the state and its momentum variance. This allowable parameter space reveals a unified geometric landscape of mixed states, allowing us to map and relate distinct models of decoherence and their respective pointer bases. Crossing the threshold of a regular, positive-definite Glauber-Sudarshan $P$-function requires the environment to actively inject momentum into the system, rather than suppress it. This enhanced momentum variance dynamically sources the decaying mode of the gravitational potential in the radiation era. While the decaying mode vanishes fast enough to preserve the temporal coherence of the Cosmic Microwave Background, its initial amplitude places severe theoretical constraints on decoherence models to avoid gravitational non-linearities. These non-linearity bounds definitively rule out decohered thermal states, while long-wavelength divergences restrict amplitude-diagonal decoherence models to fewer than 70 $e$-folds of inflation. Altogether, we present a unifying framework for evaluating the quantum-to-classical transition of the early universe.

</details>


### [351] [Spacetime Bartnik Mass Positivity and Temporal Monotonicity for Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07781)
*Lars Andersson,Marcus Khuri,Marc Mars,Walter Simon*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文定义了巴特尼克型拟局部质量，证明了其在两类黑洞相关区域上的正性和时间单调性：一是具有表观视界边界的紧致类空超曲面，二是包含表观视界的非紧致渐近平坦末端超曲面；同时证明了在相关演化场景中该质量随时间单调不减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为黑洞相关区域建立具有正性和时间单调性的巴特尼克型拟局部质量定义，以更好地描述黑洞的局部引力能量。

Method: 通过定义拟局部质量，并分别对两类域（紧致带视界边界、非紧致渐近平坦含视界）证明其严格正性；在演化场景中证明其时间单调不减性。

Result: 证明了该拟局部质量在两类域上的严格正性，以及在演化过程中的时间单调不减性。

Conclusion: 所定义的巴特尼克型拟局部质量具有良好的物理性质（正性和时间单调性），可用于黑洞局部能量分析。

Abstract: We define a quasilocal mass of Bartnik type, and establish its positivity and temporal monotonicity properties for two classes of domains associated with black holes. More precisely, we first show that the quasilocal mass is strictly positive for spacelike hypersurfaces that are: compact with apparent horizon boundary or noncompact with asymptotically flat ends and containing an apparent horizon in any admissible extension. Secondly, we show that the quasilocal mass is monotonically nondecreasing in time within evolutionary scenarios related to the two aforementioned settings.

</details>


### [352] [Does Eternal Inflation Violate the Smeared Null Energy Condition?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08003)
*Dong-Hui Yu,Yong Cai*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究永恒暴胀中随机向上涨落导致的哈勃参数增加是否违反涂抹零能量条件（SNEC）。通过福克-普朗克方程和单轨迹分析，发现系综漂移受慢滚参数和半经典压制边界约束，且存在强时间尺度层级（N_SNEC >> N_BR），表明在SNEC界被数学上逼近之前，引力反反应已使背景时空假设失效。结论：标准随机扩散驱动的永恒暴胀在半经典慢滚区域内不会固有地导致SNEC违反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 永恒暴胀中存在稀有的随机向上涨落，会局部增加哈勃参数，这与涂抹零能量条件（SNEC）对负能量沿零测地线积累的半局域界存在表观矛盾。本文旨在探究这类量子诱导的自复制过程是否违反SNEC。

Method: 采用正则单场暴胀模型，利用福克-普朗克方程分析哈勃参数的系综漂移行为，并进行单轨迹分析研究时间尺度层级关系。

Result: 哈勃参数系综漂移受慢滚参数和半经典压制的参数边界约束；单轨迹分析揭示N_SNEC >> N_BR的强时间尺度层级，说明在SNEC界被逼近前引力反反应已使背景时空假设失效。

Conclusion: 标准随机扩散驱动的永恒暴胀在半经典慢滚区域内不会固有地导致SNEC违反。

Abstract: The smeared null energy condition (SNEC) imposes a semilocal bound on the negative energy accumulated along null geodesics. In eternal inflation, rare stochastic upward fluctuations of the inflaton locally increase the Hubble parameter, creating an apparent tension with the SNEC. Focusing on a canonical single-field model, we investigate whether this quantum-induced self-reproduction violates the SNEC. Using the Fokker-Planck equation, we demonstrate that the ensemble drift of the Hubble parameter is parametrically bounded by slow-roll parameters and semiclassical suppression. Furthermore, a complementary single-trajectory analysis reveals a strong timescale hierarchy, $N_{\rm SNEC} \gg N_{\rm BR}$. This indicates that even for rare upward stochastic excursions, gravitational backreaction invalidates the background spacetime assumption long before the SNEC bound can be mathematically approached. We conclude that while standard stochastic diffusion drives eternal inflation, it does not inherently lead to SNEC violations within the semiclassical slow-roll regime.

</details>


### [353] [Degenerate Geometries as Matter-Free Physical Configurations in General Relativity: Three Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08022)
*Juri Dimaschko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种具有虫洞拓扑的退化时空构型（Rindler虫洞、Klinkhamer虫洞和Schwarzschild-Klinkhamer虫洞），在Einstein-Palatini-Cartan框架下证明它们是无物质的，并指出退化几何构成广义相对论构型空间的一个独立分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究退化时空构型是否能在无奇异物质（如 exotic matter）的条件下维持虫洞拓扑结构，并厘清退化几何与非退化几何之间的关系。

Method: 通过Rindler、Minkowski和Schwarzschild真空度规的分支坐标变换构造三种退化虫洞构型；采用Einstein-Palatini-Cartan形式体系分析其物质含量；检验从非退化时空到退化无物质构型的极限过渡是否存在。

Result: 三种退化虫洞构型在Einstein-Palatini-Cartan框架下均无物质场；它们表现出不同的物理效应（从纯拓扑结构到真实引力效应）；所有情形均不存在从非退化时空到退化无物质构型的极限过渡。

Conclusion: 退化几何不同于需要奇异物质的薄壳模型虫洞，构成广义相对论构型空间中一个独立的、无物质的分支。

Abstract: We examine three degenerate spacetime configurations with wormhole topology, obtained via branching coordinate transformations of the Rindler, Minkowski, and Schwarzschild vacuum metrics.These configurations are, respectively, a Rindler wormhole with a planar throat, and the Klinkhamer and Schwarzschild Klinkhamer wormholes with spherical throats. Within the framework of the Einstein Palatini Cartan formulation, we demonstrate that these degenerate configurations are matter free. In this regard, they differ fundamentally from their nondegenerate wormhole counterparts in the thin shell model, which require exotic matter. Nevertheless, the degenerate configurations considered here exhibit distinct physical manifestations, ranging from purely topological structures to genuine gravitational effects. Furthermore, in all three examples, we demonstrate the absence of a limiting transition from the non-degenerate spacetime to the matter free degenerate configuration. This suggests that degenerate geometries constitute an independent sector of the configuration space of general relativity.

</details>


### [354] [Warped Product Einstein Manifolds in Four Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08047)
*Jack C. M. Hughes,Joudy F. Jamal Beek,Fedor V. Kusmartsev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究四维(伪)黎曼流形上曲率张量的手性6×6矩阵表示，分析Warped积流形在1+3、2+2、3+1分解下的Einstein条件和Petrov分类，并讨论闭Riemann情形下的拓扑限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用曲率张量的手性矩阵表示，为Warped积流形的Einstein条件和Petrov分类提供统一的代数框架。

Method: 构建与Warped积分解对应的替代矩阵表示，通过相似变换关联两种表示，分类Einstein极限并推导Petrov类型。

Result: 3+1情形一般为I型，2+2为D型，1+3约束为O型；半共形平坦极限下闭Riemann Einstein Warped积必为平坦。

Conclusion: 该代数方法为Warped积流形的曲率结构和Einstein条件提供了系统分类，揭示了不同分解下的拓扑约束。

Abstract: On four-dimensional (pseudo-)Riemannian manifolds $\mathcal{M}$ the curvature tensor (viewed as an endomorphism on 2-forms) admits a chiral $6 \times 6$ matrix representation which decomposes into four $3 \times 3$ blocks. $\mathcal{M}$ is Einstein if and only if the off-diagonal blocks vanish. If the manifold is a warped product $\mathcal{M} = F \times_f B$, then there exists an alternative matrix representation relative to the decomposition of the 2-forms into spaces induced by the exterior algebra on both the base and the fiber. These two representations are not independent and a similarity transformation can be found between them. We construct these matrices and associated transformations for $1+3$, $2+2$ and $3+1$ warped products, giving classifications for the Einstein limits from this algebraic perspective. Using this, one can easily Petrov classify the Einstein warped products for each case considered: $3+1$ are generically type-\RNum{1}, $2+2$ are type-D while $1+3$ are constrained to be type-O. In the closed Riemannian case, there are a number of topological restrictions on these manifolds that we discuss: in the half-conformally flat limit, each of these Einstein warped products must be flat.

</details>


### [355] [Higher-dimensional quantum-corrected Oppenheimer-Snyder model with a cosmological constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08182)
*Shudi Jiang,Jianhui Lin,Xiangdong Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将高维量子Oppenheimer-Snyder模型扩展到含宇宙学常数的情况，重点研究了AdS时空中量子修正小黑洞的热力学性质，发现温度不再发散而趋于零，且热容表现出由量子修正引起的额外相变特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了探究宇宙学常数对高维量子Oppenheimer-Snyder模型的影响，特别是在扩展相空间形式下对比量子修正黑洞与经典黑洞的热力学行为差异。

Method: 将高维量子Oppenheimer-Snyder模型推广至包含宇宙学常数的情形，并在扩展相空间形式体系中分析AdS情况的热力学性质。

Result: 在AdS时空中，量子修正的小黑洞温度不再发散而是趋于零；热容表现出由量子修正诱导的额外相变特征。

Conclusion: 量子效应对黑洞热力学有着深远影响，改变了经典黑洞的温度发散行为并引入了新的相变机制。

Abstract: We extended the higher-dimensional quantum Oppenheimer-Snyder model to the case with a cosmological constant. For AdS case, we discuss its thermodynamic properties in extend phase space formalism and make comparison with classical black holes. For quantum-corrected small black holes in AdS spacetime, the temperature no longer diverges but tends to zero. Additionally, the heat capacity exhibits characteristic behavior indicative of an extra phase transition induced by quantum corrections, highlighting the profound impact of quantum effects on black hole thermodynamics.

</details>


### [356] [Non conservative conformal Killing gravity: coupling the dark sector with curvature and matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08213)
*Salvatore Capozziello,Carlo Alberto Mantica,Luca Guido Molinari*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Harada引力（包含Rastall引力作为特例），其中能量-动量张量不守恒。场方程被写为爱因斯坦方程形式，但源项补充了一个无散的共形Killing张量和一个与度规成比例、与标量曲率和能量-动量张量迹呈线性关系的张量。这些项可作为暗扇区的自然候选，并导致暗扇区与物质内容耦合。该场方程是Rastall引力的共形Killing扩展，并包含单模引力。在FRW背景下，CMB观测将参数限制为暗扇区仅与能量-动量张量迹耦合。给出了暗扇区张量的显式形式，以及弗里德曼方程和连续性方程。尘埃物质和暗流体的能量-动量张量之和守恒，尘埃能量密度随尺度函数的演化指数为-3/(1+τ)，受与暗流体耦合参数τ的修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Harada引力（Rastall引力的推广）中非守恒能量-动量张量框架下，暗扇区与物质耦合的机制，以及该理论在宇宙学中的可观测效应。

Method: 将Harada引力场方程重新表述为爱因斯坦方程形式，引入无散共形Killing张量和度规相关项作为暗扇区候选；在FRW背景下结合CMB观测限制参数，推导修改后的弗里德曼方程和连续性方程。

Result: CMB观测将参数限制为暗扇区仅与能量-动量张量迹耦合；给出了暗扇区张量的显式形式；尘埃物质与暗流体总能量-动量张量守恒；尘埃能量密度演化指数修正为-3/(1+τ)。

Conclusion: Harada引力的共形Killing扩展为暗扇区提供了自然候选，且宇宙学观测可行；暗扇区仅通过迹与物质耦合，修改了尘埃物质的演化行为。

Abstract: The so called Harada gravity with non conserved energy-momentum tensor is here taken into account. It includes Rastall gravity as a special case. The field equations are written as Einstein equations where the source is supplemented by a divergence-free conformal Killing tensor and a tensor proportional to the metric, linear in the scalar curvature and the trace of the energy-momentum tensor. These terms can be natural candidates for dark sector and give rise to a coupling of the dark sector with the matter content. The field equations are the conformal Killing extension of Rastall gravity, and include Unimodular gravity. In a Friedmann-Robertson-Walker background, the Cosmic Microwave Background restricts parameters so that the dark sector only couples with the trace of the energy-momentum tensor. The explicit form of the tensor for the dark sector is found, and the Friedmann and continuity equations are presented, with a standard cosmological analysis. The sum of energy-momentum tensors of dust matter and of dark fluid is conserved, and the dust energy density evolves with the scale function with exponent -3/(1+tau), modified by the coupling tau with the dark fluid.

</details>


### [357] [Stimulated Emission from Boson Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08220)
*Yu An,Xian-Hui Ge,Yun-Gui Gong,Yun-Long Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文发现超辐射云（超轻玻色子围绕旋转黑洞形成的“引力原子”）可通过受激辐射机制放大引力波，其放大效应可使信号强度提升数个数量级，填补现有探测器灵敏度空白，为探测超轻粒子和克尔时空环境提供新窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波信号极其微弱，这是当前及下一代探测器的核心挑战。虽然超辐射云已被认为是超越标准模型物理的探针，但它们主动发射和调制引力辐射的能力尚未被充分研究。

Method: 将玻色子云与随机引力波背景的相互作用形式化，推导了支配该放大的严格选择定则和阈值条件；通过受激辐射机制类比天体物理脉泽（maser）进行分析。

Result: 发射率对玻色子质量有临界依赖，相比自发过程可实现数个数量级的增强；这些放大信号能弥合地面干涉仪与脉冲星计时阵列之间的灵敏度差距。

Conclusion: 超辐射云可有效增强原本无法探测的信号，为探索超轻场和克尔时空环境提供新的观测前沿。

Abstract: Gravitational-waves from astrophysical sources are characterized by their extreme faintness, which remains a primary obstacle for both current and next generation detectors. While rotating black holes dressed in superradiant clouds of ultralight bosons are recognized as promising probes of physics beyond the Standard Model, their capacity to actively emit and modulate gravitational radiation remains largely unexamined. Here we demonstrate that these gravitational atoms can function as natural amplifiers of gravitational-waves via a stimulated emission mechanism analogous to astrophysical masers. By formalizing the interaction between the bosonic cloud and an ambient stochastic gravitational-wave background, we establish the rigorous selection rules and threshold conditions that govern this amplification. Our analysis reveals that the emission rate depends critically on the boson mass, potentially yielding an enhancement of several orders of magnitude over spontaneous processes. For representative mass ranges, these amplified signals bridge the sensitivity gap between ground-based interferometers and pulsar timing arrays. These findings suggest that superradiant clouds can effectively boost previously undetectable signals, offering a novel observational frontier for exploring ultralight fields and the Kerr spacetime environment.

</details>


### [358] [Hawking Emission from Black Holes Evaporating toward Wormholes and the Accuracy of the WKB Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08351)
*Bekir Can Lütfüoğlu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文重新分析了两种黑洞家族（Simpson-Visser黑洞反弹几何和Casadio-Fabbri-Mazzacurati膜世界几何）在趋近宏观虫洞构型时的霍金辐射。通过直接数值散射替代WKB灰体因子近似，计算光子与无质量狄拉克通道的发射谱和积分光度，发现定性结论不变但定量结果差异显著：WKB在接近虫洞端点时会严重高估剩余光度，半衰期系数修正可达约85倍，表明需要直接数值计算而非屏障顶WKB估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对这两种可趋近虫洞构型的黑洞背景的霍金辐射分析依赖于WKB灰体因子近似，但其准确性在远离Schwarzschild极限时未经严格检验，尤其是在低温端点附近的蒸发率对低频灰体因子尾端高度敏感。

Method: 采用直接数值散射方法替代WKB近似，分别计算光子和无质量狄拉克通道的灰体因子，进而重新计算发射谱和积分光度，并与WKB结果进行定量对比。

Result: 定性上：趋近虫洞端点时黑洞冷却、总通量显著抑制、残余辐射以费米子为主。定量上：靠近Schwarzschild极限时WKB较准确，远离该极限时误差可很大，接近冷端点时误差可达数量级，WKB会严重高估剩余光度。固定参数下Simpson-Visser半衰期系数被低估了约85倍。

Conclusion: 对于趋近虫洞类端点的黑洞，可靠的蒸发率计算需要直接数值灰体因子，而非仅依赖屏障顶WKB估计。

Abstract: We revisit Hawking radiation from two black-hole families that can approach macroscopic wormhole configurations: the Simpson--Visser black-bounce geometry and the Casadio--Fabbri--Mazzacurati braneworld geometry. The earlier analysis of these backgrounds relied on WKB greybody factors. Here we replace that approximation by direct numerical scattering for the photon and massless Dirac channels and then recompute the emission spectra and integrated luminosities. The qualitative picture remains the same: as the wormhole endpoint is approached the black holes cool, the total flux is strongly suppressed, and the residual emission becomes increasingly fermion dominated. The quantitative picture, however, changes substantially. Close to the Schwarzschild limit the WKB estimates are reasonably accurate, but far from that limit the error can be large, and near the cold endpoint it can reach orders of magnitude. In particular, the WKB calculation can substantially overestimate the remaining luminosity precisely in the regime where the evaporation rate is most sensitive to the low-frequency tail of the greybody factors. In a fixed-parameter Simpson--Visser half-decay estimate, the direct-greybody luminosities increase the WKB lifetime coefficient by a factor of about 85. These results show that reliable evaporation rates for black holes evolving toward wormhole-like endpoints require direct numerical greybody factors rather than barrier-top WKB estimates alone.

</details>


### [359] [Frenet-Serret equations with variable proper acceleration in Minkowski spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08456)
*Ivan Perez-Roman,Michael R. R. Good,Yen Chin Ong,Haret C. Rosu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究闵可夫斯基时空中类时世界线的Frenet-Serret方程，考虑依赖于固有时变化的曲率和挠率，对应于非均匀固有加速度的相对论运动。通过Gram-Schmidt方法构建四维标架，将内禀Frenet-Serret参数与运动学量（固有加速度、四维急动度、四维加加速度）联系起来，并给出解析算例阐明非恒定加速度和挠率如何改变加速相对论运动的几何性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Frenet-Serret框架到含有时变曲率和挠率的类时世界线，以描述非均匀加速的相对论运动，并系统建立内禀几何参数与运动学量之间的对应关系。

Method: 采用Gram-Schmidt正交化方法从四维速度和其导数构建四维标架，将Frenet-Serret参数与固有加速度、四维急动度、四维加加速度等运动学量关联；随后考虑急动度不变量和挠率的简单解析情形，求解显式曲率轮廓并简化Frenet-Serret方程。

Result: 建立了Frenet-Serret参数与四维急动度、加加速度等运动学量之间的解析关系；通过解析算例获得了非恒定曲率和挠率下的显式曲率轮廓及简化Frenet-Serret方程。

Conclusion: 非恒定加速度和挠率会显著改变加速相对论运动的几何结构；本文的Frenet-Serret框架为描述含时变曲率和挠率的类时世界线提供了系统方法。

Abstract: We study Frenet-Serret equations for timelike worldlines in Minkowski spacetime with proper-time-dependent curvature and torsion. This corresponds to relativistic motion with non-uniform proper acceleration and, when torsion is included, to trajectories whose Frenet-Serret frame rotates beyond the acceleration plane. Using the Gram-Schmidt construction of the tetrad from the four-velocity and its derivatives, we relate the intrinsic Frenet-Serret parameters to kinematic quantities such as proper acceleration, four-jerk, and four-snap. We then consider simple analytic cases for the jerk invariant and torsion, obtaining explicit curvature profiles and reduced Frenet-Serret equations. These examples clarify how non-constant acceleration and torsion modify the geometry of accelerated relativistic motion.

</details>


### [360] [Curvature-induced scalarization of charged AdS black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08507)
*Chao-Ming Zhang,Yun Soo Myung,Lina Zhang,De-Cheng Zou,Fu-Wen Shu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了负宇宙学常数对爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-标量-高斯-邦内特理论中标量化现象的影响，揭示了RN-AdS黑洞在GB耦合下的标量不稳定性受BF界约束，发现GB⁺和GB⁻两种标量化分支，并证实了从标量化AdS黑洞到RN-AdS黑洞的二级相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究负宇宙学常数（AdS背景）如何影响高斯-邦内特标量化现象，特别是RN-AdS黑洞在标量扰动下的不稳定性条件，以及标量化黑洞的存在性与相变行为。

Method: 通过引入标量耦合常数η到GB项，分析RN-AdS黑洞在标量场扰动下的有效质量μ²_eff，运用Breitenlohner-Freedman（BF）界判据判定不稳定性阈值；对比不同η参数区间（正负值）的标量化分支结构，并利用吉布斯自由能分析相变性质。

Result: 1）标量化onset由BF界而非仅μ²_eff<0决定，η>2.25（Λ=-0.5）时出现AdS快子不稳定性；2）0<η<2.25时发生GB⁺自发标量化，η<0时出现单分支GB⁻标量化；3）GB⁺标量化在η_th≤η<2.25区间仅存在单分支（n=0基态分支）；4）吉布斯自由能表明从标量化AdS黑洞到RN-AdS黑洞的相变为二级相变。

Conclusion: AdS背景下GB标量化与平直时空有本质区别，BF界是关键约束条件；标量化黑洞仅存在单一稳定分支而非无限分支；相变是二级的。

Abstract: We investigate how a negative cosmological constant affects the Gauss-Bonnet (GB) scalarization in the Einstein-Maxwell-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet theory with a scalar coupling constant $η$ to GB term. We focus on the instability of Reissner-Nordström-AdS (RN-AdS) black holes under a scalar perturbation governed by an effective mass $μ^2_{\text{eff}}$ sourced by the GB term. Unlike the asymptotically flat spacetime case, the onset of scalarization is not merely determined by $μ^2_{\text{eff}} < 0$, but it is constrained by the Breitenlohner-Freedman (BF) bound. In case that the BF bound is violated ($η>2.25$ with $Λ=-0.5$), one may find AdS-tachyoinic instability. We find that for $0<η<2.25$, the GB$^+$ scalarization may be performed through spontaneous scalarization, while for $η<0$ the GB$^-$ scalarization is found to give the single branch of scalarized AdS black holes. For the GB$^+$ scalarization in $η_{th}\leη<2.25$ with $η_{th}$ threshold instability, we obtain the single branch ($n=0$ fundamental branch) of scalarized AdS black holes, contradicting to infinite branches. Finally, it is observed from Gibbs free energy that a phase transition from scalarized AdS black holes to RNAdS black hole occur natually and it is confirmed to be second-order.

</details>


### [361] [Shadow of Rotating Black Hole Immersed in Dark Matter Halo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08616)
*Yong-You Liu,Tian-Yun Ye,Zhen Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用纽曼-詹尼斯算法构建了浸没在广义双幂律暗物质晕中的旋转黑洞度规，分析了其视界结构、光子轨道和阴影性质，并利用Sgr A*的阴影观测对暗物质晕参数空间进行了约束，表明黑洞阴影观测为探测暗物质分布提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件视界望远镜对黑洞阴影的观测为探测强引力区域和黑洞环境提供了独特机会，而理论上星系中心黑洞周围应存在暗物质晕，可能对黑洞阴影产生可观测影响，因此有必要研究暗物质晕对黑洞阴影的修正效应。

Method: 从静态暗物质黑洞解出发，利用纽曼-詹尼斯算法构建旋转黑洞度规，得到带有暗物质修正质量函数的类克尔度规；随后分析视界结构、能层、不稳定球面光子轨道及阴影特性，并结合Sgr A*阴影观测数据进行参数约束。

Result: 暗物质晕通常会增大视界半径、将光子层向外推移并修正黑洞阴影，这些效应在快速旋转黑洞中更加显著；通过Sgr A*阴影观测，获得了暗物质晕参数空间的有效约束，并发现了晕参数、黑洞自旋和观测倾角之间的相关性。

Conclusion: 黑洞阴影观测为探测超大质量黑洞附近的暗物质分布提供了一种有前景的探测手段。

Abstract: The recent black hole shadow observations by the Event Horizon Telescope provide a unique opportunity to probe both the strong gravity regime and the environment surrounding supermassive black holes. Since dark matter halos are expected to exist around galactic-center black holes, they may leave observable signatures on black hole shadows. In this work, we investigate the spacetime structure, photon dynamics, and shadow properties of a rotating black hole immersed in a generalized double power law dark matter halo. Starting from a static dark matter black hole solution, we employ the Newman--Janis algorithm to construct its rotating counterpart and obtain a Kerr-like metric with a dark matter-modified mass function. We analyze the horizon structure, ergosphere, and unstable spherical photon orbits, and find that the dark matter halo generally enlarges the horizon, shifts the photon shell outward, and modifies the black hole shadow. The effects become more pronounced for rapidly rotating black holes. Using the shadow observation of Sgr A*, we further constrain the dark matter halo parameter space and identify correlations among the halo parameters, black hole spin, and observer inclination angle. Our results suggest that black hole shadow observations provide a promising probe of dark matter distributions near supermassive black holes.

</details>


### [362] [Hawking Radiation from the Dymnikova Regular Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08631)
*Milena Skvortsova*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究Dymnikova正则黑洞的霍金辐射，发现灰体因子变化不大，但霍金温度急剧下降导致光度被强烈抑制，黑洞趋近冷残余体，寿命是截止时间而非完全蒸发时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Dymnikova正则黑洞（用光滑de Sitter核替代中心奇点）的霍金辐射特性，探究其蒸发过程的物理图像与标准Schwarzschild黑洞的差异。

Method: 计算标准模型检验场和引力子的灰体因子，对比精确数值散射结果与WKB估计，利用所得谱估计绝热蒸发历史。

Result: 灰体因子阈值随几何趋近残余体仅轻微移动；霍金温度急剧下降主导了总光度的抑制；光子、轻费米子和引力子通道在终点附近均衰减，引力贡献始终次主导；残余无质量通量趋向费米子主导，因光子通道被更高效地抑制。

Conclusion: 黑洞仅渐近趋近冷残余体，因此所引用的寿命估计应理解为到达近极端构型的截止时间，而非完全蒸发时间。

Abstract: We study Hawking radiation of the Dymnikova regular black hole. This model replaces the central singularity by a smooth de Sitter core while remaining Schwarzschild-like far from the hole, and its black-hole branch ends in a cold extremal remnant. We compute the greybody factors of the Standard Model test fields and gravitons, compare the precise numerical scattering results with WKB estimates, and use the resulting spectra to estimate an adiabatic evaporation history. The main effect is not a dramatic change in the transmission probabilities: the greybody thresholds move only slightly as the geometry approaches the remnant. Instead, the rapid decrease of the Hawking temperature strongly suppresses the total luminosity. The photon, light-fermion and graviton channels all fade near the endpoint, with the gravitational contribution remaining subdominant. The residual massless flux becomes increasingly fermion dominated because the photon channel is suppressed more efficiently. The black hole approaches the cold remnant only asymptotically, so the quoted lifetime estimates should be interpreted as cutoff times to near-extremal configurations rather than as complete evaporation times.

</details>


### [363] [Forward Construction of Vacuum Initial Data with Borderline Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08716)
*Xuantao Chen,Sergiu Klainerman*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用自由数据形式化方法，结合基于有效均匀化的新规范条件，通过前向积分构造了爱因斯坦真空约束方程的解，特别构造了具有最小甚至边界衰减的解，并将用于构造导致 trapped surface 形成的短脉冲柯西数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有有限衰减（包括最小和边界衰减）的爱因斯坦真空约束方程的一般解，以服务于闵可夫斯基空间稳定性研究以及短脉冲柯西数据构造。

Method: 利用 \cite{CK25} 的自由数据形式化方法进行前向积分，并引入基于有效均匀化的新规范条件。

Result: 构造了具有有限衰减的解，包括最小衰减和边界衰减情形，这些解对应于 \cite{Shen23}、\cite{Shen24} 中与闵可夫斯基空间稳定性相关的设定。

Conclusion: 本文发展的技术将在后续工作中用于构造导致 trapped surface 形成的一般短脉冲柯西数据，从而推广 \cite{LiYu} 的结果。

Abstract: We make use of the free data formalism developed in \cite{CK25} to construct solutions of the Einstein vacuum constraint equations by integrating in the forward direction. This, together with a new gauge condition based on effective uniformization, allows us to construct general solutions with limited decay at spacelike infinity. In particular, we construct solutions with minimal and even borderline decay, as considered in \cite{Shen23}, \cite{Shen24} in connection with the stability of the Minkowski space. In a forthcoming paper, we make use of the techniques we develop here to identify and construct a general class of short-pulse Cauchy data that lead to the formation of trapped surfaces, extending the well-known result of \cite{LiYu}.

</details>


### [364] [Non-Perturbative Bounds on Cosmological Backreaction, the Non-Linear Scale, and Gauge-Invariant Mutual Information from the Matter Power Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08764)
*Bob Osano*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文将介观粗粒化框架应用于宇宙学微扰理论和反作用问题，推导了三个主要结果：(i)基于Gibbs-Bogoliubov不等式得到运动学反作用QD的非微扰下界；(ii)证明了介观累积展开的收敛半径等于物质功率谱的非线性尺度，解释了标准微扰论在k>kNL时失效的原因；(iii)推导了高斯物质场下元胞间互信息的精确表达式，并给出了反作用修正的度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将介观粗粒化框架应用于宇宙学微扰理论和反作用问题，解决标准微扰论在非线性尺度上的失效问题，并建立反作用效应的严格数学基础。

Method: 采用~Osano等人提出的介观粗粒化框架，结合Gibbs-Bogoliubov不等式推导反作用下界，利用KAM定理解释收敛半径，通过信息论方法计算互信息。

Result: (i)运动学反作用QD存在非微扰下界，不能低于线性微扰理论值；(ii)介观累积展开收敛半径为O(kNL^{-1})；(iii)高斯场下元胞互信息精确表达式为I(i,j)=-1/2 ln(1-r_ij^2)，ΛCDM模型下最近邻互信息约为0.10。

Conclusion: 建立了反作用效应的严格数学框架，为非线性宇宙学微扰理论提供了新的理论基础，同时为从观测数据中计算反作用修正提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We apply the mesoscopic coarse-graining framework of~\cite{OsanoMeso,OsanoExtensivity,OsanoPerturbation} to three problems in Cosmological Perturbation Theory and the backreaction debate. \textbf{(i)}~A non-perturbative lower bound on the kinematic backreaction $\QD$ in the Buchert equations, derived from the Gibbs--Bogoliubov inequality: $\QD$ cannot be suppressed below its linear-perturbation-theoryvalue, regardless of the degree of non-linearity, provided the system satisfies stability and temperedness. \textbf{(ii)}~The radius of convergence of the mesoscopic cumulant expansion equals $O(\kNL^{-1})$, the non-linear scale of the matter power spectrum, providing a KAM-theorem explanation for why standard perturbation theory fails at $k>\kNL$. \textbf{(iii)}~For a Gaussian matter field, the inter-cell mutual information is exactly $I(i,j)=-\tfrac{1}{2}\ln(1-r_{ij}^2)$, gauge-invariant at linear order and computable directly from the observed $P(k)$; for $Λ$CDM at $\ell=50\,\mathrm{Mpc}\,h^{-1}$, $I_{\rm NN}\approx 0.10$.The total mutual information gives a data-computable measure of the backreaction correction to the FRW free energy. The gauge proof holds at linear order; the KAM identification is exact; the backreaction bound rests on a stated conjecture.

</details>


### [365] [Line-of-sight acceleration in compact binaries with higher harmonics and eccentricity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08838)
*Soumen Roy,Justin Janquart*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文重新推导了视线加速度效应，并将其实现于包含进动和高阶模式的准圆形波形模型以及偏心波形模型中。利用该模型分析LIGO/Virgo第三次观测运行的部分GWTC事件，未发现显著的视线加速度证据，并指出高阶谐波间不一致处理会导致有偏结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接探测引力波为探索致密双星并合的起源提供了独特机会，但双星的形成通道仍存在激烈争论。部分双星可能形成于动力学环境或活动星系核中，这些环境中的双星会受到来自周围环境的视线加速度，从而在引力波信号中留下特征印记。已有模型对视线加速度的处理存在不一致性，需要更鲁棒的框架来准确探测这类环境信号。

Method: 重新推导视线加速度效应，将其实现于最先进的准圆形波形模型（包含进动和高阶模式）中，并首次一致性地将其应用于偏心波形模型的所有贡献谐波。利用该改进模型对LIGO/Virgo第三次观测运行的部分GWTC事件进行视线加速度推断分析。

Result: 在所分析的GWTC事件中未发现显著的视线加速度证据。同时表明，高阶谐波间对视线加速度的不一致处理会导致有偏的推断结论。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个鲁棒的框架，用于在当前及未来的引力波观测中揭示视线加速度效应，从而能够更精确地探测致密双星形成中的环境特征信号。

Abstract: Direct detections of gravitational waves provide a unique opportunity to probe the astrophysical origin of compact binary mergers. The formation channels of these systems remain highly debated, and a fraction may originate in dynamical environments or active galactic nuclei. Binaries formed in such environments are expected to experience line-of-sight acceleration from their surroundings, which can imprint characteristic signatures on the observed gravitational-wave signal. Here, we re-derive the line-of-sight acceleration effects and implement them in state-of-the-art quasi-circular waveform models with precession and higher-order modes. We also implement the corrections in eccentric waveform models, applying them consistently to all contributing harmonics. Using this model, we investigate the impact of these effects on the inference of line-of-sight acceleration and analyze a few interesting GWTC events observed during the third observing run of LIGO and Virgo. We find no substantial evidence for line-of-sight acceleration in these events. We also show that an inconsistent treatment of line-of-sight acceleration between higher harmonics can lead to biased conclusions. Our model provides a robust framework for uncovering line-of-sight acceleration in current and future gravitational-wave observations, enabling more accurate probes of environmental signatures in compact-binary formation.

</details>


### [366] [Electromagnetism from two matter spaces: mutual helicity and the nondegenerate completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08917)
*Hyeong-Chan Kim*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明，一般麦克斯韦场可在物质空间框架中由两个独立物质空间流表示：单流只能捕捉退化的螺旋性扇区（满足F∧F=0），而引入双流后，总场F=G^(1)+G^(2)中每个扇区各自退化但互螺旋性项恢复了被单流理论排除的不变量，进而重建了完整的电磁学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的单物质空间流表示只能处理退化的（F∧F=0）电磁场，无法描述一般麦克斯韦场。作者希望通过引入双流框架，将非退化的一般电磁场纳入物质空间形式体系中，并阐明其螺旋性结构。

Method: 将电磁场强度张量F分解为两个独立流对应的扇区场G^(1)和G^(2)之和，每个扇区场均为各自三维物质空间上二形式的拉回，满足G^(I)∧G^(I)=0。通过分析自螺旋性与互螺旋性的分解，以及变分原理，重建了带源的麦克斯韦方程。

Result: 双流框架成功恢复了一般电磁场的全部结构：F∧F=2G^(1)∧G^(2)作为扇区间互螺旋量出现；总螺旋性分解为两个自螺旋性贡献和一个互螺旋性项；仅当互螺旋性为零时场退化为单流扇区；变分原理恢复源项麦克斯韦方程。

Conclusion: 一般电磁学可通过两个独立物质空间扇区的最小耦合完成来重建，非退化麦克斯韦场必然携带非零互螺旋性。

Abstract: We show that generic Maxwell fields can be represented within the matter-space framework by introducing two independent matter-space flows. In the one-flow formulation the electromagnetic field strength is the pull-back of a two-form on a three-dimensional matter space and therefore satisfies $F\wedge F=0$, so that a single flow captures only a degenerate, helicity-carrying sector. The minimal completion is obtained by writing $$F=G^{(1)}+G^{(2)},$$ where each sector field $G^{(I)}$ is the pull-back of a matter-space two-form from an independent flow. Each sector is individually degenerate, $G^{(I)}\wedge G^{(I)}=0$, while the full field satisfies $F\wedge F=2G^{(1)}\wedge G^{(2)}$; the invariant excluded by the one-flow theory is thus recovered as an inter-flow quantity. We interpret this structure in terms of mutual helicity: the total helicity decomposes into two self-helicity contributions and a mutual term whose exterior derivative is $F\wedge F$. Hence a configuration with vanishing mutual helicity lies in the degenerate sector, whereas a nondegenerate Maxwell field necessarily carries a nontrivial mutual helicity. A variational principle for the total field recovers the sourced Maxwell equation, the two matter-space variations combining into the full equation whenever the sector kernels intersect trivially. Generic electromagnetism is thereby reconstructed as the minimal coupled completion of two individually degenerate matter-space sectors.

</details>


### [367] [Static axisymmetric Einstein spaces with a cosmological constant and the limitation of canonical Weyl coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09177)
*Sheref Nasereldin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明在四维静态轴对称真空度规中，标准Weyl坐标形式（W=ρ）仅在宇宙学常数Λ=0时局部允许；当Λ≠0时，面积函数不再是调和函数，因此标准Weyl形式不可行。Kottler度规给出了非零Λ情形下面积函数方程的简单显式例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Weyl形式通过将两个Killing轨道的面积函数识别为二维轨道空间上的调和坐标来获得，这一构造在Ricci平坦真空中有效，但在宇宙学常数非零的爱因斯坦空间中不再成立。本文旨在探讨非零Λ情形下广义正交传递静态轴对称度规的约化场方程。

Method: 考虑广义正交传递静态轴对称线元，推导约化的Einstein-Λ场方程，分析标准Weyl选择W=ρ的局部可允许性条件，并以Kottler度规作为显式例子。

Result: 标准Weyl选择W=ρ局部可允许当且仅当Λ=0；当Λ≠0时，面积函数不再是调和函数。Kottler度规给出了非零Λ情形下面积函数方程的最简单显式例子。

Conclusion: "Weyl度规不允许Λ≠0"这一论断仅在假设度规采用标准Weyl坐标形式时才是精确的。问题不在于静态性或轴对称性，而在于面积函数不再具有调和性。

Abstract: The canonical Weyl form for four-dimensional static axisymmetric vacuum metrics is obtained by identifying the area function of the two Killing orbits with a harmonic coordinate on the twodimensional orbit space. This construction is valid in Ricci-flat vacuum, but it is no longer available in Einstein spaces with nonzero cosmological constant. In this paper, we consider the generalized orthogonally transitive static axisymmetric line element and derive the reduced Einstein- $Λ$ field equations. We show that the canonical Weyl choice $W=ρ$ is locally admissible if and only if $Λ=0$. The Kottler metric gives the simplest explicit example of the resulting equation for the area function. Thus, the statement that "Weyl metrics do not allow $Λ\neq 0$ " is precise only when the metric is assumed to be in canonical Weyl coordinates. The issue is not staticity or axisymmetry, but rather the fact that the area function is no longer harmonic.

</details>


### [368] [Detectability to extreme mass ratio inspirals with alternative space-based detector networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09238)
*Chao Zhang,Gang Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了LISA、天琴、太极等空间引力波探测器组成的联合观测网络对极端质量比旋近(EMRI)信号的探测能力与参数约束效果。相比单探测器，多探测器网络可将天区定位精度提升最多两个数量级，一个月联合观测的参数约束效果可与LISA单独一年观测相当。此外，分析了同时存在两个EMRI信号时的区分能力与参数测量精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRIs是空间引力波探测的重要目标，LISA、天琴、太极等任务预计2035年左右运行，需要研究多探测器联合观测是否能提升对EMRI信号的探测与参数估计能力，特别是在信号重叠等复杂场景下的表现。
